Needs a physical iPhone: every visual and animated effect (the leather grain and foil shader, the highlight sweep and tilt,
the cover swing, the page flip and its shading, the stamp landing, the pinch zoom, the typewriter, the map pins), the camera,
location permission and the geofence in the real world, haptics, Reduce Motion, VoiceOver, the native action sheets and alerts,
keyboard behavior, and the flows in `DEVICE-CHECKLIST.md`. Jest checks the math and that components render; it cannot see.

===== DECISIONS.md =====

# Decisions (mobile)

Judgment calls made while building the Nostos app from the Last Call app. Open questions: `../OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`.

- **Navigation: three tabs.** Passport (home), Stamp (a large center button that opens the composer) and Map. No feed, no bell: with
  `SOCIAL_ENABLED` off (the default) nothing social exists in the app. A gear on the Passport tab opens Settings. Edit profile moved
  into Settings (the Passport tab is the book now), and the Private account switch is hidden in personal mode.
- **The "checking account" state** (`auth/account-state.ts`, `features/account/account-gate.tsx`): while auth or `/api/me`
  is loading the screens say "Checking your account"; when `/api/me` failed they offer Try again; only a real guest sees
  "Sign in". This fixes the known device bug where a slow load read as signed out.
- **The place list comes from `/api/places/nearby`,** computed on the server from the phone's position, but the server
  checks the geofence again on the stamp. The composer picks the place for you only when exactly one geofence has you inside.
- **Song picking** goes through a tiny module (`features/stamps/song-draft.ts`) instead of route params, because the picker
  is a modal over the composer. The picker needs a network; with none it shows an error with Try again.
- **The age gate is on the phone first.** The birthdate is typed as MM/DD/YYYY, auto-formatted, checked locally, and an
  under-21 value is never sent or stored. Sign-up sends it once (the server checks again and keeps nothing). The guest
  gate checks and discards it. Nothing is remembered about a refusal (OPEN-QUESTIONS L2b).
- **Going public needs a confirm alert** (`goingPublicAlert`) that lists what changes: all stamps (photos, captions, songs)
  and every place, to anyone, signed in or not, including past stamps. "Keep private" does nothing.
- **Report reasons** are labels with categories: underage, stalking and "not at this place" were added. A report on a stamp
  you reported hides it from you for the session.
- **Distances** always go through `formatDistance`: from 1000 m up it is kilometers ("1.8 km").
- **Error reporting is off.** `lib/report-error.ts` loads Sentry only when the build sets `EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED=1` and an
  https DSN; no DSN is committed, the SDK is lazy-loaded, and events are scrubbed of user, request and breadcrumbs. The
  native config plugin is deliberately not in `app.json` (it needs an auth token to upload source maps). Turning Sentry on
  needs the plugin, a development build and the privacy answers in OPEN-QUESTIONS L5.
- **Dependencies removed** because nothing uses them any more: the bottom-sheet library, the date-time picker, blur, glass
  effect and `@expo/ui`. `DEPENDENCIES.md` lists them.
- **Theme tokens only.** The lint rules from Last Call stay: no raw colors or sizes outside `src/theme`, no emoji, SF Symbols.

## Session 2: the passport book
- **One position number drives the whole book.** `pos` (the spread position, 2.37 is 37 percent through a turn) is a Reanimated
  shared value; each turning page reads `clamp(pos - k, 0, 1)`. Two turning pages are always mounted, one lying on each side, over
  the pages they reveal. When a flip settles, JS state catches up to a position that is already right, so nothing flickers. Holding a
  page halfway is not a special state: the page follows the finger and stays where it stops.
- **Worklet math lives in files that start with `"worklet";`.** A plain imported function cannot be called on the UI thread, and Jest
  runs everything on one thread, so it cannot catch a missing one. `worklet-files.test.ts` reads the files and fails instead.
- **The closed cover is page-sized and the open book scales down to fit.** A phone is not wide enough for two pages at the cover's
  size, so the stage shrinks (`openScale`) as the cover opens. Pages are drawn at cover size and scaled, so text is sharp.
- **Stamps are SVG, not Skia.** `react-native-svg` has text on a path, renders in Jest, and is enough for flat brass ink. Skia is used
  where it earns it: the leather grain shader and the foil gradient. Ink "not taking" is a scatter of small dots painted in the
  paper's color, not true transparency.
- **A stamp is a pure function of its place** (`stamp-design.ts`): shape and figure from the place id, border family from the
  neighborhood id, so one neighborhood reads as a set. Variety is shape and design, never color.
- **The visa spread's pages are half the screen wide, so the caption, date and song sit in a typed slip under the spread**, not on the
  pages: at that size a 280 character caption does not fit a page. The scrapbook layout is saved as shares of the page.
- **The caption types out over a full, invisible copy of itself**, so the text wraps once and a screen reader has all of it from the
  start. With Reduce Motion it appears at once.
- **Photos come from the in-app camera only** (`expo-camera`, up to six). The library path was removed from the composer (it stays
  for the profile photo). A stamp's photos are proof you were there.
- **The location note on the composer says what is true:** the server saves the phone's position with the stamp, to audit the check,
  and never shows it. The earlier plan to say "never stored" would have been false. OPEN-QUESTIONS L6.
- **The map draws only places in view, at most 250,** lit ones first. Your position is a small bone ring, not the system's blue dot
  (nothing in the interface is another hue).
- **First run is remembered in `expo-secure-store`** (one key), so there is no extra storage dependency.
- **The Design gallery is development only:** its routes are behind `Stack.Protected guard={__DEV__}`, the screens redirect home when
  `__DEV__` is false, and the entries (Welcome, Settings) are conditional on `__DEV__`. It uses made-up data and no server.
- **The web pages follow the palette** (`backend/src/app/globals.css`): the old status colors all map to brass, dim brass or bone. The
  legal text is unchanged because it is a legal text (OPEN-QUESTIONS L7).
- **Nothing visual has been run on a phone.** See `DEVICE-CHECKLIST.md` and the session review.


## 2026-10-02 Codex integration

- The authorized combined working tree remains uncommitted on build-codex. Claude's source branch and checkout are not modified.
- `stageTransform` is explicitly workletized at function scope. Its regression uses the actual installed Babel plugin and executes
  the serialized code, rather than relying on Reanimated mocks. Native rendering and motion remain unverified.
- The development API fallback is port 3001 to match this lane's backend dev script; `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL` still takes precedence.
  A regression checks both LAN and localhost against backend/package.json. Release URL requirements are unchanged.

- Age eligibility uses `America/New_York` calendar parts, matching the backend rather than device-local dates. Summer and winter
  midnight boundaries are tested in independent Node processes under UTC, New York, Tokyo and Honolulu. Birthdates are still
  absent from refused responses; this fixes an implementation boundary and makes no legal decision.


===== ../OPEN-QUESTIONS.md =====

# Open questions

Decisions Nostos needs from the user (or from the lawyer session). Each has a default already built, so nothing is
blocked. Legal questions are never decided by the builder.

## Product

| # | Question | Default built | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Ratings.** `photo.rating` stays in the table and becomes optional. It is not shown in the UI for v1. Do you want it back, or dropped in a later migration? | Stored when sent, never shown. | `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` |
| 2 | **Follow requests show requesters in the limited view** (handle and badge, no name or photo) when they are private and you do not follow them. Show name and photo? | Limited, per the privacy rules. | `backend/src/server/services/people.ts#listFollowRequests` |
| 3 | **GPS spoofing.** A stamp is only as trustworthy as the phone's reported location. Worth a server-side signal later (velocity checks)? | Geofence on the submitted coordinates only. | `backend/src/server/services/photos.ts` |
| 4 | **DESIGN.md section 8 was not in the build prompt** ("DESIGN.md: see section 8"). DESIGN.md was written from the prompt's other sections, with an empty "Chosen reference". Fill that section to enable phase 5. | Phase 5 skipped until filled. | `DESIGN.md` |
| 5 | **Source repo location.** `~/Downloads/lastcall-feature-social` did not exist. The fork was made from `/Users/charlesjinn/lastcall` (branch main, clean, has backend/, mobile/, HANDOFF.md). Confirm that is the right source. | Forked from `/Users/charlesjinn/lastcall`. | n/a |
| 6 | **Reputation.** The accuracy score and consensus are removed, so nothing recomputes reputation. The column stays (default 0.5), visible only to the user and moderators. Keep it for moderation use or drop it? | Kept, moderator-only, no job. | `backend/prisma/schema.prisma` |
| 7 | **The place catalog.** `pnpm venues:import` still imports only bars, pubs and nightclubs from OpenStreetMap in the East Village and Lower East Side. Nostos is about any place in NYC (parks, cafes, landmarks, shops). Which categories and which area should the real catalog cover, and who may add a place? The seed includes six landmarks by hand. | Bars only through the importer; manual or seeded places otherwise. | `backend/src/lib/osm.ts` |
| 8 | **Stamp frequency.** One stamp per person per place per 15 minutes, 20 an hour. For a passport you may want "once per place per day", or repeat visits shown differently. | The old 15 minute cooldown. | `backend/src/server/services/geofence.ts` |

## Legal (for the lawyer session; nothing here is decided by the builder)

| # | Question | Default built |
|---|---|---|
| L1 | **Song metadata licensing.** Stamps store song title, artist and album art URL from Apple's public iTunes Search API, proxied through our backend, no audio playback. Are the iTunes Search API terms and artwork usage acceptable for an app that stores and displays this metadata? Is attribution required? | Metadata only, proxied, no user identifiers sent, short timeout and rate limit. |
| L2 | **Age gate.** A birthdate check blocks under-21s at signup and guest browsing and stores nothing about them. What exactly may and must we do (storing a refusal flag to stop retries, COPPA and state rules, wording)? | Nothing stored about a refused attempt, so a retry is possible. |
| L3 | **NY SAFE for Kids Act.** The feed is strictly chronological from followed accounts, no ranking, no personalization, to stay outside "addictive feed". Is that sufficient? | Chronological feed only. |
| L4 | **Geolocation data.** Coordinates are used for the geofence check and stripped from uploads; stamp coordinates are not shown to viewers. What disclosures does the privacy policy need? | Policy text carried over from Last Call and needs a rewrite for Nostos. |
| L2b | **Age gate retries.** Nothing is stored about someone who fails the 21+ check, so they can try again with a different birthdate. Is that acceptable, or must we remember a refusal (for example a flag on the device)? The app stores nothing and never sends an under-21 birthdate to the server. | No memory of a refusal. |
| L5 | **Sentry and privacy labels.** Error reporting is built but off (needs `EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED=1` and a DSN at build time). If you turn it on, does the App Store privacy label need "Diagnostics", is a data processing agreement needed, and where should the Sentry project live (EU or US)? Events carry no user, request or breadcrumbs. | Off. No DSN in the repo. |

| L6 | **What the stamp screen says about your position.** The server saves the phone's coordinates with each stamp (columns `lat`, `lng` on the `photo` table) as the record of the geofence check; they are never in any payload. The screen says: "When you stamp, our server checks where your phone is. That position is saved with the stamp and is never shown in the app." Is that wording enough, how long may the coordinates be kept, and should they be dropped after the check instead? (Related to L4. The nearby-places and place-page endpoints do not store the position.) | Saved with the stamp, never shown. Text in `mobile/src/features/stamps/stamp-flow.ts` (`HONESTY`). |
| L7 | **The privacy policy and terms still describe the social layer** (private and public accounts, follow requests, a feed, likes, blocking). Nostos is strictly personal for this version: with `SOCIAL_ENABLED` off none of that exists and only you can see your passport. The pages were only recolored to black and brass this session; the text is unchanged because it is a legal text. It needs a rewrite for personal mode, and the "Who can see what" section should say only you. | Text unchanged. Pages: `backend/src/app/privacy/page.tsx`, `terms/page.tsx`. |

  live-content migration is deployed? The migration stops without inventing content or changing those records
  (`backend/prisma/migrations/20261003140000_live_stamp_content/migration.sql:42`). The isolated Codex databases migrated

# App Store listing: Nostos (draft)

Everything to paste into App Store Connect, as a first draft. Placeholders are marked **REPLACE**. Age rating, privacy
labels and review notes touch legal questions that are open in `../OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`; treat them as drafts until a
lawyer has seen them. Nothing here has been submitted.

## Basics

| Field | Value |

## Promotional text (170)

Go somewhere in New York, get a stamp. Your profile is a passport of places, with a photo, a few words or a song from each.

## Description

Go somewhere. Get a stamp.

Nostos is a passport for New York. Every time you go somewhere, you can stamp it: a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix.
Your profile is your passport, a collection of every place you have been.

ONLY WHEN YOU'RE REALLY THERE
You can only stamp a place when your phone is inside its geofence. We check on our server, so every stamp is a real visit.

PRIVATE BY DEFAULT
Only people you approve see your stamps and places. Make your passport public if you want to, and Nostos tells you exactly
what that changes first.

YOUR FEED, IN ORDER
Home shows stamps from the people you follow, newest first. No ranking, no suggestions, nothing you didn't choose.

SAFE BY DESIGN
Report any stamp or account in two taps. Block anyone. Moderators review reports, and you can delete your account from the
app at any time. Nostos is for adults 21 and over.

## Keywords (100)

`passport,stamps,nyc,new york,places,check in,travel,photos,music,friends,private,memories`

## What's new (1.0)

First release.

## Privacy ("nutrition label"), draft

Tracking: **No.** No analytics, ads or tracking SDKs. Error reporting (Sentry) is off by default; if a build turns it on,
add "Diagnostics" (see `../OPEN-QUESTIONS.md` L5).

| Data type | Collected | Linked to user | Used for tracking | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Contact info: Email address | Yes | Yes | No | App functionality (account, sign-in) |
| Contact info: Name | Yes | Yes | No | App functionality (the display name on your passport) |
| Identifiers: User ID | Yes | Yes | No | App functionality |
| Location: Precise location | Yes | Yes | No | App functionality (confirm you are at a place when you stamp; the position is kept with the stamp for auditing and is never shown to anyone) |
| User content: Photos | Yes | Yes | No | App functionality (location data is stripped from the file) |
| User content: Other user content | Yes | Yes | No | App functionality (captions, the song you attach, likes) |
| Usage data: Product interaction | Yes | Yes | No | App functionality (who you follow and block) |
| Birthdate | **No** | | | Checked once for 21+ and never stored |

Location is when-in-use only. After account deletion, stamps are hidden and the account is anonymized.

## App Review information

- **Sign-in required?** No: reviewers can tap **Look around first** (a birthdate check) and look up public passports.
  Stamping, following and likes need an account.
- **Demo account** (geofence-exempt): Email `appreview@lastcall.dev`, Password **REPLACE**.
- **Notes for the reviewer:**

  > Nostos is a passport for New York: you stamp places you visit with a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix. A stamp
  > can only be made within the place's geofence, checked on our server. Because you are likely outside New York, the
  > demo account above is exempt from that check so you can try stamping from anywhere. Its stamps are visible only to
  > that account.
  >
  > To try it: sign in with the demo account, tap the Stamp tab, choose a place from the list, add a photo, a caption or a
  > song, and tap Stamp. Song search uses Apple's public iTunes Search API through our server; nothing plays.
  >
  > User-generated content: every stamp and profile can be reported (the "..." menu) and its author blocked. Reports go to
  > moderators, who can hide stamps and suspend accounts. The Terms prohibit objectionable content and abusive users, and
  > objectionable language is filtered on the server.
  >
  > Account deletion: Passport (gear) > Delete account.
  >
  > Location is requested with an explanation screen first, and only while the app is in use. Nostos is for people 21 and
  > older: sign-up and guest browsing ask for a birthdate, which is checked once and never stored.

- **Contact:** **REPLACE** name, phone, email.

===== DEVICE-CHECKLIST.md =====

# Device checklist

Things tests and bundling can't prove. Run them on a real iPhone in Expo Go against the local backend, freshly seeded
(`cd backend && pnpm db:seed && pnpm dev`; `cd mobile && npx expo start`). Password for every seed account:
`lastcall-dev` (or `DEV_SEED_PASSWORD`; the App Review account uses `APP_REVIEW_PASSWORD` if set).

Seed accounts: `avaa` (private, four requests waiting), `maya_r` (public, verified, most stamps), `theo_k`, `nia_s`,
`kofi_b` (private), `moth_hopeful` (private, follows no one), `blocker_blake` and `blocked_bea` (a blocked pair),
`mod_morgan` (moderator, one report in the queue), `admin`, `appreview` (stamps from anywhere, visible only to itself).

> Session 2 made Nostos strictly personal (`SOCIAL_ENABLED` off). The sections below about the feed, likes, follows, requests,
> other people's passports and Blocked users apply only when you run with the social layer on (backend `SOCIAL_ENABLED=1` and
> `EXPO_PUBLIC_SOCIAL_ENABLED=1`). With it off, start at "Book (session 2): phone checks" at the end of this file.

## First launch and the age gate
- [ ] Welcome screen: "Go somewhere. Get a stamp." with Create account, Sign in, Look around first. No mention of bars,
      vibes or venues.
- [ ] Look around first: asks your birthdate. Typing digits adds the slashes. A birthdate under 21 shows "Nostos is for
      people 21 and older." with no Continue button. Force-quit and reopen: it asks again (nothing was remembered).
- [ ] A birthdate that is 21 today lets you in. A guest sees Home with "A passport for New York" and Find people; the
      Stamp, Activity and Passport tabs ask to sign in.
- [ ] Create account: a Birthdate field replaces the old "I'm 21" checkbox. An under-21 birthdate stops on the phone.

## "Checking account" (known device bug)
- [ ] Sign in, then turn on Airplane Mode and relaunch: Passport and the Stamp screen say "Couldn't check your account"
      with Try again, never "Sign in". With the network back, Try again loads them.
- [ ] While signing in on a slow connection, the screens say "Checking your account", not "Sign in".

## Home feed
- [ ] As `maya_r`: stamps from people she follows, newest first. Pull to refresh. Scroll to the end: more load.
- [ ] As `moth_hopeful`: "Nothing here yet" and Find people. No stamps from strangers, no suggestions.
- [ ] Like a stamp: the heart fills at once and the count goes up; tap again to unlike. Your own stamps show the count
      but you can't like them.
- [ ] The "..." on someone else's stamp: Report stamp and Block @handle. On your own stamp there is no menu in the feed.
- [ ] Nothing anywhere says "Anonymous".

## Passport
- [ ] Your own: photo, name, handle, bio, Stamps / Places / Followers / Following counts, Edit profile, Follow requests
      (with the waiting count), a gear for Settings.
- [ ] Stamps view: a 3-column grid, newest first; photo tiles, and plain tiles with the caption or a note icon for
      stamps without a photo. Each tile names its place. Scrolling to the end loads more.
- [ ] Places view: a small dark map with a dot for every place, and a list with the stamp count; tapping a row opens the
      latest stamp there. Distances anywhere over 1000 m read in km ("1.8 km", never "1815 m").
- [ ] Another person's public passport: Follow becomes Following at once.
- [ ] A private passport you don't follow: handle, badge if verified, generic avatar, "This account is private" and
      Follow. Nothing else. Follow becomes Requested; tap again to cancel. A pending request still shows nothing.
- [ ] Verified badge: a small champagne diamond after the handle on `maya_r` (passport, feed, search, lists).
- [ ] The blocked pair: as `blocker_blake`, search for `blocked_bea`: no result; her passport is "not available".

## Making a stamp
- [ ] Tap Stamp (the middle tab). In Settings > Location make sure location is allowed. Near a place, it is listed with
      "You're here"; with exactly one place around, it is chosen for you.
- [ ] Add a photo (camera or library), a caption (the counter stops at 280), a song (search "bowie": results with art,
      title, artist; pick one; it shows with a remove button). Any one of the three is enough; with none, Stamp is off and
      the screen says what is missing.
- [ ] Stamp: it opens the new stamp. It appears at the top of your Stamps view and in your followers' feeds.
- [ ] Try a place that is not near you (in development, pick one from "pretend to be at" and then another):
      the server's message appears as written: "You need to be within 100 m of this place to stamp it (you're 140 m away)".
- [ ] Stamp the same place again within 15 minutes: a "try again in N min" message.
- [ ] Song search with Airplane Mode on shows an error with Try again, not a blank list.

## Stamp detail
- [ ] Photo (if any), caption, a song row, who and where, likes, how long ago. Tapping the author opens their passport.
- [ ] Yours: "..." has Edit and Delete from profile. Edit changes the caption and song only; Save is off until something
      changes; Cancel asks first. Delete asks, then the stamp is gone from your passport and everyone's feed for good.
- [ ] Someone else's: "..." has Report stamp (reasons include "Under 21 or looks underage", "Stalking or unwanted
      contact" and "Not at this place") and Block.
- [ ] A stamp that is not available (removed, hidden, a private account) says "This stamp isn't available" with Close.

## Edit profile and privacy
- [ ] Change the photo, name and bio (counter stops at 150). Save is off until something changes.
- [ ] Switch Private off and Save: an alert "Make your passport public?" lists what changes (all stamps and every place,
      to anyone, signed in or not; follows without asking). "Keep private" does nothing; "Make public" saves, then asks
      "Approve N pending requests?" when requests are waiting.
- [ ] Switch back to private: no alert is needed, and strangers lose access again.

## Follow requests, Activity, lists
- [ ] Approve and Decline remove the row at once; the count on the passport updates; "Approve all" asks first.
- [ ] Activity (its own tab): requests, new followers, and likes with the stamp's thumbnail (opens the stamp). No
      "featured" entries.
- [ ] Followers and Following lists open; on your own followers, Remove asks first.
- [ ] Settings: Following list, Blocked users (with Unblock), Privacy policy, Terms, Support, Sign out, Delete account.
      Moderation appears only for `mod_morgan` and `admin`.

## Moderation (as `mod_morgan`)
- [ ] Moderation: the queue has one stamp report; Hide, Dismiss and Restore each ask for an optional note. The Hidden tab
      lists the hidden stamp with Restore; the Log tab lists actions and says it is permanent. An underage or stalking
      report shows "Urgent" and sits at the top.

## Delete account
- [ ] Settings > Delete account: the list says stamps are removed, follows and likes gone. Needs the password. After
      deleting you land on the welcome screen with "Your account is deleted."

## No blank screens
- [ ] Open every screen above with a slow connection and with Airplane Mode: each shows a skeleton, a message or an error
      with Try again, never an empty screen. Swipe down on each modal to dismiss.

## Book (session 2): phone checks

- [ ] Cover: foil highlight follows a finger drag, follows tilt, shimmers when idle, and stops with Reduce Motion on.
- [ ] Open animation: the cover swings on its left edge, shades while turning, and the page under it is shadowed then clears. With Reduce Motion it crossfades.
- [ ] Bearer page: your photo or initial, name, handle, passport number, issue date and the two machine lines sit inside the page rule without clipping, at the smallest and largest text sizes; a long bio stops at three lines.
- [ ] Pages: a spread shows two pages with a dark fold down the middle, thin page edges on the outer sides that grow with the pages on that side, a page number in each bottom outer corner, faint guilloche behind the stamps and wave bands at the top. Stamps sit three to a page, slightly tilted and never colliding. A pinned stamp has a brass ribbon.
- [ ] Passport tab: opens on the closed cover with three counts under it and a gear at the top right. Tap the cover: it swings open on its left edge, the book slides to center and shrinks to fit two pages, and hands over to the bearer spread with no jump.
- [ ] Page flip: drag from the outer edge of a page and it follows the finger, shading as it goes edge-on; stop halfway and it stays halfway; let go past halfway or with a flick and it springs over, with a light haptic; let go before halfway and it springs back. Tap the outer edge to turn one page. Dragging or tapping the left edge of the first spread closes the book. Nothing turns when you drag from the middle of a page, and stamps stay tappable. With Reduce Motion on, pages crossfade.
- [ ] Page flip: Previous, Next and Close under the book work with VoiceOver, and the label reads "Pages 1 and 2".
- [ ] Stamps: each place has its own brass stamp (shape, figure, name on an arc or in lines, neighborhood underneath), the same every time you open the book. Places in one neighborhood share a border style. Ink looks slightly uneven with small gaps. Long names shrink and stay inside the ring. Nothing is any color but brass.
- [ ] Completion stamp: a finished neighborhood has a foil stamp (rays, ring, "COMPLETE", the name) whose gold looks like the cover foil.
- [ ] Stamp landing: the stamp hovers large, falls fast into its slot, the page shakes and a haptic knock lands at the moment of impact, a ring of ink spreads and a brass glow flares then fades. Photos pin on one by one afterwards. "Added to page N" and the neighborhood progress appear. A finished neighborhood shows the foil stamp. With Reduce Motion: a plain fade, no shake or flare.
- [ ] Worklets: the cover foil, the cover swing and the page flip run on the UI thread (the math files carry a "worklet" directive, checked by `worklet-files.test.ts`); if a red screen says "non-worklet function on the UI thread", check the called helper and its worklet directive.
  `book-state.ts#stageTransform` has a function-level directive and an installed-plugin transformation regression; the physical UI thread still needs verification.
- [ ] Visa spread (open any stamp): the stamp inked on the left page, tilted; the photos on the right page stuck down at angles, up to six, none off the page; a typed slip below with date and time (New York time), place, neighborhood, page, caption and, if there is one, the song as a ticket stub with notches.
- [ ] Visa spread: drag a photo (yours only) and it lifts, follows the finger, stays on the page, comes to the top and stays where you leave it after closing and reopening; a stamp of someone else's, if social is on, can't be rearranged.
- [ ] Photo viewer: tap a photo for full screen; pinch to zoom up to 4x, drag when zoomed, double tap to zoom in and out, "n of m" and arrows when there are several, close button.
- [ ] Caption typewriter: the caption types out at a steady pace without the text reflowing; with Reduce Motion it appears at once.
- [ ] Menu (ellipsis): Pin to passport or Unpin, Edit, Delete from profile; a fifth pin is refused with a message.
- [ ] Index: the "Index" row under the open book lists neighborhoods, each with a ring that fills with brass as you visit its places, "visited/total", and "COMPLETE" when finished; started ones are tappable and turn the book to that neighborhood's first page, the others are dimmed and say "Not started" to VoiceOver. An empty passport says what the index is for.
- [ ] Stamp flow: the composer opens with the location note, then "Here" (places you are inside) and "Nearby" with distances in km from 1000 m up. Choose a place far away: the "Not quite there yet" card appears (no alarm), the Stamp button is dimmed, and walking inside the geofence turns it on.
- [ ] In-app camera: "Take a photo" opens the camera inside the app (no library option anywhere), each shot lands in a strip with a remove button, the count reads "n of 6", the shutter turns off at six, Done returns to the composer with the photos. With camera access off, the screen explains why and offers Settings.
- [ ] Stamping: Stamp uploads every photo, then the landing plays (see "Stamp landing"), Done returns to the passport. A refusal from the server shows its own sentence with Try again.
- [ ] Map: dark map; places you have stamped are little brass stamps in a glowing ring, the rest are small dim dots, your position is a small bone ring (no blue dot). Pan and zoom stay smooth with many places (only those in view are drawn, at most 250). Tap a pin: it grows and a bottom sheet rises with the name, neighborhood, "Stamped n times. Last on ..." or "Not stamped yet.", and the distance in m or km; "Stamp here" appears only when you are inside the geofence and opens the composer. Swipe the sheet down or tap the map to close. The OpenStreetMap credit shows for imported places.
- [ ] Welcome: black screen, the closed passport shimmering as a hero (following a finger), the emblem and NOSTOS in brass at the top, "Go somewhere. Get a stamp." and "Only you can see it.", three buttons. No map.
- [ ] Under-21: entering an under-21 birthdate (guest gate) replaces the form with one calm sentence, the dim emblem and Close. Nothing is sent or remembered.
- [ ] Settings: "Your passport" group with Edit profile (name, bio, photo; no Private switch) and "Only you can see your passport"; list headers in dim brass typewriter capitals; Customize row with a lock; legal links open; Sign out; Delete account.
- [ ] Delete account: the warning card lists what happens, needs the password, ends on Welcome with "Your account is deleted."
- [ ] First run: a brand-new account sees "This is your passport" under the closed cover once; "Got it" or opening the cover removes it for good; it does not come back after relaunching the app.
- [ ] Place page (from a pin's sheet, or tap the place name on a stamp): name, neighborhood, address, "You're here" or "n km away", your stamps by night with their little stamps, tap one to open it; "You haven't stamped this place yet." when none.
- [ ] Legal pages in a browser (privacy, terms, support, delete account): black page, brass links, serif titles; the text itself is unchanged and still describes the social layer (OPEN-QUESTIONS L7).
- [ ] Design gallery (development builds only): open it from the Welcome screen ("Design gallery (development)") or from Settings under Development, or with the link `nostos://design` (in Expo Go: `exp://<your Mac's address>:8081/--/design`). Each section opens on made-up data with no account and no server: palette, type, spacing, controls, cover (drag it, tilt the phone), opening cover (button), bearer page, pages and spread, page flip (drag an edge), stamps (shapes, borders, figures), completion stamp, landing (Replay), visa spread (drag a photo, tap for full screen), photo viewer, index, map pins and sheet, ticket stub. In a release build the gallery is not reachable.

## Codex integration device checks

- [ ] With device timezone set to Tokyo or Honolulu, birthday eligibility changes only at midnight in New York, including winter.
- [ ] Song picker: type, loading, results, selection, no results, upstream failure and retry work through the native search bar.

The iOS JavaScript/Hermes export completed in this continuation; this is packaging evidence, not a native device run.

===== jest.setup.tsx =====

// The social layer is tested with the flag on; flag-off tests load modules with it unset (see config.test.tsx).
process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SOCIAL_ENABLED = "1";
/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports */
jest.mock("expo-haptics", () => ({
  impactAsync: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
  notificationAsync: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
  ImpactFeedbackStyle: { Light: "light" },
  NotificationFeedbackType: { Success: "success", Warning: "warning", Error: "error" },
}));

jest.mock("expo-secure-store", () => {
  const store = new Map<string, string>();
  return {
    AFTER_FIRST_UNLOCK_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY: 1,
    getItemAsync: jest.fn(async (k: string) => store.get(k) ?? null),
    setItemAsync: jest.fn(async (k: string, v: string) => void store.set(k, v)),
    deleteItemAsync: jest.fn(async (k: string) => void store.delete(k)),
  };
});

jest.mock("expo-symbols", () => {
  const { View } = require("react-native");
  return { SymbolView: (props: object) => <View {...props} /> };
});

jest.mock("react-native-worklets", () => require("react-native-worklets/src/mock"));
jest.mock("react-native-reanimated", () => ({ ...require("react-native-reanimated/mock"), useReducedMotion: () => false }));

// Skia draws on a GPU canvas that doesn't exist in Jest. Components render as plain Views (Canvas) or nothing, so the book's
// structure, labels and logic can be tested; how it looks is a device check (DEVICE-CHECKLIST.md).
jest.mock("@shopify/react-native-skia", () => {
  const { View } = require("react-native");
  const passthrough = ({ children }: { children?: React.ReactNode }) => <>{children}</>;
  const target: Record<string, unknown> = {
    Canvas: ({ children, ...rest }: { children?: React.ReactNode }) => <View {...rest}>{children}</View>,
    vec: (x: number, y: number) => ({ x, y }),
    useFont: () => null,
    useImage: () => null,
    Skia: { Path: { Make: () => ({ moveTo() {}, lineTo() {}, close() {}, addCircle() {} }), MakeFromSVGString: () => ({}) }, RuntimeEffect: { Make: () => null }, Color: (c: string) => c },
  };
  return new Proxy(target, { get: (t, k) => (k === "__esModule" ? true : k in t ? t[k as string] : passthrough) });
});
jest.mock("expo-sensors", () => ({ DeviceMotion: { isAvailableAsync: async () => false, addListener: () => ({ remove() {} }), setUpdateInterval() {} } }));
jest.mock("expo-camera", () => ({ CameraView: () => null, useCameraPermissions: () => [{ granted: true }, jest.fn()] }));

===== src/config.ts =====

/**
 * Build-time switches. SOCIAL_ENABLED (EXPO_PUBLIC_SOCIAL_ENABLED=1) turns the social layer back on: feed, follows, likes,
 * activity, blocks, other people's passports. Default off: Nostos v1 is strictly personal. The backend has the same flag
 * (SOCIAL_ENABLED) and 404s every social route when it is off.
 */
export const SOCIAL_ENABLED = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SOCIAL_ENABLED === "1";


===== ../backend/prisma/schema.prisma =====

// Nostos data model.
// Conventions: snake_case tables/columns, all timestamps stored in UTC (timestamptz).
// A stamp is a row in `photo` (the table name is kept to avoid migration churn; API and UI say "stamp").
// A place is a row in `venue` (same reason). There is deliberately NO accuracy or ranking data anywhere.

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider  = "postgresql"
  /// Pooled connection for the app (Neon: the "-pooler" host with ?pgbouncer=true). Local: same as DIRECT_URL.
  url       = env("DATABASE_URL")
  /// Direct (unpooled) connection, used by `prisma migrate`.
  directUrl = env("DIRECT_URL")
}

enum Role {
  USER
  MODERATOR
  ADMIN
}

enum SessionClient {
  WEB
  IOS
}

enum ContentType {
  PHOTO
  COMMENT
  REPORT
  /// A user account (profile reports; suspension decisions).
  USER
}

/// Why something was reported. UNDERAGE and STALKING are read first by moderators.
enum FlagCategory {
  SPAM
  HARASSMENT
  SEXUAL_OR_VIOLENT
  NOT_AT_PLACE
  STALKING
  UNDERAGE
  PRIVATE_INFO
  OTHER
}

enum FlagStatus {
  OPEN
  RESOLVED
}

enum ModerationAction {
  FLAGGED
  AUTO_HIDE
  HIDE
  DISMISS
  /// Moderator reviewed hidden content (e.g. auto-hidden after 3 flags) and restored it.
  RESTORE
  /// System: the author deleted their account, so their personal photos were hidden.
  ACCOUNT_DELETED
  /// Moderator removed an abusive account (can't sign in; sessions revoked).
  SUSPEND
  UNSUSPEND
}

enum VerificationAction {
  GRANT
  REVOKE
}

model User {
  id              String    @id @default(cuid())
  email           String    @unique
  handle          String    @unique
  displayName     String    @map("display_name")
  passwordHash    String    @map("password_hash")
  role            Role      @default(USER)
  /// Moderation reputation in [0.2, 1.5]; new accounts start at 0.5. Visible only to the user and moderators.
  reputation      Float     @default(0.5)
  createdAt       DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Set when the account was deleted (anonymized). The row stays so stamps and the moderation log keep their history.
  deletedAt       DateTime? @map("deleted_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  termsAcceptedAt DateTime? @map("terms_accepted_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  termsVersion    String?   @map("terms_version")
  /// App Review / QA accounts: may stamp from anywhere. Their stamps are stored but visible only to themselves.
  /// Settable only by an admin or the seed.
  geofenceExempt  Boolean   @default(false) @map("geofence_exempt")
  /// Set by a moderator for abusive accounts: can't sign in, all sessions revoked. Content stays until moderated.
  suspendedAt     DateTime? @map("suspended_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Private accounts (the default for every account): only approved followers see the profile, stamps and places.
  /// See src/server/privacy.ts.
  isPrivate       Boolean   @default(true) @map("is_private")
  /// Max 150 characters, content-filtered.
  bio             String    @default("")
  /// Profile photo (re-encoded square JPEG). Shown only to viewers allowed to see the full profile.
  avatarKey       String?   @map("avatar_key")
  /// Verified badge, handpicked by an admin. Every grant and revoke is in the append-only verification_event log.
  /// Badge only: it never changes visibility, ordering or anything else.
  verifiedAt      DateTime? @map("verified_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)

  sessions               Session[]
  photos                 Photo[]
  following              Follow[]                 @relation("follower")
  followers              Follow[]                 @relation("followee")
  flags                  Flag[]
  moderationEvents       ModerationEvent[]
  blocking               Block[]                  @relation("blocker")
  blockedBy              Block[]                  @relation("blocked")
  followRequestsSent     FollowRequest[]          @relation("requester")
  followRequestsReceived FollowRequest[]          @relation("requestTarget")
  likes                  PhotoLike[]
  verificationEvents     VerificationEvent[]      @relation("verifiedUser")
  verificationActions    VerificationEvent[]      @relation("verifier")
  rateEvents             RateEvent[]
  pinned                 PinnedStamp[]
  completions            NeighborhoodCompletion[]

  @@map("app_user")
}

model Session {
  /// sha256 of the random token stored in the cookie; the raw token never touches the DB.
  id        String        @id
  userId    String        @map("user_id")
  /// WEB sessions ride in an httpOnly cookie; IOS sessions are bearer tokens kept in the iOS keychain.
  client    SessionClient @default(WEB)
  expiresAt DateTime      @map("expires_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  createdAt DateTime      @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user      User          @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([userId])
  @@map("session")
}

/// A place (the table keeps its old name). Name, location and geofence only: no live data.
model Venue {
  id             String        @id @default(cuid())
  slug           String        @unique
  name           String
  address        String
  neighborhood   String
  lat            Float
  lng            Float
  /// Radius in meters within which a phone may stamp this place (server-checked).
  geofenceMeters Int           @default(100) @map("geofence_meters")
  /// The NYC neighborhood the place is in (assigned from the boundaries), for the passport's collections.
  neighborhoodId String?       @map("neighborhood_id")
  hood           Neighborhood? @relation(fields: [neighborhoodId], references: [id])
  /// OpenStreetMap element ("node/123", "way/456") for imported places (ODbL: attribute "© OpenStreetMap contributors").
  osmId          String?       @unique @map("osm_id")
  createdAt      DateTime      @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  updatedAt      DateTime      @updatedAt @map("updated_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)

  photos Photo[]
  flags  Flag[]

  @@map("venue")
}

/// A stamp: one visit to a place, with a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix (a CHECK requires at least one).
/// Stamps are immutable except caption, song, moderation state (hidden_at) and removed_at (final); a trigger enforces it.
model Photo {
  /// Immutable database transaction of creation; photos may only be inserted in that transaction.
  creationTransaction String @default(dbgenerated("txid_current()::text")) @map("creation_transaction")
  id           String        @id @default(cuid())
  userId       String        @map("user_id")
  venueId      String        @map("venue_id")
  /// null = a stamp without a photo.
  storageKey   String?       @unique @map("storage_key")
  mimeType     String?       @map("mime_type")
  /// Up to 280 characters (CHECK). Empty string when there is no caption.
  caption      String
  /// Song metadata only (no audio): from Apple's iTunes Search API, via our backend.
  songTitle    String?       @map("song_title")
  songArtist   String?       @map("song_artist")
  songArtUrl   String?       @map("song_art_url")
  songSourceId String?       @map("song_source_id")
  /// Where the phone was when it stamped (kept for auditing the geofence check; never sent to viewers).
  lat          Float
  lng          Float
  createdAt    DateTime      @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  hiddenAt     DateTime?     @map("hidden_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Optional 1 to 10 (CHECK). Not shown in the UI for v1. Immutable.
  rating       Int?          @db.SmallInt
  editedAt     DateTime?     @map("edited_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// The author removed it from their profile ("delete" means hide). Final: a trigger refuses to clear it.
  removedAt    DateTime?     @map("removed_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Snapshot at write time: false when stamped by a geofence-exempt (App Review / QA) account: visible to its author only.
  countable    Boolean       @default(true)
  user         User          @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  venue        Venue         @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])
  likes        PhotoLike[]
  photos       StampPhoto[]
  pins         PinnedStamp[]

  @@index([venueId, createdAt])
  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  @@index([createdAt, id])
  @@map("photo")
}

/// A neighborhood (NYC Neighborhood Tabulation Area, 2020; public NYC Open Data). `boundary` is an array of outer rings,
/// each an array of [lng, lat]; the bbox columns make point lookups cheap. Loaded by `pnpm neighborhoods:import`.
model Neighborhood {
  id          String                   @id
  name        String
  borough     String
  boundary    Json
  minLat      Float                    @map("min_lat")
  maxLat      Float                    @map("max_lat")
  minLng      Float                    @map("min_lng")
  maxLng      Float                    @map("max_lng")
  places      Venue[]
  completions NeighborhoodCompletion[]

  @@map("neighborhood")
}

/// Earned once, when a person has stamped every place in a neighborhood (a set of at least 3). Never taken back, even if
/// places are added later. Owner only.
model NeighborhoodCompletion {
  userId         String       @map("user_id")
  neighborhoodId String       @map("neighborhood_id")
  completedAt    DateTime     @map("completed_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  stampId        String       @map("stamp_id")
  user           User         @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  neighborhood   Neighborhood @relation(fields: [neighborhoodId], references: [id])

  @@id([userId, neighborhoodId])
  @@map("neighborhood_completion")
}

/// One photo on a stamp (up to 6 live ones, CHECK position 0 to 5 and a trigger). The scrapbook layout (x, y as 0..1 of the
/// page, rotation in degrees) is the owner's to change; the file itself never changes. Removing a photo sets removed_at
/// (final); rows are never deleted (trigger).
model StampPhoto {
  id         String    @id @default(cuid())
  stampId    String    @map("stamp_id")
  storageKey String    @unique @map("storage_key")
  mimeType   String    @map("mime_type")
  position   Int       @db.SmallInt
  x          Float?
  y          Float?
  rotation   Float?
  createdAt  DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  removedAt  DateTime? @map("removed_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  stamp      Photo     @relation(fields: [stampId], references: [id])

  @@index([stampId, position])
  @@map("stamp_photo")
}

/// The owner's pinned stamps (up to 4), shown as ribbons on the first spread of the passport. Owner only: nothing
/// outside /api/me reads this table. Unpinning deletes the row (a pin is a layout choice, not content).
model PinnedStamp {
  userId   String   @map("user_id")
  stampId  String   @map("stamp_id")
  position Int      @db.SmallInt
  pinnedAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("pinned_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user     User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  stamp    Photo    @relation(fields: [stampId], references: [id])

  @@id([userId, stampId])
  @@unique([userId, position])
  @@map("pinned_stamp")
}

model Follow {
  followerId String   @map("follower_id")
  followeeId String   @map("followee_id")
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  follower   User     @relation("follower", fields: [followerId], references: [id])
  followee   User     @relation("followee", fields: [followeeId], references: [id])

  @@id([followerId, followeeId])
  @@index([followeeId])
  @@map("follow")
}

/// A pending request to follow a private account. Approving it creates the follow row (every follow row is an
/// approved follow), declining or cancelling deletes the request.
model FollowRequest {
  requesterId String   @map("requester_id")
  targetId    String   @map("target_id")
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  requester   User     @relation("requester", fields: [requesterId], references: [id])
  target      User     @relation("requestTarget", fields: [targetId], references: [id])

  @@id([requesterId, targetId])
  @@index([targetId, createdAt])
  @@map("follow_request")
}

/// One like per user per stamp. Likes are reactions, not content: unliking deletes the row. Likes never affect anything else.
model PhotoLike {
  userId    String   @map("user_id")
  photoId   String   @map("photo_id")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  /// Write-time snapshot: false for geofence-exempt (App Review / QA) accounts, whose likes never count.
  countable Boolean  @default(true)
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  photo     Photo    @relation(fields: [photoId], references: [id])

  @@id([userId, photoId])
  @@index([photoId, createdAt])
  @@map("photo_like")
}

/// Append-only log of verified-badge grants and revokes (trigger rejects UPDATE and DELETE). A note is required.
model VerificationEvent {
  id        String             @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String             @map("user_id")
  action    VerificationAction
  note      String
  /// null = system (e.g. the account was deleted).
  actorId   String?            @map("actor_id")
  createdAt DateTime           @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user      User               @relation("verifiedUser", fields: [userId], references: [id])
  actor     User?              @relation("verifier", fields: [actorId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  @@map("verification_event")
}

/// Rate-limit ledger for social actions (follows and requests, likes, posts). Counted under a per-user advisory
/// lock; rows older than a day are pruned by the cron job.
model RateEvent {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String   @map("user_id")
  kind      String
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])

  @@index([userId, kind, createdAt])
  @@index([createdAt])
  @@map("rate_event")
}

model Flag {
  id         String       @id @default(cuid())
  targetType ContentType  @map("target_type")
  targetId   String       @map("target_id")
  /// null for USER targets (profile reports).
  venueId    String?      @map("venue_id")
  flaggerId  String       @map("flagger_id")
  reason     String
  category   FlagCategory @default(OTHER)
  status     FlagStatus   @default(OPEN)
  createdAt  DateTime     @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  flagger    User         @relation(fields: [flaggerId], references: [id])
  venue      Venue?       @relation(fields: [venueId], references: [id])

  /// One OPEN flag per user per item (partial unique index in migration 3); after a review you may flag again.
  @@index([targetType, targetId, flaggerId])
  @@index([status, createdAt])
  @@map("flag")
}

/// Append-only audit log. UPDATE and DELETE are rejected by a database trigger (see migration).
model ModerationEvent {
  id         String           @id @default(cuid())
  action     ModerationAction
  targetType ContentType      @map("target_type")
  targetId   String           @map("target_id")
  /// null = system action (e.g. auto-hide after 3 distinct flags)
  actorId    String?          @map("actor_id")
  note       String?
  createdAt  DateTime         @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  actor      User?            @relation(fields: [actorId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@index([targetType, targetId])
  @@map("moderation_event")
}

/// Failed login attempts, for the email+IP throttle. Rows older than a day are pruned by the cron job.
model LoginAttempt {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  email     String
  ip        String
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)

  @@index([email, ip, createdAt])
  @@index([createdAt])
  @@map("login_attempt")
}

/// A blocks B. Symmetric in effect: neither sees the other's stamps or profile, and neither can follow the other.
model Block {
  blockerId String   @map("blocker_id")
  blockedId String   @map("blocked_id")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz(3)
  blocker   User     @relation("blocker", fields: [blockerId], references: [id])
  blocked   User     @relation("blocked", fields: [blockedId], references: [id])

  @@id([blockerId, blockedId])
  @@index([blockedId])
  @@map("block")
}


===== src/api/contracts.ts =====

/**
 * COPIED VERBATIM from backend/src/contracts/api.ts (this repo, commit a8fd412).
 * Do not edit here: change the backend contract, then re-copy with `npm run sync:contracts`.
 */
/**
 * Nostos HTTP API contract: the single source of truth for clients (the Expo iOS app copies this file
 * verbatim into src/api/contracts.ts). Depends only on `zod` **v3** (written against 3.25; install `zod@^3.25`,
 * not v4). Every response schema is `.strict()`, and
 * tests/integration/contracts.test.ts calls every route and fails if a response drifts from these schemas.
 * Human-readable reference: API.md.
 *
 * Conventions: JSON bodies; timestamps are ISO-8601 UTC strings. Errors are `{ error: <code>, message: <human sentence> }`.
 * A stamp is one visit to a place: a photo, a caption, a song, or any mix. Nothing here is ever "Anonymous":
 * a stamp is only returned to people who may see its author's full profile.
 */
import { z } from "zod";

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Primitives and enums
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const IsoDate = z.string().datetime({ offset: true });
export const Role = z.enum(["USER", "MODERATOR", "ADMIN"]);
/** What can be reported or moderated. The database calls a stamp "PHOTO"; the API says STAMP. */
export const TargetType = z.enum(["STAMP", "USER"]);
/** Why something is reported. Moderators read UNDERAGE and STALKING first. */
export const FlagCategory = z.enum(["SPAM", "HARASSMENT", "SEXUAL_OR_VIOLENT", "NOT_AT_PLACE", "STALKING", "UNDERAGE", "PRIVATE_INFO", "OTHER"]);
export const ModerationAction = z.enum(["FLAGGED", "AUTO_HIDE", "HIDE", "DISMISS", "RESTORE", "ACCOUNT_DELETED", "SUSPEND", "UNSUSPEND"]);

export const ErrorResponse = z
  .object({
    /** Machine-readable code, e.g. "unauthorized", "invalid_token", "forbidden", "rate_limited", "objectionable_content", "private_account". */
    error: z.string(),
    /** Human sentence, safe to show inline (e.g. "You need to be within 100 m of this place to stamp it (you're 140 m away)"). */
    message: z.string(),
  })
  .strict();

/** ODbL attribution; show wherever OpenStreetMap-sourced place data appears. */
export const Attribution = z.object({ text: z.literal("© OpenStreetMap contributors"), url: z.string().url(), license: z.literal("ODbL-1.0") }).strict();

export const PublicUser = z.object({ id: z.string(), handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), role: Role }).strict();

/** "requested" = a pending request to a private account. */
export const FollowState = z.enum(["none", "requested", "following"]);
/**
 * A person in a list (search, followers, following, requests). The handle and verified badge always; displayName
 * and avatarUrl are null when the account is private and the viewer doesn't follow it (show a generic avatar).
 */
export const PersonView = z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string().nullable(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** A person plus the viewer's follow state toward them (drives Follow / Requested / Following buttons). */
export const PersonRow = PersonView.extend({ follow: FollowState, isSelf: z.boolean() }).strict();
export const PlaceRef = z.object({ id: z.string(), slug: z.string(), name: z.string(), neighborhood: z.string(), neighborhoodId: z.string().nullable() }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Auth and account
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const SignupRequest = z
  .object({
    email: z.string().trim().email().max(200),
    password: z.string().min(10).max(200),
    displayName: z.string().trim().min(1).max(60),
    handle: z.string().trim().toLowerCase().regex(/^[a-z0-9_]{3,24}$/),
    acceptTerms: z.literal(true),
    /** YYYY-MM-DD. Checked once for 21+ (403 `underage` if not) and never stored. */
    birthdate: z.string().refine((v) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v), "Enter your birthdate as YYYY-MM-DD"),
  })
  .strict();
export const LoginRequest = z.object({ email: z.string().trim().email().max(200), password: z.string().max(200) }).strict();
/** POST /api/auth/login and /api/auth/signup with header `X-Client: ios`. */
export const TokenResponse = z.object({ token: z.string(), expiresAt: IsoDate, user: PublicUser }).strict();
/** Same endpoints without X-Client (web): the session is set as an httpOnly cookie. */
export const WebAuthResponse = z.object({ user: PublicUser }).strict();
export const OkResponse = z.object({ ok: z.literal(true) }).strict();
export const LogoutAllResponse = z.object({ ok: z.literal(true), revoked: z.number().int() }).strict();

export const MeResponse = z
  .object({
    user: z
      .object({
        id: z.string(), email: z.string(), handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), role: Role, memberSince: IsoDate, reputation: z.number(),
        isPrivate: z.boolean(), bio: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean(),
      })
      .strict(),
    /** Badge count for the Follow requests entry. */
    social: z.object({ pendingFollowRequests: z.number().int() }).strict(),
    /** geofenceExempt: App Review / QA account that can stamp anywhere; its stamps are visible only to itself. */
    settings: z.object({ geofenceExempt: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    terms: z
      .object({ acceptedVersion: z.string().nullable(), acceptedAt: IsoDate.nullable(), currentVersion: z.string(), needsAcceptance: z.boolean() })
      .strict(),
  })
  .strict();
export const AcceptTermsRequest = z.object({ version: z.string() }).strict();
export const AcceptTermsResponse = z.object({ acceptedVersion: z.string(), acceptedAt: IsoDate }).strict();
export const DeleteAccountRequest = z.object({ password: z.string().min(1).max(200) }).strict();
export const DeleteAccountResponse = z.object({ deleted: z.literal(true) }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Places
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** A place: name, location and geofence. No live data. */
export const Place = z
  .object({
    id: z.string(), slug: z.string(), name: z.string(),
    /** May be "" for imported places without a mapped street address; show the neighborhood instead. */
    address: z.string(),
    neighborhood: z.string(), lat: z.number(), lng: z.number(),
    /** Radius in meters within which a phone may stamp this place (the server checks it again on every stamp). */
    geofenceMeters: z.number().int(),
    /** The NYC neighborhood the place is in, when known. */
    neighborhoodId: z.string().nullable(),
    fromOsm: z.boolean(),
  })
  .strict();
export const PlacesResponse = z.object({ places: z.array(Place), attribution: Attribution.nullable() }).strict();
export const PlaceResponse = z.object({ place: Place }).strict();
/** GET /api/places/nearby?lat=&lng=: nearest first. `inside` = within its geofence right now. The position is never stored. */
export const NearbyPlacesResponse = z.object({ places: z.array(Place.extend({ distanceM: z.number().int(), inside: z.boolean() }).strict()) }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Stamps
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Like count (App Review likes excluded) and whether you liked it. */
export const LikeSummary = z.object({ count: z.number().int(), viewerLiked: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** Album art only from Apple's artwork CDN, over https (the server enforces the same rule). */
export const AppleArtUrl = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .max(500)
  .refine((u) => {
    try {
      const url = new URL(u);
      return url.protocol === "https:" && /(^|\.)mzstatic\.com$/.test(url.hostname);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }, "Song art must be an https link from Apple's artwork servers");
/** Song metadata only (no audio). */
export const Song = z.object({ title: z.string(), artist: z.string(), artUrl: z.string().nullable(), sourceId: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
/**
 * A stamp. Photo URLs are short-lived signed URLs (15-20 min): refetch rather than caching URLs. There are no
 * coordinates, storage keys or user ids anywhere in it. `hidden` / `removed` are true only for staff viewers.
 */
/** One photo on a stamp. x and y are 0..1 of the page and rotation is degrees: null until the owner lays them out. */
export const StampPhotoView = z
  .object({ id: z.string(), url: z.string(), position: z.number().int().min(0).max(5), x: z.number().nullable(), y: z.number().nullable(), rotation: z.number().nullable() })
  .strict();
export const StampView = z
  .object({
    id: z.string(),
    /** The first photo, or null. */
    photoUrl: z.string().nullable(),
    /** Up to 6 photos in page order. */
    photos: z.array(StampPhotoView).max(6),
    caption: z.string(),
    song: Song.nullable(),
    /** Page and slot in the owner's passport, oldest first, three slots to a page (src/lib/layout.ts). null if hidden or removed. */
    page: z.number().int().min(1).nullable(),
    slot: z.number().int().min(1).max(3).nullable(),
    createdAt: IsoDate,
    editedAt: IsoDate.nullable(),
    place: PlaceRef,
    author: z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    mine: z.boolean(),
    likes: LikeSummary,
    hidden: z.boolean(),
    removed: z.boolean(),
  })
  .strict();
export const StampResponse = z.object({ stamp: StampView }).strict();
/**
 * POST /api/places/:id/stamps (multipart/form-data): optional `photo` file, `caption`, `songTitle`, `songArtist`,
 * `songArtUrl`, `songSourceId`, `lat`, `lng` (and an optional `rating` 1-10 that v1 doesn't show). At least one of
 * photo, caption or song. 403 outside the geofence, with a message that says how far away you are.
 */
/** Progress through a neighborhood: visited places over total, and whether this stamp finished the set (a set is at least 3 places). */
export const NeighborhoodProgress = z
  .object({ neighborhoodId: z.string(), name: z.string(), borough: z.string(), visited: z.number().int(), total: z.number().int(), complete: z.boolean(), justCompleted: z.boolean(), newPlace: z.boolean() })
  .strict();
/** `progress` is null when the place has no neighborhood (or the stamp is an App Review stamp). */
export const CreateStampResponse = z.object({ stamp: StampView, progress: NeighborhoodProgress.nullable() }).strict();
/** GET /api/me/neighborhoods: the passport's index. Started first. Owner only. */
export const NeighborhoodIndexResponse = z
  .object({
    neighborhoods: z.array(z.object({ neighborhoodId: z.string(), name: z.string(), borough: z.string(), visited: z.number().int(), total: z.number().int(), complete: z.boolean(), completedAt: IsoDate.nullable(), percent: z.number().int() }).strict()),
    totals: z.object({ neighborhoodsStarted: z.number().int(), neighborhoodsComplete: z.number().int() }).strict(),
  })
  .strict();
/** PATCH /api/stamps/:id: caption and song; send only changed fields. `song: null` removes the song. */
export const StampEditRequest = z
  .object({
    caption: z.string().trim().max(280),
    song: z.object({ title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200), artist: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200), artUrl: AppleArtUrl.nullish(), sourceId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100).nullish() }).strict().nullable(),
  })
  .partial()
  .strict()
  .refine((v) => Object.keys(v).length > 0, "Nothing to change");
/** PATCH /api/stamps/:id/photos: the scrapbook layout. Owner only. POST rejects later additions (400 for the owner, 404 for foreign/missing stamps); POST .../photos/:photoId/remove removes one. */
export const PhotoLayoutRequest = z
  .object({
    photos: z
      .array(z.object({ id: z.string().min(1).max(64), position: z.number().int().min(0).max(5).optional(), x: z.number().min(0).max(1).nullable().optional(), y: z.number().min(0).max(1).nullable().optional(), rotation: z.number().min(-45).max(45).nullable().optional() }).strict())
      .min(1)
      .max(6),
  })
  .strict();
/** GET and PUT /api/me/pins: your pinned stamps (up to 4), owner only. PUT body is `{ stampIds }`. */
export const PinsRequest = z.object({ stampIds: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(64)).max(4) }).strict();
export const PinsResponse = z.object({ stamps: z.array(StampView).max(4) }).strict();
export const RemoveStampResponse = z.object({ removed: z.literal(true) }).strict();
/** PUT/DELETE /api/stamps/:id/like. */
export const LikeResponse = z.object({ liked: z.boolean(), count: z.number().int(), viewerLiked: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** Feed and passport grid, newest first. `nextBefore` = an opaque cursor: pass it as ?before= for the next page; null = the end. */
export const StampsPageResponse = z.object({ stamps: z.array(StampView), nextBefore: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
export const FeedResponse = StampsPageResponse;
/** The passport, OLDEST first so page 1 stays page 1. `nextAfter` = pass it as ?after= for the next page; null = the end. */
export const UserStampsResponse = z.object({ stamps: z.array(StampView), nextAfter: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
export const UserPlacesResponse = z
  .object({
    places: z.array(
      z
        .object({
          place: z.object({ id: z.string(), slug: z.string(), name: z.string(), neighborhood: z.string(), lat: z.number(), lng: z.number() }).strict(),
          stamps: z.number().int(),
          latestStamp: z.object({ id: z.string(), createdAt: IsoDate, photoUrl: z.string().nullable(), caption: z.string() }).strict(),
        })
        .strict(),
    ),
  })
  .strict();

/** GET /api/songs/search?q= : metadata from Apple's iTunes Search API, proxied. */
export const SongResult = z.object({ title: z.string(), artist: z.string(), album: z.string().nullable(), artUrl: z.string().nullable(), sourceId: z.string() }).strict();
export const SongSearchResponse = z.object({ songs: z.array(SongResult) }).strict();

/** GET /api/places/:id/mine?lat=&lng=: your own page for a place. `visits` stack your stamps by night; `inside` is null without a position. */
export const MyPlacePageResponse = z
  .object({
    place: Place.omit({ fromOsm: true }).extend({ fromOsm: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    stamps: z.array(StampView),
    visits: z.array(z.object({ day: z.string(), stamps: z.array(StampView) }).strict()),
    inside: z.boolean().nullable(),
    distanceM: z.number().int().nullable(),
  })
  .strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// People
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const UserSearchResponse = z.object({ users: z.array(PersonRow) }).strict();
export const Relationship = z
  .object({
    isSelf: z.boolean(),
    /** The viewer's follow state toward this account. */
    follow: FollowState,
    followsYou: z.boolean(),
    mutual: z.boolean(),
  })
  .strict();
/**
 * `access: "limited"`: a private account the viewer doesn't follow. Show the handle, the badge, a generic avatar,
 * "This account is private" and Follow (Request) / Requested. Everything else, including every stamp and place, is
 * only in `access: "full"`.
 */
export const ProfileResponse = z.discriminatedUnion("access", [
  z
    .object({
      access: z.literal("full"),
      user: z
        .object({
          handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), bio: z.string(), verified: z.boolean(), isPrivate: z.boolean(),
          memberSince: IsoDate,
          /** Private: present for yourself and for moderators, otherwise null. */
          reputation: z.number().nullable(),
          counts: z.object({ stamps: z.number().int(), places: z.number().int(), followers: z.number().int(), following: z.number().int() }).strict(),
        })
        .strict(),
      relationship: Relationship,
    })
    .strict(),
  z
    .object({
      access: z.literal("limited"),
      user: z.object({ handle: z.string(), verified: z.boolean(), isPrivate: z.literal(true) }).strict(),
      relationship: Relationship,
    })
    .strict(),
]);
export const FollowingResponse = z
  .object({ following: z.array(z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean(), mutual: z.boolean() }).strict()) })
  .strict();
/** PUT follow: public account -> "following"; private account -> "requested". DELETE (unfollow or cancel) -> "none". */
export const FollowResponse = z.object({ state: FollowState }).strict();
const StampRef = z.object({ id: z.string(), url: z.string().nullable(), place: z.object({ name: z.string(), slug: z.string() }).strict() }).strict();
/** GET /api/me/activity: newest first, last 30 days (pending requests of any age). In-app only. */
export const ActivityResponse = z
  .object({
    items: z.array(
      z.discriminatedUnion("kind", [
        z.object({ kind: z.literal("follow_request"), at: IsoDate, person: PersonView }).strict(),
        z.object({ kind: z.literal("new_follower"), at: IsoDate, person: PersonView }).strict(),
        z.object({ kind: z.literal("like"), at: IsoDate, person: PersonView, stamp: StampRef }).strict(),
      ]),
    ),
  })
  .strict();
/** GET /api/users/:handle/followers and /following (403 `private_account` without full access). */
export const PeopleListResponse = z.object({ people: z.array(PersonRow) }).strict();
export const FollowRequestsResponse = z.object({ requests: z.array(z.object({ person: PersonView, requestedAt: IsoDate }).strict()) }).strict();
export const FollowRequestDecisionRequest = z.object({ approve: z.boolean() }).strict();
export const FollowRequestDecisionResponse = z.object({ approved: z.boolean() }).strict();
export const ApproveAllResponse = z.object({ approved: z.number().int() }).strict();
export const RemoveFollowerResponse = z.object({ removed: z.literal(true) }).strict();
/** PATCH /api/me/profile: send only changed fields. */
export const ProfileSettingsRequest = z
  .object({ displayName: z.string().trim().min(1).max(60), bio: z.string().trim().max(150), isPrivate: z.boolean() })
  .partial()
  .strict();
export const ProfileSettingsResponse = z
  .object({
    profile: z.object({ handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), bio: z.string(), isPrivate: z.boolean(), avatarUrl: z.string().nullable(), verified: z.boolean() }).strict(),
    /** After switching to public, offer "Approve N pending requests?" when this is above 0. */
    pendingRequests: z.number().int(),
  })
  .strict();
/** POST /api/me/avatar (multipart `photo`) and DELETE /api/me/avatar. */
export const AvatarResponse = z.object({ avatarUrl: z.string().nullable() }).strict();
export const BlockResponse = z.object({ blocked: z.boolean() }).strict();
/** Your blocks, newest first. Unblock with DELETE /api/users/:handle/block. */
export const BlocksResponse = z.object({ blocks: z.array(z.object({ handle: z.string(), blockedAt: IsoDate }).strict()) }).strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Flags and moderation
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** targetId: the stamp id; for USER, the account id or handle. */
export const FlagRequest = z.object({ targetType: TargetType, targetId: z.string().min(1).max(64), reason: z.string().trim().min(3).max(300), category: FlagCategory.default("OTHER") }).strict();
export const FlagResponse = z.object({ flag: z.object({ id: z.string() }).strict(), autoHidden: z.boolean() }).strict();
export const ModActionRequest = z
  .object({ targetType: TargetType, targetId: z.string().min(1).max(64), action: z.enum(["HIDE", "DISMISS", "RESTORE", "SUSPEND", "UNSUSPEND"]), note: z.string().trim().max(300).optional() })
  .strict();
export const ModActionResponse = z.object({ target: z.object({ id: z.string(), hidden: z.boolean() }).strict().nullable() }).strict();
export const ModQueueItem = z
  .object({
    targetType: TargetType,
    targetId: z.string(),
    /** The place of a stamp; null for USER (account) reports. */
    venue: z.object({ name: z.string(), slug: z.string() }).strict().nullable(),
    content: z
      .object({
        kind: TargetType,
        preview: z.string(),
        /** Signed URL for photos (moderators can see hidden ones). */
        url: z.string().optional(),
        author: z.string(),
        authorRep: z.number(),
        /** Hidden (stamp) or suspended (account) time. */
        hiddenAt: IsoDate.nullable(),
        createdAt: IsoDate,
      })
      .strict()
      .nullable(),
    flags: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), reason: z.string(), category: FlagCategory, createdAt: IsoDate, by: z.string(), byRole: Role }).strict()),
    history: z.array(z.object({ action: ModerationAction, by: z.string(), note: z.string().nullable(), at: IsoDate }).strict()),
  })
  .strict();
export const ModQueueResponse = z.object({ items: z.array(ModQueueItem) }).strict();
/** Hidden stamps and suspended accounts (newest first) for the Restore / Unsuspend screen. */
export const ModHiddenResponse = z
  .object({
    items: z.array(
      z
        .object({
          targetType: TargetType, targetId: z.string(), venue: z.object({ name: z.string(), slug: z.string() }).strict().nullable(),
          preview: z.string(), url: z.string().optional(), author: z.string(), hiddenAt: IsoDate,
        })
        .strict(),
    ),
  })
  .strict();
export const ModLogResponse = z
  .object({
    events: z.array(
      z.object({ id: z.string(), action: ModerationAction, targetType: z.enum(["PHOTO", "COMMENT", "REPORT", "USER"]), targetId: z.string(), by: z.string(), note: z.string().nullable(), createdAt: IsoDate }).strict(),
    ),
  })
  .strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Admin (web only; not used by the iOS app)
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const AdminUserFlagsResponse = z.object({ user: z.object({ handle: z.string(), geofenceExempt: z.boolean() }).strict() }).strict();
export const AdminUserSearchResponse = z
  .object({
    users: z.array(
      z
        .object({
          handle: z.string(), displayName: z.string(), email: z.string(), role: Role, isPrivate: z.boolean(),
          verified: z.boolean(), verifiedAt: IsoDate.nullable(), suspended: z.boolean(), memberSince: IsoDate,
        })
        .strict(),
    ),
  })
  .strict();
/** POST /api/admin/users/:handle/verification. The note is required and goes into the append-only log. */
export const VerificationRequest = z.object({ verified: z.boolean(), note: z.string().trim().min(3).max(300) }).strict();
export const VerificationResponse = z.object({ user: z.object({ handle: z.string(), verified: z.boolean(), verifiedAt: IsoDate.nullable() }).strict() }).strict();
export const VerificationLogResponse = z
  .object({ events: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), handle: z.string(), action: z.enum(["GRANT", "REVOKE"]), note: z.string(), by: z.string(), at: IsoDate }).strict()) })
  .strict();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Ops
// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const CronResponse = z.object({ ok: z.literal(true), prunedLoginAttempts: z.number().int(), prunedRateEvents: z.number().int(), ms: z.number() }).strict();

export const HealthResponse = z
  .object({ ok: z.literal(true), db: z.literal("up"), env: z.string(), commit: z.string().nullable(), ms: z.number() })
  .strict();
