===== src/auth/auth-context.tsx =====

import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { api } from "@/api";
import { qk } from "@/api/keys";
import type { TokenResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { tokenStore } from "./token-store";

/**
 * - signedOut: first launch or after sign-out; shows the welcome screen.
 * - guest: browsing the public map without an account (Guideline 5.1.1(v)); posting asks to sign in.
 * - signedIn: has a token.
 */
export type AuthStatus = "loading" | "signedOut" | "guest" | "signedIn";

type AuthContextValue = {
  status: AuthStatus;
  signIn: (res: TokenResponse) => Promise<void>;
  /** Forget the token locally at once, then revoke it on the server (best effort). */
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  /** Forget the token without calling the server (after account deletion). */
  forget: () => Promise<void>;
  browseAsGuest: () => void;
};

const GUEST_KEY = "nostos.guest";

// A one-time message for the welcome screen after the account is deleted.
let farewell: string | null = null;
export const farewellNotice = {
  set: (m: string) => {
    farewell = m;
  },
  /** Read once; later reads return null. */
  take: (): string | null => {
    const m = farewell;
    farewell = null;
    return m;
  },
};
const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [hasToken, setHasToken] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
  const [guest, setGuest] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void Promise.all([tokenStore.load(), SecureStore.getItemAsync(GUEST_KEY).catch(() => null)]).then(([token, g]) => {
      if (!alive) return;
      setGuest(g === "1");
      setHasToken(!!token);
    });
    const unsubscribe = tokenStore.subscribe((token) => {
      setHasToken(!!token);
      // Never show one account's cached data to the next.
      if (!token) queryClient.clear();
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      unsubscribe();
    };
  }, [queryClient]);

  const signIn = useCallback(
    async (res: TokenResponse) => {
      // Load the account first, so the app stays mounted
      // instead of passing through the account gate. If it fails, the gate handles it.
      const me = await api.me.get(res.token).catch(() => null);
      queryClient.clear();
      if (me) queryClient.setQueryData(qk.me, me);
      await tokenStore.set(res.token);
    },
    [queryClient],
  );

  const endGuest = useCallback(() => {
    setGuest(false);
    void SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(GUEST_KEY).catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);

  const forget = useCallback(async () => {
    endGuest();
    await tokenStore.clear();
  }, [endGuest]);

  const signOut = useCallback(async () => {
    endGuest();
    const previous = await tokenStore.clear();
    if (previous) void api.auth.logout(previous).catch(() => undefined);
  }, [endGuest]);

  const browseAsGuest = useCallback(() => {
    setGuest(true);
    void SecureStore.setItemAsync(GUEST_KEY, "1").catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);

  const status: AuthStatus = hasToken === null ? "loading" : hasToken ? "signedIn" : guest ? "guest" : "signedOut";
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ status, signIn, signOut, forget, browseAsGuest }), [status, signIn, signOut, forget, browseAsGuest]);
  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider");
  return ctx;
}


===== src/theme/__tests__/midnight.test.ts =====

/**
 * The Midnight rules, enforced: black and brass only, nothing white, and no color literal anywhere but the theme file.
 * This runs in the gate (npm run check), next to the ESLint rule `local/no-raw-design-values`.
 */
import * as fs from "node:fs";
import * as path from "node:path";

import { colors, midnight, palette } from "../midnight";

const SRC = path.resolve(__dirname, "../..");
const THEME_FILE = path.join(SRC, "theme", "midnight.ts");

const HEX = /#(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{8}|[0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{4}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})\b/g;
const FUNC = /\b(?:rgba?|hsla?|hwb|lab|lch|oklab|oklch)\s*\([^)]*\)/g;
const NAMED = /\b(?:color|Color|tint|Tint|background|border)\w*\s*[:=]\s*\{?\s*["'`](white|black|red|green|blue|yellow|orange|purple|pink|gray|grey|silver|gold|ivory|snow|whitesmoke|ghostwhite|azure)["'`]/g;

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
  return fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((d) => {
    const p = path.join(dir, d.name);
    if (d.isDirectory()) return d.name === "__tests__" || d.name === "node_modules" ? [] : walk(p);
    return /\.(ts|tsx)$/.test(d.name) && !/\.d\.ts$/.test(d.name) ? [p] : [];
  });
}

const toRgb = (hex: string): [number, number, number] => {
  const h = hex.replace("#", "");
  const full = h.length === 3 || h.length === 4 ? h.split("").map((c) => c + c).join("") : h;
  return [parseInt(full.slice(0, 2), 16), parseInt(full.slice(2, 4), 16), parseInt(full.slice(4, 6), 16)];
};
const key = (rgb: readonly number[]) => rgb.join(",");
/** White: every channel at or above 245 (bone, #E8E4DC, is the lightest allowed value). */
const isWhite = (rgb: readonly number[]) => Math.min(...rgb) >= 245;

describe("the Midnight palette", () => {
  const allowed = new Set(Object.values(palette).map((h) => key(toRgb(h))));

  it("is black, two near-black surfaces, brass, dim brass and three bone tones, and nothing else", () => {
    expect(Object.values(palette).map((h) => h.toUpperCase())).toEqual(["#000000", "#0B0B0D", "#141418", "#C9A24B", "#8F7434", "#E8E4DC", "#A09C94", "#6B6862"]);
    expect(midnight.name).toBe("Midnight");
  });

  it("every color in the theme file is one of those colors, alone or with an alpha", () => {
    const src = fs.readFileSync(THEME_FILE, "utf8").replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, "").replace(/\/\/.*$/gm, "");
    const hexes = src.match(HEX) ?? [];
    expect(hexes.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(8);
    for (const h of hexes) expect([h, allowed.has(key(toRgb(h)))]).toEqual([h, true]);
    // rgba(...) literals are written from named brass/bone/black rgb strings; check the strings.
    for (const m of src.matchAll(/(?:BRASS|BONE|BLACK)_RGB = "([^"]+)"/g)) expect([m[1], allowed.has(m[1]!)]).toEqual([m[1], true]);
    for (const f of src.match(FUNC) ?? []) {
      const nums = f.match(/\d+/g)!.slice(0, 3).map(Number);
      // Template-built rgba (`rgba(${BRASS_RGB},0.18)`) has no digits before the interpolation: those are checked above.
      if (nums.length === 3) expect([f, allowed.has(key(nums))]).toEqual([f, true]);
    }
  });

  it("nothing in the theme is white, and no token resolves to a white or a non-palette color", () => {
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(colors)) {
      if (value === "transparent") continue;
      const m = /^rgba\((\d+),(\d+),(\d+),[\d.]+\)$/.exec(value);
      const rgb = m ? [Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]), Number(m[3])] : toRgb(value);
      expect([name, isWhite(rgb), allowed.has(key(rgb))]).toEqual([name, false, true]);
    }
    expect(Math.max(...Object.values(palette).map((h) => toRgb(h).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 3))).toBeLessThan(245);
  });

  it("errors and states are brass, never another hue", () => {
    expect([colors.success, colors.warning, colors.danger]).toEqual([palette.brass, palette.brass, palette.brass]);
  });

  it("the white detector works", () => {
    expect(isWhite(toRgb("#FFFFFF"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isWhite(toRgb("#FAFAFA"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isWhite(toRgb("#fff"))).toBe(true);
    expect(isWhite(toRgb("#E8E4DC"))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("no color literal outside the theme file", () => {
  const files = walk(SRC).filter((f) => f !== THEME_FILE && !f.endsWith(path.join("api", "contracts.ts")));

  it("scans the whole app", () => {
    expect(files.length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
  });

  it("finds no hex, rgb(a), hsl(a) or named color in any source file", () => {
    const offenders: string[] = [];
    for (const f of files) {
      const src = fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8").replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, "").replace(/(^|[^:])\/\/.*$/gm, "$1");
      for (const m of [...(src.match(HEX) ?? []), ...(src.match(FUNC) ?? []), ...[...src.matchAll(NAMED)].map((x) => x[0])]) offenders.push(`${path.relative(SRC, f)}: ${m}`);
    }
    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("uses no white anywhere in the app, including the config", () => {
    for (const f of ["../app.json", "../eas.json"]) {
      const src = fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(SRC, f), "utf8");
      for (const h of src.match(HEX) ?? []) expect([f, h, isWhite(toRgb(h))]).toEqual([f, h, false]);
    }
    const app = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(SRC, "../app.json"), "utf8")).expo;
    expect(app.backgroundColor).toBe("#000000");
  });

  it("the detector catches a planted offender", () => {
    expect("const x = { color: '#FFFFFF' }".match(HEX)).toEqual(["#FFFFFF"]);
    expect("backgroundColor: 'white'".match(NAMED)?.length).toBe(1);
    expect("shadowColor: \"rgba(255,255,255,0.4)\"".match(FUNC)?.length).toBe(1);
  });
});

===== src/theme/midnight.ts =====

/**
 * MIDNIGHT: the one theme. Black and brass gold, nothing else. This is the ONLY file in the app where a color literal may
 * appear (a test fails on any literal elsewhere, and on any white value anywhere). Every visual decision (colors, type,
 * radii, spacing, shadows, motion, stamp styles) is read from this object, so a screen can be replaced without touching
 * the look: imported screens just use `midnight` (or the `colors`, `type`, `space` ... re-exports in this folder).
 *
 * Rules of the palette:
 *  - No other hue. Not even for errors: errors and states are brass, dim brass, bone text, icons and words.
 *  - Nothing is white. The lightest value is bone (#E8E4DC). User photos and album art are exempt; UI chrome is not.
 *  - Color is never the only signal: pair it with a word, a shape or an icon.
 */

/** The eight solid colors. Everything else in this file is one of these with an alpha. */
export const palette = {
  black: "#000000",
  ink: "#0B0B0D",
  inkRaised: "#141418",
  brass: "#C9A24B",
  dimBrass: "#8F7434",
  bone: "#E8E4DC",
  boneDim: "#A09C94",
  boneFaint: "#6B6862",
} as const;

const BRASS_RGB = "201,162,75";
const BONE_RGB = "232,228,220";
const BLACK_RGB = "0,0,0";

export const colors = {
  /** Screen background: pure black, so the OLED turns off and the brass glows. */
  background: palette.black,
  /** Page stock and cards. */
  surface: palette.ink,
  elevated: palette.inkRaised,
  /** Fine rules and borders: brass at low strength. */
  hairline: `rgba(${BRASS_RGB},0.18)`,

  textPrimary: palette.bone,
  textSecondary: palette.boneDim,
  textTertiary: palette.boneFaint,

  /** The one accent: brass. Primary buttons, selection, foil. */
  accent: palette.brass,
  accentPressed: palette.dimBrass,
  /** Text and icons on brass. */
  onAccent: palette.black,

  /** The verified badge is brass like everything that matters. */
  verified: palette.brass,
  onVerified: palette.black,

  /** States share the palette. They are told apart by words and icons, never by hue. */
  success: palette.brass,
  warning: palette.brass,
  danger: palette.brass,

  /** Behind sheets and over photos. */
  scrim: `rgba(${BLACK_RGB},0.6)`,
  pressedOverlay: `rgba(${BRASS_RGB},0.08)`,
  transparent: "transparent",

  // The brass family, named for the passport.
  black: palette.black,
  ink: palette.ink,
  inkRaised: palette.inkRaised,
  brass: palette.brass,
  dimBrass: palette.dimBrass,
  /** Foil glow: a soft brass halo around stamps, the cover and active pins. */
  brassGlow: `rgba(${BRASS_RGB},0.35)`,
  brassGlowSoft: `rgba(${BRASS_RGB},0.14)`,
  /** The moving highlight on foil: bone, not white. */
  foilHighlight: `rgba(${BONE_RGB},0.5)`,
  bone: palette.bone,
  boneDim: palette.boneDim,
  boneFaint: palette.boneFaint,
  /** Shadow tint (black) and the paper grain (bone at very low strength). */
  shadow: palette.black,
  grain: `rgba(${BONE_RGB},0.045)`,
  /** Leather: near-black with a brass sheen at the edges. */
  leather: palette.ink,
  leatherSheen: `rgba(${BRASS_RGB},0.07)`,
} as const;

export type ColorToken = keyof typeof colors;

/** Type families. Titles and stamp text: a refined serif. Dates and captions: a typewriter mono. UI: the system sans (SF Pro). */
export const fonts = {
  serif: "CormorantGaramond_600SemiBold",
  serifItalic: "CormorantGaramond_500Medium_Italic",
  serifRegular: "CormorantGaramond_500Medium",
  mono: "CourierPrime_400Regular",
  monoBold: "CourierPrime_700Bold",
  /** The system font (undefined = platform default, SF Pro on iOS). */
  sans: undefined,
} as const;

/**
 * Shadows. Layered, not flat: a tight contact shadow plus a wide soft one. iOS shadow props; shadows are black, and a
 * brass glow variant is used for lit foil.
 */
export const shadows = {
  contact: { shadowColor: colors.shadow, shadowOpacity: 0.55, shadowRadius: 3, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 2 } },
  soft: { shadowColor: colors.shadow, shadowOpacity: 0.5, shadowRadius: 18, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 10 } },
  book: { shadowColor: colors.shadow, shadowOpacity: 0.8, shadowRadius: 34, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 22 } },
  page: { shadowColor: colors.shadow, shadowOpacity: 0.6, shadowRadius: 8, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 3 } },
  glow: { shadowColor: colors.brass, shadowOpacity: 0.55, shadowRadius: 14, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 0 } },
} as const;

/** Motion for the book. Springs, never linear tweens. Quiet everywhere except the foil, the page flip and the stamp landing. */
export const bookMotion = {
  /** Cover opening: a heavy swing that settles. */
  coverOpen: { damping: 18, stiffness: 90, mass: 1.1 },
  /** Page turn released by the finger. */
  pageFlip: { damping: 20, stiffness: 140, mass: 0.9 },
  /** The stamp slamming down. */
  stampSlam: { damping: 11, stiffness: 260, mass: 0.7 },
  /** Small UI. */
  snap: { damping: 22, stiffness: 320, mass: 0.6 },
  durations: { fast: 150, standard: 250, ink: 420, glow: 900, crossfade: 220, shimmer: 2600 },
} as const;

/** How stamps look. Variety comes from SHAPE and DESIGN, never color: ink is always brass. */
export const stampStyle = {
  ink: colors.brass,
  inkDim: colors.dimBrass,
  glow: colors.brassGlow,
  /** 0..1 strength of the rough, uneven edge and of the missing ink. */
  roughness: 0.55,
  partialFill: 0.18,
  /** Stroke widths for rings, rules and text in a stamp (pt at a 120 pt stamp). */
  strokes: { ring: 3, hair: 1.2, text: 2 },
  /** Type inside a stamp, in the same 120 pt box: name, neighborhood line, and the completion stamp's words. */
  text: { name: 11, area: 6.5, arcTrack: 1.2, bannerTrack: 1, completeWord: 13, completeTrack: 2, completeArea: 7 },
  /** Largest tilt a stamp is given, in degrees. */
  maxTiltDeg: 11,
  /** Overlap allowed between neighbors, as a share of the stamp. */
  maxOverlap: 0.12,
} as const;

/** Line weights and radii for the book's drawn parts (Skia and SVG take plain numbers, so they live in the theme). */
export const bookStyle = {
  /** Cover border: a thick line and a hairline. */
  borderThick: 2,
  borderHair: 0.8,
  /** Emblem strokes, in unit-circle space (the emblem is scaled up). */
  emblemRing: 0.022,
  emblemInner: 0.012,
  emblemTick: 0.01,
  /** Page guilloche, rules and the ribbon. */
  pageLine: 0.6,
  pageRule: 1,
  /** Gutter fold, as a share of a page's width. */
  gutterShare: 0.09,
  /** Visible page-stack edge lines. */
  edgeLine: 1,
  edgeGap: 2,
  maxEdgeLines: 6,
} as const;

/** The whole theme as one object, for components that take "the theme". */
export const midnight = { name: "Midnight", palette, colors, fonts, shadows, bookMotion, bookStyle, stampStyle } as const;
export type Theme = typeof midnight;


===== src/theme/motion.ts =====

/**
 * Motion tokens. Springs, not linear easing. Nothing settles later than `maxDurationMs`.
 * Spring configs use Reanimated's duration-based springs so the 350 ms cap holds.
 */
export const maxDurationMs = 350;

export const spring = {
  /** Sheet snaps: springy with a little overshoot. */
  sheet: { duration: 340, dampingRatio: 0.78 },
  /** Selected marker scale-up. */
  pop: { duration: 280, dampingRatio: 0.6 },
  /** Small UI (chips, toggles). */
  snappy: { duration: 220, dampingRatio: 0.85 },
} as const;

export const timing = {
  fast: 150,
  standard: 250,
  /** Packed marker glow: one breath. Repeats, but each cycle is well under the cap per phase. */
  glowPhase: 1200,
  skeletonPulse: 900,
} as const;

/** Scale for the selected marker. */
export const selectedMarkerScale = 1.25;
/** Opacity of a disabled/dimmed control. */
export const dimmedOpacity = 0.45;
export const pressedOpacity = 0.7;


===== src/theme/typography.ts =====

/** SF Pro (the system font) on the iOS type scale. Sizes are size/line-height in points. */
import type { TextStyle } from "react-native";

import { fonts } from "./midnight";

const scale = {
  // Titles are set in the serif (the family carries the weight, so fontWeight stays regular).
  largeTitle: { fontFamily: fonts.serif, fontSize: 38, lineHeight: 44, fontWeight: "400" },
  title: { fontFamily: fonts.serif, fontSize: 30, lineHeight: 36, fontWeight: "400" },
  title2: { fontFamily: fonts.serif, fontSize: 24, lineHeight: 30, fontWeight: "400" },
  /** Stamp text, plaques and page titles in the book. */
  serifLabel: { fontFamily: fonts.serif, fontSize: 18, lineHeight: 22, fontWeight: "400", letterSpacing: 1.5 },
  /** Dates, captions, page numbers: the typewriter. */
  typewriter: { fontFamily: fonts.mono, fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 22, fontWeight: "400" },
  typewriterSmall: { fontFamily: fonts.mono, fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 16, fontWeight: "400", letterSpacing: 0.5 },
  headline: { fontSize: 17, lineHeight: 22, fontWeight: "600" },
  body: { fontSize: 17, lineHeight: 22, fontWeight: "400" },
  callout: { fontSize: 16, lineHeight: 21, fontWeight: "400" },
  subhead: { fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 20, fontWeight: "400" },
  footnote: { fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 18, fontWeight: "400" },
  caption: { fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 16, fontWeight: "400" },
} as const satisfies Record<string, TextStyle>;

export const type = scale;
export type TypeVariant = keyof typeof scale;

/** Emphasis weights applied on top of a variant. */
export const weight = { regular: "400", medium: "500", semibold: "600", bold: "700" } as const;

/** Counts and money line up in columns. */
export const tabular: TextStyle = { fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] };

/**
 * Dynamic Type: text scales with the user's setting. Layouts are built to hold at
 * least up to the XL/XXXL sizes; beyond this multiplier text stops growing.
 */
export const maxFontSizeMultiplier = 1.6;


===== src/api/__tests__/base-url.test.ts =====

import { readFileSync } from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { resolveBaseUrl, resolveMediaUrl } from "../base-url";

describe("resolveBaseUrl", () => {
  it("connects to the backend dev server configured for this lane, including localhost fallback", () => {
    const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "../../../../backend/package.json"), "utf8"));
    const port = /--port(?:=|\s+)(\d+)/.exec(pkg.scripts.dev)?.[1];
    expect(port).toBe("3001");
    expect(new URL(resolveBaseUrl(undefined, "192.168.1.20:8081", true)).port).toBe(port);
    expect(new URL(resolveBaseUrl(undefined, undefined, true)).port).toBe(port);
  });

  it("prefers EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL and trims trailing slashes", () => {
    expect(resolveBaseUrl("https://preview.nostos.app/", "192.168.1.20:8081", true)).toBe("https://preview.nostos.app");
  });
  it("uses the Metro host on the LAN in development", () => {
    expect(resolveBaseUrl(undefined, "192.168.1.20:8081", true)).toBe("http://192.168.1.20:3001");
    expect(resolveBaseUrl("", "192.168.1.20:8081/--/", true)).toBe("http://192.168.1.20:3001");
  });
  it("refuses to guess in release builds", () => {
    expect(() => resolveBaseUrl(undefined, undefined, false)).toThrow(/EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL/);
  });
});

describe("resolveMediaUrl", () => {
  it("resolves local-storage paths against the API host (development)", () => {
    expect(resolveMediaUrl("http://192.168.1.20:3001", "/api/uploads/p/abc.jpg?exp=1&sig=x")).toBe("http://192.168.1.20:3001/api/uploads/p/abc.jpg?exp=1&sig=x");
  });
  it("leaves protocol-relative URLs alone", () => {
    expect(resolveMediaUrl("https://preview.nostos.app", "//cdn.example/p.jpg")).toBe("//cdn.example/p.jpg");
  });
  it("leaves signed R2 URLs alone", () => {
    const r2 = "https://acct.r2.cloudflarestorage.com/nostos-photos-preview/p/abc.jpg?X-Amz-Signature=1";
    expect(resolveMediaUrl("https://preview.nostos.app", r2)).toBe(r2);
  });
});


===== src/api/__tests__/client.test.ts =====

import { ApiError, BAD_RESPONSE_MESSAGE, createApi, errorText, OFFLINE_MESSAGE, onlyUnknownKeys, parseResponse } from "../client";
import { NearbyPlacesResponse, PlacesResponse } from "../contracts";

type Call = { url: string; init: RequestInit };

function jsonResponse(status: number, body: unknown): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
}

function setup(respond: (call: Call) => Response | Promise<Response>, token: string | null = "tok_abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz") {
  const calls: Call[] = [];
  const onInvalidToken = jest.fn();
  const api = createApi({
    baseUrl: "https://api.example.test/",
    getToken: () => token,
    onInvalidToken,
    fetch: (async (url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
      const call = { url, init };
      calls.push(call);
      return respond(call);
    }) as unknown as typeof fetch,
  });
  return { api, calls, onInvalidToken };
}

const place = {
  id: "v1", slug: "velvet", name: "Velvet", address: "1 Ave A", neighborhood: "East Village", lat: 40.72, lng: -73.98, geofenceMeters: 100, neighborhoodId: null, fromOsm: true,
};
const placesBody = { places: [place], attribution: { text: "© OpenStreetMap contributors", url: "https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright", license: "ODbL-1.0" } };

describe("api client", () => {
  it("sends the bearer token and X-Client: ios, and parses the response", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, placesBody));
    const res = await api.places.list();
    expect(res.places[0].name).toBe("Velvet");
    expect(calls[0].url).toBe("https://api.example.test/api/places");
    const h = calls[0].init.headers as Record<string, string>;
    expect(h.Authorization).toBe("Bearer tok_abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz");
    expect(h["X-Client"]).toBe("ios");
    expect(h["Content-Type"]).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("omits Authorization when signed out and sends JSON bodies", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup(
      () => jsonResponse(201, { token: "t".repeat(40), expiresAt: "2026-12-24T00:00:00.000Z", user: { id: "u1", handle: "sam", displayName: "Sam", role: "USER" } }),
      null,
    );
    await api.auth.login({ email: "sam@example.com", password: "pw" });
    const h = calls[0].init.headers as Record<string, string>;
    expect(h.Authorization).toBeUndefined();
    expect(h["Content-Type"]).toBe("application/json");
    expect(JSON.parse(String(calls[0].init.body))).toEqual({ email: "sam@example.com", password: "pw" });
  });

  it("surfaces the server's message and code unchanged (geofence 403)", async () => {
    const message = "You need to be within 100 m of this place to stamp it (you're 140 m away)";
    const { api } = setup(() => jsonResponse(403, { error: "forbidden", message }));
    const err = await api.stamps.get("s1").catch((e) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "forbidden", message });
    expect(err.isClientError).toBe(true);
  });

  it("surfaces cooldowns (429) with the server's message", async () => {
    const message = "You already stamped this place. Try again in 12 min.";
    const { api } = setup(() => jsonResponse(429, { error: "rate_limited", message }));
    await expect(api.stamps.get("s1")).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "rate_limited", message });
  });

  it("calls onInvalidToken only for 401 invalid_token", async () => {
    const dead = setup(() => jsonResponse(401, { error: "invalid_token", message: "Your session has ended. Sign in again." }));
    await expect(dead.api.me.get()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "invalid_token" });
    expect(dead.onInvalidToken).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);

    const wrong = setup(() => jsonResponse(401, { error: "invalid_credentials", message: "Wrong email or password." }));
    await expect(wrong.api.auth.login({ email: "a@b.co", password: "x" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "invalid_credentials" });
    expect(wrong.onInvalidToken).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("maps network failures to an offline error", async () => {
    const { api } = setup(() => {
      throw new TypeError("Network request failed");
    });
    await expect(api.places.list()).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 0, code: "offline", message: OFFLINE_MESSAGE });
  });

  it("maps non-JSON errors and contract mismatches to a generic message", async () => {
    const gateway = setup(() => new Response("<html>502</html>", { status: 502 }));
    await expect(gateway.api.places.list()).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 502, code: "bad_response", message: BAD_RESPONSE_MESSAGE });

    const spy = jest.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const drift = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { ...placesBody, places: [{ ...place, geofenceMeters: "wide" }] }));
    await expect(drift.api.places.list()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "bad_response" });
    spy.mockRestore();
  });

  it("encodes path segments and query strings", async () => {
    const spy = jest.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const { api, calls } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { users: [] }));
    await api.users.search("a b&c");
    expect(calls[0].url).toBe("https://api.example.test/api/users?q=a%20b%26c");
    await api.users.follow("x/y", true).catch(() => undefined);
    expect(calls[1].url).toBe("https://api.example.test/api/users/x%2Fy/follow");
    expect(calls[1].init.method).toBe("PUT");
    spy.mockRestore();
  });

  it("uses the right verbs for toggles", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { state: "following" }));
    await api.users.follow("sam", true);
    await api.users.follow("sam", false);
    expect(calls.map((c) => c.init.method)).toEqual(["PUT", "DELETE"]);
  });

  it("sends the position to nearby places as a query and nothing else", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { places: [] }));
    await api.places.nearby({ lat: 40.7265, lng: -73.9845 });
    expect(calls[0].url).toBe("https://api.example.test/api/places/nearby?lat=40.7265&lng=-73.9845");
    expect(calls[0].init.method).toBe("GET");
  });
});

describe("forward compatibility", () => {
  it("accepts responses whose only problem is a field this app doesn't know yet", () => {
    const spy = jest.spyOn(console, "info").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const withNewField = { ...placesBody, places: [{ ...place, newThing: 1 }], alsoNew: true };
    const parsed = parseResponse(PlacesResponse, withNewField, "/api/places");
    expect(parsed.places[0].name).toBe("Velvet");
    spy.mockRestore();
  });

  it("still accepts unknown keys inside a nested object", () => {
    const spy = jest.spyOn(console, "info").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const body = { places: [{ ...place, distanceM: 140, inside: false, extra: "x" }] };
    expect(parseResponse(NearbyPlacesResponse, body, "/nearby")).toMatchObject({ places: [{ distanceM: 140 }] });
    spy.mockRestore();
  });

  it("rejects missing or mistyped fields", () => {
    const r = PlacesResponse.safeParse({ ...placesBody, places: [{ ...place, geofenceMeters: "100" }] });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
    if (!r.success) expect(onlyUnknownKeys(r.error.issues)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("hardening", () => {
  it("times out hung requests", async () => {
    const onInvalidToken = jest.fn();
    const api = createApi({
      baseUrl: "https://api.example.test",
      getToken: () => null,
      onInvalidToken,
      timeoutMs: 20,
      fetch: ((_url: string, init: RequestInit) =>
        new Promise((_resolve, reject) => {
          init.signal?.addEventListener("abort", () => reject(new Error("aborted")));
        })) as unknown as typeof fetch,
    });
    await expect(api.places.list()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "timeout" });
  });

  it("still recognizes invalid_token when the error body gains a field", async () => {
    const { api, onInvalidToken } = setup(() => jsonResponse(401, { error: "invalid_token", message: "Sign in again.", requestId: "r1" }));
    await expect(api.me.get()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "invalid_token" });
    expect(onInvalidToken).toHaveBeenCalledWith("tok_abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz");
  });

  it("recognizes invalid_token from WWW-Authenticate when the body is unreadable", async () => {
    const { api, onInvalidToken } = setup(
      () => new Response("nope", { status: 401, headers: { "WWW-Authenticate": 'Bearer realm="nostos", error="invalid_token"' } }),
    );
    await expect(api.me.get()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "invalid_token" });
    expect(onInvalidToken).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("never reports a dead token for a request that sent none", async () => {
    const { api, onInvalidToken } = setup(() => jsonResponse(401, { error: "invalid_token", message: "x" }), null);
    await expect(api.places.list()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "invalid_token" });
    expect(onInvalidToken).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("uses its own sentence for 5xx even when the body is JSON", async () => {
    const { api } = setup(() => jsonResponse(500, { error: "internal", message: "PrismaClientKnownRequestError at line 3" }));
    await expect(api.places.list()).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 500, message: BAD_RESPONSE_MESSAGE });
  });

  it("logout sends the token it is given, not the current one", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { ok: true }), "tok_current_current_current");
    await api.auth.logout("tok_previous_previous_previous");
    expect((calls[0].init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization).toBe("Bearer tok_previous_previous_previous");
  });
});

describe("errorText", () => {
  it("keeps server sentences written for people and replaces validation and 5xx text", () => {
    expect(errorText(new ApiError(429, "rate_limited", "Try again in 12 min."), "fallback")).toBe("Try again in 12 min.");
    expect(errorText(new ApiError(400, "invalid_input", "email: Invalid email"), "Check your email.")).toBe("Check your email.");
    expect(errorText(new ApiError(503, "bad_response", "x"), "fallback")).toBe("fallback");
    expect(errorText(new Error("boom"), "fallback")).toBe("fallback");
  });
});

describe("stamp and social endpoints", () => {
  const stamp = {
    id: "s1", photoUrl: "https://cdn.example.test/s1.jpg", page: 1, slot: 1, photos: [{ id: "ph1", url: "https://cdn.example.test/s1.jpg", position: 0, x: null, y: null, rotation: null }], caption: "hi", song: { title: "Heroes", artist: "David Bowie", artUrl: null, sourceId: "1" },
    createdAt: "2026-09-24T02:00:00.000Z", editedAt: null, place: { id: "v1", slug: "velvet", name: "Velvet", neighborhood: "East Village", neighborhoodId: null },
    author: { handle: "sam", displayName: "Sam", avatarUrl: null, verified: false }, mine: false, likes: { count: 3, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false,
  };

  it("pages the feed and a passport grid with the server's opaque cursor, encoded", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup((c) => jsonResponse(200, c.url.includes("/api/feed") ? { stamps: [], nextBefore: null } : { stamps: [], nextAfter: null }));
    await api.feed();
    await api.feed("2026-09-24T02:00:00.000Z_s1");
    await api.users.stamps("sam");
    await api.users.stamps("sam", "2026-09-24T02:00:00.000Z_s1");
    expect(calls.map((c) => c.url)).toEqual([
      "https://api.example.test/api/feed",
      "https://api.example.test/api/feed?before=2026-09-24T02%3A00%3A00.000Z_s1",
      "https://api.example.test/api/users/sam/stamps",
      "https://api.example.test/api/users/sam/stamps?after=2026-09-24T02%3A00%3A00.000Z_s1",
    ]);
  });

  it("likes with PUT and unlikes with DELETE", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { liked: true, count: 4, viewerLiked: true }));
    await api.stamps.like("s1", true);
    await api.stamps.like("s1", false);
    expect(calls.map((c) => [c.init.method, c.url])).toEqual([
      ["PUT", "https://api.example.test/api/stamps/s1/like"],
      ["DELETE", "https://api.example.test/api/stamps/s1/like"],
    ]);
  });

  it("removes a stamp with POST /remove, never DELETE", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { removed: true }));
    await api.stamps.remove("s1");
    expect([calls[0].init.method, calls[0].url]).toEqual(["POST", "https://api.example.test/api/stamps/s1/remove"]);
  });

  it("edits only the fields it is given, and a null song removes it", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { stamp }));
    await api.stamps.edit("s1", { song: null });
    expect(calls[0].init.method).toBe("PATCH");
    expect(JSON.parse(String(calls[0].init.body))).toEqual({ song: null });
  });

  it("parses a stamp with its author, song and place", async () => {
    const { api } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { stamp }));
    const res = await api.stamps.get("s1");
    expect(res.stamp.author).toEqual({ handle: "sam", displayName: "Sam", avatarUrl: null, verified: false });
    expect(res.stamp.song?.title).toBe("Heroes");
    expect(res.stamp.place.slug).toBe("velvet");
  });

  it("searches songs with an encoded query", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup(() => jsonResponse(200, { songs: [{ title: "Heroes", artist: "David Bowie", album: null, artUrl: null, sourceId: "1" }] }));
    const res = await api.songs.search("david & bowie");
    expect(calls[0].url).toBe("https://api.example.test/api/songs/search?q=david%20%26%20bowie");
    expect(res.songs[0].sourceId).toBe("1");
  });

  it("decides a follow request and approves all", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup((c) => jsonResponse(200, c.url.endsWith("approve-all") ? { approved: 4 } : { approved: true }));
    await api.me.decideRequest("sam", false);
    await api.me.approveAllRequests();
    expect(calls[0].url).toBe("https://api.example.test/api/me/follow-requests/sam");
    expect(JSON.parse(String(calls[0].init.body))).toEqual({ approve: false });
    expect([calls[1].init.method, calls[1].url]).toEqual(["POST", "https://api.example.test/api/me/follow-requests/approve-all"]);
  });

  it("surfaces 403 private_account on a follower list and on a stamps grid", async () => {
    const { api } = setup(() => jsonResponse(403, { error: "private_account", message: "This account is private." }));
    await expect(api.users.people("sam", "followers")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "private_account" });
    await expect(api.users.stamps("sam")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "private_account" });
  });
});


===== src/api/__tests__/contracts-sync.test.ts =====

/** The contract must stay a verbatim copy of the backend's (when the backend repo is on this machine). */
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "fs";
import { join } from "path";

// mobile/src/api/__tests__ -> repo root -> backend/
const backendFile = join(process.env.NOSTOS_BACKEND ?? join(__dirname, "../../../../backend"), "src/contracts/api.ts");
const local = readFileSync(join(__dirname, "../contracts.ts"), "utf8");

describe("contracts.ts", () => {
  it("names its source", () => {
    expect(local).toMatch(/COPIED VERBATIM from backend\/src\/contracts\/api\.ts/);
  });

  (existsSync(backendFile) ? it : it.skip)("matches the backend contract byte for byte", () => {
    const body = local.replace(/^\/\*\*\n \* COPIED VERBATIM[\s\S]*?\*\/\n/, "");
    expect(body).toBe(readFileSync(backendFile, "utf8"));
  });
});


===== src/api/__tests__/live.test.ts =====

/**
 * Live contract check: runs the app's client against a real backend and parses every response with
 * the app's copy of the contract. Opt-in (it writes to that backend's database):
 *
 *   NOSTOS_LIVE_API=http://localhost:3000 npx jest src/api/__tests__/live.test.ts
 *
 * Uses the dev seed accounts (password `lastcall-dev`), including the geofence-exempt @appreview.
 */
import { createApi, type Api } from "../client";

const BASE = process.env.NOSTOS_LIVE_API;
const PASSWORD = process.env.NOSTOS_LIVE_PASSWORD ?? "lastcall-dev";
const d = BASE ? describe : describe.skip;

async function signedIn(email: string): Promise<Api> {
  let token: string | null = null;
  const api = createApi({ baseUrl: BASE!, getToken: () => token, onInvalidToken: () => (token = null) });
  const res = await api.auth.login({ email, password: PASSWORD });
  token = res.token;
  return api;
}

d("live backend", () => {
  jest.setTimeout(60_000);
  // Surface contract mismatches loudly instead of the generic "Something went wrong".
  const errors: unknown[] = [];
  beforeAll(() => jest.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation((...a) => void errors.push(a)));
  afterAll(() => expect(errors).toEqual([]));

  it("public endpoints parse", async () => {
    const api = createApi({ baseUrl: BASE!, getToken: () => null, onInvalidToken: () => undefined });
    const { places, attribution } = await api.places.list();
    expect(places.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(attribution === null || attribution.text === "© OpenStreetMap contributors").toBe(true);
    const one = await api.places.get(places[0]!.slug);
    expect(one.place.id).toBe(places[0]!.id);
    await api.users.search("ma");
  });

  it("a signed-in user: me, nearby places, feed, passport, lists, requests, activity", async () => {
    const api = await signedIn("appreview@lastcall.dev");
    const me = await api.me.get();
    expect(me.settings.geofenceExempt).toBe(true);
    const { places } = await api.places.list();
    const p = places[0]!;
    const near = await api.places.nearby({ lat: p.lat, lng: p.lng });
    expect(near.places[0]?.inside).toBe(true);
    await api.feed();
    await api.me.following();
    await api.me.blocks();
    await api.me.activity();
    await api.me.followRequests();
    const self = await api.users.get(me.user.handle);
    expect(self.relationship.isSelf).toBe(true);
    await api.users.stamps(me.user.handle);
    await api.users.places(me.user.handle);
  });

  it("moderator screens parse", async () => {
    const api = await signedIn("mod_morgan@lastcall.dev");
    await api.mod.queue();
    await api.mod.hidden();
    await api.mod.log();
  });

  it("social: a private passport is limited to a stranger, and the stamp lists are refused", async () => {
    const ava = await signedIn("avaa@lastcall.dev");
    const handle = (await ava.me.get()).user.handle;
    const own = await ava.users.get(handle);
    expect(own.access).toBe("full");
    const grid = await ava.users.stamps(handle);
    if (grid.nextAfter) await ava.users.stamps(handle, grid.nextAfter);
    if (grid.stamps[0]) expect((await ava.stamps.get(grid.stamps[0].id)).stamp.mine).toBe(true);

    // The seed keeps @avaa private, and @moth_hopeful follows no one.
    const stranger = await signedIn("moth_hopeful@lastcall.dev");
    const limited = await stranger.users.get(handle);
    expect(limited.access).toBe("limited");
    await expect(stranger.users.people(handle, "followers")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "private_account" });
    await expect(stranger.users.stamps(handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "private_account" });
    await expect(stranger.users.places(handle)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: "private_account" });
    if (grid.stamps[0]) await expect(stranger.stamps.get(grid.stamps[0].id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
  });

  it("a dead token is reported as invalid_token", async () => {
    let cleared = false;
    const api = createApi({ baseUrl: BASE!, getToken: () => "x".repeat(40), onInvalidToken: () => (cleared = true) });
    await expect(api.me.get()).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401, code: "invalid_token" });
    expect(cleared).toBe(true);
  });
});


===== src/api/base-url.ts =====

/**
 * Resolves the API base URL.
 * - `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL` wins (set per EAS profile: preview deploy for TestFlight, production for release).
 * - In development without it, use the Mac running Metro: Expo Go already reaches it over the LAN,
 *   and the backend's `pnpm dev` listens on port 3001 on the same machine.
 */
export const DEV_API_PORT = 3001;

export function resolveBaseUrl(envUrl: string | undefined, hostUri: string | undefined, isDev: boolean): string {
  const fromEnv = envUrl?.trim();
  if (fromEnv) return fromEnv.replace(/\/+$/, "");
  if (isDev && hostUri) {
    const host = hostUri.split("/")[0].replace(/:\d+$/, "");
    if (host) return `http://${host}:${DEV_API_PORT}`;
  }
  if (isDev) return `http://localhost:${DEV_API_PORT}`;
  throw new Error("EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL is not set for this build. Set it in eas.json.");
}

/** Relative media paths (the backend's local storage driver) resolve against the API host; absolute URLs pass through. */
export function resolveMediaUrl(base: string, url: string): string {
  // "/path" is relative to the API host; "//host/path" (protocol-relative) is not.
  return /^\/(?!\/)/.test(url) ? `${base.replace(/\/+$/, "")}${url}` : url;
}


===== src/api/client.ts =====

/**
 * Typed HTTP client for the Nostos API (see backend/API.md).
 *
 * Every response is parsed with the zod schemas in ./contracts. The server decides every rule
 * (geofence, cooldowns, permissions); on failure the client surfaces the server's
 * `message` unchanged through `ApiError` so screens can show it inline.
 */
import type { z } from "zod";

import * as C from "./contracts";

export type ApiErrorCode =
  | "unauthorized"
  | "invalid_token"
  | "invalid_credentials"
  | "invalid_input"
  | "bad_request"
  | "objectionable_content"
  | "private_account"
  | "song_search_unavailable"
  | "forbidden"
  | "wrong_password"
  | "account_suspended"
  | "not_found"
  | "conflict"
  | "rate_limited"
  | "too_large"
  | "unsupported_type"
  | "length_required"
  // Client-side codes
  | "offline"
  | "timeout"
  | "cancelled"
  | "bad_response"
  | (string & {});

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code: ApiErrorCode;

  constructor(status: number, code: ApiErrorCode, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
  }

  /** 4xx errors are final: retrying the same request gives the same answer. */
  get isClientError(): boolean {
    return this.status >= 400 && this.status < 500;
  }
}

export const OFFLINE_MESSAGE = "You're offline. Check your connection and try again.";
export const TIMEOUT_MESSAGE = "Nostos isn't responding. Check your connection and try again.";
export const BAD_RESPONSE_MESSAGE = "Something went wrong on our end. Try again in a moment.";

/** JSON requests give up after this long (iOS would otherwise wait about 60 s). */
export const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;
export const UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS = 90_000;

export type ApiConfig = {
  baseUrl: string;
  getToken: () => string | null;
  /**
   * Called on a dead-token response (401 `invalid_token`) with the token that request sent, so a
   * late answer for a previous account never signs out the current one.
   */
  onInvalidToken: (sentToken: string) => void;
  fetch?: typeof fetch;
  timeoutMs?: number;
};

/** Error bodies are parsed leniently: an extra field must never hide an `invalid_token`. */
const LenientError = C.ErrorResponse.passthrough();

/** Sentences for errors whose server text isn't written for this screen (validation, 5xx). */
export function errorText(e: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  if (!(e instanceof ApiError)) return fallback;
  if (e.code === "invalid_input" || e.code === "bad_request" || e.status >= 500) return fallback;
  return e.message;
}

type Method = "GET" | "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH" | "DELETE";

/**
 * True when a parse failure is only about keys the app doesn't know yet. Responses are `.strict()`
 * so the backend's drift tests catch contract changes, but a shipped app must keep working when a
 * newer backend adds a field. Anything else (missing field, wrong type) is still a hard failure.
 */
export function onlyUnknownKeys(issues: z.ZodIssue[]): boolean {
  return (
    issues.length > 0 &&
    issues.every((issue) => {
      if (issue.code === "unrecognized_keys") return true;
      if (issue.code === "invalid_union") return issue.unionErrors.some((e) => onlyUnknownKeys(e.issues));
      return false;
    })
  );
}

export function parseResponse<S extends z.ZodTypeAny>(schema: S, json: unknown, path: string): z.infer<S> {
  const result = schema.safeParse(json);
  if (result.success) return result.data;
  if (onlyUnknownKeys(result.error.issues)) {
    if (__DEV__) console.info(`[api] ${path}: response has fields this app version doesn't know yet`);
    return json as z.infer<S>;
  }
  if (__DEV__) console.error(`[api] ${path}: response did not match the contract`, result.error.issues);
  throw new ApiError(0, "bad_response", BAD_RESPONSE_MESSAGE);
}

async function errorFrom(res: Response): Promise<ApiError> {
  let body: unknown = null;
  try {
    body = await res.json();
  } catch {
    // Non-JSON error (proxy page, gateway timeout): fall through to a generic message.
  }
  if (res.status >= 500) return new ApiError(res.status, "bad_response", BAD_RESPONSE_MESSAGE);
  const parsed = LenientError.safeParse(body);
  if (parsed.success) return new ApiError(res.status, parsed.data.error, parsed.data.message);
  // The backend also names a dead bearer token in WWW-Authenticate (RFC 6750).
  if (res.status === 401 && /error="invalid_token"/.test(res.headers.get("WWW-Authenticate") ?? "")) {
    return new ApiError(401, "invalid_token", "Your session has ended. Sign in again.");
  }
  return new ApiError(res.status, "bad_request", BAD_RESPONSE_MESSAGE);
}

export function createApi(config: ApiConfig) {
  const doFetch = config.fetch ?? fetch;
  const base = config.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "");
  const timeoutMs = config.timeoutMs ?? REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS;

  function headers(token: string | null, extra?: Record<string, string>): Record<string, string> {
    const h: Record<string, string> = { Accept: "application/json", "X-Client": "ios", ...extra };
    if (token) h.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
    return h;
  }

  async function handle<S extends z.ZodTypeAny>(res: Response, schema: S, path: string, sentToken: string | null): Promise<z.infer<S>> {
    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await errorFrom(res);
      if (err.status === 401 && err.code === "invalid_token" && sentToken) config.onInvalidToken(sentToken);
      throw err;
    }
    let json: unknown;
    try {
      json = await res.json();
    } catch {
      throw new ApiError(res.status, "bad_response", BAD_RESPONSE_MESSAGE);
    }
    return parseResponse(schema, json, path);
  }

  async function request<S extends z.ZodTypeAny>(
    method: Method,
    path: string,
    schema: S,
    body?: unknown,
    opts?: { token?: string | null },
  ): Promise<z.infer<S>> {
    const token = opts && "token" in opts ? (opts.token ?? null) : config.getToken();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    let timedOut = false;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      timedOut = true;
      controller.abort();
    }, timeoutMs);
    let res: Response;
    try {
      res = await doFetch(`${base}${path}`, {
        method,
        headers: headers(token, body === undefined ? undefined : { "Content-Type": "application/json" }),
        body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
    } catch {
      throw timedOut ? new ApiError(0, "timeout", TIMEOUT_MESSAGE) : new ApiError(0, "offline", OFFLINE_MESSAGE);
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
    return handle(res, schema, path, token);
  }

  const enc = encodeURIComponent;

  return {
    baseUrl: base,
    /** Absolute URL of a public web page on the same host (legal pages). */
    webUrl: (path: string) => `${base}${path}`,

    auth: {
      signup: (body: C.SignupRequest) => request("POST", "/api/auth/signup", C.TokenResponse, body),
      login: (body: C.LoginRequest) => request("POST", "/api/auth/login", C.TokenResponse, body),
      /** Revoke a specific token (the one just cleared locally). */
      logout: (token: string) => request("POST", "/api/auth/logout", C.OkResponse, undefined, { token }),
    },

    me: {
      /** Pass `token` to load an account before switching to it (sign-in). */
      get: (token?: string) => request("GET", "/api/me", C.MeResponse, undefined, token ? { token } : undefined),
      acceptTerms: (version: string) => request("POST", "/api/me/terms", C.AcceptTermsResponse, { version }),
      deleteAccount: (password: string) => request("POST", "/api/me/delete", C.DeleteAccountResponse, { password }),
      blocks: () => request("GET", "/api/me/blocks", C.BlocksResponse),
      following: () => request("GET", "/api/me/following", C.FollowingResponse),
      profile: () => request("GET", "/api/me/profile", C.ProfileSettingsResponse),
      /** Send only changed fields. */
      updateProfile: (body: C.ProfileSettingsRequest) => request("PATCH", "/api/me/profile", C.ProfileSettingsResponse, body),
      /** Upload a new photo with `upload("/api/me/avatar", AvatarResponse, form)`. */
      removeAvatar: () => request("DELETE", "/api/me/avatar", C.AvatarResponse),
      followRequests: () => request("GET", "/api/me/follow-requests", C.FollowRequestsResponse),
      decideRequest: (handle: string, approve: boolean) =>
        request("POST", `/api/me/follow-requests/${enc(handle)}`, C.FollowRequestDecisionResponse, { approve }),
      approveAllRequests: () => request("POST", "/api/me/follow-requests/approve-all", C.ApproveAllResponse),
      removeFollower: (handle: string) => request("DELETE", `/api/me/followers/${enc(handle)}`, C.RemoveFollowerResponse),
      activity: () => request("GET", "/api/me/activity", C.ActivityResponse),
    },

    places: {
      list: () => request("GET", "/api/places", C.PlacesResponse),
      get: (idOrSlug: string) => request("GET", `/api/places/${enc(idOrSlug)}`, C.PlaceResponse),
      /** Places inside or near the geofence of a position, nearest first. The server never stores the position. */
      /** Your own page for a place: your stamps there (stacked by night) and whether you are inside its geofence now. */
      mine: (idOrSlug: string, pos?: { lat: number; lng: number }) =>
        request("GET", `/api/places/${enc(idOrSlug)}/mine${pos ? `?lat=${pos.lat}&lng=${pos.lng}` : ""}`, C.MyPlacePageResponse),
      nearby: (pos: { lat: number; lng: number }) => request("GET", `/api/places/nearby?lat=${pos.lat}&lng=${pos.lng}`, C.NearbyPlacesResponse),
      /** Upload a stamp with `upload("/api/places/:id/stamps", CreateStampResponse, form)`. */
    },

    stamps: {
      /** Add photos with `upload("/api/stamps/:id/photos", StampResponse, form)`. */
      layout: (id: string, body: C.PhotoLayoutRequest) => request("PATCH", `/api/stamps/${enc(id)}/photos`, C.StampResponse, body),
      removePhoto: (id: string, photoId: string) => request("POST", `/api/stamps/${enc(id)}/photos/${enc(photoId)}/remove`, C.StampResponse),
      get: (id: string) => request("GET", `/api/stamps/${enc(id)}`, C.StampResponse),
      /** Caption and song only; send only changed fields (`song: null` removes the song). */
      edit: (id: string, body: C.StampEditRequest) => request("PATCH", `/api/stamps/${enc(id)}`, C.StampResponse, body),
      /** "Delete from profile": hides the stamp for good (nothing is ever deleted). */
      remove: (id: string) => request("POST", `/api/stamps/${enc(id)}/remove`, C.RemoveStampResponse),
      like: (id: string, like: boolean) => request(like ? "PUT" : "DELETE", `/api/stamps/${enc(id)}/like`, C.LikeResponse),
    },

    /** Home feed: people you follow, newest first. Pass the previous page's `nextBefore` for the next page. */
    feed: (before?: string | null) => request("GET", `/api/feed${before ? `?before=${enc(before)}` : ""}`, C.FeedResponse),

    /** Your pinned stamps (up to 4) and the neighborhood index. Owner only. */
    pins: {
      get: () => request("GET", "/api/me/pins", C.PinsResponse),
      set: (stampIds: string[]) => request("PUT", "/api/me/pins", C.PinsResponse, { stampIds }),
    },
    neighborhoods: () => request("GET", "/api/me/neighborhoods", C.NeighborhoodIndexResponse),

    songs: {
      search: (q: string) => request("GET", `/api/songs/search?q=${enc(q)}`, C.SongSearchResponse),
    },

    users: {
      search: (q: string) => request("GET", `/api/users?q=${enc(q)}`, C.UserSearchResponse),
      get: (handle: string) => request("GET", `/api/users/${enc(handle)}`, C.ProfileResponse),
      follow: (handle: string, follow: boolean) => request(follow ? "PUT" : "DELETE", `/api/users/${enc(handle)}/follow`, C.FollowResponse),
      block: (handle: string, block: boolean) => request(block ? "POST" : "DELETE", `/api/users/${enc(handle)}/block`, C.BlockResponse),
      /** The passport's stamps, OLDEST first (page 1 stays page 1); pass the previous page's `nextAfter` for the next page. */
      stamps: (handle: string, after?: string | null) =>
        request("GET", `/api/users/${enc(handle)}/stamps${after ? `?after=${enc(after)}` : ""}`, C.UserStampsResponse),
      places: (handle: string) => request("GET", `/api/users/${enc(handle)}/places`, C.UserPlacesResponse),
      /** 403 `private_account` without full access to the profile. */
      people: (handle: string, list: "followers" | "following") => request("GET", `/api/users/${enc(handle)}/${list}`, C.PeopleListResponse),
    },

    flags: {
      create: (body: C.FlagRequest) => request("POST", "/api/flags", C.FlagResponse, body),
    },

    mod: {
      queue: () => request("GET", "/api/mod/queue", C.ModQueueResponse),
      hidden: () => request("GET", "/api/mod/hidden", C.ModHiddenResponse),
      log: () => request("GET", "/api/mod/log", C.ModLogResponse),
      act: (body: z.infer<typeof C.ModActionRequest>) => request("POST", "/api/mod/actions", C.ModActionResponse, body),
    },

    /**
     * Multipart upload with progress (fetch has no upload progress on React Native).
     * Rejects with `offline`/`timeout`, or `cancelled` when `signal` aborts.
     */
    upload<S extends z.ZodTypeAny>(
      path: string,
      schema: S,
      form: FormData,
      opts: { onProgress?: (fraction: number) => void; signal?: AbortSignal } = {},
    ): Promise<z.infer<S>> {
      const token = config.getToken();
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
        xhr.open("POST", `${base}${path}`);
        xhr.timeout = UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS;
        for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(headers(token))) xhr.setRequestHeader(k, v);
        if (opts.onProgress) {
          const onProgress = opts.onProgress;
          xhr.upload.onprogress = (e) => {
            if (e.lengthComputable && e.total > 0) onProgress(Math.min(1, e.loaded / e.total));
          };
        }
        const onAbort = () => xhr.abort();
        opts.signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort);
        const done = () => opts.signal?.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
        xhr.onerror = () => {
          done();
          reject(new ApiError(0, "offline", OFFLINE_MESSAGE));
        };
        xhr.ontimeout = () => {
          done();
          reject(new ApiError(0, "timeout", TIMEOUT_MESSAGE));
        };
        xhr.onabort = () => {
          done();
          reject(new ApiError(0, "cancelled", "Upload cancelled."));
        };
        xhr.onload = () => {
          done();
          if (xhr.status === 0) return reject(new ApiError(0, "offline", OFFLINE_MESSAGE));
          const res = new Response(xhr.responseText, {
            status: xhr.status,
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "WWW-Authenticate": xhr.getResponseHeader("WWW-Authenticate") ?? "" },
          });
          handle(res, schema, path, token).then(resolve, reject);
        };
        if (opts.signal?.aborted) {
          done();
          return reject(new ApiError(0, "cancelled", "Upload cancelled."));
        }
        xhr.send(form);
      });
    },
  };
}

export type Api = ReturnType<typeof createApi>;


===== src/api/index.ts =====

import Constants from "expo-constants";

import { resolveBaseUrl, resolveMediaUrl } from "./base-url";
import { createApi } from "./client";
import { tokenStore } from "@/auth/token-store";

export { ApiError, errorText } from "./client";
export type { Api } from "./client";

export const api = createApi({
  baseUrl: resolveBaseUrl(process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL, Constants.expoConfig?.hostUri, __DEV__),
  getToken: () => tokenStore.current(),
  onInvalidToken: (sent) => tokenStore.invalidate(sent),
});

/**
 * Photo URLs are absolute signed R2 URLs in preview/production, but the backend's local storage
 * driver (development) returns paths like `/api/uploads/...`. Resolve those against the API host.
 */
export function mediaUrl(url: string): string {
  return resolveMediaUrl(api.baseUrl, url);
}


===== src/api/keys.ts =====

/** React Query keys. Kept apart from the hooks so auth code can use them without an import cycle. */
export const qk = {
  me: ["me"] as const,
  places: ["places"] as const,
  nearby: (lat: number, lng: number) => ["places", "nearby", lat, lng] as const,
  feed: ["feed"] as const,
  following: ["me", "following"] as const,
  blocks: ["me", "blocks"] as const,
  profileSettings: ["me", "profile"] as const,
  placePage: (id: string, lat: number | null, lng: number | null) => ["places", "mine", id, lat, lng] as const,
  pins: ["me", "pins"] as const,
  neighborhoods: ["me", "neighborhoods"] as const,
  followRequests: ["me", "follow-requests"] as const,
  activity: ["me", "activity"] as const,
  /** `user(handle)` is a prefix of the grid and places keys, so invalidating a passport refreshes its tabs too. */
  user: (handle: string) => ["user", handle] as const,
  userStamps: (handle: string) => ["user", handle, "stamps"] as const,
  userPlaces: (handle: string) => ["user", handle, "places"] as const,
  /** Under ["users", "connections"] so follow toggles patch these rows (see features/people/use-follow.ts). */
  people: (handle: string, list: "followers" | "following") => ["users", "connections", handle, list] as const,
  stamp: (id: string) => ["stamp", id] as const,
  userSearch: (q: string) => ["users", "search", q] as const,
  songSearch: (q: string) => ["songs", "search", q] as const,
  modQueue: ["mod", "queue"] as const,
  modHidden: ["mod", "hidden"] as const,
  modLog: ["mod", "log"] as const,
};


===== src/api/queries.ts =====

/** Query keys and shared hooks for server state. */
import { keepPreviousData, useInfiniteQuery, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { api } from "./index";
import { qk } from "./keys";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";

export { qk };

export function useMe() {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.me, queryFn: () => api.me.get(), enabled: status === "signedIn", staleTime: 60_000 });
}

export function usePlaces() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.places, queryFn: api.places.list, staleTime: 5 * 60_000 });
}

/** Places inside or near the geofence of a position, nearest first. The position is rounded so a few steps don't refetch. */
export function useNearbyPlaces(pos: { lat: number; lng: number } | null) {
  const lat = pos ? Math.round(pos.lat * 10_000) / 10_000 : 0;
  const lng = pos ? Math.round(pos.lng * 10_000) / 10_000 : 0;
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.nearby(lat, lng), queryFn: () => api.places.nearby({ lat, lng }), enabled: pos !== null, staleTime: 30_000 });
}

/** Home feed: people you follow, newest first, 30 per page. Pages come from the server's opaque `nextBefore` cursor. */
export function useFeed() {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: qk.feed,
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => api.feed(pageParam),
    initialPageParam: null as string | null,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.nextBefore,
    enabled: status === "signedIn",
  });
}

export function useFollowing() {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.following, queryFn: api.me.following, enabled: status === "signedIn", staleTime: 60_000, refetchInterval: 60_000 });
}

export function useUserProfile(handle: string) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.user(handle), queryFn: () => api.users.get(handle) });
}

/** A passport's stamps, oldest first, 30 per page. */
export function useUserStamps(handle: string, enabled = true) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: qk.userStamps(handle),
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => api.users.stamps(handle, pageParam),
    initialPageParam: null as string | null,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.nextAfter,
    enabled,
  });
}

export function useUserPlaces(handle: string, enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.userPlaces(handle), queryFn: () => api.users.places(handle), enabled });
}

/** Followers or following of a passport you have full access to. */
export function usePeopleList(handle: string, list: "followers" | "following") {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.people(handle, list), queryFn: () => api.users.people(handle, list) });
}

/** Your own page for a place: your stamps there by night, and whether you are inside its geofence now. The position is rounded so a few steps don't refetch. */
export function usePlacePage(id: string, pos: { lat: number; lng: number } | null) {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const lat = pos ? Math.round(pos.lat * 10_000) / 10_000 : null;
  const lng = pos ? Math.round(pos.lng * 10_000) / 10_000 : null;
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: qk.placePage(id, lat, lng),
    queryFn: () => api.places.mine(id, lat !== null && lng !== null ? { lat, lng } : undefined),
    enabled: status === "signedIn" && id !== "",
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });
}

/** Your pinned stamps (up to 4). Owner only. */
export function usePins() {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.pins, queryFn: () => api.pins.get(), enabled: status === "signedIn", staleTime: 60_000 });
}

/** Your neighborhoods and how far through each you are. Owner only. */
export function useNeighborhoodIndex() {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.neighborhoods, queryFn: () => api.neighborhoods(), enabled: status === "signedIn", staleTime: 60_000 });
}

export function useProfileSettings() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.profileSettings, queryFn: api.me.profile });
}

export function useFollowRequests() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.followRequests, queryFn: api.me.followRequests });
}

export function useActivity() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.activity, queryFn: api.me.activity });
}

export function useStamp(id: string) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.stamp(id), queryFn: () => api.stamps.get(id) });
}

export function useUserSearch(q: string) {
  const query = q.trim();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: qk.userSearch(query),
    queryFn: () => api.users.search(query),
    enabled: query.length >= 2,
    staleTime: 30_000,
    // Keep the last results on screen while the next query loads (no skeleton flash per keystroke).
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
  });
}

export function useSongSearch(q: string) {
  const query = q.trim();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: qk.songSearch(query),
    queryFn: () => api.songs.search(query),
    enabled: query.length >= 2,
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
    retry: false,
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
  });
}

export function useBlocks() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.blocks, queryFn: api.me.blocks });
}

export function useModQueue() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.modQueue, queryFn: api.mod.queue, refetchInterval: 60_000 });
}

export function useModHidden(enabled: boolean) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.modHidden, queryFn: api.mod.hidden, enabled });
}

export function useModLog(enabled: boolean) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.modLog, queryFn: api.mod.log, enabled });
}


===== src/api/query-client.ts =====

import NetInfo from "@react-native-community/netinfo";
import { focusManager, MutationCache, onlineManager, QueryCache, QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { AppState, type AppStateStatus } from "react-native";

import { configureNetwork, isOffline } from "@/lib/network";
import { reportError } from "@/lib/report-error";

import { ApiError } from "./client";

export function createQueryClient(): QueryClient {
  return new QueryClient({
    // Unexpected failures (bugs, contract drift, 5xx) go to the error reporter, which is off unless the build enables it.
    queryCache: new QueryCache({ onError: (error, query) => reportError(error, { query: String(query.queryKey[0]) }) }),
    mutationCache: new MutationCache({ onError: (error) => reportError(error) }),
    defaultOptions: {
      queries: {
        staleTime: 15_000,
        // 4xx answers are final (not found, forbidden); only retry transient failures.
        retry: (count, error) => !(error instanceof ApiError && error.isClientError) && count < 2,
      },
      mutations: { retry: false },
    },
  });
}

/** Refetch on app foreground and pause queries while offline. Call once at startup. */
export function wireReactQueryToApp(): () => void {
  configureNetwork();
  const unsubscribeNet = NetInfo.addEventListener((state) => {
    onlineManager.setOnline(!isOffline(state));
  });
  const sub = AppState.addEventListener("change", (s: AppStateStatus) => focusManager.setFocused(s === "active"));
  return () => {
    unsubscribeNet();
    sub.remove();
  };
}


===== src/lib/__tests__/age-timezones.test.js =====

/* global describe, it, expect, __dirname */
import { execFileSync } from "child_process";
import path from "path";

const fixtures = [
  ["2026-10-03T03:59:59Z", "10/03/2005", false, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 2 }],
  ["2026-10-03T04:00:00Z", "10/03/2005", true, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 3 }],
  ["2026-01-02T04:59:59Z", "01/02/2005", false, { y: 2026, m: 1, d: 1 }],
  ["2026-01-02T05:00:00Z", "01/02/2005", true, { y: 2026, m: 1, d: 2 }],
];

// A separate Node process uses the real device-zone Date getters, without Jest's mocked process.env.
const script = `
  const fs = require('fs'), vm = require('vm'), babel = require('@babel/core');
  const filename = process.argv[1];
  const {code} = babel.transformSync(fs.readFileSync(filename,'utf8'), {
    filename, babelrc: false, configFile: false,
    presets: ['@babel/preset-typescript'], plugins: ['@babel/plugin-transform-modules-commonjs']
  });
  const context = {exports: {}};
  vm.runInNewContext(code, context);
  const fixtures = JSON.parse(process.argv[2]);
  process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(fixtures.map(([instant,birth]) => ({
    today: context.exports.today(new Date(instant)),
    check: context.exports.checkBirthdate(birth,new Date(instant))
  }))));
`;

describe("New York age boundary in every device timezone", () => {
  it.each(["UTC", "America/New_York", "Asia/Tokyo", "Pacific/Honolulu"])("%s agrees with the backend in summer and winter", (zone) => {
    const results = JSON.parse(execFileSync(process.execPath, ["-e", script, path.join(__dirname, "..", "age.ts"), JSON.stringify(fixtures)], {
      cwd: path.resolve(__dirname, "../../.."), env: { ...process.env, TZ: zone }, encoding: "utf8",
    }));
    for (const [i, [, birth, ok, today]] of fixtures.entries()) {
      expect(results[i].today).toEqual(today);
      expect(results[i].check.ok).toBe(ok);
      if (ok) {
        const [month, day, year] = birth.split("/");
        expect(results[i].check.apiDate).toBe(`${year}-${month}-${day}`);
      }
      else {
        expect(results[i].check).toMatchObject({ reason: "underage" });
        expect(results[i].check).not.toHaveProperty("apiDate");
      }
    }
  });
});


===== src/lib/__tests__/age.test.ts =====

import { ageOn, checkBirthdate, formatBirthdateInput, parseBirthdate, toApiDate, UNDERAGE_NOTICE } from "../age";

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-02T16:00:00Z"); // 2026-10-02 in New York

describe("formatBirthdateInput", () => {
  it.each([
    ["", ""],
    ["0", "0"],
    ["05", "05"],
    ["050", "05/0"],
    ["0505", "05/05"],
    ["05051", "05/05/1"],
    ["05/05/1990", "05/05/1990"],
    ["0505199012345", "05/05/1990"],
    ["ab05-05x1990", "05/05/1990"],
  ])("%s -> %s", (raw, out) => expect(formatBirthdateInput(raw)).toBe(out));
});

describe("parseBirthdate and toApiDate", () => {
  it("accepts real dates and renders the API form", () => {
    expect(parseBirthdate("05/05/1990")).toEqual({ y: 1990, m: 5, d: 5 });
    expect(toApiDate({ y: 1990, m: 5, d: 5 })).toBe("1990-05-05");
    expect(parseBirthdate("02/29/2000")).toEqual({ y: 2000, m: 2, d: 29 });
  });
  it("rejects impossible dates and wrong shapes", () => {
    for (const bad of ["02/30/1990", "13/01/1990", "00/10/1990", "05/05/90", "5/5/1990", "1990-05-05", "", "02/29/2001"]) expect(parseBirthdate(bad)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("ageOn", () => {
  it("counts a birthday from its own date", () => {
    expect(ageOn({ y: 2005, m: 10, d: 2 }, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 1 })).toBe(20);
    expect(ageOn({ y: 2005, m: 10, d: 2 }, { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 2 })).toBe(21);
  });
});

describe("checkBirthdate (21+)", () => {
  it("passes someone who turns 21 today, with the API's date form", () => {
    expect(checkBirthdate("10/02/2005", NOW)).toEqual({ ok: true, apiDate: "2005-10-02" });
    expect(checkBirthdate("05/05/1990", NOW)).toEqual({ ok: true, apiDate: "1990-05-05" });
  });
  it("turns away someone a day short, with one plain sentence, and no date to send", () => {
    const r = checkBirthdate("10/03/2005", NOW);
    expect(r).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "underage", message: UNDERAGE_NOTICE });
    expect(JSON.stringify(r)).not.toMatch(/2005|10\/03/);
  });
  it("treats unfinished, impossible, ancient and future dates as a format problem, not an age", () => {
    for (const bad of ["", "10/0", "02/30/1990", "01/01/1850", "01/01/2031"]) expect(checkBirthdate(bad, NOW)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, reason: "format" });
  });
});

===== src/lib/__tests__/report-error.test.ts =====

import * as fs from "node:fs";

import { ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import { _resetErrorReporting, initErrorReporting, isReportable, reportError, scrub, sentryDsn } from "../report-error";

const DSN = "https://abc123@o0.ingest.sentry.io/1";
const fake = () => ({ init: jest.fn(), captureException: jest.fn() });

beforeEach(() => {
  _resetErrorReporting();
  jest.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
});
afterEach(() => jest.restoreAllMocks());

describe("Sentry is off by default", () => {
  it("needs the flag AND an https DSN", () => {
    expect(sentryDsn({})).toBeNull();
    expect(sentryDsn({ EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN: DSN })).toBeNull();
    expect(sentryDsn({ EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED: "1" })).toBeNull();
    expect(sentryDsn({ EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED: "true", EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN: DSN })).toBeNull();
    expect(sentryDsn({ EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED: "1", EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN: "http://insecure/1" })).toBeNull();
    expect(sentryDsn({ EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED: "1", EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN: DSN })).toBe(DSN);
  });

  it("does not even load the SDK when off, and reporting is a no-op", () => {
    const load = jest.fn();
    expect(initErrorReporting({}, load)).toBe(false);
    expect(load).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(() => reportError(new Error("boom"))).not.toThrow();
  });

  it("no DSN is committed in the app config or the environment files", () => {
    for (const f of ["app.json", "eas.json", "package.json"]) expect(fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8")).not.toMatch(/ingest\.sentry\.io|sentry\.io\/\d/);
    expect(fs.readFileSync("app.json", "utf8")).not.toMatch(/SENTRY/);
  });
});

describe("when the build turns it on", () => {
  const env = { EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED: "1", EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN: DSN };

  it("initializes without personal data and with a scrubber", () => {
    const s = fake();
    expect(initErrorReporting(env, () => s)).toBe(true);
    const opts = s.init.mock.calls[0][0];
    expect(opts).toMatchObject({ dsn: DSN, sendDefaultPii: false, tracesSampleRate: 0 });
    expect(opts.beforeSend).toBe(scrub);
  });

  it("reports bugs and contract drift, not expected 4xx answers or being offline", () => {
    const s = fake();
    initErrorReporting(env, () => s);
    reportError(new Error("unexpected"));
    reportError(new ApiError(0, "bad_response", "x"));
    reportError(new ApiError(500, "bad_response", "x"));
    reportError(new ApiError(403, "forbidden", "You need to be within 100 m"));
    reportError(new ApiError(401, "invalid_credentials", "Wrong email or password"));
    reportError(new ApiError(0, "offline", "You're offline."));
    expect(s.captureException).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    expect(isReportable(new ApiError(404, "not_found", "x"))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("scrub removes the user, the request, breadcrumbs and the server name", () => {
    const event = { message: "m", user: { id: "u1", ip_address: "1.2.3.4" }, request: { url: "https://x/api/users/sam" }, breadcrumbs: [{ message: "GET /api/users/sam" }], server_name: "iphone", exception: { values: [] } };
    expect(scrub(event)).toEqual({ message: "m", exception: { values: [] } });
  });

  it("a broken SDK never breaks the app", () => {
    expect(initErrorReporting(env, () => { throw new Error("native module missing"); })).toBe(false);
    const s = fake();
    s.captureException.mockImplementation(() => { throw new Error("sdk crashed"); });
    initErrorReporting(env, () => s);
    expect(() => reportError(new Error("x"))).not.toThrow();
  });
});


===== src/lib/__tests__/rng.test.ts =====

import { hashString, seeded } from "../rng";

describe("seeded randomness", () => {
  it("hashes strings the same way every time, and differently for different strings", () => {
    expect(hashString("place_1")).toBe(hashString("place_1"));
    expect(hashString("place_1")).not.toBe(hashString("place_2"));
    expect(hashString("")).toBe(0x811c9dc5);
  });
  it("gives the same sequence for the same seed", () => {
    const a = seeded("x"), b = seeded("x"), c = seeded("y");
    const seq = (r: ReturnType<typeof seeded>) => Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => r.next());
    expect(seq(a)).toEqual(seq(b));
    expect(seq(seeded("x"))).not.toEqual(seq(c));
  });
  it("stays in range", () => {
    const r = seeded(7);
    for (let i = 0; i < 500; i++) {
      const n = r.next();
      expect(n).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      expect(n).toBeLessThan(1);
      expect(r.int(6)).toBeLessThan(6);
      const x = r.range(-3, 3);
      expect(x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(-3);
      expect(x).toBeLessThan(3);
    }
  });
});

/** VoiceOver reads `message` whenever it appears or changes (iOS ignores accessibilityLiveRegion). */
export function useAnnounce(message: string | null | undefined): void {
