===== src/lib/age.ts =====

/**
 * The 21+ age gate on the phone. The birthdate is typed as MM/DD/YYYY, checked here and, for sign-up, sent to the server
 * once (which checks it again and never stores it). It is never kept on the device either: nothing about an
 * under-21 attempt is saved anywhere, and an under-21 birthdate is never sent to the server at all.
 * The age boundary is the New York calendar date, matching the backend, regardless of the phone timezone.
 */
export const MIN_AGE = 21;
export const UNDERAGE_NOTICE = "Nostos is for people 21 and older.";

export type YMD = { y: number; m: number; d: number };

/** Auto-format as the person types: digits only, slashes added after the month and day. "0505" -> "05/05". */
export function formatBirthdateInput(raw: string): string {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 8);
  const parts = [digits.slice(0, 2), digits.slice(2, 4), digits.slice(4, 8)].filter((p) => p !== "");
  return parts.join("/");
}

/** "MM/DD/YYYY" to a real calendar date, or null ("02/30/1990" and "13/01/1990" are not dates). */
export function parseBirthdate(input: string): YMD | null {
  const m = /^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/.exec(input.trim());
  if (!m) return null;
  const [mo, d, y] = [Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]), Number(m[3])];
  const probe = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
  if (probe.getUTCFullYear() !== y || probe.getUTCMonth() !== mo - 1 || probe.getUTCDate() !== d) return null;
  return { y, m: mo, d };
}

/** The API's form of a birthdate: YYYY-MM-DD. */
export function toApiDate(b: YMD): string {
  return `${String(b.y).padStart(4, "0")}-${String(b.m).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(b.d).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

/** Whole years between a birthdate and a day. A birthday counts from its own date. */
export function ageOn(birth: YMD, today: YMD): number {
  const had = today.m > birth.m || (today.m === birth.m && today.d >= birth.d);
  return today.y - birth.y - (had ? 0 : 1);
}

const newYorkDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
  timeZone: "America/New_York", year: "numeric", month: "numeric", day: "numeric",
});

export function today(now = new Date()): YMD {
  const parts = newYorkDate.formatToParts(now);
  const value = (type: "year" | "month" | "day") => Number(parts.find((p) => p.type === type)!.value);
  return { y: value("year"), m: value("month"), d: value("day") };
}

export type AgeCheck = { ok: true; apiDate: string } | { ok: false; reason: "format" | "underage"; message: string };

/** Check what was typed. `underage` carries the one sentence to show; nothing is stored either way. */
export function checkBirthdate(input: string, now = new Date()): AgeCheck {
  const birth = parseBirthdate(input);
  const t = today(now);
  if (!birth || birth.y < 1900 || ageOn(birth, t) < 0) return { ok: false, reason: "format", message: "Enter your birthdate as MM/DD/YYYY." };
  if (ageOn(birth, t) < MIN_AGE) return { ok: false, reason: "underage", message: UNDERAGE_NOTICE };
  return { ok: true, apiDate: toApiDate(birth) };
}


===== src/lib/format.ts =====

/** Display formatting. Pure functions: no rules, only words and numbers. */

/** Server distance in meters to "140 m" or "1.8 km". Anything from 1000 m up is kilometers. */
export function formatDistance(meters: number): string {
  const m = Math.max(0, Math.round(meters));
  if (m < 1000) return `${m} m`;
  const km = m / 1000;
  return `${km < 10 ? km.toFixed(1) : Math.round(km)} km`;
}

/** "just now", "4 min ago", "2 h ago", "3 d ago". `now` should be the server's time when available. */
export function formatAge(iso: string, now: Date | string = new Date()): string {
  const nowMs = typeof now === "string" ? Date.parse(now) : now.getTime();
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor((nowMs - Date.parse(iso)) / 1000));
  if (s < 60) return "just now";
  const min = Math.floor(s / 60);
  if (min < 60) return `${min} min ago`;
  const h = Math.floor(min / 60);
  if (h < 24) return `${h} h ago`;
  return `${Math.floor(h / 24)} d ago`;
}

/** "1 stamp", "4 stamps". */
export function plural(n: number, one: string, many = `${one}s`): string {
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;
}

/** Initial for avatars: first letter of the name or handle, uppercased. */
export function initial(name: string | null | undefined): string {
  const ch = (name ?? "").trim().replace(/^@/, "").charAt(0);
  return ch ? ch.toUpperCase() : "?";
}


===== src/lib/geo.ts =====

import type { Region } from "react-native-maps";

/** East Village and Lower East Side, framed for a portrait phone. */
export const HOME_REGION: Region = {
  latitude: 40.7222,
  longitude: -73.9855,
  latitudeDelta: 0.026,
  longitudeDelta: 0.018,
};

export type Coords = { lat: number; lng: number };

/**
 * Great-circle distance in meters. Only used for "moved enough to re-ping presence" and the
 * Nearby sort; the geofence decision is always the server's.
 */
export function distanceMeters(a: Coords, b: Coords): number {
  const R = 6371000;
  const toRad = (d: number) => (d * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dLat = toRad(b.lat - a.lat);
  const dLng = toRad(b.lng - a.lng);
  const h = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(toRad(a.lat)) * Math.cos(toRad(b.lat)) * Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2;
  return 2 * R * Math.asin(Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(h)));
}


===== src/lib/haptics.ts =====

import * as Haptics from "expo-haptics";

/** Light impact on chip selection, success on a report or RSVP, warning on an error. */
export const haptics = {
  select: () => void Haptics.impactAsync(Haptics.ImpactFeedbackStyle.Light).catch(() => undefined),
  success: () => void Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Success).catch(() => undefined),
  warning: () => void Haptics.notificationAsync(Haptics.NotificationFeedbackType.Warning).catch(() => undefined),
};


===== src/lib/layout.ts =====

/**
 * Where a stamp sits in the passport book. A mirror of the server's `backend/src/lib/layout.ts` (the server computes the page
 * and slot it returns; the app uses this for the next empty slot and for jumping to a page). The same test vectors run on both.
 */
export const SLOTS_PER_PAGE = 3;
export const PAGES_PER_SPREAD = 2;

export type PageSlot = { page: number; slot: number };

export function pageAndSlot(index: number): PageSlot {
  if (!Number.isInteger(index) || index < 0) throw new RangeError("index must be a whole number, 0 or more");
  return { page: Math.floor(index / SLOTS_PER_PAGE) + 1, slot: (index % SLOTS_PER_PAGE) + 1 };
}

/** The spread (1-based) a page is on: pages 1 and 2 are spread 1. */
export const spreadOfPage = (page: number): number => Math.ceil(page / PAGES_PER_SPREAD);

/** The next empty slot after `count` live stamps. */
export const nextSlot = (count: number): PageSlot => pageAndSlot(count);

export const LAYOUT_VECTORS: readonly [number, number, number][] = [
  [0, 1, 1], [1, 1, 2], [2, 1, 3], [3, 2, 1], [5, 2, 3], [6, 3, 1], [11, 4, 3], [12, 5, 1], [29, 10, 3],
];


===== src/lib/links.ts =====

import * as WebBrowser from "expo-web-browser";

import { api } from "@/api";
import { colors } from "@/theme";

export const LEGAL = {
  terms: "/terms",
  privacy: "/privacy",
  support: "/support",
} as const;

/** Opens one of the backend's public pages in the in-app browser (SFSafariViewController). */
export function openWebPage(path: string): void {
  void WebBrowser.openBrowserAsync(api.webUrl(path), {
    controlsColor: colors.accent,
    toolbarColor: colors.background,
    dismissButtonStyle: "close",
    presentationStyle: WebBrowser.WebBrowserPresentationStyle.PAGE_SHEET,
  }).catch(() => undefined);
}

/** Any external https page (attribution, support) in the in-app browser. */
export function openExternal(url: string): void {
  void WebBrowser.openBrowserAsync(url, {
    controlsColor: colors.accent,
    toolbarColor: colors.background,
    dismissButtonStyle: "close",
    presentationStyle: WebBrowser.WebBrowserPresentationStyle.PAGE_SHEET,
  }).catch(() => undefined);
}


===== src/lib/network.ts =====

import NetInfo, { useNetInfo, type NetInfoState } from "@react-native-community/netinfo";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { AccessibilityInfo } from "react-native";

import { api } from "@/api";

let configured = false;

/**
 * Reachability is checked against our own health endpoint, not NetInfo's default Google URL (blocked
 * on some networks, which would show a false "offline"). Call once at startup.
 */
export function configureNetwork(): void {
  if (configured) return;
  configured = true;
  NetInfo.configure({ reachabilityUrl: api.webUrl("/api/health"), reachabilityTest: async (r) => r.status === 200 });
}

/** One definition of offline for the banner and for pausing queries. Unknown (null) counts as online. */
export function isOffline(state: Pick<NetInfoState, "isConnected" | "isInternetReachable">): boolean {
  return state.isConnected === false || state.isInternetReachable === false;
}

export function useOffline(): boolean {
  return isOffline(useNetInfo());
}

/** Announce going offline and coming back, once for the whole app (mounted in the root layout). */
export function useOfflineAnnouncer(): void {
  const offline = useOffline();
  const previous = useRef(offline);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (previous.current === offline) return;
    previous.current = offline;
    AccessibilityInfo.announceForAccessibility(offline ? "You're offline." : "Back online.");
  }, [offline]);
}


===== src/lib/report-error.ts =====

/**
 * The one place the app reports unexpected errors from. Off by default: nothing leaves the phone unless the build sets
 * EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED=1 and EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN to an https DSN. No DSN is committed anywhere.
 *
 * What is reported: the error, its stack, the app version and OS. What is not: no user, no email, no request, no
 * breadcrumbs (they hold API paths with handles), no IP address. Sentry is told not to attach personal data, and
 * `scrub` drops anything that slipped in. See OPEN-QUESTIONS.md L5 for the privacy-label question.
 */
import { ApiError } from "@/api/client";

type SentryLike = {
  init: (options: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
  captureException: (error: unknown, hint?: { extra?: Record<string, unknown> }) => void;
};

export type SentryEnv = { EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED?: string; EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN?: string };

/** The DSN to use, or null when reporting is off (not enabled, or no https DSN set). */
export function sentryDsn(env: SentryEnv): string | null {
  if (env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED !== "1") return null;
  const dsn = env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN?.trim() ?? "";
  return /^https:\/\/\S+$/.test(dsn) ? dsn : null;
}

/** Remove everything about the person and their requests from an event before it is sent. */
export function scrub<E extends Record<string, unknown>>(event: E): E {
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = { ...event };
  for (const key of ["user", "request", "breadcrumbs", "server_name"]) delete out[key];
  return out as E;
}

let client: SentryLike | null = null;

/** Start reporting if (and only if) the build turned it on. Safe to call more than once. Returns whether it is on. */
// The SDK is loaded lazily on purpose: when reporting is off, the module is never touched.
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports
const loadSentry = (): SentryLike => require("@sentry/react-native") as SentryLike;

export function initErrorReporting(env: SentryEnv = process.env as SentryEnv, load: () => SentryLike = loadSentry): boolean {
  if (client) return true;
  const dsn = sentryDsn(env);
  if (!dsn) return false;
  try {
    const sentry = load();
    sentry.init({ dsn, sendDefaultPii: false, tracesSampleRate: 0, attachScreenshot: false, enableAutoSessionTracking: false, beforeSend: scrub });
    client = sentry;
    return true;
  } catch {
    // A broken error reporter must never break the app.
    return false;
  }
}

/** Errors worth a person's attention: bugs and contract drift. Not a bad password, a 404, a geofence refusal or being offline. */
export function isReportable(error: unknown): boolean {
  if (error instanceof ApiError) return error.code === "bad_response" || error.status >= 500;
  return true;
}

/** Report an unexpected error. Logs in development; sends only when reporting is on. Never throws. */
export function reportError(error: unknown, context?: Record<string, unknown>): void {
  if (__DEV__) console.warn("[error]", error, context ?? "");
  if (!client || !isReportable(error)) return;
  try {
    client.captureException(error, context ? { extra: context } : undefined);
  } catch {
    // Reporting is best effort.
  }
}

/** For tests: forget the client. */
export function _resetErrorReporting() {
  client = null;
}


===== src/lib/rng.ts =====

/** Deterministic randomness for the book: the same input always gives the same look. */

/** FNV-1a: a string to a 32-bit number. */
export function hashString(input: string): number {
  let h = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {
    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193) >>> 0;
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}

/** mulberry32: a small seeded generator. `next()` is in [0, 1). */
export function seeded(seed: number | string) {
  let a = (typeof seed === "string" ? hashString(seed) : seed) >>> 0;
  const next = () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
  return {
    next,
    /** A float in [min, max). */
    range: (min: number, max: number) => min + next() * (max - min),
    /** An integer in [0, n). */
    int: (n: number) => Math.floor(next() * n),
    pick: <T,>(items: readonly T[]): T => items[Math.floor(next() * items.length)]!,
  };
}


===== src/lib/use-confirm-discard.ts =====

import { useNavigation } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { Alert } from "react-native";

/** Ask before leaving a form with unsaved changes (back button or swipe). */
export function useConfirmDiscard(dirty: boolean): void {
  const navigation = useNavigation();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dirty) return;
    return navigation.addListener("beforeRemove", (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      Alert.alert("Discard changes?", "Your edits haven't been saved.", [
        { text: "Keep editing", style: "cancel" },
        { text: "Discard", style: "destructive", onPress: () => navigation.dispatch(e.data.action) },
      ]);
    });
  }, [dirty, navigation]);
}


===== src/lib/validation.ts =====

import { checkBirthdate } from "./age";

/**
 * Field hints for the sign-up form, mirroring the server's rules so people see problems before
 * submitting. The server still validates and its message wins.
 */
export const HANDLE_RE = /^[a-z0-9_]{3,24}$/;
export const MIN_PASSWORD = 10;
const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

export type SignupFields = { email: string; password: string; handle: string; displayName: string; birthdate: string };
export type SignupErrors = Partial<Record<keyof SignupFields, string>>;

export function isEmail(s: string): boolean {
  return EMAIL_RE.test(s.trim());
}

export function validateSignup(f: SignupFields, now = new Date()): SignupErrors {
  const e: SignupErrors = {};
  if (!isEmail(f.email)) e.email = "Enter a valid email address.";
  if (f.password.length < MIN_PASSWORD) e.password = `Use at least ${MIN_PASSWORD} characters.`;
  if (!HANDLE_RE.test(f.handle.trim().toLowerCase())) e.handle = "3 to 24 lowercase letters, numbers or underscores.";
  if (!f.displayName.trim()) e.displayName = "Enter the name people will see.";
  const age = checkBirthdate(f.birthdate, now);
  if (!age.ok) e.birthdate = age.message;
  return e;
}


===== src/auth/__tests__/token-store.test.ts =====

import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";

import { tokenStore } from "../token-store";

describe("tokenStore", () => {
  afterEach(async () => {
    await tokenStore.clear();
  });

  it("ignores a dead-token report for a previous account", async () => {
    await tokenStore.set("token-b");
    tokenStore.invalidate("token-a");
    expect(tokenStore.current()).toBe("token-b");
    tokenStore.invalidate("token-b");
    expect(tokenStore.current()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("does not sign in when the keychain write fails", async () => {
    (SecureStore.setItemAsync as jest.Mock).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("keychain"));
    await expect(tokenStore.set("token-c")).rejects.toThrow("keychain");
    expect(tokenStore.current()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns the cleared token so it can be revoked", async () => {
    await tokenStore.set("token-d");
    await expect(tokenStore.clear()).resolves.toBe("token-d");
  });
});


===== src/auth/account-state.ts =====

import { useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { useAuth, type AuthStatus } from "./auth-context";

/**
 * What the app knows about the account right now, for deciding what an account-only control (Stamp, Passport) shows:
 *  - checking: a token is being validated or /api/me is loading. Never show "Sign in" in this state.
 *  - error: signed in, but /api/me failed. Offer a retry, not a sign-in.
 *  - guest: signed out or browsing as a guest. Sign-in is the right prompt.
 *  - ready: signed in and the account loaded.
 */
export type AccountState = "checking" | "error" | "guest" | "ready";

export function accountState(status: AuthStatus, me: { isSuccess: boolean; isError: boolean }): AccountState {
  if (status === "loading") return "checking";
  if (status === "signedOut" || status === "guest") return "guest";
  if (me.isSuccess) return "ready";
  if (me.isError) return "error";
  return "checking";
}

export function useAccountState(): { state: AccountState; retry: () => void; retrying: boolean } {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const me = useMe();
  return { state: accountState(status, me), retry: () => void me.refetch(), retrying: me.isFetching };
}


===== src/auth/token-store.ts =====

/**
 * The bearer token lives in the iOS keychain (expo-secure-store), with an in-memory copy so
 * requests can read it synchronously. Listeners hear about sign-in, sign-out and dead tokens.
 */
import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";

const KEY = "nostos.token";
const OPTIONS: SecureStore.SecureStoreOptions = { keychainAccessible: SecureStore.AFTER_FIRST_UNLOCK_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY };

type Listener = (token: string | null) => void;

let memory: string | null = null;
const listeners = new Set<Listener>();

function emit() {
  for (const l of listeners) l(memory);
}

export const tokenStore = {
  current: () => memory,

  async load(): Promise<string | null> {
    try {
      memory = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(KEY, OPTIONS);
    } catch {
      memory = null;
    }
    return memory;
  },

  /** Persist first, then switch: a keychain failure throws and the user stays signed out. */
  async set(token: string): Promise<void> {
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(KEY, token, OPTIONS);
    memory = token;
    emit();
  },

  /** Forget locally right away; the keychain delete follows. Returns the token that was cleared. */
  async clear(): Promise<string | null> {
    const previous = memory;
    memory = null;
    emit();
    await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(KEY, OPTIONS).catch(() => undefined);
    return previous;
  },

  /** A 401 `invalid_token` for `sentToken`: sign out only if that's still the current token. */
  invalidate(sentToken: string): void {
    if (memory === null || memory !== sentToken) return;
    void tokenStore.clear();
  },

  subscribe(listener: Listener): () => void {
    listeners.add(listener);
    return () => listeners.delete(listener);
  },
};


===== src/app/(app)/(tabs)/_layout.tsx =====

import { router, Tabs } from "expo-router";
import { SymbolView } from "expo-symbols";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { SOCIAL_ENABLED } from "@/config";
import { colors, shadows, size, space } from "@/theme";

/**
 * Passport (home), Stamp (the large center button) and Map. With the social layer off there is nothing else: no feed, no
 * bell. (With SOCIAL_ENABLED on, Feed and Activity come back as extra tabs.)
 */
export default function TabsLayout() {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const social = SOCIAL_ENABLED ? undefined : null;
  return (
    <Tabs
      initialRouteName="passport"
      screenOptions={{
        headerShown: false,
        sceneStyle: { backgroundColor: colors.background },
        tabBarStyle: { backgroundColor: colors.background, borderTopColor: colors.hairline, height: size.hitTarget + space.xl },
        tabBarActiveTintColor: colors.brass,
        tabBarInactiveTintColor: colors.dimBrass,
      }}
    >
      <Tabs.Screen name="index" options={{ href: social, title: "Feed", tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <SymbolView name="house.fill" tintColor={color} size={size.icon} /> }} />
      <Tabs.Screen name="passport" options={{ title: "Passport", tabBarAccessibilityLabel: "Passport", tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <SymbolView name="book.closed.fill" tintColor={color} size={size.icon} /> }} />
      <Tabs.Screen
        name="stamp"
        options={{
          title: "Stamp",
          tabBarAccessibilityLabel: "Stamp a place",
          // A large raised brass button in the middle of the bar.
          tabBarButton: () => (
            <View style={styles.center} pointerEvents="box-none">
              <Pressable
                onPress={() => router.push(status === "signedIn" ? "/stamp/new" : "/sign-in")}
                accessibilityRole="button"
                accessibilityLabel="Stamp a place"
                accessibilityHint="Opens a list of the places you are at"
                style={({ pressed }) => [styles.stampButton, pressed && styles.pressed]}
              >
                <Icon name="seal.fill" color="onAccent" size={size.iconLarge} />
              </Pressable>
            </View>
          ),
        }}
      />
      <Tabs.Screen name="map" options={{ title: "Map", tabBarAccessibilityLabel: "Map", tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <SymbolView name="map.fill" tintColor={color} size={size.icon} /> }} />
      <Tabs.Screen name="activity" options={{ href: social, title: "Activity", tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <SymbolView name="bell.fill" tintColor={color} size={size.icon} /> }} />
    </Tabs>
  );
}

const BUTTON = size.avatarLarge - space.xs;

===== src/app/(app)/(tabs)/activity.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet } from "react-native";

import { errorText, mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import type { ActivityResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useActivity } from "@/api/queries";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { PersonRow } from "@/features/people/person-row";
import { formatAge } from "@/lib/format";
import { colors, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

type Item = ActivityResponse["items"][number];

/** In-app activity, last 30 days: follow requests, new followers, likes on your stamps. No push. */
export default function Activity() {
  const activity = useActivity();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const items = activity.data?.items ?? [];

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await activity.refetch().finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  return (
    <ScrollView
      style={styles.root}
      contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
      contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
      refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
    >
      {activity.isPending ? (
        <SkeletonRows count={6} />
      ) : activity.isError ? (
        <InlineError message={errorText(activity.error, "Couldn't load activity.")} onRetry={() => void activity.refetch()} retrying={activity.isFetching} />
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
          Nothing yet. Follows and likes show up here.
        </Text>
      ) : (
        <ListGroup>
          {items.map((item, i) => (
            <ActivityRow key={`${item.kind}-${item.at}-${i}`} item={item} />
          ))}
        </ListGroup>
      )}
    </ScrollView>
  );
}

function ActivityRow({ item }: { item: Item }) {
  const age = formatAge(item.at);
  const openStamp = (id: string) => router.push({ pathname: "/stamp/[id]", params: { id } });
  switch (item.kind) {
    case "follow_request":
      return <PersonRow person={item.person} subtitle={`Asked to follow you · ${age}`} onPress={() => router.push("/follow-requests")} accessibilityHint="Opens follow requests" />;
    case "new_follower":
      return <PersonRow person={item.person} subtitle={`Started following you · ${age}`} />;
    case "like":
      return (
        <PersonRow
          person={item.person}
          subtitle={`Liked your stamp at ${item.stamp.place.name} · ${age}`}
          trailing={item.stamp.url ? <Thumb url={item.stamp.url} /> : undefined}
          onPress={() => openStamp(item.stamp.id)}
          accessibilityHint="Opens the stamp"
        />
      );
  }
}

function Thumb({ url }: { url: string }) {
  return <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(url) }} style={styles.thumb} contentFit="cover" transition={150} accessible={false} />;
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.lg },
  thumb: { width: size.avatar, height: size.avatar, borderRadius: radius.chip - space.xxs, backgroundColor: colors.elevated },
});


===== src/app/(app)/(tabs)/index.tsx =====

import { FlashList } from "@shopify/flash-list";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { RefreshControl, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useFeed } from "@/api/queries";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { AccountGate } from "@/features/account/account-gate";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { StampCard } from "@/features/stamps/stamp-card";
import { useFlaggedIds } from "@/features/moderation/flagged-store";
import { colors, hairlineWidth, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/**
 * Home: stamps from the people you follow, newest first, 30 at a time. Strictly chronological: no ranking, no
 * suggestions, nothing from people you don't follow. Pull to refresh; more loads near the end.
 */
export default function Home() {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      {status === "guest" ? (
        <GuestHome />
      ) : (
        <AccountGate title="Home" message="Sign in to see stamps from the people you follow.">
          <Feed />
        </AccountGate>
      )}
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

function GuestHome() {
  return (
    <View style={styles.guest}>
      <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
        A passport for New York
      </Text>
      <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary" style={styles.center}>
        Go somewhere, get a stamp. Sign in to see stamps from the people you follow, or look up a public passport.
      </Text>
      <Button title="Find people" variant="secondary" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/people")} />
      <Button title="Sign in" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/sign-in")} />
    </View>
  );
}

function Feed() {
  const feed = useFeed();
  const flagged = useFlaggedIds();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const stamps = (feed.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.stamps) ?? []).filter((s) => !flagged.has(s.id));

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await feed.refetch().finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  if (feed.isPending) return <SkeletonRows count={4} />;
  if (feed.isError && !feed.data) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.pad}>
        <InlineError message={errorText(feed.error, "Couldn't load your feed.")} onRetry={() => void feed.refetch()} retrying={feed.isFetching} />
      </View>
    );
  }
  return (
    <FlashList
      data={stamps}
      keyExtractor={(s) => s.id}
      renderItem={({ item }) => <StampCard stamp={item} />}
      ItemSeparatorComponent={Separator}
      refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      onEndReachedThreshold={1}
      onEndReached={() => {
        if (feed.hasNextPage && !feed.isFetchingNextPage && !feed.isFetchNextPageError) void feed.fetchNextPage();
      }}
      ListEmptyComponent={
        <View style={styles.empty}>
          <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
            Nothing here yet
          </Text>
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary" style={styles.center}>
            Stamps from the people you follow show up here, newest first. Follow some people, or go stamp a place yourself.
          </Text>
          <Button title="Find people" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/people")} />
        </View>
      }
      ListFooterComponent={
        feed.isFetchNextPageError ? (
          <View style={styles.pad}>
            <InlineError message="Couldn't load more stamps." onRetry={() => void feed.fetchNextPage()} retrying={feed.isFetchingNextPage} />
          </View>
        ) : feed.isFetchingNextPage ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={1} />
        ) : null
      }
      contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
    />
  );
}

function Separator() {
  return <View style={styles.separator} />;
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  list: { paddingBottom: space.xxxl },
  separator: { height: hairlineWidth, backgroundColor: colors.hairline, marginHorizontal: screenPadding },
  pad: { padding: screenPadding },
  empty: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.sm, padding: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.xxxl },
  guest: { flex: 1, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", gap: space.sm, padding: screenPadding },
  center: { textAlign: "center" },
});

===== src/app/(app)/_layout.tsx =====

import { Stack } from "expo-router";

import { stackOptions } from "@/components/ui/stack-options";
import { colors, radius } from "@/theme";

export default function AppLayout() {
  return (
    <Stack screenOptions={stackOptions}>
      <Stack.Screen name="(tabs)" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="place/[id]" options={{ title: "Place" }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="stamp/[id]" options={{ title: "Stamp" }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="stamp/new" options={{ title: "New stamp", presentation: "modal" }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="stamp/camera" options={{ presentation: "fullScreenModal", headerShown: false }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="stamp/landed" options={{ headerShown: false, gestureEnabled: false, animation: "fade" }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="stamp/edit" options={{ title: "Edit stamp", presentation: "modal" }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="stamp/song" options={{ title: "Add a song", presentation: "modal" }} />
      {/* Pushed from a passport, a stamp or the feed. */}
      <Stack.Screen name="user/[handle]" options={{ title: "" }} />
      <Stack.Screen
        name="location-permission"
        options={{ presentation: "formSheet", sheetAllowedDetents: [0.6], sheetGrabberVisible: true, sheetCornerRadius: radius.sheet, headerShown: false, contentStyle: { backgroundColor: colors.surface } }}
      />
      <Stack.Screen
        name="customize"
        options={{ presentation: "formSheet", sheetAllowedDetents: [0.4], sheetGrabberVisible: true, sheetCornerRadius: radius.sheet, headerShown: false, contentStyle: { backgroundColor: colors.surface } }}
      />
      <Stack.Screen name="settings/index" options={{ title: "Settings", headerLargeTitle: true }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="edit-profile" options={{ title: "Edit profile", presentation: "modal" }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="follow-requests" options={{ title: "Follow requests" }} />

      <Stack.Screen name="settings/delete-account" options={{ title: "Delete account" }} />
      <Stack.Screen name="moderation" options={{ title: "Moderation", headerLargeTitle: true }} />
    </Stack>
  );
}


===== src/app/(app)/connections.tsx =====

import { Stack, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { ApiError, errorText } from "@/api";
import { useMe, usePeopleList } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { PersonRow } from "@/features/people/person-row";
import { followButton, useFollow } from "@/features/people/use-follow";
import { useRemoveFollower } from "@/features/people/use-requests";
import { colors, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Followers or Following of a profile. On your own followers: Remove. Elsewhere: Follow / Requested / Following. */
export default function Connections() {
  const { handle, list: listParam } = useLocalSearchParams<{ handle: string; list: string }>();
  const list = listParam === "following" ? "following" : "followers";
  const people = usePeopleList(handle, list);
  const me = useMe();
  const follow = useFollow();
  const remove = useRemoveFollower();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const ownFollowers = list === "followers" && me.data?.user.handle === handle;
  const rows = people.data?.people ?? [];
  const isPrivate = people.error instanceof ApiError && people.error.code === "private_account";

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await people.refetch().finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: list === "followers" ? "Followers" : "Following" }} />
      <ScrollView
        contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      >
        {people.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={5} />
        ) : isPrivate ? (
          <View style={styles.private}>
            <Icon name="lock.fill" color="textSecondary" size={size.icon} />
            <Text variant="headline">This account is private</Text>
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              Follow @{handle} to see who they follow and who follows them.
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : people.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(people.error, "Couldn't load this list.")} onRetry={() => void people.refetch()} retrying={people.isFetching} />
        ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            {list === "followers" ? "No followers yet." : "Not following anyone yet."}
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup>
            {rows.map((p) => {
              if (p.isSelf) return <PersonRow key={p.handle} person={p} subtitle="You" />;
              if (ownFollowers) {
                const label = `Remove @${p.handle} as a follower`;
                return (
                  <PersonRow
                    key={p.handle}
                    person={p}
                    actions={[{ label, onPress: () => remove.confirm(p.handle) }]}
                    trailing={<Button title="Remove" variant="secondary" compact block={false} onPress={() => remove.confirm(p.handle)} accessibilityLabel={label} />}
                  />
                );
              }
              const b = followButton(p.follow, p.handle);
              const toggle = () => follow.toggle(p.handle, p.follow);
              return (
                <PersonRow
                  key={p.handle}
                  person={p}
                  actions={[{ label: b.label, onPress: toggle }]}
                  trailing={
                    <Button
                      title={b.title}
                      variant={b.variant}
                      compact
                      block={false}
                      onPress={toggle}
                      accessibilityLabel={b.label}
                      accessibilityState={{ busy: follow.isPendingFor(p.handle) }}
                    />
                  }
                />
              );
            })}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.lg },
  private: { gap: space.xs, alignItems: "flex-start" },
});

===== src/app/(app)/edit-profile.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router, Stack } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { ActionSheetIOS, Alert, Pressable, StyleSheet, Switch, View } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { AvatarResponse, type ProfileSettingsResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { qk, useProfileSettings, useUserProfile } from "@/api/queries";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { BIO_MAX, goesPublic, goingPublicAlert, NAME_MAX, privacyNote, profileChanges, profileProblem, shouldOfferApproveAll, type ProfileValues } from "@/features/people/edit-profile";
import { confirmApproveAll, useApproveAll } from "@/features/people/use-requests";
import { pickFromLibrary } from "@/features/photos/pick";
import { prepareImage } from "@/features/photos/prepare";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { SOCIAL_ENABLED } from "@/config";
import { useConfirmDiscard } from "@/lib/use-confirm-discard";
import { colors, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Profile photo, display name, bio (150) and the Private account toggle. */
export default function EditProfile() {
  const settings = useProfileSettings();
  const data = settings.data;
  if (!data)
    return (
      <FormScreen>
        {settings.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(settings.error, "Couldn't load your profile.")} onRetry={() => void settings.refetch()} retrying={settings.isFetching} />
        ) : (
          <SkeletonRows count={4} />
        )}
      </FormScreen>
    );
  return <EditForm data={data} />;
}

function EditForm({ data }: { data: ProfileSettingsResponse }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const initial: ProfileValues = { displayName: data.profile.displayName, bio: data.profile.bio, isPrivate: data.profile.isPrivate };
  const [values, setValues] = useState(initial);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const approveAll = useApproveAll();
  const profile = useUserProfile(data.profile.handle);
  const change = profileChanges(initial, values);
  const dirty = Object.keys(change).length > 0;
  const problem = profileProblem(values);
  useConfirmDiscard(dirty && !saved);

  const refreshProfile = () => {
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.me, exact: true });
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.profileSettings });
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.user(data.profile.handle) });
  };

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.me.updateProfile(change),
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      haptics.success();
      setSaved(true);
      refreshProfile();
      if (shouldOfferApproveAll(change, res.pendingRequests)) {
        // Going public doesn't approve anyone by itself.
        confirmApproveAll(res.pendingRequests, () => approveAll.mutate(), () => router.back());
        return;
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(() => router.back());
    },
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const photo = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (action: "choose" | "remove") => {
      if (action === "remove") return api.me.removeAvatar();
      const picked = await pickFromLibrary();
      if (!picked) return null;
      const prepared = await prepareImage(picked.uri);
      const form = new FormData();
      // React Native's FormData takes a file descriptor object for local files.
      form.append("photo", { uri: prepared.uri, name: "avatar.jpg", type: "image/jpeg" } as unknown as Blob);
      return api.upload("/api/me/avatar", AvatarResponse, form);
    },
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      if (!res) return;
      haptics.success();
      toast.show(res.avatarUrl ? "Photo updated" : "Photo removed", "neutral");
      refreshProfile();
    },
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const avatarUrl = photo.data !== undefined && photo.data !== null ? photo.data.avatarUrl : data.profile.avatarUrl;

  const choosePhoto = () => {
    const options = avatarUrl ? ["Choose photo", "Remove photo", "Cancel"] : ["Choose photo", "Cancel"];
    ActionSheetIOS.showActionSheetWithOptions(
      { options, cancelButtonIndex: options.length - 1, destructiveButtonIndex: avatarUrl ? 1 : undefined, tintColor: colors.accent, userInterfaceStyle: "dark" },
      (i) => {
        if (i === 0) photo.mutate("choose");
        if (avatarUrl && i === 1) photo.mutate("remove");
      },
    );
  };

  /** Switching private to public changes what strangers can see, so it needs an explicit yes first. */
  const onSave = () => {
    if (!goesPublic(initial, change)) return save.mutate();
    const counts = profile.data?.access === "full" ? profile.data.user.counts : undefined;
    const a = goingPublicAlert(counts);
    Alert.alert(a.title, a.message, [
      { text: "Keep private", style: "cancel" },
      { text: a.confirm, style: "destructive", onPress: () => save.mutate() },
    ]);
  };

  const set = (k: "displayName" | "bio") => (t: string) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, [k]: t }));

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          headerLeft: () => <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => router.back()} />,
          headerRight: () => <HeaderButton label="Save" bold onPress={onSave} disabled={!dirty || !!problem || save.isPending} />,
        }}
      />
      <Pressable
        onPress={choosePhoto}
        disabled={photo.isPending}
        accessibilityRole="button"
        accessibilityLabel={avatarUrl ? "Change profile photo" : "Add profile photo"}
        accessibilityState={{ busy: photo.isPending }}
        style={styles.photo}
      >
        <Avatar name={values.displayName} uri={avatarUrl} size={size.avatarLarge} variant="title" />
        <Text variant="callout" color="accent" weight="semibold">
          {photo.isPending ? "Uploading…" : avatarUrl ? "Change photo" : "Add photo"}
        </Text>
      </Pressable>
      {photo.error && <InlineError message={errorText(photo.error, "Couldn't update your photo. Try again.")} />}

      <FieldGroup footer={`${values.bio.trim().length}/${BIO_MAX}`} errors={[problem && dirty ? problem : null]}>
        <Field label="Name" value={values.displayName} onChangeText={set("displayName")} maxLength={NAME_MAX} autoCapitalize="words" textContentType="name" returnKeyType="next" />
        <Field
          label="Bio"
          value={values.bio}
          onChangeText={set("bio")}
          maxLength={BIO_MAX}
          placeholder="A line about you"
          multiline
          accessibilityHint={`Up to ${BIO_MAX} characters`}
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      {SOCIAL_ENABLED && (
      <View style={styles.privateWrap}>
        <View style={styles.privateRow}>
          <Text variant="body" style={styles.flex}>
            Private account
          </Text>
          <Switch
            value={values.isPrivate}
            onValueChange={(isPrivate) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, isPrivate }))}
            trackColor={{ true: colors.accent, false: colors.elevated }}
            accessibilityLabel="Private account"
            accessibilityHint={privacyNote(values.isPrivate)}
          />
        </View>
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary" style={styles.note}>
          {privacyNote(values.isPrivate)}
        </Text>
      </View>
      )}

      {save.error && <InlineError message={errorText(save.error, "Couldn't save. Check the fields and try again.")} />}
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  photo: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.xs, paddingTop: space.md },
  privateWrap: { gap: space.xs },
  privateRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    minHeight: size.fieldHeight,
    paddingHorizontal: space.md,
    backgroundColor: colors.surface,
    borderRadius: radius.card,
  },
  flex: { flex: 1 },
  note: { paddingHorizontal: space.md },
});


===== src/app/(app)/follow-requests.tsx =====

import { Stack } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useFollowRequests } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { PersonRow } from "@/features/people/person-row";
import { confirmApproveAll, useApproveAll, useDecideRequest } from "@/features/people/use-requests";
import { formatAge } from "@/lib/format";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/** People asking to follow your private account: Approve or Decline each, or approve them all. */
export default function FollowRequests() {
  const requests = useFollowRequests();
  const decide = useDecideRequest();
  const approveAll = useApproveAll();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const rows = requests.data?.requests ?? [];

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await requests.refetch().finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          headerRight:
            rows.length > 1
              ? () => <HeaderButton label="Approve all" disabled={approveAll.isPending} onPress={() => confirmApproveAll(rows.length, () => approveAll.mutate())} />
              : undefined,
        }}
      />
      <ScrollView
        contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      >
        {requests.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={4} />
        ) : requests.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(requests.error, "Couldn't load requests.")} onRetry={() => void requests.refetch()} retrying={requests.isFetching} />
        ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            No requests right now.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup footer="Approved people see your stamps and places.">
            {rows.map(({ person, requestedAt }) => {
              const approve = () => decide.mutate({ handle: person.handle, approve: true });
              const decline = () => decide.mutate({ handle: person.handle, approve: false });
              return (
                <PersonRow
                  key={person.handle}
                  person={person}
                  subtitle={formatAge(requestedAt)}
                  actions={[
                    { label: `Approve @${person.handle}`, onPress: approve },
                    { label: `Decline @${person.handle}`, onPress: decline },
                  ]}
                  trailing={
                    <View style={styles.buttons}>
                      <Button title="Approve" compact block={false} onPress={approve} accessibilityLabel={`Approve @${person.handle}`} />
                      <Button title="Decline" variant="secondary" compact block={false} onPress={decline} accessibilityLabel={`Decline @${person.handle}`} />
                    </View>
                  }
                />
              );
            })}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.lg },
  buttons: { flexDirection: "row", gap: space.xs },
});


===== src/app/(app)/location-permission.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Linking, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { locationStore, useLocationState } from "@/features/location/location-store";
import { colors, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Pre-permission screen: one line on why, then the system prompt. Denied gets a path to Settings. */
export default function LocationPermission() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { permission } = useLocationState();
  const [asking, setAsking] = useState(false);
  const denied = permission === "denied";

  const ask = async () => {
    setAsking(true);
    const result = await locationStore.request().finally(() => setAsking(false));
    if (result === "granted") router.back();
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + space.md }]}>
      <View style={styles.body}>
        <View style={styles.badge}>
          <Icon name="location.fill" color="accent" size={size.iconLarge} />
        </View>
        <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
          {denied ? "Location is off" : "Find places near you"}
        </Text>
        <Text color="textSecondary">
          {denied
            ? "Nostos can't see your location. Turn it on in Settings to find the places near you and to stamp them."
            : "Nostos uses your location while you're using the app to find the places near you and to confirm you're there when you stamp one. Your position isn't shown to anyone."}
        </Text>
      </View>
      <View style={styles.actions}>
        {denied ? (
          <Button title="Open Settings" onPress={() => void Linking.openSettings()} />
        ) : (
          <Button title="Continue" onPress={() => void ask()} loading={asking} />
        )}
        <Button title="Not now" variant="plain" onPress={() => router.back()} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.surface, paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.xxxl, justifyContent: "space-between" },
  body: { gap: space.sm },
  badge: {
    width: size.avatarLarge,
    height: size.avatarLarge,
    borderRadius: radius.largeCard,
    backgroundColor: colors.elevated,
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    marginBottom: space.xs,
  },
  actions: { gap: space.xs },
});


===== src/app/(app)/moderation.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText, mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import type { TargetType, ModQueueItem } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useModHidden, useModLog, useModQueue } from "@/api/queries";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SegmentedControl } from "@/components/ui/segmented-control";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { toast, ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { actionWord, reputationText } from "@/features/moderation/format";
import { formatAge, plural } from "@/lib/format";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { colors, hairlineWidth, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

type Tab = "queue" | "hidden" | "log";
type Target = { targetType: TargetType; targetId: string; author: string; venue: string | null; autoHidden?: boolean };
type Action = "HIDE" | "DISMISS" | "RESTORE" | "SUSPEND" | "UNSUSPEND";

const NOUN: Record<TargetType, string> = { STAMP: "Stamp", USER: "Account" };
/** The log keeps the database's names, including content types older entries may carry. */
const LOG_NOUN: Record<string, string> = { PHOTO: "Stamp", COMMENT: "Comment", REPORT: "Report", USER: "Account" };
const VERB: Record<Action, { title: string; done: string; destructive: boolean }> = {
  HIDE: { title: "Hide", done: "Hidden.", destructive: true },
  DISMISS: { title: "Dismiss flags", done: "Flags dismissed.", destructive: false },
  RESTORE: { title: "Restore", done: "Restored.", destructive: false },
  SUSPEND: { title: "Suspend account", done: "Account suspended.", destructive: true },
  UNSUSPEND: { title: "Unsuspend", done: "Account unsuspended.", destructive: false },
};

/** Moderators: the flag queue grouped by item, hidden content to restore, and the log. */
export default function Moderation() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("queue");
  const queue = useModQueue();
  const hidden = useModHidden(tab === "hidden");
  const log = useModLog(tab === "log");
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);

  const act = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { targetType: TargetType; targetId: string; action: Action; note?: string }) => api.mod.act(v),
    onSuccess: (_r, v) => {
      haptics.success();
      toast.show(VERB[v.action].done);
      for (const key of [["mod"], ["feed"], ["user"], ["stamp"]]) void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: key });
    },
    onError: (e) => {
      haptics.warning();
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't do that. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
  });

  /** Confirm, naming the item, with an optional note (kept in the moderation log). */
  const confirm = (t: Target, action: Action) => {
    const v = VERB[action];
    const what = t.targetType === "USER" ? t.author : `${NOUN[t.targetType].toLowerCase()} by ${t.author}${t.venue ? ` at ${t.venue}` : ""}`;
    const effect = action === "DISMISS" && t.autoHidden ? " It will be visible again." : "";
    const { targetType, targetId } = t;
    Alert.prompt(
      `${v.title}: ${what}?`,
      `Add a note for the log (optional).${effect}`,
      [
        { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
        { text: v.title, style: v.destructive ? "destructive" : "default", onPress: (note?: string) => act.mutate({ targetType, targetId, action, note: note?.trim() || undefined }) },
      ],
      "plain-text",
      "",
      "default",
    );
  };

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    const q = tab === "queue" ? queue : tab === "hidden" ? hidden : log;
    await q.refetch().finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <ScrollView
        contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      >
        <SegmentedControl
          segments={[
            { key: "queue", label: queue.data ? `Queue (${queue.data.items.length})` : "Queue" },
            { key: "hidden", label: "Hidden" },
            { key: "log", label: "Log" },
          ]}
          value={tab}
          onChange={setTab}
          accessibilityLabel="Moderation sections"
        />

        {tab === "queue" &&
          (queue.isPending ? (
            <SkeletonRows count={4} />
          ) : queue.isError ? (
            <InlineError message={errorText(queue.error, "Couldn't load the queue.")} onRetry={() => void queue.refetch()} retrying={queue.isFetching} />
          ) : queue.data.items.length === 0 ? (
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              Nothing to review. The queue is clear.
            </Text>
          ) : (
            queue.data.items.map((item) => (
              <QueueCard
                key={`${item.targetType}:${item.targetId}`}
                item={item}
                busy={act.isPending && act.variables?.targetId === item.targetId}
                onAct={(a) =>
                  confirm(
                    {
                      targetType: item.targetType,
                      targetId: item.targetId,
                      author: item.content?.author ?? "unknown",
                      venue: item.venue?.name ?? null,
                      autoHidden: !!item.content?.hiddenAt && item.history.some((h) => h.action === "AUTO_HIDE"),
                    },
                    a,
                  )
                }
              />
            ))
          ))}

        {tab === "hidden" &&
          (hidden.isPending ? (
            <SkeletonRows count={4} />
          ) : hidden.isError ? (
            <InlineError message={errorText(hidden.error, "Couldn't load hidden items.")} onRetry={() => void hidden.refetch()} retrying={hidden.isFetching} />
          ) : hidden.data.items.length === 0 ? (
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              Nothing is hidden or suspended.
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <ListGroup>
              {hidden.data.items.map((h) => (
                <ListRow
                  key={`${h.targetType}:${h.targetId}`}
                  leading={h.url ? <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(h.url) }} style={styles.thumb} contentFit="cover" accessibilityLabel={`Hidden photo by ${h.author}`} /> : undefined}
                  title={h.preview || NOUN[h.targetType]}
                  subtitle={`${NOUN[h.targetType]} by ${h.author}${h.venue ? ` at ${h.venue.name}` : ""} · ${formatAge(h.hiddenAt)}`}
                  trailing={
                    <Button
                      title={h.targetType === "USER" ? "Unsuspend" : "Restore"}
                      variant="plain"
                      compact
                      block={false}
                      onPress={() => confirm({ targetType: h.targetType, targetId: h.targetId, author: h.author, venue: h.venue?.name ?? null }, h.targetType === "USER" ? "UNSUSPEND" : "RESTORE")}
                      accessibilityLabel={`${h.targetType === "USER" ? "Unsuspend" : "Restore"} ${NOUN[h.targetType].toLowerCase()} by ${h.author}`}
                    />
                  }
                />
              ))}
            </ListGroup>
          ))}

        {tab === "log" &&
          (log.isPending ? (
            <SkeletonRows count={6} />
          ) : log.isError ? (
            <InlineError message={errorText(log.error, "Couldn't load the log.")} onRetry={() => void log.refetch()} retrying={log.isFetching} />
          ) : log.data.events.length === 0 ? (
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              No moderation actions yet.
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <ListGroup footer="The log is permanent: entries are never edited or removed.">
              {log.data.events.map((e) => (
                <ListRow
                  key={e.id}
                  title={`${actionWord(e.action)} \u00B7 ${LOG_NOUN[e.targetType] ?? e.targetType}`}
                  subtitle={`${e.by} \u00B7 ${formatAge(e.createdAt)}${e.note ? ` \u00B7 \u201C${e.note}\u201D` : ""}`}
                />
              ))}
            </ListGroup>
          ))}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

function QueueCard({ item, busy, onAct }: { item: ModQueueItem; busy: boolean; onAct: (a: Action) => void }) {
  const c = item.content;
  const isUser = item.targetType === "USER";
  const hidden = !!c?.hiddenAt;
  // Flags are listed above; the history shows only what moderators (or auto-hide) did.
  const history = item.history.filter((h) => h.action !== "FLAGGED");
  const who = `${NOUN[item.targetType].toLowerCase()} by ${c?.author ?? "unknown"}`;
  return (
    <View style={styles.card}>
      <View style={styles.cardHead}>
        <Badge label={NOUN[item.targetType]} />
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary" style={styles.flex} numberOfLines={1}>
          {item.venue ? item.venue.name : "Account report"} · {plural(item.flags.length, "flag")}
        </Text>
        {c?.hiddenAt && <Badge label={isUser ? "Suspended" : "Hidden"} />}
      </View>

      {c ? (
        <View style={styles.preview}>
          {c.url && <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(c.url) }} style={styles.photo} contentFit="cover" accessibilityLabel="Flagged stamp" />}
          {!!c.preview && <Text variant="body">{c.preview}</Text>}
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
            {c.author} · {reputationText(c.authorRep)} · {formatAge(c.createdAt)}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
          The content is no longer available.
        </Text>
      )}

      <View style={styles.flags}>
        {item.flags.map((f) => (
          <View key={f.id} style={styles.flag}>
            <Text variant="subhead">
              {f.category === "UNDERAGE" || f.category === "STALKING" ? "Urgent: " : ""}&ldquo;{f.reason}&rdquo;
            </Text>
            <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
              {f.by}
              {f.byRole !== "USER" ? ` · ${f.byRole.toLowerCase()}` : ""} · {formatAge(f.createdAt)}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>

      {history.length > 0 && (
        <View style={styles.flag}>
          {history.map((h, i) => (
            <Text key={i} variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
              {actionWord(h.action)} by {h.by} · {formatAge(h.at)}
              {h.note ? ` \u00B7 \u201C${h.note}\u201D` : ""}
            </Text>
          ))}
        </View>
      )}

      <View style={styles.actions}>
        {isUser ? (
          hidden ? (
            <Button title="Unsuspend" variant="secondary" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("UNSUSPEND")} accessibilityLabel={`Unsuspend ${c?.author}`} />
          ) : (
            <Button title="Suspend" variant="destructive" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("SUSPEND")} accessibilityLabel={`Suspend ${c?.author}`} />
          )
        ) : (
          <Button title={hidden ? "Keep hidden" : "Hide"} variant="destructive" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("HIDE")} accessibilityLabel={`${hidden ? "Keep hidden" : "Hide"} ${who}`} />
        )}
        <Button title="Dismiss" variant="secondary" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("DISMISS")} accessibilityLabel={`Dismiss flags on ${who}`} />
        {!isUser && hidden && <Button title="Restore" variant="plain" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("RESTORE")} accessibilityLabel={`Restore ${who}`} />}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.xs, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.md },
  card: { gap: space.sm, padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  cardHead: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.xs },
  preview: { gap: space.xs },
  photo: { height: size.heroHeight, borderRadius: radius.chip, backgroundColor: colors.elevated },
  flags: { gap: space.xs, paddingTop: space.xs, borderTopWidth: hairlineWidth, borderTopColor: colors.hairline },
  flag: { gap: space.xxs },
  actions: { flexDirection: "row", gap: space.xs, flexWrap: "wrap" },
  thumb: { width: size.avatar, height: size.avatar, borderRadius: radius.chip },
  flex: { flex: 1 },
});


===== src/app/(app)/people.tsx =====

import { Stack } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useUserSearch } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { PersonRow } from "@/features/people/person-row";
import { followButton, useFollow } from "@/features/people/use-follow";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/** Find people by name or handle and follow them. Native search bar in the navigation bar. */
export default function People() {
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const search = useUserSearch(query);
  const follow = useFollow();

  // Search as you type, after a short pause.
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setQuery(input.trim().replace(/^@/, "")), 250);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [input]);

  const users = search.data?.users ?? [];

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          headerSearchBarOptions: {
            placeholder: "Search by name or handle",
            autoCapitalize: "none",
            autoFocus: true,
            hideWhenScrolling: false,
            barTintColor: colors.surface,
            tintColor: colors.accent,
            textColor: colors.textPrimary,
            onChangeText: (e) => setInput(e.nativeEvent.text),
          },
        }}
      />
      <ScrollView contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic" keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" keyboardDismissMode="on-drag" contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        {query.length < 2 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            Type at least two letters.
          </Text>
        ) : search.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={3} />
        ) : search.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(search.error, "Couldn't search right now.")} onRetry={() => void search.refetch()} retrying={search.isFetching} />
        ) : users.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            No one matches {"“"}
            {query}
            {"”"}.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup>
            {users.map((u) => {
              const b = followButton(u.follow, u.handle);
              const toggle = () => follow.toggle(u.handle, u.follow);
              return (
                <PersonRow
                  key={u.handle}
                  person={u}
                  subtitle={u.isSelf ? "You" : undefined}
                  actions={u.isSelf ? [] : [{ label: b.label, onPress: toggle }]}
                  trailing={
                    u.isSelf ? undefined : <Button
                      title={b.title}
                      variant={b.variant}
                      compact
                      block={false}
                      onPress={toggle}
                      accessibilityLabel={b.label}
                      accessibilityState={{ busy: follow.isPendingFor(u.handle) }}
                    />
                  }
                />
              );
            })}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.lg },
});


===== src/app/(app)/place/[id].tsx =====

import { router, Stack, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { usePlacePage } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { AccountGate } from "@/features/account/account-gate";
import { StampMark } from "@/features/book/StampMark";
import { useUserCoords } from "@/features/location/location-store";
import { dayLabel, stampLine, standing, visitSummary } from "@/features/place/place-page";
import { formatDistance } from "@/lib/format";
import { colors, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Your page for one place: your stamps there, night by night, and whether you are inside its geofence right now. */
export default function PlacePage() {
  return (
    <AccountGate title="Place" message="Sign in to see your stamps at this place.">
      <Page />
    </AccountGate>
  );
}

function Page() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const here = useUserCoords();
  const q = usePlacePage(id ?? "", here);
  const data = q.data;
  const stand = data ? standing(data, formatDistance) : null;
  const summary = data ? visitSummary(data.visits) : null;

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: data?.place.name ?? "Place" }} />
      <ScrollView contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic" contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        {data ? (
          <>
            <View style={styles.head}>
              <Text variant="largeTitle" color="brass" accessibilityRole="header">
                {data.place.name}
              </Text>
              <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textSecondary">
                {[data.place.neighborhood, data.place.address].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
              </Text>
              {stand?.text ? (
                <Text variant="typewriter" color={stand.canStamp ? "brass" : "dimBrass"} accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
                  {stand.text}
                </Text>
              ) : null}
              {stand?.canStamp ? <Button title="Stamp here" icon="seal.fill" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/stamp/new")} /> : null}
            </View>

            {summary ? (
              <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass">
                {summary.toUpperCase()}
              </Text>
            ) : (
              <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
                You haven&apos;t stamped this place yet.
              </Text>
            )}

            {data.visits.map((visit) => (
              <View key={visit.day} style={styles.night}>
                <Text variant="serifLabel" color="brass" accessibilityRole="header">
                  {dayLabel(visit.day)}
                </Text>
                {visit.stamps.map((s) => (
                  <Pressable
                    key={s.id}
                    onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: "/stamp/[id]", params: { id: s.id } })}
                    accessibilityRole="button"
                    accessibilityLabel={`Stamp, ${stampLine(s)}`}
                    accessibilityHint="Opens the stamp"
                    style={styles.row}
                  >
                    <StampMark input={{ placeId: s.place.id, placeName: s.place.name, neighborhoodId: s.place.neighborhoodId, neighborhoodName: s.place.neighborhood }} size={size.avatarLarge} />
                    <Text variant="typewriter" color="textPrimary" style={styles.line} numberOfLines={3}>
                      {stampLine(s)}
                    </Text>
                  </Pressable>
                ))}
              </View>
            ))}
          </>
        ) : q.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(q.error, "Couldn't load this place.")} onRetry={() => void q.refetch()} retrying={q.isFetching} />
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.head} accessible accessibilityRole="progressbar" accessibilityLabel="Loading this place">
            <Skeleton width="70%" height={size.iconLarge} />
            <Skeleton width="45%" />
          </View>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { padding: screenPadding, gap: space.lg, paddingBottom: space.xxxl },
  head: { gap: space.xs },
  night: { gap: space.xs, padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  row: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.md, minHeight: size.hitTarget },
  line: { flex: 1 },
});


===== src/app/(app)/settings/blocked.tsx =====

import { ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useBlocks } from "@/api/queries";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { useBlock } from "@/features/moderation/content-actions";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

export default function Blocked() {
  const blocks = useBlocks();
  const block = useBlock();
  const list = blocks.data?.blocks ?? [];

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <ScrollView contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic" contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        {blocks.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={3} />
        ) : blocks.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(blocks.error, "Couldn't load blocked users.")} onRetry={() => void blocks.refetch()} retrying={blocks.isFetching} />
        ) : list.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            You haven&apos;t blocked anyone.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup footer="Blocked people can't see your stamps or profile, and you can't see theirs.">
            {list.map((b) => (
              <ListRow
                key={b.handle}
                leading={<Avatar name={b.handle} />}
                title={`@${b.handle}`}
                trailing={
                  <Button
                    title="Unblock"
                    variant="secondary"
                    compact
                    block={false}
                    loading={block.isPending && block.variables?.handle === b.handle}
                    onPress={() => block.mutate({ handle: b.handle, block: false })}
                    accessibilityLabel={`Unblock @${b.handle}`}
                  />
                }
              />
            ))}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.md },
});


===== src/app/(app)/settings/delete-account.tsx =====

import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { api, ApiError, errorText } from "@/api";
import { farewellNotice, useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { colors, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";

const WHAT_HAPPENS = [
  "Your name, handle, email and password are erased.",
  "Your stamps are removed from every passport, and so are your follows and likes.",
  "You\u2019re signed out on every device. This can\u2019t be undone.",
];

const FAREWELL = "Your account is deleted.";

/** Guideline 5.1.1(v): delete the account from inside the app. Two taps from Profile to the button. */
export default function DeleteAccount() {
  const { forget } = useAuth();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");

  const del = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      try {
        return await api.me.deleteAccount(password);
      } catch (e) {
        // A retry after a timeout that actually succeeded: the token is already dead, so it's done.
        if (e instanceof ApiError && e.code === "invalid_token") {
          farewellNotice.set(FAREWELL);
          return { deleted: true as const };
        }
        throw e;
      }
    },
    onMutate: () => farewellNotice.set(FAREWELL),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      // The account is gone; the token already stopped working. Sign out locally only.
      await forget();
    },
    onError: () => {
      farewellNotice.take();
      haptics.warning();
    },
  });

  const confirm = () => {
    if (!password || del.isPending) return;
    Alert.alert("Delete your account?", "This can't be undone.", [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      { text: "Delete", style: "destructive", onPress: () => del.mutate() },
    ]);
  };

  const wrongPassword = del.error instanceof ApiError && del.error.code === "wrong_password";

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <View style={styles.warning}>
        <Icon name="exclamationmark.triangle.fill" color="danger" size={size.iconLarge} />
        <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
          Delete your account
        </Text>
        {WHAT_HAPPENS.map((line) => (
          <View key={line} style={styles.bullet}>
            <View style={styles.dot} />
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary" style={styles.flex}>
              {line}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>

      <FieldGroup footer="Enter your password to confirm." errors={[wrongPassword ? "That password isn't right." : null]}>
        <Field
