          label="Password"
          placeholder="Required"
          value={password}
          invalid={wrongPassword}
          onChangeText={(v) => {
            setPassword(v);
            if (del.error) del.reset();
          }}
          secureTextEntry
          autoComplete="current-password"
          textContentType="password"
          returnKeyType="done"
          onSubmitEditing={confirm}
          testID="delete-password"
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      {del.error && !wrongPassword && <InlineError message={errorText(del.error, "Couldn't delete your account. Try again.")} />}
      <Button title="Delete account" variant="destructive" onPress={confirm} disabled={!password} loading={del.isPending} />
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  warning: { gap: space.sm, padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  bullet: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: space.sm },
  dot: { width: space.xs, height: space.xs, borderRadius: radius.pill, backgroundColor: colors.textTertiary, marginTop: space.xs },
  flex: { flex: 1 },
});


===== src/app/(app)/settings/index.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { Alert, RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { useState } from "react";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useFollowing, useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { VerifiedBadge } from "@/components/ui/verified-badge";
import { LEGAL, openWebPage } from "@/lib/links";
import { SOCIAL_ENABLED } from "@/config";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/** Settings: edit your profile, the moderation entry for staff, account and legal. (With the social layer on: who I follow and blocked users too.) */
export default function Settings() {
  const me = useMe();
  const following = useFollowing();
  const { signOut } = useAuth();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const user = me.data?.user;
  const staff = user?.role === "MODERATOR" || user?.role === "ADMIN";

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await Promise.all([me.refetch(), following.refetch()]).finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  const confirmSignOut = () =>
    Alert.alert("Sign out?", undefined, [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      { text: "Sign out", style: "destructive", onPress: () => void signOut() },
    ]);

  const list = following.data?.following ?? [];

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <ScrollView
        contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      >
        <ListGroup header="Your passport">
          <ListRow icon="person.crop.circle" title="Edit profile" subtitle={user ? `${user.displayName} · @${user.handle}` : undefined} chevron onPress={() => router.push("/edit-profile")} accessibilityHint="Change your name, bio or photo" />
          {!SOCIAL_ENABLED && <ListRow icon="lock.fill" iconColor="dimBrass" title="Only you can see your passport" subtitle="Nothing is shared, and there is no feed." />}
        </ListGroup>

        {staff && (
          <ListGroup>
            <ListRow icon="shield.lefthalf.filled" iconColor="accent" title="Moderation" chevron onPress={() => router.push("/moderation")} />
          </ListGroup>
        )}

        {SOCIAL_ENABLED && (
        <ListGroup header="Following">
          {following.isPending ? (
            <View style={styles.pad}>
              <Skeleton width="60%" />
            </View>
          ) : following.isError ? (
            <View style={styles.pad}>
              <InlineError message={errorText(following.error, "Couldn't load who you follow.")} onRetry={() => void following.refetch()} />
            </View>
          ) : list.length === 0 ? (
            <View style={styles.empty}>
              <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
                You&apos;re not following anyone yet.
              </Text>
              <Button title="Find people" variant="plain" compact block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/people")} />
            </View>
          ) : (
            list.map((f) => (
              <ListRow
                key={f.handle}
                leading={<Avatar name={f.displayName} uri={f.avatarUrl} />}
                title={f.displayName}
                titleAccessory={f.verified ? <VerifiedBadge accessible={false} /> : undefined}
                accessibilityLabel={[f.displayName, `@${f.handle}`, f.verified ? "verified" : null].filter(Boolean).join(", ")}
                subtitle={`@${f.handle}`}
                chevron
                onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: "/user/[handle]", params: { handle: f.handle } })}
              />
            ))
          )}
          {list.length > 0 && <ListRow icon="person.badge.plus" iconColor="accent" title="Find people" titleColor="accent" onPress={() => router.push("/people")} />}
        </ListGroup>
        )}

        <ListGroup>
          <ListRow icon="lock.fill" iconColor="dimBrass" title="Customize, coming soon" titleColor="textSecondary" chevron onPress={() => router.push("/customize")} accessibilityHint="Opens a note about what is coming" />
        </ListGroup>

        <ListGroup header="Settings">
          {SOCIAL_ENABLED && <ListRow icon="hand.raised.fill" title="Blocked users" chevron onPress={() => router.push("/settings/blocked")} />}
          <ListRow icon="lock.fill" title="Privacy policy" external onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.privacy)} />
          <ListRow icon="doc.text.fill" title="Terms" external onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.terms)} />
          <ListRow icon="questionmark.circle.fill" title="Support" external onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.support)} />
        </ListGroup>

        {__DEV__ ? (
          <ListGroup header="Development">
            <ListRow icon="paintpalette.fill" title="Design gallery" chevron onPress={() => router.push("/design")} accessibilityHint="Every visual piece on made-up data" />
          </ListGroup>
        ) : null}

        <ListGroup>
          <ListRow icon="rectangle.portrait.and.arrow.right" title="Sign out" onPress={confirmSignOut} />
          <ListRow
            icon="trash.fill"
            iconColor="danger"
            title="Delete account"
            titleColor="danger"
            onPress={() => router.push("/settings/delete-account")}
            accessibilityHint="Opens the account deletion screen"
          />
        </ListGroup>

        {user && (
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary" style={styles.since}>
            Member since {new Date(user.memberSince).toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "long", year: "numeric" })}
          </Text>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.xl },
  pad: { padding: space.md },
  empty: { padding: space.md, gap: space.xs, alignItems: "flex-start" },
  since: { paddingHorizontal: space.md },
});


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/[id].tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router, Stack, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { Alert, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { api, ApiError, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { qk, usePins, useStamp } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { toast, ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { a11yActions, showActions, useStampActions, type ContentAction } from "@/features/moderation/content-actions";
import { dropStamp } from "@/features/stamps/cache";
import { LikeButton } from "@/features/stamps/like-button";
import { useLike } from "@/features/stamps/use-like";
import { formatAge } from "@/lib/format";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { SOCIAL_ENABLED } from "@/config";
import { pinChange } from "@/features/book/pin-change";
import { VisaSpread } from "@/features/book/VisaSpread";
import { colors, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** A stamp: photo, caption, song, place, author, likes. Yours: Edit and Delete from profile. Others': Report and Block. */
export default function StampDetail() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const stamp = useStamp(id);
  const s = stamp.data?.stamp;
  const actions = useActions(s);
  const gone = stamp.error instanceof ApiError && (stamp.error.status === 404 || stamp.error.status === 403);

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: s ? s.place.name : "Stamp",
          headerRight: () =>
            s && actions.length > 0 ? (
              <Pressable onPress={() => showActions(actions)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="More options" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.headerButton}>
                <Icon name="ellipsis.circle" color="accent" size={size.icon} />
              </Pressable>
            ) : null,
        }}
      />
      <ScrollView contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic" contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        {s ? (
          <Detail stamp={s} actions={actions} />
        ) : gone ? (
          <View style={styles.pad}>
            <Text variant="title2">This stamp isn&apos;t available</Text>
            <Text color="textSecondary">It may have been removed, or the account may be private.</Text>
            <Button title="Close" variant="secondary" compact block={false} onPress={() => router.back()} />
          </View>
        ) : stamp.isError ? (
          <View style={styles.pad}>
            <InlineError message={errorText(stamp.error, "Couldn't load this stamp.")} onRetry={() => void stamp.refetch()} retrying={stamp.isFetching} />
          </View>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Skeleton width="100%" height={size.postPhotoSkeleton} />
            <View style={styles.pad}>
              <Skeleton width="40%" />
              <Skeleton width="70%" />
            </View>
          </>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

function Detail({ stamp, actions }: { stamp: StampView; actions: ContentAction[] }) {
  const like = useLike();
  return (
    <View {...a11yActions(actions)}>
      <VisaSpread stamp={stamp} />
      <View style={styles.pad}>
        <View style={styles.footer}>
          {SOCIAL_ENABLED && <LikeButton likes={stamp.likes} disabled={stamp.mine} onPress={() => like.toggle(stamp.id, stamp.likes)} />}
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
            {formatAge(stamp.createdAt)}
          </Text>
        </View>
        {stamp.mine && (
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
            Only you can see this stamp.
          </Text>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

/** Yours: Edit and Delete from profile (hides it for good). Others': Report and Block. */
function useActions(stamp: StampView | undefined): ContentAction[] {
  const build = useStampActions();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const pins = usePins();
  const setPins = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (ids: string[]) => api.pins.set(ids),
    onSuccess: (r) => {
      haptics.success();
      queryClient.setQueryData(qk.pins, r);
      toast.show("Updated your pinned stamps.");
    },
    onError: (e) => {
      haptics.warning();
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't change that. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
  });
  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => api.stamps.remove(id),
    onSuccess: (_r, id) => {
      haptics.success();
      toast.show("Deleted from your profile.");
      dropStamp(queryClient, id);
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.feed });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["user"] });
      router.back();
    },
    onError: (e) => {
      haptics.warning();
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't delete that. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
  });
  if (!stamp) return [];
  const extra: ContentAction[] = stamp.mine
    ? [
        {
          label: pins.data?.stamps.some((p) => p.id === stamp.id) ? "Unpin from passport" : "Pin to passport",
          onPress: () => {
            const change = pinChange((pins.data?.stamps ?? []).map((p) => p.id), stamp.id);
            if (change.kind === "full") Alert.alert("Four stamps are pinned", "Unpin one first, then pin this one.");
            else setPins.mutate(change.ids);
          },
        },
        { label: "Edit", onPress: () => router.push({ pathname: "/stamp/edit", params: { id: stamp.id } }) },
        {
          label: "Delete from profile",
          destructive: true,
          onPress: () =>
            Alert.alert("Delete from your profile?", "It's removed from your passport and everyone's feed for good.", [
              { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
              { text: "Delete", style: "destructive", onPress: () => remove.mutate(stamp.id) },
            ]),
        },
      ]
    : [];
  return build({ id: stamp.id, authorHandle: stamp.author.handle, mine: stamp.mine, extra });
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingBottom: space.xxxl },
  pad: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, gap: space.md },
  footer: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.md },
  headerButton: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "flex-end", justifyContent: "center" },
});


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/camera.tsx =====

import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from "expo-camera";
import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Linking, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { photoDraft, usePhotoDraft } from "@/features/stamps/photo-draft";
import { MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS, photoCount } from "@/features/stamps/stamp-flow";
import { prepareImage } from "@/features/photos/prepare";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { colors, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/**
 * The in-app camera for a stamp's photos, up to six. Photos are taken here and nowhere else: they are proof you were at the place.
 * Each shot goes straight into the stamp being made; Done goes back to it.
 */
export default function StampCamera() {
  const [permission, request] = useCameraPermissions();
  const ref = useRef<CameraView>(null);
  const photos = usePhotoDraft();
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [note, setNote] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const full = photos.length >= MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS;

  const shoot = async () => {
    if (!ref.current || busy || full) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setNote(null);
    try {
      const shot = await ref.current.takePictureAsync({ quality: 1, exif: false, skipProcessing: false });
      const prepared = await prepareImage(shot.uri);
      if (photoDraft.add(prepared.uri)) haptics.select();
    } catch {
      setNote("Couldn't take that photo. Try again.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  if (!permission) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.center} accessibilityRole="progressbar" accessibilityLabel="Opening the camera">
        <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
          Opening the camera…
        </Text>
      </View>
    );
  }
  if (!permission.granted) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.center}>
        <Icon name="camera.fill" color="dimBrass" size={size.iconLarge} />
        <Text variant="title2" align="center">
          Camera access
        </Text>
        <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary" align="center">
          Nostos takes your stamp photos here, inside the app, so they show where you really are.
        </Text>
        <Button
          title={permission.canAskAgain ? "Allow the camera" : "Open Settings"}
          block={false}
          onPress={() => (permission.canAskAgain ? void request() : void Linking.openSettings())}
        />
        <Button title="Not now" variant="plain" block={false} onPress={() => router.back()} />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <CameraView ref={ref} style={styles.preview} facing="back" onCameraReady={() => setReady(true)} accessibilityLabel="Camera" />
      <View style={styles.top}>
        <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="brass" accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
          {photoCount(photos.length)}
        </Text>
      </View>
      <View style={styles.bottom}>
        {photos.length > 0 ? (
          <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={styles.strip}>
            {photos.map((uri, i) => (
              <View key={`${uri}-${i}`} style={styles.thumb}>
                <Image source={{ uri }} style={styles.fill} contentFit="cover" accessibilityLabel={`Photo ${i + 1}`} />
                <Pressable onPress={() => photoDraft.remove(i)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`Remove photo ${i + 1}`} hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.remove}>
                  <Icon name="xmark" color="textPrimary" size={size.iconTiny} />
                </Pressable>
              </View>
            ))}
          </ScrollView>
        ) : null}
        {note ? (
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary" align="center" accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
            {note}
          </Text>
        ) : null}
        <View style={styles.controls}>
          <View style={styles.side} />
          <Pressable
            onPress={() => void shoot()}
            disabled={!ready || busy || full}
            accessibilityRole="button"
            accessibilityLabel={full ? "Six photos taken" : "Take photo"}
            accessibilityState={{ disabled: !ready || busy || full }}
            style={[styles.shutter, (!ready || busy || full) && styles.shutterOff]}
          >
            <View style={styles.shutterInner} />
          </Pressable>
          <View style={styles.side}>
            <Button title="Done" variant="plain" block={false} onPress={() => router.back()} />
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const THUMB = size.listRow;
const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.black },
  center: { flex: 1, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", gap: space.md, padding: screenPadding, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  preview: { flex: 1 },
  fill: { width: "100%", height: "100%" },
  top: { position: "absolute", top: space.xxxl + space.md, alignSelf: "center", paddingHorizontal: space.sm, paddingVertical: space.xxs, borderRadius: radius.pill, backgroundColor: colors.scrim },
  bottom: { position: "absolute", left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.sm, backgroundColor: colors.scrim },
  strip: { gap: space.xs, paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.sm },
  thumb: { width: THUMB, height: THUMB, borderRadius: radius.chip, overflow: "hidden", backgroundColor: colors.elevated },
  remove: { position: "absolute", top: space.xxs, right: space.xxs, width: size.iconSmall + space.xs, height: size.iconSmall + space.xs, borderRadius: radius.pill, backgroundColor: colors.scrim, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  controls: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  side: { width: size.avatarLarge, alignItems: "flex-end" },
  shutter: { width: size.avatarLarge + space.xs, height: size.avatarLarge + space.xs, borderRadius: radius.pill, borderWidth: size.focusRingWidth, borderColor: colors.brass, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  shutterOff: { opacity: 0.45 },
  shutterInner: { width: size.avatarLarge - space.md, height: size.avatarLarge - space.md, borderRadius: radius.pill, backgroundColor: colors.brass },
});


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/edit.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router, Stack, useFocusEffect, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { Song, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useStamp } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { CAPTION_MAX, stampChanges } from "@/features/stamps/composer";
import { patchStamp } from "@/features/stamps/cache";
import { songDraft } from "@/features/stamps/song-draft";
import { SongRow } from "@/features/stamps/song-row";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { useConfirmDiscard } from "@/lib/use-confirm-discard";
import { size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Edit a stamp's caption and song. The photo, the place and the time can't change. */
export default function EditStamp() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const stamp = useStamp(id);
  if (!stamp.data) {
    return (
      <FormScreen>
        {stamp.isError ? <InlineError message={errorText(stamp.error, "Couldn't load this stamp.")} onRetry={() => void stamp.refetch()} retrying={stamp.isFetching} /> : <SkeletonRows count={3} />}
      </FormScreen>
    );
  }
  return <EditForm stamp={stamp.data.stamp} />;
}

function EditForm({ stamp }: { stamp: StampView }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [caption, setCaption] = useState(stamp.caption);
  const [song, setSong] = useState<Song | null>(stamp.song);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const change = stampChanges(stamp, caption, song);
  const dirty = Object.keys(change).length > 0;
  // A stamp with no photo must keep a caption or a song.
  const empty = stamp.photoUrl === null && caption.trim() === "" && song === null;
  useConfirmDiscard(dirty && !saved);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      const picked = songDraft.take();
      if (picked) setSong(picked.song);
    }, []),
  );

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.stamps.edit(stamp.id, change),
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      haptics.success();
      setSaved(true);
      patchStamp(queryClient, stamp.id, () => res.stamp);
      requestAnimationFrame(() => router.back());
    },
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: "Edit stamp",
          headerLeft: () => <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => router.back()} />,
          headerRight: () => <HeaderButton label="Save" bold onPress={() => save.mutate()} disabled={!dirty || empty || save.isPending} />,
        }}
      />
      <FieldGroup footer={`${caption.length}/${CAPTION_MAX}`} errors={[empty ? "Add a caption or a song, since this stamp has no photo." : null]}>
        <Field label="Caption" value={caption} onChangeText={setCaption} maxLength={CAPTION_MAX} placeholder="What's it like?" multiline />
      </FieldGroup>
      {song ? (
        <SongRow
          song={song}
          trailing={
            <Pressable onPress={() => setSong(null)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Remove song" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.remove}>
              <Icon name="xmark.circle.fill" color="textTertiary" size={size.icon} />
            </Pressable>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <Button title="Add a song" icon="music.note" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push("/stamp/song")} />
      )}
      {save.error && <InlineError message={errorText(save.error, "Couldn't save. Try again.")} />}
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({ remove: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" } });


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/landed.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useWindowDimensions } from "react-native";

import { useMe, useUserStamps } from "@/api/queries";
import { LandingScene } from "@/features/book/LandingScene";
import { landingDraft } from "@/features/stamps/landing-draft";
import { BOOK_ASPECT } from "@/features/book/dimensions";

/** A new stamp lands on its page. Reached only from the composer; with nothing to show it goes back to the passport. */
export default function Landed() {
  const landed = landingDraft.peek();
  const { width, height } = useWindowDimensions();
  const me = useMe();
  const stamps = useUserStamps(me.data?.user.handle ?? "", !!landed && !!me.data);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!landed) router.dismissAll();
  }, [landed]);
  if (!landed) return null;

  const pageWidth = Math.floor(Math.min(width * 0.7, (height * 0.5) / BOOK_ASPECT));
  const others = (stamps.data?.pages ?? []).flatMap((p) => p.stamps).filter((s) => s.page === landed.stamp.page && s.id !== landed.stamp.id);
  return (
    <LandingScene
      stamp={landed.stamp}
      others={others}
      progress={landed.progress}
      pageWidth={pageWidth}
      pageHeight={Math.floor(pageWidth * BOOK_ASPECT)}
      onDone={() => {
        landingDraft.clear();
        router.dismissAll();
      }}
    />
  );
}


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/new.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { router, Stack, useFocusEffect } from "expo-router";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { CreateStampResponse, type NearbyPlacesResponse, type Song } from "@/api/contracts";
import { qk } from "@/api/keys";
import { useNearbyPlaces, usePlaces } from "@/api/queries";
import { AccountGate } from "@/features/account/account-gate";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { devSimulator } from "@/features/location/dev-simulator";
import { locationStore, usePermissionRefresh, useLocationState, useUserCoords } from "@/features/location/location-store";
import { SimulatedLocationChip } from "@/features/location/sim-chip";
import { autoPlace, CAPTION_MAX, orderPlaces, stampFields, stampProblem } from "@/features/stamps/composer";
import { landingDraft } from "@/features/stamps/landing-draft";
import { photoDraft, usePhotoDraft } from "@/features/stamps/photo-draft";
import { songDraft } from "@/features/stamps/song-draft";
import { HONESTY, MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS, photoCount, placeRowText, placeSections, stampReadiness } from "@/features/stamps/stamp-flow";
import { SongRow } from "@/features/stamps/song-row";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { useConfirmDiscard } from "@/lib/use-confirm-discard";
import { colors, hairlineWidth, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";

type Nearby = NearbyPlacesResponse["places"][number];

/**
 * The stamp composer. Pick the place you're at (the list comes from where your phone is; the server checks the
 * geofence again), add a photo, a caption, a song, any mix, then Stamp. If you're outside the geofence the server's
 * message, with how far away you are, is shown as it is.
 */
export default function NewStamp() {
  return (
    <AccountGate title="Stamp a place" message="Sign in to stamp the places you go.">
      <Composer />
    </AccountGate>
  );
}

function Composer() {
  usePermissionRefresh();
  // Leaving the composer any way at all drops the photos taken for it.
  useEffect(() => () => photoDraft.clear(), []);
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { permission } = useLocationState();
  const coords = useUserCoords();
  const nearby = useNearbyPlaces(coords);
  const [chosenId, setChosenId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const photoUris = usePhotoDraft();
  const [caption, setCaption] = useState("");
  const [song, setSong] = useState<Song | null>(null);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  const draft = { photoUris, caption, song };
  const dirty = !done && (photoUris.length > 0 || caption.trim() !== "" || song !== null);
  useConfirmDiscard(dirty);

  const places = orderPlaces(nearby.data?.places ?? []);
  // The place you picked, or (when exactly one geofence has you inside) that one without asking.
  const place: Nearby | null = places.find((p) => p.id === chosenId) ?? autoPlace(places);

  // A song picked on the song screen comes back here when this screen regains focus.
  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      const picked = songDraft.take();
      if (picked) setSong(picked.song);
    }, []),
  );

  const stamp = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      if (!place) throw new Error("no place");
      // A fresh fix right now: the server checks these coordinates, not the ones the place list was built from.
      const here = (await locationStore.currentForPosting()) ?? coords;
      if (!here) throw new Error("no location");
      const form = new FormData();
      // React Native's FormData takes a file descriptor object for local files.
      for (const [i, uri] of photoUris.entries()) form.append("photos", { uri, name: `photo-${i + 1}.jpg`, type: "image/jpeg" } as unknown as Blob);
      for (const [k, v] of stampFields(draft, here)) form.append(k, v);
      return api.upload(`/api/places/${encodeURIComponent(place.id)}/stamps`, CreateStampResponse, form, { onProgress: setProgress });
    },
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      setDone(true);
      photoDraft.clear();
      landingDraft.set(res.stamp, res.progress);
      for (const key of [["feed"], ["user"], qk.neighborhoods, qk.pins]) void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: key });
      // The landing says "Stamped" itself, with the haptic knock at the moment of impact.
      requestAnimationFrame(() => router.replace("/stamp/landed"));
    },
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const problem = stampProblem(draft, place?.id ?? null);

  const readiness = stampReadiness({ place, problem, busy: stamp.isPending });
  const sections = placeSections(places);

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: "New stamp",
          headerLeft: () => <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => { photoDraft.clear(); router.back(); }} />,
        }}
      />

      <View style={styles.honesty} accessible accessibilityLabel={`${HONESTY.title}. ${HONESTY.body}`}>
        <Icon name="location.fill" color="dimBrass" size={size.iconSmall} />
        <View style={styles.honestyText}>
          <Text variant="serifLabel" color="brass">
            {HONESTY.title}
          </Text>
          <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textSecondary">
            {HONESTY.body}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.section}>
        <Text variant="serifLabel" color="dimBrass" style={styles.heading} accessibilityRole="header">
          WHERE ARE YOU?
        </Text>
        <SimulatedLocationChip />
        {permission !== "granted" && !coords ? (
          <View style={styles.notice}>
            <Icon name="location.fill" color="accent" size={size.iconLarge} />
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              {permission === "denied" ? "Location is off. Turn it on in Settings to stamp the places you're at." : "Nostos checks that you're at a place before it gives you a stamp."}
            </Text>
            <Button title={permission === "denied" ? "Open settings" : "Use my location"} variant="secondary" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/location-permission")} />
          </View>
        ) : nearby.isPending || !coords ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={2} />
        ) : nearby.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(nearby.error, "Couldn't find places near you.")} onRetry={() => void nearby.refetch()} retrying={nearby.isFetching} />
        ) : places.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            No places near you yet. Nostos stamps places in New York.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          sections.map((group) => (
            <ListGroup key={group.title} header={group.title}>
              {group.places.map((p) => {
                const row = placeRowText(p);
                const selected = p.id === place?.id;
                return (
                  <ListRow
                    key={p.id}
                    icon={selected ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "circle"}
                    iconColor={selected ? "accent" : "textTertiary"}
                    title={row.title}
                    subtitle={row.subtitle}
                    accessibilityLabel={`${row.spoken}${selected ? ", selected" : ""}`}
                    onPress={() => setChosenId(p.id)}
                  />
                );
              })}
            </ListGroup>
          ))
        )}
        {__DEV__ && devSimulator && <DevPlaces />}
      </View>

      <View style={styles.section}>
        <Text variant="serifLabel" color="dimBrass" style={styles.heading} accessibilityRole="header">
          PHOTOS, CAPTION OR SONG
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.photos}>
          {photoUris.map((uri, i) => (
            <View key={`${uri}-${i}`} style={styles.thumb}>
              <Image source={{ uri }} style={styles.fill} contentFit="cover" accessibilityLabel={`Photo ${i + 1}`} />
              <Pressable onPress={() => photoDraft.remove(i)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`Remove photo ${i + 1}`} hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.removePhoto}>
                <Icon name="xmark" color="textPrimary" size={size.iconTiny} />
              </Pressable>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
        {photoUris.length < MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS ? (
          <Button title={photoUris.length === 0 ? "Take a photo" : `Take another photo (${photoCount(photoUris.length)})`} icon="camera.fill" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push("/stamp/camera")} />
        ) : (
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
            Six photos is the most a stamp holds.
          </Text>
        )}

        <FieldGroup footer={`${caption.length}/${CAPTION_MAX}`}>
          <Field label="Caption" value={caption} onChangeText={setCaption} maxLength={CAPTION_MAX} placeholder="What's it like?" multiline accessibilityHint={`Up to ${CAPTION_MAX} characters`} />
        </FieldGroup>

        {song ? (
          <SongRow
            song={song}
            trailing={
              <Pressable onPress={() => setSong(null)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Remove song" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.removeSong}>
                <Icon name="xmark.circle.fill" color="textTertiary" size={size.icon} />
              </Pressable>
            }
          />
        ) : (
          <Button title="Add a song" icon="music.note" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push("/stamp/song")} />
        )}
      </View>

      {readiness.calm ? (
        <View style={styles.calm} accessible accessibilityLiveRegion="polite" accessibilityLabel={`${readiness.calm.title}. ${readiness.calm.body}`}>
          <Icon name="figure.walk" color="dimBrass" size={size.iconLarge} />
          <View style={styles.honestyText}>
            <Text variant="title2" color="brass">
              {readiness.calm.title}
            </Text>
            <Text variant="typewriter" color="textSecondary">
              {readiness.calm.body}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      ) : null}
      {readiness.hint && !stamp.isPending ? (
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary" accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
          {readiness.hint}
        </Text>
      ) : null}
      {stamp.isPending ? (
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary" accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
          {photoUris.length > 0 ? `Stamping… ${Math.round(progress * 100)}%` : "Stamping…"}
        </Text>
      ) : null}
      {/* The server's own sentence, e.g. "You need to be within 100 m of this place to stamp it (you're 140 m away)". */}
      {stamp.error && <InlineError message={errorText(stamp.error, "Couldn't stamp that. Try again.")} onRetry={() => stamp.mutate()} retrying={stamp.isPending} />}

      <Button title="Stamp" icon="seal.fill" loading={stamp.isPending} disabled={!readiness.ready} onPress={() => stamp.mutate()} accessibilityHint={readiness.ready ? "Stamps this place" : undefined} />
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

/** Development only: pretend to be at a place, to try the geofence without going there. */
function DevPlaces() {
  const places = usePlaces();
  if (!devSimulator) return null;
  const list = (places.data?.places ?? []).slice(0, 6);
  return (
    <ListGroup header="Development: pretend to be at">
      {list.map((p) => (
        <ListRow key={p.id} icon="location.slash" title={p.name} subtitle={p.neighborhood} onPress={() => devSimulator?.set({ lat: p.lat, lng: p.lng })} />
      ))}
    </ListGroup>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  section: { gap: space.sm },
  heading: { paddingHorizontal: space.md },
  honesty: { flexDirection: "row", gap: space.sm, padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderWidth: hairlineWidth, borderColor: colors.hairline },
  honestyText: { flex: 1, gap: space.xxs },
  notice: { gap: space.sm, alignItems: "flex-start", padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  photos: { flexDirection: "row", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: space.xs },
  thumb: { width: size.avatarLarge + space.md, height: size.avatarLarge + space.md, borderRadius: radius.chip, overflow: "hidden", backgroundColor: colors.elevated },
  fill: { width: "100%", height: "100%" },
  removePhoto: { position: "absolute", top: space.xxs, right: space.xxs, width: size.iconSmall + space.xs, height: size.iconSmall + space.xs, borderRadius: radius.pill, backgroundColor: colors.scrim, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  removeSong: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  calm: { flexDirection: "row", gap: space.md, alignItems: "center", padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderWidth: hairlineWidth, borderColor: colors.dimBrass },
});


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/song.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { router, Stack } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import type { Song, SongResult } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useSongSearch } from "@/api/queries";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { songDraft } from "@/features/stamps/song-draft";
import { colors, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Search songs (Apple's iTunes catalog, through Nostos) and pick one for the stamp. Metadata only: nothing plays. */
export default function SongPicker() {
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const search = useSongSearch(query);

  // Search as you type, after a short pause.
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setQuery(input.trim()), 350);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [input]);

  const choose = (r: SongResult) => {
    const song: Song = { title: r.title, artist: r.artist, artUrl: r.artUrl, sourceId: r.sourceId };
    songDraft.choose(song);
    router.back();
  };
  const songs = search.data?.songs ?? [];

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: "Add a song",
          headerLeft: () => <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => router.back()} />,
          headerSearchBarOptions: {
            placeholder: "Song or artist",
            autoCapitalize: "none",
            autoFocus: true,
            hideWhenScrolling: false,
            barTintColor: colors.surface,
            tintColor: colors.accent,
            textColor: colors.textPrimary,
            onChangeText: (e) => setInput(e.nativeEvent.text),
          },
        }}
      />
      <ScrollView contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic" keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" keyboardDismissMode="on-drag" contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        {query.length < 2 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            Type a song or an artist.
          </Text>
        ) : search.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={4} />
        ) : search.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(search.error, "Couldn't search for songs right now.")} onRetry={() => void search.refetch()} retrying={search.isFetching} />
        ) : songs.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            Nothing found for {"“"}
            {query}
            {"”"}.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup footer="Song info from Apple Music. Nostos doesn't play music.">
            {songs.map((s) => (
              <ListRow
                key={s.sourceId}
                leading={<Art url={s.artUrl} />}
                title={s.title}
                subtitle={[s.artist, s.album].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
                accessibilityLabel={`${s.title} by ${s.artist}`}
                accessibilityHint="Adds this song to your stamp"
                onPress={() => choose(s)}
              />
            ))}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => {
            songDraft.choose(null);
            router.back();
          }}
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityLabel="No song"
          style={styles.none}
        >
          <Text variant="callout" color="accent">
            No song
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

function Art({ url }: { url: string | null }) {
  return url ? (
    <Image source={{ uri: url, cacheKey: url }} style={styles.art} contentFit="cover" transition={150} accessible={false} />
  ) : (
    <View style={styles.art}>
      <Icon name="music.note" color="textSecondary" size={size.icon} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.lg },
  art: { width: size.avatarLarge - space.md, height: size.avatarLarge - space.md, borderRadius: radius.chip, backgroundColor: colors.elevated, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", overflow: "hidden" },
  none: { minHeight: size.hitTarget, justifyContent: "center" },
});

===== src/app/_layout.tsx =====

import { CormorantGaramond_500Medium, CormorantGaramond_500Medium_Italic, CormorantGaramond_600SemiBold } from "@expo-google-fonts/cormorant-garamond";
import { CourierPrime_400Regular, CourierPrime_700Bold } from "@expo-google-fonts/courier-prime";
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useFonts } from "expo-font";
import { router, Stack } from "expo-router";
import * as SplashScreen from "expo-splash-screen";
import { StatusBar } from "expo-status-bar";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import { GestureHandlerRootView } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import { SafeAreaProvider } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { createQueryClient, wireReactQueryToApp } from "@/api/query-client";
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { stackOptions } from "@/components/ui/stack-options";
import { useOfflineAnnouncer } from "@/lib/network";
import { initErrorReporting } from "@/lib/report-error";
import { colors } from "@/theme";

void SplashScreen.preventAutoHideAsync().catch(() => undefined);
// Off unless the build sets EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED=1 and a DSN (see lib/report-error.ts).
initErrorReporting();

export default function RootLayout() {
  const [queryClient] = useState(createQueryClient);
  useEffect(() => wireReactQueryToApp(), []);

  return (
    <GestureHandlerRootView style={styles.root}>
      <SafeAreaProvider>
        <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
          <AuthProvider>
            <StatusBar style="light" />
            <RootNavigator />
          </AuthProvider>
        </QueryClientProvider>
      </SafeAreaProvider>
    </GestureHandlerRootView>
  );
}

function RootNavigator() {
  useOfflineAnnouncer();
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const me = useMe();
  // The serif and the typewriter. If they fail to load the system fonts are used and the app still opens.
  const [fontsLoaded, fontError] = useFonts({ CormorantGaramond_500Medium, CormorantGaramond_500Medium_Italic, CormorantGaramond_600SemiBold, CourierPrime_400Regular, CourierPrime_700Bold });
  const signedIn = status === "signedIn";
  const meReady = signedIn && !!me.data;
  const needsTerms = meReady && me.data?.terms.needsAcceptance === true;
  const ready = status !== "loading" && !(signedIn && me.isLoading) && (fontsLoaded || !!fontError);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready) SplashScreen.hide();
  }, [ready]);

  // Order matters: when a guard flips, the router lands on the first available screen.
  return (
    <Stack screenOptions={stackOptions}>
      <Stack.Protected guard={status === "signedOut" || status === "loading"}>
        <Stack.Screen name="welcome" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
      <Stack.Protected guard={signedIn && !meReady}>
        <Stack.Screen name="connect" options={{ headerShown: false, gestureEnabled: false }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
      <Stack.Protected guard={needsTerms}>
        <Stack.Screen name="terms-update" options={{ headerShown: false, gestureEnabled: false }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
      <Stack.Protected guard={status === "guest" || (meReady && !needsTerms)}>
        <Stack.Screen name="(app)" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
      {/* Development builds only: every visual piece on made-up data, reachable without an account (see features/gallery). */}
      <Stack.Protected guard={__DEV__}>
        <Stack.Screen name="design/index" options={{ title: "Design gallery" }} />
        <Stack.Screen name="design/[section]" options={{ title: "Design gallery" }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
      <Stack.Protected guard={!signedIn}>
        <Stack.Screen name="age-gate" options={{ title: "Age check", presentation: "modal", headerLeft: CloseButton }} />
        <Stack.Screen name="sign-in" options={{ title: "Sign in", presentation: "modal", headerLeft: CloseButton }} />
        <Stack.Screen name="sign-up" options={{ title: "Create account", presentation: "modal", headerLeft: CloseButton }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
    </Stack>
  );
}

function CloseButton() {
  return <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => router.back()} />;
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({ root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background } });


===== src/app/age-gate.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { EmblemMark } from "@/components/brand/emblem-mark";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { checkBirthdate, formatBirthdateInput } from "@/lib/age";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { size, space } from "@/theme";

/**
 * The age gate for looking around without an account. The birthdate is checked here and thrown away: it isn't sent
 * anywhere or saved, and nothing is remembered about someone who isn't old enough.
 */
export default function AgeGate() {
  const { browseAsGuest } = useAuth();
  const [birthdate, setBirthdate] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [underage, setUnderage] = useState(false);

  const submit = () => {
    const check = checkBirthdate(birthdate);
    if (check.ok) {
      setMessage(null);
      browseAsGuest();
      return;
    }
    haptics.warning();
    setMessage(check.message);
    setUnderage(check.reason === "underage");
  };

  if (underage) {
    // Calm, one sentence, no way forward. Nothing was sent and nothing is kept.
    return (
      <FormScreen>
        <View style={styles.turnedAway}>
          <EmblemMark size={size.avatarLarge} color="dimBrass" />
          <Text variant="title2" align="center" accessibilityRole="header">
            {message}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <Button title="Close" variant="plain" onPress={() => router.back()} />
      </FormScreen>
    );
  }

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <View style={styles.intro}>
        <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
          Before you look around
        </Text>
        <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
          Nostos is for people 21 and older. Enter your birthdate. We check it on your phone and don&apos;t keep it.
        </Text>
      </View>
      <FieldGroup errors={[message]}>
        <Field
          label="Birthdate"
          placeholder="MM/DD/YYYY"
          value={birthdate}
          onChangeText={(v) => {
            setBirthdate(formatBirthdateInput(v));
            setMessage(null);
            setUnderage(false);
          }}
          invalid={!!message}
          keyboardType="number-pad"
          autoComplete="birthdate-full"
          maxLength={10}
          returnKeyType="done"
          onSubmitEditing={submit}
          testID="age-gate-birthdate"
        />
      </FieldGroup>
      <Button title="Continue" onPress={submit} disabled={birthdate.length < 10} />
      <Button title="Cancel" variant="plain" onPress={() => router.back()} />
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({ intro: { gap: space.xs }, turnedAway: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.lg, paddingVertical: space.xxxl } });


===== src/app/connect.tsx =====

import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { BrandMark } from "@/components/brand/brand-mark";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/**
 * Signed in, but the account hasn't loaded yet (just signed in, or offline at launch). The app
 * waits here so the Terms check can't be skipped.
 */
export default function Connect() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const me = useMe();
  const { signOut } = useAuth();
  const failed = me.isError || (me.isPending && me.fetchStatus === "paused");

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { paddingTop: insets.top + space.md, paddingBottom: insets.bottom + space.md }]}>
      <BrandMark />
      <View style={styles.center}>
        {failed ? (
          <InlineError
            message={me.isError ? errorText(me.error, "Couldn't load your account. Try again.") : "You're offline. Connect to the internet to continue."}
            onRetry={() => void me.refetch()}
            retrying={me.isFetching}
          />
        ) : (
          <ActivityIndicator color={colors.textSecondary} accessibilityLabel="Loading your account" />
        )}
      </View>
      {failed && <Button title="Sign out" variant="plain" onPress={() => void signOut()} />}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  center: { flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" },
});


===== src/app/design/[section].tsx =====

import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Stack, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { ScrollView, StyleSheet } from "react-native";

import { createQueryClient } from "@/api/query-client";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { DesignGuard } from "@/features/gallery/DesignGuard";
import { GALLERY_SECTIONS } from "@/features/gallery/sections";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/** One section of the Design gallery. */
export default function DesignSection() {
  const { section } = useLocalSearchParams<{ section: string }>();
  const found = GALLERY_SECTIONS.find((s) => s.id === section);
  // Some pieces (the visa spread) use the data layer; give them a private client that never talks to anything.
  const [client] = useState(createQueryClient);
  return (
    <DesignGuard>
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: found?.title ?? "Design gallery" }} />
      <ScrollView style={styles.root} contentContainerStyle={styles.content} contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic">
        {found ? (
          <QueryClientProvider client={client}>
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              {found.note}
            </Text>
            <found.Component />
          </QueryClientProvider>
        ) : (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            There is no section called {section}.
          </Text>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </DesignGuard>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { padding: screenPadding, gap: space.lg, paddingBottom: space.xxxl },
});

===== src/app/sign-in.tsx =====

import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { TextInput } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { FormHeader } from "@/components/brand/form-header";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { isEmail } from "@/lib/validation";

export default function SignIn() {
  const { signIn } = useAuth();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [emailError, setEmailError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const passwordRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);

  const login = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.auth.login({ email: email.trim(), password }),
    onSuccess: (res) => signIn(res),
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const canSubmit = email.trim().length > 0 && password.length > 0;
  const submit = () => {
    if (!canSubmit || login.isPending) return;
    if (!isEmail(email)) {
      haptics.warning();
      setEmailError("Enter a valid email address.");
      return;
    }
    login.mutate();
  };

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <FormHeader title="Welcome back" line="Your passport is where you left it." />
      <FieldGroup errors={[emailError]}>
        <Field
          label="Email"
          placeholder="name@example.com"
          value={email}
          invalid={!!emailError}
          onChangeText={(v) => {
            setEmail(v);
            setEmailError(null);
          }}
          autoCapitalize="none"
          autoComplete="email"
          textContentType="username"
          keyboardType="email-address"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => passwordRef.current?.focus()}
          autoFocus
        />
        <Field
          ref={passwordRef}
          label="Password"
          placeholder="Required"
          value={password}
          onChangeText={setPassword}
          secureTextEntry
          autoComplete="current-password"
          textContentType="password"
          returnKeyType="go"
          onSubmitEditing={submit}
        />
      </FieldGroup>
      {login.error && <InlineError message={errorText(login.error, "Couldn't sign in. Check your email and password and try again.")} />}
      <Button title="Sign in" onPress={submit} disabled={!canSubmit} loading={login.isPending} />
      <Button title="New here? Create an account" variant="plain" onPress={() => router.replace("/sign-up")} />
    </FormScreen>
  );
}


===== src/app/sign-up.tsx =====

import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { FormHeader } from "@/components/brand/form-header";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { useAnnounce } from "@/lib/a11y";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { LEGAL, openWebPage } from "@/lib/links";
import { checkBirthdate, formatBirthdateInput } from "@/lib/age";
import { MIN_PASSWORD, validateSignup, type SignupErrors, type SignupFields } from "@/lib/validation";
import { space } from "@/theme";

export default function SignUp() {
  const { signIn } = useAuth();
  const [fields, setFields] = useState<SignupFields>({ email: "", password: "", handle: "", displayName: "", birthdate: "" });
  const [terms, setTerms] = useState(false);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<SignupErrors>({});
  const [consentError, setConsentError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const emailRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const passwordRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const handleRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const displayNameRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const birthdateRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  useAnnounce(consentError);

  const set = (k: keyof SignupFields) => (v: string) => {
    setFields((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: k === "handle" ? v.toLowerCase() : k === "birthdate" ? formatBirthdateInput(v) : v }));
    if (errors[k]) setErrors((e) => ({ ...e, [k]: undefined }));
  };

  const signup = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      api.auth.signup({
        email: fields.email.trim(),
        password: fields.password,
        handle: fields.handle.trim().toLowerCase(),
        displayName: fields.displayName.trim(),
        acceptTerms: true,
        // Checked on the phone first, and again by the server, which never stores it. An under-21 date never gets this far.
        birthdate: checkBirthdate(fields.birthdate).ok ? (checkBirthdate(fields.birthdate) as { apiDate: string }).apiDate : "",
      }),
    onSuccess: (res) => signIn(res),
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const submit = () => {
    if (signup.isPending) return;
    const e = validateSignup(fields);
    setErrors(e);
    const consent = !terms ? "Agree to the Terms and Privacy policy to continue." : null;
    setConsentError(consent);
    if (e.email) emailRef.current?.focus();
    else if (e.password) passwordRef.current?.focus();
    else if (e.handle) handleRef.current?.focus();
    else if (e.displayName) displayNameRef.current?.focus();
    else if (e.birthdate) birthdateRef.current?.focus();
    if (Object.keys(e).length || consent) {
      haptics.warning();
      return;
    }
    signup.mutate();
  };

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <FormHeader title="Get your passport" line="For adults 21 and over. Only you can see it." />
      <FieldGroup errors={[errors.email, errors.password]} footer={`Passwords need at least ${MIN_PASSWORD} characters.`}>
        <Field
          ref={emailRef}
          label="Email"
          placeholder="name@example.com"
          value={fields.email}
          onChangeText={set("email")}
          invalid={!!errors.email}
          autoCapitalize="none"
          autoComplete="email"
          textContentType="username"
          keyboardType="email-address"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => passwordRef.current?.focus()}
        />
        <Field
          ref={passwordRef}
          label="Password"
          placeholder={`${MIN_PASSWORD}+ characters`}
          value={fields.password}
          onChangeText={set("password")}
          invalid={!!errors.password}
          secureTextEntry
          autoComplete="new-password"
          textContentType="newPassword"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => handleRef.current?.focus()}
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      <FieldGroup errors={[errors.handle, errors.displayName]} footer="Your handle is shown on your stamps.">
        <Field
          ref={handleRef}
          label="Handle"
          placeholder="lowercase_name"
          value={fields.handle}
          onChangeText={set("handle")}
          invalid={!!errors.handle}
          autoCapitalize="none"
          autoCorrect={false}
          textContentType="none"
          autoComplete="off"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => displayNameRef.current?.focus()}
        />
        <Field
          ref={displayNameRef}
          label="Name"
          placeholder="Shown on your profile"
          value={fields.displayName}
          onChangeText={set("displayName")}
          invalid={!!errors.displayName}
          autoComplete="name"
          textContentType="name"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => birthdateRef.current?.focus()}
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      <FieldGroup errors={[errors.birthdate]} footer="Nostos is for people 21 and older. We check your birthdate once and don't keep it.">
        <Field
          ref={birthdateRef}
          label="Birthdate"
          placeholder="MM/DD/YYYY"
          value={fields.birthdate}
          onChangeText={set("birthdate")}
          invalid={!!errors.birthdate}
          keyboardType="number-pad"
          autoComplete="birthdate-full"
          textContentType="none"
          maxLength={10}
          returnKeyType="done"
          testID="signup-birthdate"
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      <View style={styles.consents}>
        <Checkbox checked={terms} onChange={setTerms} invalid={!!consentError && !terms} accessibilityLabel="I agree to the Terms and Privacy policy">
          <Text variant="body">I agree to the Terms and Privacy policy</Text>
        </Checkbox>
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
          There&apos;s no tolerance for abusive content or users. Anyone can report a stamp or an account.
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.links}>
          <Button title="Terms" variant="plain" compact block={false} onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.terms)} accessibilityRole="link" />
          <Button title="Privacy policy" variant="plain" compact block={false} onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.privacy)} accessibilityRole="link" />
        </View>
        {consentError && (
          <Text variant="footnote" color="danger">
            {consentError}
          </Text>
        )}
      </View>

      {signup.error && <InlineError message={errorText(signup.error, "Couldn't create your account. Check the details and try again.")} />}
      <Button title="Create account" onPress={submit} loading={signup.isPending} />
      <Button title="Have an account? Sign in" variant="plain" onPress={() => router.replace("/sign-in")} />
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  consents: { gap: space.xs },
  links: { flexDirection: "row", marginLeft: -space.md },
});


===== src/app/terms-update.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { qk, useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { LEGAL, openWebPage } from "@/lib/links";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/** Shown when the account accepted an older Terms version. */
export default function TermsUpdate() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { signOut } = useAuth();
  const me = useMe();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const version = me.data?.terms.currentVersion;

  const accept = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.me.acceptTerms(version ?? ""),
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.me }),
  });

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { paddingTop: insets.top + space.xxxl, paddingBottom: insets.bottom + space.md }]}>
      <View style={styles.body}>
        <Text variant="title" accessibilityRole="header">
          Updated terms
        </Text>
        <Text color="textSecondary">We&apos;ve updated our Terms and Privacy policy. Review them to keep using Nostos.</Text>
        <View style={styles.links}>
          <Button title="Read the Terms" variant="plain" block={false} compact onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.terms)} />
          <Button title="Read the Privacy policy" variant="plain" block={false} compact onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.privacy)} />
        </View>
      </View>
      <View style={styles.actions}>
        {accept.error && <InlineError message={errorText(accept.error, "Couldn't save that. Try again.")} />}
        <Button title="Agree and continue" onPress={() => accept.mutate()} loading={accept.isPending} disabled={!version} />
        <Button title="Sign out" variant="plain" onPress={() => void signOut()} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, justifyContent: "space-between" },
  body: { gap: space.sm },
  links: { marginTop: space.xs, marginLeft: -space.md },
  actions: { gap: space.xs },
});


===== src/app/welcome.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, useWindowDimensions, View } from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { farewellNotice } from "@/auth/auth-context";

export default function Welcome() {

            </Text>

          Go somewhere. Get a stamp.
        </Text>
        <Text variant="body" color="textSecondary">
          Your passport for New York: a photo, a few words or a song from every place you go. Only you can see it.
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.actions}>
          <Button title="Create account" onPress={() => router.push("/sign-up")} />

        </View>
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
          For adults 21 and over.
        </Text>
        {__DEV__ ? <Button title="Design gallery (development)" variant="plain" compact block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/design")} /> : null}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

===== src/features/account/__tests__/account-gate.test.tsx =====

/** "Sign in to post" must never show while the account is still loading or failed to load. */
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import { accountState } from "@/auth/account-state";
import { AccountGate } from "../account-gate";

let mockState: ReturnType<typeof import("@/auth/account-state").useAccountState>;
const retry = jest.fn();
jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/auth/account-state", () => ({ ...jest.requireActual("@/auth/account-state"), useAccountState: () => mockState }));

describe("accountState", () => {
  const me = (isSuccess: boolean, isError: boolean) => ({ isSuccess, isError });
  it("is checking while auth or /api/me is loading, never 'guest'", () => {
    expect(accountState("loading", me(false, false))).toBe("checking");
    expect(accountState("signedIn", me(false, false))).toBe("checking");
  });
  it("is an error when signed in but /api/me failed", () => {
    expect(accountState("signedIn", me(false, true))).toBe("error");
  });
  it("is a guest only when there is no account, and ready once /api/me loaded", () => {
    expect(accountState("signedOut", me(false, false))).toBe("guest");
    expect(accountState("guest", me(false, false))).toBe("guest");
    expect(accountState("signedIn", me(true, false))).toBe("ready");
  });
});

describe("AccountGate", () => {
  const render_ = async () => render(<AccountGate title="Stamp a place" message="Sign in to stamp the places you go."><></></AccountGate>);

  it("checking: says so, and offers no sign-in", async () => {
    mockState = { state: "checking", retry, retrying: false };
    await render_();
    expect(screen.getByText("Checking your account")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Sign in")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Sign in to/)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("error: offers Try again, not Sign in", async () => {
    mockState = { state: "error", retry, retrying: false };
    await render_();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Couldn't check your account/)).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Try again"));
    expect(retry).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Sign in")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("guest: the sign-in prompt", async () => {
    mockState = { state: "guest", retry, retrying: false };
    await render_();
    expect(screen.getByText("Stamp a place")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Sign in")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("ready: shows the screen", async () => {
    mockState = { state: "ready", retry, retrying: false };
    await render(<AccountGate title="t" message="m"><></></AccountGate>);
    expect(screen.queryByText("Checking your account")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Sign in")).toBeNull();
  });
});


===== src/features/account/__tests__/age-gate-screens.test.tsx =====

/** The 21+ gate: the sign-up form and the guest gate turn away under-21s and keep nothing. */
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";

import { testQueryClient } from "@/test/query-client";
import AgeGate from "@/app/age-gate";
import SignUp from "@/app/sign-up";

const mockBrowse = jest.fn();
const mockSignup = jest.fn();
const mockSignIn = jest.fn();

jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn(), back: jest.fn(), replace: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/auth/auth-context", () => ({ useAuth: () => ({ browseAsGuest: mockBrowse, signIn: mockSignIn }) }));
jest.mock("@/api", () => {
  const client = jest.requireActual("@/api/client");
  return { api: { auth: { signup: (b: unknown) => mockSignup(b) } }, ApiError: client.ApiError, errorText: client.errorText };
});

const wrap = async (ui: React.ReactElement) => render(<QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}>{ui}</QueryClientProvider>);
const yearsAgo = (years: number, dayOffset = 0) => {
  const d = new Date();
  d.setFullYear(d.getFullYear() - years);
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + dayOffset);
  const mm = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${mm}${dd}${d.getFullYear()}`;
};

beforeEach(() => {
  mockBrowse.mockClear();
  mockSignup.mockReset();
  mockSignIn.mockClear();
  jest.mocked(SecureStore.setItemAsync).mockClear();
});

describe("guest age gate", () => {
  it("lets in someone who is 21 today, and formats the date as they type", async () => {
    await wrap(<AgeGate />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("age-gate-birthdate"), yearsAgo(21));
    expect(screen.getByTestId("age-gate-birthdate").props.value).toMatch(/^\d{2}\/\d{2}\/\d{4}$/);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Continue"));
    expect(mockBrowse).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("turns away someone a day short of 21: one sentence, no way forward, nothing saved", async () => {
    await wrap(<AgeGate />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("age-gate-birthdate"), yearsAgo(21, 1));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Continue"));
    expect(mockBrowse).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.getByText("Nostos is for people 21 and older.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Continue")).toBeNull();
    expect(SecureStore.setItemAsync).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("asks again for an impossible date instead of guessing", async () => {
    await wrap(<AgeGate />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("age-gate-birthdate"), "02301990");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Continue"));
    expect(mockBrowse).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.getByText("Enter your birthdate as MM/DD/YYYY.")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe("sign-up age gate", () => {
  async function fill(birthdate: string) {
    await wrap(<SignUp />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText("Email"), "sam@example.com");
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText("Password"), "long enough pw");
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText("Handle"), "sam_s");
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText("Name"), "Sam");
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("signup-birthdate"), birthdate);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("I agree to the Terms and Privacy policy"));
  }

  it("has a birthdate field in place of the old 21+ checkbox", async () => {
    await wrap(<SignUp />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId("signup-birthdate")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText("I'm 21 or older")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("sends the birthdate to the server once, as YYYY-MM-DD, when the person is 21 or older", async () => {
    mockSignup.mockResolvedValue({ token: "t", expiresAt: "2027-01-01T00:00:00.000Z", user: { id: "u", handle: "sam_s", displayName: "Sam", role: "USER" } });
    await fill("05051990");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Create account"));
    expect(mockSignup).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(mockSignup.mock.calls[0][0]).toMatchObject({ email: "sam@example.com", birthdate: "1990-05-05", acceptTerms: true });
    expect(mockSignup.mock.calls[0][0]).not.toHaveProperty("confirm21");
  });

  it("never sends an under-21 birthdate: it stops on the phone and says why", async () => {
    await fill(yearsAgo(20));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Create account"));
    expect(mockSignup).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.getAllByText("Nostos is for people 21 and older.").length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});


===== src/features/account/__tests__/customize.test.tsx =====

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import Customize from "@/app/(app)/customize";

const mockBack = jest.fn();
jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { back: () => mockBack() } }));

describe("Customize teaser", () => {
  it("is a quiet note with a lock: nothing to buy, nothing to set up, and a way out", async () => {
    await render(<Customize />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Customize")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Nothing to set up yet, and nothing to buy/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/\$|price|buy now|subscribe|purchase|upgrade/i)).toBeNull();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Close"));
    expect(mockBack).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

/** The restyled Settings and Welcome screens: what is there in personal mode. */
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
