===== src/features/account/__tests__/tabs.test.tsx =====

/** With the social layer off the bar is exactly Passport, Stamp and Map. No feed, no bell. */
import { render } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import type { ReactElement } from "react";

import TabsLayout from "@/app/(app)/(tabs)/_layout";

type ScreenProps = { name: string; options?: { href?: null; title?: string } };
const mockScreens: ScreenProps[] = [];
let mockInitial = "";
let mockFlag = false;

jest.mock("@/config", () => ({ get SOCIAL_ENABLED() { return mockFlag; } }));
jest.mock("@/auth/auth-context", () => ({ useAuth: () => ({ status: "signedIn" }) }));
jest.mock("expo-router", () => {
  const Tabs = function MockTabs({ children, initialRouteName }: { children: ReactElement[]; initialRouteName: string }) {
    mockInitial = initialRouteName;
    return <>{children}</>;
  };
  Tabs.Screen = function MockScreen(props: ScreenProps) {
    mockScreens.push(props);
    return null;
  };
  return { Tabs, router: { push: jest.fn() } };
});

const visible = () => mockScreens.filter((s) => s.options?.href !== null).map((s) => s.name);

describe("tabs", () => {
  beforeEach(() => (mockScreens.length = 0));

  it("social off: exactly Passport, Stamp and Map, with Passport as home", async () => {
    mockFlag = false;
    await render(<TabsLayout />);
    expect(visible()).toEqual(["passport", "stamp", "map"]);
    expect(mockInitial).toBe("passport");
    expect(visible()).not.toContain("index");
    expect(visible()).not.toContain("activity");
  });

  it("social on: the feed and the bell come back", async () => {
    mockFlag = true;
    await render(<TabsLayout />);
    expect(visible()).toEqual(["index", "passport", "stamp", "map", "activity"]);
  });
});


===== src/features/account/account-gate.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { useAccountState } from "@/auth/account-state";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { colors, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/**
 * Shows `children` once the account is ready. While the account is still loading it says so ("Checking your account"),
 * when /api/me failed it offers Try again, and only a real guest gets "Sign in". Screens that need an account use this
 * so a slow or failed load never reads as "you're signed out".
 */
export function AccountGate({ title, message, children }: { title: string; message: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { state, retry, retrying } = useAccountState();
  if (state === "ready") return <>{children}</>;
  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      {state === "checking" && (
        <View style={styles.block} accessibilityRole="progressbar" accessibilityLabel="Checking your account">
          <Skeleton width={size.avatarLarge} height={size.avatarLarge} round />
          <Text variant="headline">Checking your account</Text>
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            One moment.
          </Text>
        </View>
      )}
      {state === "error" && (
        <View style={styles.block}>
          <InlineError message="Couldn't check your account. Your connection may be down." onRetry={retry} retrying={retrying} />
        </View>
      )}
      {state === "guest" && (
        <View style={styles.block}>
          <Icon name="person.crop.circle" color="textSecondary" size={size.iconLarge} />
          <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
            {title}
          </Text>
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary" style={styles.center}>
            {message}
          </Text>
          <Button title="Sign in" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/sign-in")} />
          <Button title="Create account" variant="plain" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/sign-up")} />
        </View>
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, justifyContent: "center", paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  block: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.sm },
  center: { textAlign: "center" },
});


===== src/features/book/BearerPage.tsx =====

import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { bookStyle, colors, space } from "@/theme";
import { type Bearer, bearerFields, machineZone } from "./bearer";
import { PageStock } from "./PageStock";

/** The first page: the holder's photo and details, and the machine-readable zone along the bottom. Yours alone to see. */
export function BearerPage({ width, height, bearer, avatarUrl }: { width: number; height: number; bearer: Bearer; avatarUrl: string | null }) {
  const fields = bearerFields(bearer);
  const zone = machineZone(bearer);
  const pad = Math.round(width * 0.09);
  const photo = Math.round(width * 0.3);
  return (
    <PageStock width={width} height={height} spine="left">
      <View style={[styles.body, { padding: pad }]} accessible accessibilityLabel={`Passport of ${bearer.displayName}, at ${bearer.handle}. ${fields.map((f) => `${f.label.toLowerCase()}: ${f.value}`).join(". ")}`}>
        <Text variant="serifLabel" color="brass" align="center">
          NOSTOS PASSPORT
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.row}>
          <View style={[styles.photo, { width: photo, height: Math.round(photo * 1.25) }]}>
            <Avatar name={bearer.displayName} uri={avatarUrl} size={photo - 6} variant="title" />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.fields}>
            {fields.map((f) => (
              <View key={f.label}>
                <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass">
                  {f.label}
                </Text>
                <Text variant="typewriter" color="textPrimary" numberOfLines={f.label === "REMARKS" ? 3 : 1}>
                  {f.value}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        </View>
        <View style={styles.zone} accessibilityElementsHidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
          {zone.map((line, i) => (
            <Text key={i} variant="typewriterSmall" color="boneFaint" numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit style={styles.mono}>
              {line}
            </Text>
          ))}
        </View>
      </View>
    </PageStock>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

  fields: { flex: 1, gap: space.sm },
  zone: { gap: space.xxs },
  mono: { letterSpacing: 0 },
});


===== src/features/book/Cover.tsx =====

import { CormorantGaramond_600SemiBold } from "@expo-google-fonts/cormorant-garamond";
import { CourierPrime_400Regular } from "@expo-google-fonts/courier-prime";
import { Canvas, Fill, Group, LinearGradient, Path, RadialGradient, RoundedRect, Shader, Skia, Text as SkiaText, useFont, vec } from "@shopify/react-native-skia";
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { GestureDetector } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import { useDerivedValue } from "react-native-reanimated";

import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { bookStyle, colors, shadows, space } from "@/theme";
import { emblem } from "./emblem";
import { trackedOffsets } from "./foil";
import { useFoilSweep } from "./use-foil-sweep";

export type CoverCounts = { stamps: number; places: number; neighborhoods: number };

/** Leather: pebbled grain from a small shader. The two colors come from the theme, so the grain is black and ink, nothing else. */
const LEATHER = Skia.RuntimeEffect.Make(`
uniform half4 dark;
uniform half4 light;
float h(float2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, float2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
half4 main(float2 xy) {
  float2 c = floor(xy / 2.2);
  float n = h(c) * 0.6 + h(floor(xy / 5.0)) * 0.4;
  float cell = smoothstep(0.30, 0.78, n);
  float t = cell * 0.8 + h(c + 7.0) * 0.2;
  return mix(dark, light, half(t));
}`);

const WORDMARK = "NOSTOS";
const SUBTITLE = "PASSPORT";

/**
 * The cover: black leather with brass foil. The wordmark and the emblem are one foil layer whose highlight sweeps with a
 * drag, with device tilt where the phone has it, and in a slow idle shimmer. Tapping opens the book.
 */
export function Cover({ width, height, counts, onOpen, name, showCounts = true }: { width: number; height: number; counts: CoverCounts; onOpen?: () => void; name?: string; showCounts?: boolean }) {
  const { sweep, gesture } = useFoilSweep(width);
  const titleFont = useFont(CormorantGaramond_600SemiBold, Math.round(width * 0.135));
  const subFont = useFont(CourierPrime_400Regular, Math.round(width * 0.036));
  const start = useDerivedValue(() => vec(width * (sweep.value - 0.45), height * 0.15));
  const end = useDerivedValue(() => vec(width * (sweep.value + 0.45), height * 0.55));

  const e = useMemo(() => emblem(), []);
  const paths = useMemo(
    () => ({ rings: e.rings.map((d) => Skia.Path.MakeFromSVGString(d)), ticks: Skia.Path.MakeFromSVGString(e.ticks), star: Skia.Path.MakeFromSVGString(e.star), door: Skia.Path.MakeFromSVGString(e.door) }),
    [e],
  );
  const cx = width / 2;
  const emblemR = width * 0.25;
  const emblemY = height * 0.4;
  const inset = width * 0.05;

  // Wordmark letters, tracked.
  const letters = useMemo(() => {
    if (!titleFont) return null;
    const widths = WORDMARK.split("").map((ch) => titleFont.measureText(ch).width);
    const { xs, total } = trackedOffsets(widths, width * 0.022);
    return { xs, total };
  }, [titleFont, width]);
  const sub = useMemo(() => {
    if (!subFont) return null;
    const widths = SUBTITLE.split("").map((ch) => subFont.measureText(ch).width);
    return trackedOffsets(widths, width * 0.03);
  }, [subFont, width]);

  const label = `Open your passport${name ? `, ${name}` : ""}. ${counts.stamps} stamps, ${counts.places} places, ${counts.neighborhoods} neighborhoods.`;

  return (
    <View style={styles.wrap}>
      <GestureDetector gesture={gesture}>
        <Pressable onPress={onOpen} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={label} accessibilityHint="Opens the book" style={[{ width, height }, styles.book]}>
          <Canvas style={{ width, height }}>
            {/* Leather */}
            <RoundedRect x={0} y={0} width={width} height={height} r={width * 0.045}>
              {LEATHER ? <Shader source={LEATHER} uniforms={{ dark: Skia.Color(colors.black), light: Skia.Color(colors.inkRaised) }} /> : null}
            </RoundedRect>
            <RoundedRect x={0} y={0} width={width} height={height} r={width * 0.045}>
              <LinearGradient start={vec(0, 0)} end={vec(width, height)} colors={[colors.leatherSheen, colors.transparent, colors.scrim]} />
            </RoundedRect>
            {/* Vignette */}
            <Fill>
              <RadialGradient c={vec(cx, height * 0.45)} r={height * 0.8} colors={[colors.transparent, colors.scrim]} />
            </Fill>
            {/* Inset brass border: a thick line and a hairline, like blind-stamped foil */}
            <RoundedRect x={inset} y={inset} width={width - inset * 2} height={height - inset * 2} r={width * 0.03} style="stroke" strokeWidth={bookStyle.borderThick}>
              <LinearGradient start={start} end={end} colors={[colors.dimBrass, colors.brass, colors.foilHighlight, colors.brass, colors.dimBrass]} positions={[0, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1]} />
            </RoundedRect>
            <RoundedRect x={inset * 1.5} y={inset * 1.5} width={width - inset * 3} height={height - inset * 3} r={width * 0.022} style="stroke" strokeWidth={bookStyle.borderHair} color={colors.dimBrass} />
            {/* Foil: emblem and wordmark share one moving highlight */}
            <Group transform={[{ translateX: cx }, { translateY: emblemY }, { scale: emblemR }]}>
              {paths.rings.map((p, i) => (p ? <Path key={i} path={p} style="stroke" strokeWidth={i === 0 ? bookStyle.emblemRing : bookStyle.emblemInner}><FoilGradient start={start} end={end} width={width} /></Path> : null))}
              {paths.ticks ? <Path path={paths.ticks} style="stroke" strokeWidth={bookStyle.emblemTick}><FoilGradient start={start} end={end} width={width} /></Path> : null}
              {paths.star ? <Path path={paths.star} style="fill"><FoilGradient start={start} end={end} width={width} /></Path> : null}
              {paths.door ? <Path path={paths.door} style="fill" color={colors.black} /> : null}
            </Group>
            {titleFont && letters
              ? WORDMARK.split("").map((ch, i) => (
                  <SkiaText key={i} x={cx - letters.total / 2 + letters.xs[i]!} y={height * 0.74} text={ch} font={titleFont}>
                    <FoilGradient start={start} end={end} width={width} />
                  </SkiaText>
                ))
              : null}
            {subFont && sub
              ? SUBTITLE.split("").map((ch, i) => (
                  <SkiaText key={i} x={cx - sub.total / 2 + sub.xs[i]!} y={height * 0.81} text={ch} font={subFont} color={colors.dimBrass} />
                ))
              : null}
          </Canvas>
        </Pressable>
      </GestureDetector>
      {showCounts ? <Counts counts={counts} /> : null}
    </View>
  );
}

function FoilGradient({ start, end }: { start: ReturnType<typeof useDerivedValue<ReturnType<typeof vec>>>; end: ReturnType<typeof useDerivedValue<ReturnType<typeof vec>>>; width: number }) {
  return <LinearGradient start={start} end={end} colors={[colors.dimBrass, colors.brass, colors.foilHighlight, colors.brass, colors.dimBrass]} positions={[0, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1]} />;
}

/** Three brass numbers under the book: Stamps, Places, Neighborhoods. */
export function Counts({ counts }: { counts: CoverCounts }) {
  const items: [string, number][] = [["STAMPS", counts.stamps], ["PLACES", counts.places], ["NEIGHBORHOODS", counts.neighborhoods]];
  return (
    <View style={styles.counts} accessible accessibilityLabel={`${counts.stamps} stamps, ${counts.places} places, ${counts.neighborhoods} neighborhoods`}>
      {items.map(([label, n]) => (
        <View key={label} style={styles.count}>
          <Text variant="title" color="brass" tabular accessibilityElementsHidden>
            {n}
          </Text>
          <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass" accessibilityElementsHidden>
            {label}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ))}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.xl },
  book: { borderRadius: space.md, ...shadows.book },
  counts: { flexDirection: "row", gap: space.xl },
  count: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.xxs },
});


===== src/features/book/FirstRunHint.tsx =====

import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

import { bookStyle, colors, radius, space } from "@/theme";
import { FIRST_RUN_COPY } from "./first-run";

        {FIRST_RUN_COPY.title}

===== src/features/book/FlipBook.tsx =====

/* eslint-disable react-hooks/immutability -- Reanimated shared values are written through .value by design */
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { Gesture, GestureDetector } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import Animated, { type SharedValue, useAnimatedStyle, useReducedMotion, useSharedValue, withSpring, withTiming } from "react-native-reanimated";
import { scheduleOnRN } from "react-native-worklets";

import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { bookMotion, colors } from "@/theme";
import { DRAG_ZONE, FLICK, TAP_ZONE, castShade, dragPos, flipAngle, flipShade, settleTarget, showsBack, stepTarget, turnProgress, zoneAt } from "./flip-math";
import { PERSPECTIVE } from "./open-math";
import { type PageFace, spreadAt } from "./spreads";
import { Spread } from "./Spread";

type Props = {
  pageWidth: number;
  pageHeight: number;
  /** How many spreads the book has. */
  total: number;
  /** The spread showing, 0-based. The book follows this when it changes (a jump from the index, say). */
  spread: number;
  onSpreadChange: (index: number) => void;
  /** Turning back from the first spread closes the book. */
  onCloseBook?: () => void;
  renderFace: (face: PageFace, side: "left" | "right") => ReactNode;
};

const faceOf = (spread: number, total: number, side: "left" | "right"): PageFace | null =>
  spread < 0 || spread >= total ? null : spreadAt(spread)[side];

/**
 * The open book. It keeps one number, the position between spreads (see flip-math.ts), and draws every page from it:
 * two turning pages (one lying on the left, one on the right, each ready to turn) over the pages they reveal. A finger on
 * an outer edge drags the page, a tap on the edge turns it, and a release springs to the nearest spread. With Reduce
 * Motion on, pages crossfade instead of turning.
 */
export function FlipBook({ pageWidth: pw, pageHeight: ph, total, spread, onSpreadChange, onCloseBook, renderFace }: Props) {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const pos = useSharedValue(spread);
  const from = useSharedValue(spread);
  const startPos = useSharedValue(spread);
  const startX = useSharedValue(0);
  const zone = useSharedValue(0); // 1 = forward edge, -1 = back edge, 0 = not from an edge

  const commit = (target: number, previous: number) => {
    if (target === previous) return;
    haptics.select();
    onSpreadChange(target);
  };
  const closeBook = () => onCloseBook?.();

  // A change from outside (a jump) moves the book there.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (Math.abs(pos.value - spread) < 0.001) return;
    pos.value = reduced ? withTiming(spread, { duration: bookMotion.durations.crossfade }) : withSpring(spread, bookMotion.pageFlip);
  }, [spread, reduced, pos]);

  const settle = (target: number, origin: number, velocity: number) => {
    "worklet";
    const done = (finished?: boolean) => {
      "worklet";
      if (finished) scheduleOnRN(commit, target, origin);
    };
    pos.value = reduced ? withTiming(target, { duration: bookMotion.durations.crossfade }, done) : withSpring(target, { ...bookMotion.pageFlip, velocity: -velocity / (pw * 1.7) }, done);
  };

  const pan = Gesture.Pan()
    .withTestId("flip-pan")
    .activeOffsetX([-8, 8])
    .onBegin((e) => {
      const z = zoneAt(e.x, pw, DRAG_ZONE);
      zone.value = z === "next" ? 1 : z === "prev" ? -1 : 0;
      startX.value = e.x;
      from.value = Math.round(pos.value);
      startPos.value = pos.value;
    })
    .onUpdate((e) => {
      if (zone.value === 0) return;
      if (zone.value === -1 && from.value === 0) return; // the cover is closed by letting go, not by dragging it
      pos.value = dragPos(startPos.value, startX.value, e.x, pw, zone.value === 1 ? "next" : "prev", total);
    })
    .onEnd((e) => {
      if (zone.value === 0) return;
      if (zone.value === -1 && from.value === 0) {
        if (e.translationX > pw * 0.3 || e.velocityX > FLICK) scheduleOnRN(closeBook);
        return;
      }
      settle(settleTarget(pos.value, from.value, e.velocityX, pw, total), from.value, e.velocityX);
    });

  const tap = Gesture.Tap().withTestId("flip-tap").onEnd((e, ok) => {
    if (!ok) return;
    const z = zoneAt(e.x, pw, TAP_ZONE);
    if (z === null) return;
    const origin = Math.round(pos.value);
    if (z === "prev" && origin === 0) {
      scheduleOnRN(closeBook);
      return;
    }
    const target = stepTarget(origin, z === "next" ? 1 : -1, total);
    if (target !== origin) settle(target, origin, 0);
  });

  const s = Math.round(spread);
  const under = (index: number, side: "left" | "right") => {
    const face = faceOf(index, total, side);
    return face ? renderFace(face, side) : null;
  };
  return (
    <GestureDetector gesture={Gesture.Exclusive(pan, tap)}>
      <View collapsable={false}>
        <Spread pageWidth={pw} pageHeight={ph} spreadIndex={s} spreadTotal={total}>
          {/* The pages that lie underneath, revealed as the turning pages go over. */}
          <View style={[styles.half, { width: pw, height: ph }]}>{under(s - 1, "left")}</View>
          <View style={[styles.half, { left: pw, width: pw, height: ph }]}>{under(s + 1, "right")}</View>
          {/* Turn s-1 (lying on the left) then turn s (lying on the right): the later one is on top. */}
          {[s - 1, s].map((k) => (k >= 0 && k < total - 1 ? <TurningPage key={k} k={k} pos={pos} pw={pw} ph={ph} reduced={reduced} front={renderFace(spreadAt(k).right, "right")} back={renderFace(spreadAt(k + 1).left, "left")} /> : null))}
        </Spread>
      </View>
    </GestureDetector>
  );
}

/** One page in flight between spread k and k+1: its front is the right page of k, its back the left page of k+1. */
function TurningPage({ k, pos, pw, ph, reduced, front, back }: { k: number; pos: SharedValue<number>; pw: number; ph: number; reduced: boolean; front: ReactNode; back: ReactNode }) {
  const frontStyle = useAnimatedStyle(() => {
    const p = turnProgress(pos.value, k);
    if (reduced) return { opacity: 1 - p, transform: [] };
    return { opacity: showsBack(p) ? 0 : 1, transform: [{ perspective: PERSPECTIVE }, { rotateY: `${flipAngle(p)}deg` }] };
  });
  const backStyle = useAnimatedStyle(() => {
    const p = turnProgress(pos.value, k);
    if (reduced) return { opacity: p, transform: [{ translateX: -pw }] };
    // Mirror the back inside its own box first, then turn it with the page: it ends flat on the left, reading the right way round.
    return { opacity: showsBack(p) ? 1 : 0, transform: [{ perspective: PERSPECTIVE }, { rotateY: `${flipAngle(p)}deg` }, { translateX: pw }, { scaleX: -1 }] };
  });
  const shade = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ opacity: reduced ? 0 : flipShade(turnProgress(pos.value, k)) }));
  // The turning page shadows the page it lies over: the one on the right while it is lifting, the one on the left as it lands.
  const castRight = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ opacity: reduced ? 0 : turnProgress(pos.value, k) < 0.5 ? castShade(turnProgress(pos.value, k)) : 0 }));
  const castLeft = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ opacity: reduced ? 0 : turnProgress(pos.value, k) >= 0.5 ? castShade(turnProgress(pos.value, k)) : 0 }));
  const atRest = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ zIndex: turnProgress(pos.value, k) > 0 && turnProgress(pos.value, k) < 1 ? 2 : 1 }));
  const box = { left: pw, width: pw, height: ph };
  return (
    <Animated.View pointerEvents="box-none" style={[styles.half, box, atRest]}>
      <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.cast, { width: pw, height: ph }, castRight]} />
      <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.cast, { left: -pw, width: pw, height: ph }, castLeft]} />
      <Animated.View style={[styles.half, { width: pw, height: ph, transformOrigin: "left center" }, frontStyle]}>
        {front}
        <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.cast, { width: pw, height: ph }, shade]} />
      </Animated.View>
      <Animated.View style={[styles.half, { width: pw, height: ph, transformOrigin: "left center" }, backStyle]}>
        {back}
        <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.cast, { width: pw, height: ph }, shade]} />
      </Animated.View>
    </Animated.View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  half: { position: "absolute", top: 0, left: 0 },
  cast: { position: "absolute", top: 0, left: 0, backgroundColor: colors.black },
});

import { FlatList, Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";

/** The index: every neighborhood with a progress ring. Tap one you've started to turn to its first page. */
export function IndexList({ rows, onJump, onClose }: Props) {

    <View style={styles.root}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Text variant="title2" color="brass" accessibilityRole="header">

        </Text>

                <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="brass">
                  COMPLETE
                </Text>
              ) : item.page !== null ? (
                <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass">
                  p. {item.page}
                </Text>
              ) : null}
            </View>
          </Pressable>
        )}
      />
    </View>
  );

===== src/features/book/LandingScene.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import Animated, { Easing, useAnimatedStyle, useReducedMotion, useSharedValue, withTiming } from "react-native-reanimated";

import { mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import type { NeighborhoodProgress, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { colors, radius, shadows, space } from "@/theme";
import { LANDING, landingFrame } from "./landing";
import { landingCopy, photoPins } from "./landing-copy";
import { PageView } from "./PageView";
import { slotPlacement } from "./slots";
import { CompletionStamp, StampMark } from "./StampMark";

type Props = {
  stamp: StampView;
  /** The other stamps already on the page. */
  others: StampView[];
  progress: NeighborhoodProgress | null;
  pageWidth: number;
  pageHeight: number;
  onDone: () => void;
};

/**
 * A new stamp lands on its page: it slams into its slot, the ink spreads, the page shakes, there is a haptic knock and a flare of
 * brass, then the photos pin on and the page says where it went. A finished neighborhood earns the foil stamp.
 */
export function LandingScene({ stamp, others, progress, pageWidth: pw, pageHeight: ph, onDone }: Props) {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const t = useSharedValue(0);
  const page = stamp.page ?? 1;
  const slot = stamp.slot ?? 1;
  const place = slotPlacement(stamp.id, slot, pw, ph);
  const copy = landingCopy(stamp.page, progress);
  const pins = photoPins(stamp.photos.length, pw, stamp.id);

  useEffect(() => {
    t.value = withTiming(LANDING.total, { duration: LANDING.total, easing: Easing.linear });
    const knock = setTimeout(() => haptics.success(), reduced ? 0 : LANDING.impact);
    return () => clearTimeout(knock);
  }, [t, reduced]);

  const page$ = useAnimatedStyle(() => {
    const f = landingFrame(t.value, reduced);
    return { transform: [{ translateX: f.shakeX }, { translateY: f.shakeY }] };
  });
  const stamp$ = useAnimatedStyle(() => {
    const f = landingFrame(t.value, reduced);
    return { opacity: f.opacity, transform: [{ rotate: `${place.rotation}deg` }, { scale: f.scale }] };
  });
  const glow$ = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ opacity: landingFrame(t.value, reduced).glow }));
  const ink$ = useAnimatedStyle(() => {
    const f = landingFrame(t.value, reduced);
    return { opacity: reduced ? 0 : (1 - f.inkSpread) * 0.6, transform: [{ scale: 0.8 + f.inkSpread * 0.5 }] };
  });
  const pin$ = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ opacity: t.value > LANDING.impact + LANDING.ink ? 1 : 0 }));

  const spot = { position: "absolute" as const, left: place.cx - place.size / 2, top: place.cy - place.size / 2, width: place.size, height: place.size };
  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Animated.View style={[{ width: pw, height: ph }, page$]}>
        <PageView page={page} side={page % 2 === 1 ? "left" : "right"} width={pw} height={ph} stamps={others} />
        <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[spot, styles.glow, { borderRadius: place.size / 2 }, glow$]} />
        <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[spot, styles.ink, { borderRadius: place.size / 2 }, ink$]} />
        <Animated.View style={[spot, stamp$]} accessible accessibilityLabel={`New stamp, ${stamp.place.name}, page ${page}, slot ${slot}`}>
          <StampMark input={{ placeId: stamp.place.id, placeName: stamp.place.name, neighborhoodId: stamp.place.neighborhoodId, neighborhoodName: stamp.place.neighborhood }} size={place.size} />
        </Animated.View>
        <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.pins, pin$]}>
          {pins.map((p, i) => (
            <View key={i} style={[styles.photo, { left: p.cx - p.size / 2, width: p.size, height: p.size, transform: [{ rotate: `${p.rotation}deg` }] }]}>
              <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(stamp.photos[i]!.url) }} style={styles.fill} contentFit="cover" accessible={false} />
              <View style={styles.pin} />
            </View>
          ))}
        </Animated.View>
      </Animated.View>
      <View style={styles.words} accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
        <Text variant="title2" color="brass" align="center">
          {copy.added}
        </Text>
        {copy.progress ? (
          <Text variant="typewriter" color="textSecondary" align="center">
            {copy.progress}
          </Text>
        ) : null}
        {copy.completed && progress ? <CompletionStamp neighborhoodId={progress.neighborhoodId} name={progress.name} size={pw * 0.5} /> : null}
      </View>
      <Button title="Done" block={false} onPress={onDone} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", gap: space.xl, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  glow: { backgroundColor: colors.brassGlow, ...shadows.glow },
  ink: { borderWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth, borderColor: colors.brass, backgroundColor: colors.brassGlowSoft },
  pins: { position: "absolute", left: 0, right: 0, bottom: space.xl, height: space.xxxl * 2 },
  photo: { position: "absolute", top: 0, borderRadius: radius.chip / 4, overflow: "hidden", backgroundColor: colors.elevated, ...shadows.contact },
  fill: { width: "100%", height: "100%" },
  pin: { position: "absolute", top: space.xxs, alignSelf: "center", width: space.xs, height: space.xs, borderRadius: space.xxs, backgroundColor: colors.brass },
  words: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.xs, paddingHorizontal: space.lg },
});


===== src/features/book/OpeningCover.tsx =====

import { type ReactNode, useEffect } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import Animated, { useAnimatedStyle, useReducedMotion, useSharedValue, withSpring, withTiming } from "react-native-reanimated";
import { scheduleOnRN } from "react-native-worklets";

import { bookMotion, colors, shadows } from "@/theme";
import { coverAngle, pageShadow, PERSPECTIVE, showsInside, swingShade } from "./open-math";

type Props = {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  open: boolean;
  /** The cover, seen from outside. */
  outside: ReactNode;
  /** The first page, lying under the cover. */
  page: ReactNode;
  /** Start already open (to close a book that is open). Only read when the component mounts. */
  initialOpen?: boolean;
  /** Called when a swing has finished. */
  onSettled?: () => void;
};

/** Swings a cover open on its left edge. With reduced motion it crossfades instead. */
export function OpeningCover({ width, height, open, outside, page, initialOpen, onSettled }: Props) {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const progress = useSharedValue((initialOpen ?? open) ? 1 : 0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const done = (finished?: boolean) => {
      "worklet";
      if (finished && onSettled) scheduleOnRN(onSettled);
    };
    const target = open ? 1 : 0;
    progress.value = reduced ? withTiming(target, { duration: bookMotion.durations.crossfade }, done) : withSpring(target, bookMotion.coverOpen, done);
    // The callback is read once per swing; a new one is not a reason to start another.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [open, reduced, progress]);

  const swing = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({
    opacity: reduced ? 1 - progress.value : showsInside(progress.value) ? 0 : 1,
    transform: reduced ? [] : [{ perspective: PERSPECTIVE }, { rotateY: `${coverAngle(progress.value)}deg` }],
  }));
  const inside = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({
    opacity: reduced ? 0 : showsInside(progress.value) ? 1 : 0,
    transform: [{ perspective: PERSPECTIVE }, { rotateY: `${coverAngle(progress.value) + 180}deg` }, { scaleX: -1 }],
  }));
  const shade = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ opacity: swingShade(progress.value) }));
  const cast = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ opacity: pageShadow(progress.value) }));

  return (
    <View style={{ width, height }} pointerEvents={open ? "none" : "auto"}>
      <View style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, shadows.book]}>{page}</View>
      <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, styles.cast, cast]} />
      <Animated.View style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, styles.face, { transformOrigin: "left center" }, swing]}>
        {outside}
        <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, styles.shade, shade]} />
        <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.hingeShade} />
      </Animated.View>
      <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, styles.inside, { transformOrigin: "left center" }, inside]} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  face: { backfaceVisibility: "hidden" },
  shade: { backgroundColor: colors.black },
  hingeShade: { position: "absolute", left: 0, top: 0, bottom: 0, width: "6%", backgroundColor: colors.shadow },
  cast: { backgroundColor: colors.black },
  inside: { backgroundColor: colors.leather, backfaceVisibility: "hidden" },
});


===== src/features/book/PageStock.tsx =====

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient, Rect, Stop } from "react-native-svg";

import { bookStyle, colors } from "@/theme";

/**
 * The paper of one page: near-black stock with a fine inset rule. `spine` is the side the page is bound on; that edge is
 * shaded dark, like the fold of an open book. The shading belongs to the page, so a page in the middle of a turn carries it.
 */
export function PageStock({ width, height, spine, children }: { width: number; height: number; spine?: "left" | "right"; children?: ReactNode }) {
  const gutter = Math.round(width * bookStyle.gutterShare);
  return (
    <View style={[{ width, height }, styles.stock]}>
      <View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.rule, { margin: Math.round(width * 0.04) }]} />
      {children}
      {spine ? (
        <Svg width={gutter} height={height} style={[styles.fold, spine === "left" ? { left: 0 } : { right: 0 }]} pointerEvents="none">
          <Defs>
            <LinearGradient id={`fold-${spine}`} x1={spine === "left" ? "0" : "1"} y1="0" x2={spine === "left" ? "1" : "0"} y2="0">
              <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.shadow} stopOpacity={0.85} />
              <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.shadow} stopOpacity={0} />
            </LinearGradient>
          </Defs>
          <Rect x={0} y={0} width={gutter} height={height} fill={`url(#fold-${spine})`} />
        </Svg>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  stock: { backgroundColor: colors.surface, overflow: "hidden" },
  rule: { position: "absolute", top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, borderWidth: bookStyle.pageLine, borderColor: colors.hairline },
  fold: { position: "absolute", top: 0 },
});


===== src/features/book/PageView.tsx =====

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Svg, { Path } from "react-native-svg";

import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { bookStyle, colors } from "@/theme";
import { ribbonPath, ribbonsOnPage } from "./edges";
import { rosettePath, wavePaths } from "./guilloche";
import { PageStock } from "./PageStock";
import { StampMark } from "./StampMark";
import { type Placement, slotPlacement } from "./slots";

export type RenderStamp = (stamp: StampView, placement: Placement) => ReactNode;

type Props = {
  page: number;
  side: "left" | "right";
  width: number;
  height: number;
  stamps: readonly StampView[];
  pinnedIds?: readonly string[];
  renderStamp?: RenderStamp;
  onStampPress?: (stamp: StampView) => void;
};

/** A stamp is inked from its place's design (see stamp-design.ts). */
const defaultRender: RenderStamp = (stamp, p) => (
  <StampMark input={{ placeId: stamp.place.id, placeName: stamp.place.name, neighborhoodId: stamp.place.neighborhoodId, neighborhoodName: stamp.place.neighborhood }} size={p.size} />
);

/** One page of stamps: paper, guilloche, the page number, and up to three stamps in their slots. */
export function PageView({ page, side, width, height, stamps, pinnedIds = [], renderStamp = defaultRender, onStampPress }: Props) {
  const ribbons = new Set(ribbonsOnPage(stamps, pinnedIds));
  const band = Math.round(height * 0.05);
  const inset = Math.round(width * 0.06);
  const ribbonW = Math.round(width * 0.05);
  return (
    <PageStock width={width} height={height} spine={side === "left" ? "right" : "left"}>
      <Svg width={width} height={height} style={styles.fill} pointerEvents="none">
        <Path d={rosettePath(`page:${page}`, width / 2, height / 2, width * 0.42)} stroke={colors.brass} strokeOpacity={0.07} strokeWidth={bookStyle.pageLine} fill="none" />
        {wavePaths(`top:${page}`, width - inset * 2, band).map((d, i) => (
          <Path key={`t${i}`} d={d} transform={`translate(${inset} ${inset})`} stroke={colors.brass} strokeOpacity={0.14} strokeWidth={bookStyle.pageLine} fill="none" />
        ))}
      </Svg>
      {stamps.map((stamp) => {
        const p = slotPlacement(stamp.id, stamp.slot ?? 1, width, height);
        return (
          <Pressable
            key={stamp.id}
            onPress={onStampPress ? () => onStampPress(stamp) : undefined}
            accessibilityRole="button"
            accessibilityLabel={`Stamp, ${stamp.place.name}, page ${page}, slot ${stamp.slot}${ribbons.has(stamp.id) ? ", pinned" : ""}`}
            style={{ position: "absolute", left: p.cx - p.size / 2, top: p.cy - p.size / 2, width: p.size, height: p.size, transform: [{ rotate: `${p.rotation}deg` }] }}
          >
            {renderStamp(stamp, p)}
            {ribbons.has(stamp.id) ? (
              <Svg width={ribbonW} height={ribbonW * 2.6} style={{ position: "absolute", top: -ribbonW * 0.4, right: p.size * 0.08 }} pointerEvents="none">
                <Path d={ribbonPath(ribbonW, ribbonW * 2.6)} fill={colors.brass} fillOpacity={0.9} />
              </Svg>
            ) : null}
          </Pressable>
        );
      })}
      <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass" tabular style={[styles.number, { bottom: inset, [side === "left" ? "left" : "right"]: inset * 1.4 }]} accessibilityLabel={`Page ${page}`}>
        {page}
      </Text>
    </PageStock>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  fill: { position: "absolute", top: 0, left: 0 },
  number: { position: "absolute" },
});


===== src/features/book/PassportBook.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useReducer, useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, useWindowDimensions, View } from "react-native";
import Animated, { useAnimatedStyle, useReducedMotion, useSharedValue, withSpring, withTiming } from "react-native-reanimated";

import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { bookMotion, size, space } from "@/theme";
import { BearerPage } from "./BearerPage";
import { FirstRunHint } from "./FirstRunHint";
import { useFirstRun } from "./first-run";
import { type Phase, type PhaseEvent, isOpenish, nextPhase, spreadLabel, stageTransform } from "./book-state";
import { Counts, Cover } from "./Cover";
import { bookSize, openScale } from "./dimensions";
import { FlipBook } from "./FlipBook";
import { InsideCover } from "./InsideCover";
import { OpeningCover } from "./OpeningCover";
import { PageView } from "./PageView";
import { type PageFace, groupByPage, pageCountFor, spreadCountFor } from "./spreads";
import { IndexList } from "./IndexList";
import { indexRows, spreadForPage } from "./index-data";
import { usePassport } from "./use-passport";

/**
 * Your passport as a book: the cover, which opens on a tap to the bearer page, then spreads of stamps you turn with a finger
 * or a tap on the edge. Everything here is yours alone to see.
 */
export function PassportBook() {
  const data = usePassport();
  const { width: sw, height: sh } = useWindowDimensions();
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const [phase, send] = useReducer((p: Phase, e: PhaseEvent) => nextPhase(p, e), "closed" as Phase);
  const [spread, setSpread] = useState(0);
  const [showIndex, setShowIndex] = useState(false);
  const firstRun = useFirstRun();

  const book = useMemo(() => bookSize(sw, sh), [sw, sh]);
  const fit = useMemo(() => openScale(sw, book, space.md), [sw, book]);
  const open = useSharedValue(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const target = isOpenish(phase) ? 1 : 0;
    open.value = reduced ? withTiming(target, { duration: bookMotion.durations.crossfade }) : withSpring(target, bookMotion.coverOpen);
  }, [phase, reduced, open]);
  const stage = useAnimatedStyle(() => {
    const t = stageTransform(open.value, book.pageWidth, fit);
    return { transform: [{ translateX: t.translateX }, { scale: t.scale }] };
  });

  const byPage = useMemo(() => groupByPage(data.stamps), [data.stamps]);
  const rows = useMemo(() => indexRows(data.neighborhoods, data.stamps), [data.neighborhoods, data.stamps]);
  const pinnedIds = useMemo(() => data.pins.map((s) => s.id), [data.pins]);
  const pages = pageCountFor(data.counts.stamps);
  const spreads = spreadCountFor(data.counts.stamps);
  const bearer = { displayName: data.displayName, handle: data.handle, memberSince: data.memberSince, bio: data.bio };

  if (data.status === "error") {
    return (
      <View style={styles.center}>
        <InlineError message="Couldn't open your passport. Your connection may be down." onRetry={data.retry} />
      </View>
    );
  }
  if (data.status === "pending") {
    return (
      <View style={styles.center} accessible accessibilityRole="progressbar" accessibilityLabel="Opening your passport">
        <Skeleton width={book.width} height={book.height} />
      </View>
    );
  }

  const bearerFace = <BearerPage width={book.pageWidth} height={book.pageHeight} bearer={bearer} avatarUrl={data.avatarUrl} />;
  const renderFace = (face: PageFace, side: "left" | "right") => {
    if (face.kind === "inside-cover") return <InsideCover width={book.pageWidth} height={book.pageHeight} />;
    if (face.kind === "bearer") return bearerFace;
    return (
      <PageView
        page={face.page}
        side={side}
        width={book.pageWidth}
        height={book.pageHeight}
        stamps={byPage.get(face.page) ?? []}
        pinnedIds={pinnedIds}
        onStampPress={(s) => router.push({ pathname: "/stamp/[id]", params: { id: s.id } })}
      />
    );
  };

  const cover = (
    <Cover width={book.width} height={book.height} counts={data.counts} name={data.displayName} showCounts={false} onOpen={() => {
      firstRun.dismiss();
      send("tapCover");
    }} />
  );
  const go = (to: number) => setSpread(Math.max(0, Math.min(spreads - 1, to)));

  if (showIndex) {
    return (
      <IndexList
        rows={rows}
        onClose={() => setShowIndex(false)}
        onJump={(page) => {
          setSpread(Math.min(spreads - 1, spreadForPage(page)));
          setShowIndex(false);
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <View style={styles.stageWrap}>
        <Animated.View style={[{ width: book.pageWidth * 2, height: book.pageHeight }, stage]}>
          {phase === "open" ? (
            <FlipBook pageWidth={book.pageWidth} pageHeight={book.pageHeight} total={spreads} spread={spread} onSpreadChange={setSpread} onCloseBook={() => { setSpread(0); send("closeBook"); }} renderFace={renderFace} />
          ) : (
            <View style={[styles.rightHalf, { left: book.pageWidth, width: book.width, height: book.height }]}>
              <OpeningCover
                width={book.width}
                height={book.height}
                open={phase === "opening"}
                initialOpen={phase === "closing"}
                outside={cover}
                page={bearerFace}
                onSettled={() => send("coverSettled")}
              />
            </View>
          )}
        </Animated.View>
      </View>
      <View style={styles.footer}>
        {phase === "closed" ? (
          <>
            <Counts counts={data.counts} />
            {firstRun.show ? <FirstRunHint onDismiss={firstRun.dismiss} /> : null}
          </>
        ) : phase === "open" ? (
          <View style={styles.pager}>
            <Pressable onPress={() => (spread === 0 ? send("closeBook") : go(spread - 1))} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={spread === 0 ? "Close the passport" : "Previous page"} hitSlop={space.sm} style={styles.pagerButton}>
              <Icon name={spread === 0 ? "xmark" : "chevron.left"} color="accent" size={size.icon} />
            </Pressable>
            <Pressable onPress={() => setShowIndex(true)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Index" accessibilityHint="Lists your neighborhoods and jumps to their pages" hitSlop={space.sm} style={styles.indexButton}>
              <Icon name="list.bullet" color="dimBrass" size={size.iconSmall} />
              <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass" accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
                {spreadLabel(spread, pages)}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
            <Pressable onPress={() => go(spread + 1)} disabled={spread >= spreads - 1} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Next page" accessibilityState={{ disabled: spread >= spreads - 1 }} hitSlop={space.sm} style={styles.pagerButton}>
              <Icon name="chevron.right" color={spread >= spreads - 1 ? "textTertiary" : "accent"} size={size.icon} />
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        ) : null}
        {data.counts.stamps === 0 && phase === "open" ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary" align="center">
            Your first page is empty. Go to a place and stamp it.
          </Text>
        ) : null}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", gap: space.xl },
  center: { flex: 1, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: space.lg },
  stageWrap: { alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  rightHalf: { position: "absolute", top: 0 },
  footer: { minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "center", gap: space.sm },
  pager: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.xl },
  indexButton: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.xs, minHeight: size.hitTarget },
  pagerButton: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
});


===== src/features/book/PhotoViewer.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Modal, Pressable, StyleSheet, useWindowDimensions, View } from "react-native";
import { Gesture, GestureDetector } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import Animated, { useAnimatedStyle, useSharedValue, withSpring } from "react-native-reanimated";

import { mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import type { StampPhotoView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { bookMotion, colors, size, space } from "@/theme";
import { clampPan, clampScale, doubleTapScale } from "./zoom";

type Props = { photos: readonly StampPhotoView[]; index: number | null; onClose: () => void };

/** A photo full screen: pinch to zoom up to four times, drag when zoomed, double tap to zoom in and out. Several photos: step through them. */
export function PhotoViewer({ photos, index, onClose }: Props) {
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const shown = index === null ? null : (current ?? index);
  const close = () => {
    setCurrent(null);
    onClose();
  };
  return (
    <Modal visible={index !== null} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={close} statusBarTranslucent>
      <View style={styles.root}>
        {shown !== null && photos[shown] ? <ZoomableImage key={photos[shown]!.id} uri={mediaUrl(photos[shown]!.url)} /> : null}
        <Pressable onPress={close} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Close photo" hitSlop={space.sm} style={styles.close}>
          <Icon name="xmark" color="accent" size={size.icon} />
        </Pressable>
        {shown !== null && photos.length > 1 ? (
          <View style={styles.pager}>
            <Pressable onPress={() => setCurrent(Math.max(0, shown - 1))} disabled={shown === 0} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Previous photo" hitSlop={space.sm} style={styles.pagerButton}>
              <Icon name="chevron.left" color={shown === 0 ? "textTertiary" : "accent"} size={size.icon} />
            </Pressable>
            <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass" accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
              {shown + 1} of {photos.length}
            </Text>
            <Pressable onPress={() => setCurrent(Math.min(photos.length - 1, shown + 1))} disabled={shown === photos.length - 1} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Next photo" hitSlop={space.sm} style={styles.pagerButton}>
              <Icon name="chevron.right" color={shown === photos.length - 1 ? "textTertiary" : "accent"} size={size.icon} />
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        ) : null}
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function ZoomableImage({ uri }: { uri: string }) {
  const { width, height } = useWindowDimensions();
  const scale = useSharedValue(1);
  const startScale = useSharedValue(1);
  const tx = useSharedValue(0);
  const ty = useSharedValue(0);
  const startX = useSharedValue(0);
  const startY = useSharedValue(0);

  const pinch = Gesture.Pinch()
    .withTestId("viewer-pinch")
    .onBegin(() => {
      startScale.value = scale.value;
    })
    .onUpdate((e) => {
      scale.value = clampScale(startScale.value * e.scale);
    })
    .onEnd(() => {
      const p = clampPan(tx.value, ty.value, scale.value, width, height);
      tx.value = withSpring(p.tx, bookMotion.snap);
      ty.value = withSpring(p.ty, bookMotion.snap);
    });
  const pan = Gesture.Pan()
    .withTestId("viewer-pan")
    .onBegin(() => {
      startX.value = tx.value;
      startY.value = ty.value;
    })
    .onUpdate((e) => {
      const p = clampPan(startX.value + e.translationX, startY.value + e.translationY, scale.value, width, height);
      tx.value = p.tx;
      ty.value = p.ty;
    });
  const double = Gesture.Tap()
    .withTestId("viewer-double-tap")
    .numberOfTaps(2)
    .onEnd(() => {
      const next = doubleTapScale(scale.value);
      scale.value = withSpring(next, bookMotion.snap);
      if (next === 1) {
        tx.value = withSpring(0, bookMotion.snap);
        ty.value = withSpring(0, bookMotion.snap);
      }
    });
  const style = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ transform: [{ translateX: tx.value }, { translateY: ty.value }, { scale: scale.value }] }));

  return (
    <GestureDetector gesture={Gesture.Simultaneous(pinch, pan, double)}>
      <Animated.View style={[styles.fill, style]} accessible accessibilityLabel="Photo. Pinch to zoom, double tap to zoom in or out.">
        <Image source={{ uri }} style={styles.fill} contentFit="contain" accessible={false} />
      </Animated.View>
    </GestureDetector>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.black },
  fill: { flex: 1 },
  close: { position: "absolute", top: space.xxxl + space.md, right: space.md, minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  pager: { position: "absolute", bottom: space.xxxl, alignSelf: "center", flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.xl },
  pagerButton: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
});


===== src/features/book/ProgressRing.tsx =====

import Svg, { Circle } from "react-native-svg";

import { colors } from "@/theme";
import { ringGeometry } from "./index-data";

/** A ring that fills with brass as you visit a neighborhood's places. Complete: solid, with a glow ring. */
export function ProgressRing({ visited, total, size, stroke, complete }: { visited: number; total: number; size: number; stroke: number; complete?: boolean }) {
  const r = (size - stroke) / 2;
  const g = ringGeometry(visited, total, r);
  const c = size / 2;
  return (
    <Svg width={size} height={size} accessibilityElementsHidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
      <Circle cx={c} cy={c} r={r} fill="none" stroke={colors.hairline} strokeWidth={stroke} />
      <Circle
        cx={c}
        cy={c}
        r={r}
        fill="none"
        stroke={complete ? colors.brass : colors.dimBrass}
        strokeWidth={stroke}
        strokeLinecap="round"
        strokeDasharray={[g.filled, g.circumference]}
        transform={`rotate(-90 ${c} ${c})`}
      />
    </Svg>
  );
}


===== src/features/book/ScrapbookPage.tsx =====

/* eslint-disable react-hooks/immutability -- Reanimated shared values are written through .value by design */
import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { Gesture, GestureDetector } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import Animated, { useAnimatedStyle, useSharedValue, withSpring } from "react-native-reanimated";
import { scheduleOnRN } from "react-native-worklets";

import { mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import type { StampPhotoView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { bookMotion, bookStyle, colors, radius, shadows, space } from "@/theme";
import { PageStock } from "./PageStock";
import { type PhotoSpot, bringToFront, layoutRequest, photoShare, resolveLayout } from "./scrapbook";
import { clampCenter } from "./zoom";

type Item = { id: string; photo: StampPhotoView; spot: PhotoSpot };

type Props = {
  stampId: string;
  photos: readonly StampPhotoView[];
  width: number;
  height: number;
  /** Only the owner can rearrange. */
  editable: boolean;
  /** Called with the body to save when a photo is dropped. */
  onLayout: (body: ReturnType<typeof layoutRequest>) => void;
  onOpenPhoto: (index: number) => void;
};

/**
 * The right-hand page: up to six photos stuck down at an angle. The owner drags them about; the one being dragged comes to the top,
 * and where they end up is saved. Tap a photo to see it full screen.
 */
export function ScrapbookPage({ stampId, photos, width, height, editable, onLayout, onOpenPhoto }: Props) {
  // What is on the page: the server's layout, unless the owner has just moved something (kept until the server's layout changes).
  const key = photos.map((p) => `${p.id}:${p.position}:${p.x}:${p.y}:${p.rotation}`).join("|");
  const [local, setLocal] = useState<{ key: string; items: Item[] } | null>(null);
  const items: Item[] = local && local.key === key ? local.items : resolveLayout(photos, stampId).map((r) => ({ id: r.photo.id, photo: r.photo, spot: r.spot }));

  const photoSize = Math.round(width * photoShare(items.length));
  const drop = (id: string, spot: PhotoSpot) => {
    const moved = items.map((it) => (it.id === id ? { ...it, spot } : it));
    const next = bringToFront(moved, id);
    setLocal({ key, items: next });
    onLayout(layoutRequest(next));
  };

  return (
    <PageStock width={width} height={height} spine="left">
      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.empty}>
          <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="boneFaint" align="center">
            no photos on this page
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : null}
      {items.map((it, i) => (
        <ScrapPhoto key={it.id} item={it} index={i} size={photoSize} pageW={width} pageH={height} editable={editable} onDrop={drop} onOpen={() => onOpenPhoto(photos.findIndex((p) => p.id === it.id))} total={items.length} />
      ))}
    </PageStock>
  );
}

function ScrapPhoto({ item, index, size, pageW, pageH, editable, onDrop, onOpen, total }: { item: Item; index: number; size: number; pageW: number; pageH: number; editable: boolean; onDrop: (id: string, spot: PhotoSpot) => void; onOpen: () => void; total: number }) {
  const cx = useSharedValue(item.spot.x * pageW);
  const cy = useSharedValue(item.spot.y * pageH);
  const sx = useSharedValue(0);
  const sy = useSharedValue(0);
  const lift = useSharedValue(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    cx.value = item.spot.x * pageW;
    cy.value = item.spot.y * pageH;
  }, [item.spot.x, item.spot.y, pageW, pageH, cx, cy]);
  const rotation = item.spot.rotation;

  const drop = (x: number, y: number) => onDrop(item.id, { x: x / pageW, y: y / pageH, rotation });
  const pan = Gesture.Pan()
    .withTestId(`photo-pan-${item.id}`)
    .enabled(editable)
    .onBegin(() => {
      sx.value = cx.value;
      sy.value = cy.value;
      lift.value = withSpring(1, bookMotion.snap);
    })
    .onUpdate((e) => {
      const c = clampCenter(sx.value + e.translationX, sy.value + e.translationY, size, pageW, pageH);
      cx.value = c.cx;
      cy.value = c.cy;
    })
    .onEnd(() => {
      scheduleOnRN(drop, cx.value, cy.value);
    })
    .onFinalize(() => {
      lift.value = withSpring(0, bookMotion.snap);
    });
  const tap = Gesture.Tap()
    .withTestId(`photo-tap-${item.id}`)
    .onEnd((_e, ok) => {
      if (ok) scheduleOnRN(onOpen);
    });
  const style = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({
    left: cx.value - size / 2,
    top: cy.value - size / 2,
    transform: [{ rotate: `${rotation}deg` }, { scale: 1 + lift.value * 0.06 }],
  }));

  return (
    <GestureDetector gesture={Gesture.Exclusive(pan, tap)}>
      <Animated.View
        style={[styles.photo, { width: size, height: size, zIndex: index }, style]}
        accessible
        accessibilityRole="imagebutton"
        accessibilityLabel={`Photo ${index + 1} of ${total}`}
        accessibilityHint={editable ? "Opens it full screen. Drag to move it on the page." : "Opens it full screen"}
        accessibilityActions={[{ name: "activate" }]}
        onAccessibilityAction={onOpen}
      >
        <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(item.photo.url), cacheKey: item.photo.id }} style={styles.fill} contentFit="cover" transition={150} accessible={false} />
        <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.pin} />
      </Animated.View>
    </GestureDetector>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  empty: { flex: 1, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: space.lg },
  photo: { position: "absolute", padding: space.xxs, borderRadius: radius.chip / 4, backgroundColor: colors.elevated, borderWidth: bookStyle.pageLine, borderColor: colors.dimBrass, ...shadows.contact },
  fill: { flex: 1 },
  pin: { position: "absolute", top: -space.xxs, alignSelf: "center", width: space.xs, height: space.xs, borderRadius: space.xxs, backgroundColor: colors.brass },
});


===== src/features/book/Spread.tsx =====

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { bookStyle, colors, shadows } from "@/theme";
import { edgeLines } from "./edges";

type Props = {
  /** One page's width and height; the open book is two pages wide. */
  pageWidth: number;
  pageHeight: number;
  spreadIndex: number;
  spreadTotal: number;
  /** The pages, laid out by the caller across the two halves. */
  children: ReactNode;
};

/** The open book's frame: a shadow, and the edges of the page stacks showing on either side (more lines where more pages lie). */
export function Spread({ pageWidth, pageHeight, spreadIndex, spreadTotal, children }: Props) {
  const lines = edgeLines(spreadIndex, spreadTotal);
  const step = bookStyle.edgeLine + bookStyle.edgeGap;
  return (
    <View style={[{ width: pageWidth * 2, height: pageHeight }, styles.book]}>
      {children}
      {Array.from({ length: lines.left }, (_, i) => (
        <View key={`l${i}`} pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.edge, { left: -(i + 1) * step, height: pageHeight - (i + 1) * step * 2, top: (i + 1) * step, opacity: 1 - i * 0.12 }]} />
      ))}
      {Array.from({ length: lines.right }, (_, i) => (
        <View key={`r${i}`} pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.edge, { right: -(i + 1) * step, height: pageHeight - (i + 1) * step * 2, top: (i + 1) * step, opacity: 1 - i * 0.12 }]} />
      ))}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  book: { ...shadows.page },
  edge: { position: "absolute", width: bookStyle.edgeLine, backgroundColor: colors.dimBrass },
});


===== src/features/book/StampMark.tsx =====

import Svg, { Circle, Defs, G, LinearGradient, Path, Stop, Text as SvgText, TextPath } from "react-native-svg";

import { colors, fonts, stampStyle } from "@/theme";
import { STAMP_BOX, type StampDesign, type StampInput, completionDesign, motifPath, rayPath, ringsFor, shapePath, stampDesign } from "./stamp-design";

const MID = STAMP_BOX / 2;

/** A stamp inked on the page: brass, drawn from the place's design. The same place always looks the same. */
export function StampMark({ input, size, design }: { input: StampInput; size: number; design?: StampDesign }) {
  const d = design ?? stampDesign(input);
  const arcId = `arc-${input.placeId}`;
  const rings = ringsFor(d.border, stampStyle.strokes);
  const textSize = stampStyle.text.name * d.nameScale;
  return (
    <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox={`0 0 ${STAMP_BOX} ${STAMP_BOX}`} accessibilityElementsHidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
      <Defs>
        <Path id={arcId} d={`M ${MID - 38} ${MID} A 38 38 0 0 1 ${MID + 38} ${MID}`} />
      </Defs>
      <G opacity={d.ink}>
        {rings.map((r, i) => (
          <Path
            key={i}
            d={shapePath(d.shape, r.inset)}
            fill="none"
            stroke={stampStyle.ink}
            strokeWidth={r.width}
            strokeDasharray={r.dash ?? undefined}
            strokeLinecap={r.round ? "round" : "butt"}
          />
        ))}
        <G transform={`translate(${MID} ${d.layout === "arc" ? MID + 4 : MID - 14})`}>
          <Path d={motifPath(d.motif, d.layout === "arc" ? 15 : 12)} fill={stampStyle.ink} />
        </G>
        {d.layout === "arc" ? (
          <SvgText fill={stampStyle.ink} fontFamily={fonts.serif} fontSize={textSize} letterSpacing={stampStyle.text.arcTrack}>
            <TextPath href={`#${arcId}`} startOffset="50%" textAnchor="middle">
              {d.arcText}
            </TextPath>
          </SvgText>
        ) : (
          d.lines.map((line, i) => (
            <SvgText key={i} x={MID} y={MID + 12 + i * (textSize + 3)} fill={stampStyle.ink} fontFamily={fonts.serif} fontSize={textSize} textAnchor="middle" letterSpacing={stampStyle.text.bannerTrack}>
              {line}
            </SvgText>
          ))
        )}
        <SvgText x={MID} y={d.layout === "arc" ? MID + 40 : MID + 12 + d.lines.length * (textSize + 3) + 3} fill={stampStyle.inkDim} fontFamily={fonts.mono} fontSize={stampStyle.text.area} textAnchor="middle">
          {d.area}
        </SvgText>
      </G>
      {d.speckles.map((s, i) => (
        <Circle key={i} cx={s.cx} cy={s.cy} r={s.r} fill={colors.surface} opacity={0.9} />
      ))}
    </Svg>
  );
}

/** The foil stamp for a finished neighborhood: a ring of rays around its name, in the cover's foil. */
export function CompletionStamp({ neighborhoodId, name, size }: { neighborhoodId: string; name: string; size: number }) {
  const d = completionDesign(neighborhoodId, name);
  return (
    <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox={`0 0 ${STAMP_BOX} ${STAMP_BOX}`} accessible accessibilityLabel={`${name} complete`}>
      <Defs>
        <LinearGradient id={`foil-${neighborhoodId}`} x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
          <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.dimBrass} />
          <Stop offset="0.35" stopColor={colors.brass} />
          <Stop offset="0.5" stopColor={colors.bone} />
          <Stop offset="0.65" stopColor={colors.brass} />
          <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.dimBrass} />
        </LinearGradient>
      </Defs>
      <G transform={`translate(${MID} ${MID})`}>
        <Path d={rayPath(d.rays, 47, 56)} stroke={`url(#foil-${neighborhoodId})`} strokeWidth={stampStyle.strokes.text} />
        <Circle r={44} fill="none" stroke={`url(#foil-${neighborhoodId})`} strokeWidth={stampStyle.strokes.ring} />
        <Circle r={38} fill="none" stroke={`url(#foil-${neighborhoodId})`} strokeWidth={stampStyle.strokes.hair} />
        <Path d={motifPath("star", 11)} transform="translate(0 -14)" fill={`url(#foil-${neighborhoodId})`} />
      </G>
      <SvgText x={MID} y={MID + 12} fill={`url(#foil-${neighborhoodId})`} fontFamily={fonts.serif} fontSize={stampStyle.text.completeWord} textAnchor="middle" letterSpacing={stampStyle.text.completeTrack}>
        {d.label}
      </SvgText>
      <SvgText x={MID} y={MID + 27} fill={colors.brass} fontFamily={fonts.mono} fontSize={stampStyle.text.completeArea} textAnchor="middle">
        {d.area}
      </SvgText>
    </Svg>
  );
}


===== src/features/book/TicketStub.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import type { Song } from "@/api/contracts";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { songLabel } from "@/features/stamps/song-row";
import { bookStyle, colors, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** A song, as the stub torn off a ticket: art, title and artist, a perforated edge and a notch on each side. Metadata only. */
export function TicketStub({ song }: { song: Pick<Song, "title" | "artist" | "artUrl"> }) {
  return (
    <View style={styles.stub} accessible accessibilityLabel={songLabel(song)}>
      <View style={styles.notchLeft} />
      <View style={styles.notchRight} />
      <View style={styles.art}>
        {song.artUrl ? <Image source={{ uri: song.artUrl, cacheKey: song.artUrl }} style={styles.fill} contentFit="cover" accessible={false} /> : <Icon name="music.note" color="dimBrass" size={size.icon} />}
      </View>
      <View style={styles.perf} />
      <View style={styles.text}>
        <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass">
          SOUNDTRACK
        </Text>
        <Text variant="typewriter" color="textPrimary" numberOfLines={1}>
          {song.title}
        </Text>
        <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textSecondary" numberOfLines={1}>
          {song.artist}
        </Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const NOTCH = space.sm;
const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  stub: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.sm, padding: space.sm, borderRadius: radius.chip, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderWidth: bookStyle.pageRule, borderColor: colors.dimBrass, overflow: "hidden" },
  notchLeft: { position: "absolute", left: -NOTCH / 2, top: "50%", marginTop: -NOTCH / 2, width: NOTCH, height: NOTCH, borderRadius: NOTCH / 2, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  notchRight: { position: "absolute", right: -NOTCH / 2, top: "50%", marginTop: -NOTCH / 2, width: NOTCH, height: NOTCH, borderRadius: NOTCH / 2, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  art: { width: size.listRow - space.md, height: size.listRow - space.md, borderRadius: radius.chip / 2, backgroundColor: colors.elevated, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", overflow: "hidden" },
  fill: { width: "100%", height: "100%" },
  perf: { alignSelf: "stretch", borderLeftWidth: bookStyle.pageRule, borderStyle: "dashed", borderColor: colors.dimBrass },
  text: { flex: 1, gap: space.xxs },
});


===== src/features/book/VisaSpread.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, useWindowDimensions, View } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { patchStamp } from "@/features/stamps/cache";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";
import { bookSize } from "./dimensions";
import { PageStock } from "./PageStock";
import { PhotoViewer } from "./PhotoViewer";
import { ScrapbookPage } from "./ScrapbookPage";
import { slotPlacement } from "./slots";
import { Spread } from "./Spread";
import { StampMark } from "./StampMark";
import { TicketStub } from "./TicketStub";
import { useTypewriter } from "./typewriter";
import { visaLabel, visaStamp } from "./visa";

/**
 * One stamp as a visa spread: the stamp inked on the left page, its photos stuck into the scrapbook on the right, and a typed slip
 * underneath with the date, the place, the caption and the song as a ticket stub. The owner drags photos about; it saves itself.
 */
export function VisaSpread({ stamp, onSaveLayout }: { stamp: StampView; /** Replaces saving to the server (the Design gallery has no server). */ onSaveLayout?: (body: Parameters<typeof api.stamps.layout>[1]) => void }) {
  const { width: sw, height: sh } = useWindowDimensions();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [viewing, setViewing] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const book = bookSize(sw, sh);
  const pageW = Math.floor((sw - screenPadding * 2) / 2);
  const pageH = Math.floor(pageW * (book.pageHeight / book.pageWidth));
  const { date, time } = visaStamp(stamp.createdAt);
  const typed = useTypewriter(stamp.caption);
  const place = slotPlacement(stamp.id, stamp.slot ?? 1, pageW, pageH);

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: Parameters<typeof api.stamps.layout>[1]) => api.stamps.layout(stamp.id, body),
    onSuccess: (r) => patchStamp(queryClient, stamp.id, () => r.stamp),
    onError: (e) => {
      haptics.warning();
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't save the photo layout. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
  });

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Spread pageWidth={pageW} pageHeight={pageH} spreadIndex={0} spreadTotal={1}>
        <View style={styles.row}>
          <PageStock width={pageW} height={pageH} spine="right">
            <View style={styles.stampPage} accessible accessibilityLabel={`Stamp, ${stamp.place.name}`}>
              <View style={{ transform: [{ rotate: `${place.rotation}deg` }] }}>
                <StampMark input={{ placeId: stamp.place.id, placeName: stamp.place.name, neighborhoodId: stamp.place.neighborhoodId, neighborhoodName: stamp.place.neighborhood }} size={Math.round(pageW * 0.8)} />
              </View>
            </View>
          </PageStock>
          <ScrapbookPage stampId={stamp.id} photos={stamp.photos} width={pageW} height={pageH} editable={stamp.mine} onLayout={(body) => (onSaveLayout ? onSaveLayout(body) : save.mutate(body))} onOpenPhoto={setViewing} />
        </View>
      </Spread>

      <View style={styles.slip} accessible={false}>
        <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass" accessibilityLabel={visaLabel(stamp.place.name, stamp.place.neighborhood, stamp.createdAt, stamp.page)}>
          {[date, time].filter(Boolean).join("  ·  ")}
        </Text>
        <Pressable onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: "/place/[id]", params: { id: stamp.place.id } })} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`${stamp.place.name}. Your stamps at this place`} accessibilityHint="Opens the place page" hitSlop={space.xs}>
          <Text variant="title2" color="brass">
            {stamp.place.name}
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textSecondary">
          {stamp.place.neighborhood}
          {stamp.page ? `  ·  page ${stamp.page}` : ""}
        </Text>
        {!stamp.mine ? (
          <Text variant="subhead" color="textSecondary">
            @{stamp.author.handle} · at {stamp.place.name}
          </Text>
        ) : null}
        {stamp.caption !== "" ? (
          // The whole caption is always in place (so it wraps once and a screen reader has it all); the typed part is laid over it.
          <View>
            <Text variant="typewriter" color={typed >= stamp.caption.length ? "textPrimary" : "transparent"}>
              {stamp.caption}
            </Text>
            {typed < stamp.caption.length ? (
              <Text variant="typewriter" style={styles.typed} accessibilityElementsHidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
                {stamp.caption.slice(0, typed)}
              </Text>
            ) : null}
          </View>
        ) : null}
        {stamp.song ? <TicketStub song={stamp.song} /> : null}
        {stamp.editedAt ? (
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
            Edited
          </Text>
        ) : null}
      </View>
      <PhotoViewer photos={stamp.photos} index={viewing} onClose={() => setViewing(null)} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.xl, paddingTop: space.md },
  row: { flexDirection: "row" },
  stampPage: { flex: 1, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  slip: { alignSelf: "stretch", gap: space.xs, paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  typed: { position: "absolute", top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, color: colors.textPrimary },
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/bearer.test.tsx =====

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import { BearerPage } from "../BearerPage";
import { bearerFields, bookDate, checkDigit, documentNumber, machineZone } from "../bearer";

const bearer = { displayName: "Ava Álvarez", handle: "avaa", memberSince: "2026-10-03T04:00:00.000Z", bio: "Out late." };

describe("bearer page logic", () => {
  it("prints dates the same everywhere, in UTC", () => {
    expect(bookDate("2026-10-03T23:30:00.000Z")).toBe("03 OCT 2026");
    expect(bookDate("2027-01-01T00:00:00.000Z")).toBe("01 JAN 2027");
    expect(bookDate("nonsense")).toBe("");
  });
  it("makes a stable passport number from the handle alone", () => {
    expect(documentNumber("avaa")).toBe(documentNumber("AVAA"));
    expect(documentNumber("avaa")).toMatch(/^N\d{8}$/);
    expect(documentNumber("avaa")).not.toBe(documentNumber("morgan"));
  });
  it("computes the standard 7-3-1 check digit", () => {
    expect(checkDigit("520727")).toBe(3); // the ICAO 9303 worked example
    expect(checkDigit("L898902C3")).toBe(6);
    expect(checkDigit("<<<")).toBe(0);
  });
  it("builds two 44 character zone lines from A to Z, 0 to 9 and filler, folding accents", () => {
    const [a, b] = machineZone(bearer);
    expect(a).toHaveLength(44);
    expect(b).toHaveLength(44);
    expect(a).toMatch(/^[A-Z0-9<]{44}$/);
    expect(b).toMatch(/^[A-Z0-9<]{44}$/);
    expect(a.startsWith("P<NYCALVAREZ<<AVA")).toBe(true);
    expect(b.startsWith(documentNumber("avaa"))).toBe(true);
  });
  it("copes with names that have no Latin letters, one word, or an unreadable date", () => {
    expect(machineZone({ ...bearer, displayName: "李雷" })[0]).toMatch(/^P<NYCAVAA/);
    expect(machineZone({ ...bearer, displayName: "Cher" })[0].startsWith("P<NYCCHER<")).toBe(true);
    expect(machineZone({ ...bearer, memberSince: "x" })[1]).toHaveLength(44);
  });
  it("lists fields in reading order and leaves out remarks when there is no bio; never the email or an id", () => {
    expect(bearerFields(bearer).map((f) => f.label)).toEqual(["SURNAME AND GIVEN NAMES", "HANDLE", "PASSPORT NO.", "ISSUED", "REMARKS"]);
    expect(bearerFields({ ...bearer, bio: "  " }).map((f) => f.label)).not.toContain("REMARKS");
    expect(JSON.stringify(bearerFields(bearer))).not.toMatch(/\S+@\S+\.\S+/);
  });
});

describe("BearerPage", () => {
  it("reads as one passport page with the holder's details", async () => {
    await render(<BearerPage width={300} height={426} bearer={bearer} avatarUrl={null} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Passport of Ava Álvarez, at avaa.*issued: 03 OCT 2026/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("NOSTOS PASSPORT")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("@avaa")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/book-state.test.ts =====

import { isOpenish, nextPhase, spreadLabel, stageTransform } from "../book-state";

describe("the book's moments", () => {
  it("goes closed, opening, open, closing, closed, and ignores events that don't belong", () => {
    expect(nextPhase("closed", "tapCover")).toBe("opening");
    expect(nextPhase("opening", "coverSettled")).toBe("open");
    expect(nextPhase("open", "closeBook")).toBe("closing");
    expect(nextPhase("closing", "coverSettled")).toBe("closed");
    expect(nextPhase("closed", "coverSettled")).toBe("closed"); // a settle on mount does nothing
    expect(nextPhase("opening", "tapCover")).toBe("opening");
    expect(nextPhase("open", "tapCover")).toBe("open");
    expect(nextPhase("closing", "closeBook")).toBe("closing");
  });
  it("counts opening as open for the stage", () => {
    expect(["closed", "opening", "open", "closing"].map((p) => isOpenish(p as never))).toEqual([false, true, true, false]);
  });
});

describe("the stage", () => {
  it("centers the cover when closed and the whole spread when open, scaling down to fit", () => {
    expect(stageTransform(0, 300, 0.55)).toEqual({ scale: 1, translateX: -150 });
    expect(stageTransform(1, 300, 0.55)).toEqual({ scale: 0.55, translateX: -0 });
    const mid = stageTransform(0.5, 300, 0.5);
    expect(mid.scale).toBeCloseTo(0.75);
    expect(mid.translateX).toBeCloseTo(-56.25);
    expect(stageTransform(7, 300, 0.5).scale).toBe(0.5);
    expect(stageTransform(-3, 300, 0.5).translateX).toBe(-150);
  });
});

describe("the footer", () => {
  it("names the spread", () => {
    expect(spreadLabel(0, 4)).toBe("Bearer page");
    expect(spreadLabel(1, 4)).toBe("Pages 1 and 2");
    expect(spreadLabel(2, 4)).toBe("Pages 3 and 4");
    expect(spreadLabel(2, 3)).toBe("Page 3"); // an odd book ends on one page
  });
});
