===== src/features/book/__tests__/dimensions-emblem.test.ts =====

import { BOOK_ASPECT, bookSize, openScale } from "../dimensions";
import { doorPath, emblem, starPath, ticksPath } from "../emblem";

describe("book size", () => {
  it("fits the screen with room for the counts, keeping the passport's proportions", () => {
    for (const [w, h] of [[390, 844], [430, 932], [375, 667], [320, 568]] as const) {
      const s = bookSize(w, h);
      expect(s.width).toBeLessThanOrEqual(w * 0.84);
      expect(s.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(h * 0.62);
      expect(s.height / s.width).toBeCloseTo(BOOK_ASPECT, 1);
      expect(s.pageWidth).toBe(s.width);
    }
  });
  it("scales the open book down to fit, never up", () => {
    const s = bookSize(390, 844);
    expect(openScale(390, s, 8)).toBeLessThan(1);
    expect(openScale(2000, s, 8)).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("emblem", () => {
  const nums = (d: string) => (d.match(/-?\d+\.?\d*/g) ?? []).map(Number);
  it("is deterministic and made of closed shapes inside the unit circle", () => {
    expect(emblem()).toEqual(emblem());
    const e = emblem();
    expect(e.rings).toHaveLength(2);
    for (const d of [...e.rings, e.star, e.door]) expect(d.trim().endsWith("Z")).toBe(true);
    for (const v of nums(e.star)) expect(Math.abs(v)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    for (const v of nums(ticksPath())) expect(Math.abs(v)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
  });
  it("the star has eight points and the long ones point at north, east, south and west", () => {
    const d = starPath();
    expect((d.match(/L/g) ?? []).length).toBe(15);
    expect(d.startsWith("M 0 -0.8")).toBe(true);
  });
  it("has 48 ticks and a doorway", () => {
    expect((ticksPath().match(/M/g) ?? []).length).toBe(48);
    expect(doorPath()).toContain("A");
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/first-run.test.tsx =====

import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";

import { BrandMark } from "@/components/brand/brand-mark";
import { FIRST_RUN_COPY, FIRST_RUN_KEY, hasSeenFirstRun, markFirstRunSeen } from "../first-run";
import { PassportBook } from "../PassportBook";
import type { PassportData } from "../use-passport";

let mockData: PassportData;
jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("../use-passport", () => ({ usePassport: () => mockData }));
jest.mock("@/api", () => ({ api: {}, mediaUrl: (u: string) => u }));

const ready = (): PassportData => ({
  status: "ready", handle: "ava", displayName: "Ava Lee", avatarUrl: null, bio: "", memberSince: "2026-10-03T04:00:00.000Z", stamps: [], pins: [],
  counts: { stamps: 0, places: 0, neighborhoods: 0 }, neighborhoods: [], retry: jest.fn(),
});

describe("first run", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.mocked(SecureStore.getItemAsync).mockReset().mockResolvedValue(null);
    jest.mocked(SecureStore.setItemAsync).mockClear();
    mockData = ready();
  });

  it("remembers on the phone only that the hint was seen", async () => {
    jest.mocked(SecureStore.getItemAsync).mockResolvedValueOnce(null);
    expect(await hasSeenFirstRun()).toBe(false);
    await markFirstRunSeen();
    expect(SecureStore.setItemAsync).toHaveBeenCalledWith(FIRST_RUN_KEY, "1");
    jest.mocked(SecureStore.getItemAsync).mockResolvedValueOnce("1");
    expect(await hasSeenFirstRun()).toBe(true);
  });
  it("doesn't nag if the store can't be read", async () => {
    jest.mocked(SecureStore.getItemAsync).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("locked"));
    expect(await hasSeenFirstRun()).toBe(true);
  });
  it("shows once on the closed passport, then goes away when dismissed and stays away", async () => {
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(FIRST_RUN_COPY.title)).toBeTruthy());
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: FIRST_RUN_COPY.button }));
    expect(screen.queryByText(FIRST_RUN_COPY.title)).toBeNull();
    expect(SecureStore.setItemAsync).toHaveBeenCalledWith(FIRST_RUN_KEY, "1");
  });
  it("is not shown at all if it has been seen", async () => {
    jest.mocked(SecureStore.getItemAsync).mockResolvedValue("1");
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(SecureStore.getItemAsync).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(screen.queryByText(FIRST_RUN_COPY.title)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("counts as seen when the cover is opened", async () => {
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(FIRST_RUN_COPY.title)).toBeTruthy());
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open your passport/ }));
    expect(SecureStore.setItemAsync).toHaveBeenCalledWith(FIRST_RUN_KEY, "1");
  });
});

describe("brand mark", () => {
  it("is named Nostos for a screen reader", async () => {
    await render(<BrandMark />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Nostos")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/flip-math.test.ts =====

import { castShade, clamp, dragPos, flipAngle, flipShade, settleTarget, showsBack, stepTarget, turnProgress, zoneAt } from "../flip-math";

const PW = 300;

describe("turn progress", () => {
  it("is each turn's share of the way, so only the turn in flight is partway", () => {
    expect(turnProgress(2.37, 1)).toBe(1);
    expect(turnProgress(2.37, 2)).toBeCloseTo(0.37);
    expect(turnProgress(2.37, 3)).toBe(0);
    expect(turnProgress(0, 0)).toBe(0);
  });
  it("turns about the spine from 0 to minus 180 and shows the back past halfway", () => {
    expect(flipAngle(0)).toBe(0);
    expect(flipAngle(0.5)).toBe(-90);
    expect(flipAngle(1)).toBe(-180);
    expect(flipAngle(9)).toBe(-180);
    expect(showsBack(0.49)).toBe(false);
    expect(showsBack(0.5)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("shades live: nothing when flat, darkest edge-on, mirrored either side", () => {
    expect(flipShade(0)).toBeCloseTo(0);
    expect(flipShade(1)).toBeCloseTo(0);
    expect(flipShade(0.5)).toBeCloseTo(0.65);
    expect(flipShade(0.25)).toBeCloseTo(flipShade(0.75));
    expect(castShade(0.5)).toBeCloseTo(0.45);
    expect(castShade(0)).toBeCloseTo(0);
  });
});

describe("edges", () => {
  it("finds the outer edges and nothing in the middle", () => {
    expect(zoneAt(PW * 2 - 10, PW, 0.3)).toBe("next");
    expect(zoneAt(10, PW, 0.3)).toBe("prev");
    expect(zoneAt(PW, PW, 0.3)).toBeNull();
    expect(zoneAt(PW * 2 - PW * 0.3 - 1, PW, 0.3)).toBeNull();
    expect(zoneAt(-5, PW, 0.3)).toBeNull();
    expect(zoneAt(PW * 2 + 5, PW, 0.3)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("dragging", () => {
  it("follows the finger one to one and stops where the finger stops", () => {
    const x0 = PW * 2 - 20;
    const half = dragPos(2, x0, x0 - PW * 1.7 * 0.5, PW, "next", 6);
    expect(half).toBeCloseTo(2.5);
    expect(dragPos(2, x0, x0 - PW * 1.7 * 0.5, PW, "next", 6)).toBeCloseTo(half); // holding still changes nothing
  });
  it("only moves the way the edge turns, one spread at a time, inside the book", () => {
    const x0 = PW * 2 - 20;
    expect(dragPos(2, x0, x0 + 200, PW, "next", 6)).toBe(2); // dragging the wrong way
    expect(dragPos(2, x0, -9999, PW, "next", 6)).toBe(3); // a long drag is still one spread
    expect(dragPos(5, x0, -9999, PW, "next", 6)).toBe(5); // nothing after the last spread
    expect(dragPos(2, 20, 20 + PW * 1.7 * 0.25, PW, "prev", 6)).toBeCloseTo(1.75);
    expect(dragPos(0, 20, 9999, PW, "prev", 6)).toBe(0); // nothing before the first
  });
});

describe("letting go", () => {
  it("settles on the nearest spread", () => {
    expect(settleTarget(2.4, 2, 0, PW, 6)).toBe(2);
    expect(settleTarget(2.6, 2, 0, PW, 6)).toBe(3);
    expect(settleTarget(1.4, 2, 0, PW, 6)).toBe(1);
  });
  it("lets a flick carry it over, but only one spread", () => {
    expect(settleTarget(2.2, 2, -1500, PW, 6)).toBe(3);
    expect(settleTarget(2.8, 2, 1500, PW, 6)).toBe(2);
    expect(settleTarget(2.2, 2, -90000, PW, 6)).toBe(3);
    expect(settleTarget(2.45, 2, -100, PW, 6)).toBe(2);
  });
  it("never leaves the book", () => {
    expect(settleTarget(5.4, 5, -9999, PW, 6)).toBe(5);
    expect(settleTarget(0.3, 0, 9999, PW, 6)).toBe(0);
    expect(stepTarget(0, -1, 6)).toBe(0);
    expect(stepTarget(5, 1, 6)).toBe(5);
    expect(stepTarget(2, 1, 6)).toBe(3);
    expect(clamp(5, 0, 3)).toBe(3);
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/flipbook.test.tsx =====

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import { fireGestureHandler, getByGestureTestId } from "react-native-gesture-handler/jest-utils";
import { State } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import { Text } from "react-native";

import { FlipBook } from "../FlipBook";
import type { PageFace } from "../spreads";

const PW = 300;
const label = (f: PageFace, side: string) => `${side}:${f.kind === "stamps" ? `page ${f.page}` : f.kind}`;
const face = (f: PageFace, side: "left" | "right") => <Text>{label(f, side)}</Text>;

async function setup(spread: number, total = 4) {
  const onSpreadChange = jest.fn();
  const onCloseBook = jest.fn();
  const view = await render(<FlipBook pageWidth={PW} pageHeight={426} total={total} spread={spread} onSpreadChange={onSpreadChange} onCloseBook={onCloseBook} renderFace={face} />);
  return { onSpreadChange, onCloseBook, view };
}

describe("FlipBook", () => {
  it("draws the page it is on, the page under each turn, and nothing past either end of the book", async () => {
    await setup(0);
    // At the first spread: the bearer page (and what turns to show), but no page before the cover.
    expect(screen.getByText("right:bearer")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("left:page 1")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("right:page 2")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText("left:inside-cover")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("in the middle it holds the turning pages on both sides, so either way is ready", async () => {
    await setup(1);
    expect(screen.getByText("left:inside-cover")).toBeTruthy(); // under the page lying on the left
    expect(screen.getByText("left:page 1")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("right:page 2")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("left:page 3")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("right:page 4")).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("turns forward when the right edge is tapped, and back when the left edge is", async () => {
    const a = await setup(1);
    await fireGestureHandler(getByGestureTestId("flip-tap"), [{ state: State.BEGAN, x: PW * 2 - 6, y: 200 }, { state: State.ACTIVE, x: PW * 2 - 6, y: 200 }, { state: State.END, x: PW * 2 - 6, y: 200 }]);
    expect(a.onSpreadChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
    await a.view.unmount();
    const b = await setup(2);
    await fireGestureHandler(getByGestureTestId("flip-tap"), [{ state: State.BEGAN, x: 6, y: 200 }, { state: State.ACTIVE, x: 6, y: 200 }, { state: State.END, x: 6, y: 200 }]);
    expect(b.onSpreadChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1);
  });
  it("ignores a tap in the middle of a page, so stamps stay tappable", async () => {
    const a = await setup(1);
    await fireGestureHandler(getByGestureTestId("flip-tap"), [{ state: State.BEGAN, x: PW, y: 200 }, { state: State.ACTIVE, x: PW, y: 200 }, { state: State.END, x: PW, y: 200 }]);
    expect(a.onSpreadChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("does not turn past the last spread, and tapping the left edge of the first spread closes the book", async () => {
    const last = await setup(3);
    await fireGestureHandler(getByGestureTestId("flip-tap"), [{ state: State.BEGAN, x: PW * 2 - 6, y: 200 }, { state: State.ACTIVE, x: PW * 2 - 6, y: 200 }, { state: State.END, x: PW * 2 - 6, y: 200 }]);
    expect(last.onSpreadChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await last.view.unmount();
    const first = await setup(0);
    await fireGestureHandler(getByGestureTestId("flip-tap"), [{ state: State.BEGAN, x: 6, y: 200 }, { state: State.ACTIVE, x: 6, y: 200 }, { state: State.END, x: 6, y: 200 }]);
    expect(first.onCloseBook).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(first.onSpreadChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("turns forward when dragged from the right edge past halfway, and stays put when dragged only a little", async () => {
    const x0 = PW * 2 - 10;
    const far = await setup(1);
    await fireGestureHandler(getByGestureTestId("flip-pan"), [
      { state: State.BEGAN, x: x0, translationX: 0, velocityX: 0 },
      { state: State.ACTIVE, x: x0 - 20, translationX: -20, velocityX: -50 },
      { state: State.ACTIVE, x: x0 - 300, translationX: -300, velocityX: -50 },
      { state: State.END, x: x0 - 300, translationX: -300, velocityX: -50 },
    ]);
    expect(far.onSpreadChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
    await far.view.unmount();
    const near = await setup(1);
    await fireGestureHandler(getByGestureTestId("flip-pan"), [
      { state: State.BEGAN, x: x0, translationX: 0, velocityX: 0 },
      { state: State.ACTIVE, x: x0 - 60, translationX: -60, velocityX: -20 },
      { state: State.END, x: x0 - 60, translationX: -60, velocityX: -20 },
    ]);
    expect(near.onSpreadChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("ignores a drag that does not begin on an edge", async () => {
    const a = await setup(1);
    await fireGestureHandler(getByGestureTestId("flip-pan"), [
      { state: State.BEGAN, x: PW, translationX: 0, velocityX: 0 },
      { state: State.ACTIVE, x: PW - 250, translationX: -250, velocityX: -800 },
      { state: State.END, x: PW - 250, translationX: -250, velocityX: -800 },
    ]);
    expect(a.onSpreadChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/foil.test.ts =====

import { clamp, idleSweep, SWEEP_REST, sweepFromDrag, sweepFromTilt, trackedOffsets } from "../foil";

describe("foil highlight", () => {
  it("follows a drag across the cover and stays in range", () => {
    expect(sweepFromDrag(0.5, 0, 300)).toBe(0.5);
    expect(sweepFromDrag(0.5, 150, 300)).toBeGreaterThan(0.5);
    expect(sweepFromDrag(0.5, -150, 300)).toBeLessThan(0.5);
    expect(sweepFromDrag(0.5, 100000, 300)).toBe(1.4);
    expect(sweepFromDrag(0.5, -100000, 300)).toBe(-0.4);
  });
  it("follows tilt: flat is the rest position, tilting right or left moves the band", () => {
    expect(sweepFromTilt(0)).toBe(SWEEP_REST);
    expect(sweepFromTilt(0.3)).toBeGreaterThan(SWEEP_REST);
    expect(sweepFromTilt(-0.3)).toBeLessThan(SWEEP_REST);
    expect(sweepFromTilt(10)).toBe(1.4);
  });
  it("the idle shimmer travels both ways around the rest position", () => {
    const vals = [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75].map(idleSweep);
    expect(Math.max(...vals)).toBeGreaterThan(SWEEP_REST);
    expect(Math.min(...vals)).toBeLessThan(SWEEP_REST);
  });
  it("tracks wordmark letters out evenly", () => {
    expect(trackedOffsets([10, 20, 10], 5)).toEqual({ xs: [0, 15, 40], total: 50 });
    expect(trackedOffsets([], 5).total).toBe(0);
    expect(clamp(5, 0, 1)).toBe(1);
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/index.test.tsx =====

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import type { NeighborhoodIndexResponse, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { firstPageByNeighborhood, indexRows, ringGeometry, spreadForPage } from "../index-data";
import { IndexList } from "../IndexList";
import { PassportBook } from "../PassportBook";
import type { PassportData } from "../use-passport";

let mockData: PassportData;
jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("../use-passport", () => ({ usePassport: () => mockData }));
jest.mock("@/api", () => ({ api: {}, mediaUrl: (u: string) => u }));

const stamp = (id: string, page: number | null, hood: string | null): StampView => ({
  id, photoUrl: null, photos: [], caption: "", song: null, page, slot: page === null ? null : 1, createdAt: "2026-10-03T00:00:00.000Z", editedAt: null,
  place: { id: `p-${id}`, slug: id, name: `Place ${id}`, neighborhood: "Somewhere", neighborhoodId: hood },
  author: { handle: "ava", displayName: "Ava", avatarUrl: null, verified: false }, mine: true, likes: { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false,
});
const hood = (id: string, name: string, visited: number, total: number, complete = false): NeighborhoodIndexResponse["neighborhoods"][number] => ({
  neighborhoodId: id, name, borough: "Manhattan", visited, total, complete, completedAt: complete ? "2026-10-03T00:00:00.000Z" : null, percent: Math.round((visited / total) * 100),
});

describe("index data", () => {
  it("finds the earliest page of each neighborhood and ignores hidden stamps and places with no neighborhood", () => {
    const m = firstPageByNeighborhood([stamp("a", 5, "n1"), stamp("b", 2, "n1"), stamp("c", 4, "n2"), stamp("d", null, "n3"), stamp("e", 1, null)]);
    expect(m.get("n1")).toBe(2);
    expect(m.get("n2")).toBe(4);
    expect(m.has("n3")).toBe(false);
    expect(m.size).toBe(2);
  });
  it("builds rows with a spoken label, the page to jump to, and none for a neighborhood not started", () => {
    const rows = indexRows([hood("n1", "East Village", 4, 12), hood("n2", "Chelsea", 0, 9), hood("n3", "Soho", 3, 3, true)], [stamp("a", 2, "n1"), stamp("b", 6, "n3")]);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({ page: 2, label: "East Village, Manhattan. 4 of 12 places. First stamp on page 2." });
    expect(rows[1]).toMatchObject({ page: null, label: "Chelsea, Manhattan. 0 of 9 places. Not started." });
    expect(rows[2]!.label).toBe("Soho, Manhattan. Complete. 3 of 3 places. First stamp on page 6.");
  });
  it("turns to the spread a page is on", () => {
    expect([1, 2, 3, 4, 9].map(spreadForPage)).toEqual([1, 1, 2, 2, 5]);
  });
  it("draws a ring as a share of the circumference, never more than all of it", () => {
    const full = ringGeometry(12, 12, 10);
    expect(full.filled).toBeCloseTo(full.circumference);
    expect(ringGeometry(3, 12, 10).fraction).toBe(0.25);
    expect(ringGeometry(30, 12, 10).fraction).toBe(1);
    expect(ringGeometry(0, 0, 10).fraction).toBe(0);
    expect(ringGeometry(-2, 12, 10).fraction).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("IndexList", () => {
  it("lists neighborhoods with their progress, marks a finished one, and jumps to the page of a started one", async () => {
    const onJump = jest.fn();
    const rows = indexRows([hood("n1", "East Village", 4, 12), hood("n2", "Chelsea", 0, 9), hood("n3", "Soho", 3, 3, true)], [stamp("a", 2, "n1"), stamp("b", 6, "n3")]);
    await render(<IndexList rows={rows} onJump={onJump} onClose={jest.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("4/12")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("COMPLETE")).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /^East Village/ }));
    expect(onJump).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /^Chelsea/ }));
    expect(onJump).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); // not started: nowhere to turn to
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /^Chelsea/ }).props.accessibilityState?.disabled).toBe(true);
  });
  it("says what the index is for when there is nothing in it, and closes", async () => {
    const onClose = jest.fn();
    await render(<IndexList rows={[]} onJump={jest.fn()} onClose={onClose} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/No neighborhoods yet/)).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Close the index"));
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

describe("the index in the book", () => {
  it("opens from the book, jumps to a neighborhood's first page, and the book is on that spread", async () => {
    const stamps = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => stamp(`s${i}`, Math.floor(i / 3) + 1, i >= 6 ? "n1" : "n2")); // n1 first appears on page 3
    mockData = {
      status: "ready", handle: "ava", displayName: "Ava Lee", avatarUrl: null, bio: "", memberSince: "2026-10-03T04:00:00.000Z", stamps, pins: [],
      counts: { stamps: 10, places: 10, neighborhoods: 2 }, neighborhoods: [hood("n1", "East Village", 4, 12), hood("n2", "Chelsea", 6, 9)], retry: jest.fn(),
    };
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open your passport/ }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Index"));
    expect(screen.getByText("Index")).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /^East Village/ }));
    expect(screen.getByText("Pages 3 and 4")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/landing.test.tsx =====

import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import type { NeighborhoodProgress, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import * as Haptics from "expo-haptics";
import { LANDING, landingFrame } from "../landing";
import { landingCopy, photoPins } from "../landing-copy";
import { LandingScene } from "../LandingScene";

jest.mock("@/api", () => ({ api: {}, mediaUrl: (u: string) => u }));

const progress = (over: Partial<NeighborhoodProgress> = {}): NeighborhoodProgress => ({ neighborhoodId: "n1", name: "East Village", borough: "Manhattan", visited: 4, total: 12, complete: false, justCompleted: false, newPlace: true, ...over });
const stamp = (over: Partial<StampView> = {}): StampView => ({
  id: "s9", photoUrl: null, photos: [], caption: "", song: null, page: 3, slot: 2, createdAt: "2026-10-03T00:00:00.000Z", editedAt: null,
  place: { id: "p9", slug: "p9", name: "Katz's", neighborhood: "East Village", neighborhoodId: "n1" },
  author: { handle: "ava", displayName: "Ava", avatarUrl: null, verified: false }, mine: true, likes: { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false, ...over,
});

describe("landing timeline", () => {
  it("hovers large and invisible, then falls and gets faster until impact", () => {
    expect(landingFrame(0, false)).toMatchObject({ scale: 1.7, opacity: 0 });
    expect(landingFrame(LANDING.rise, false).scale).toBeCloseTo(1.7);
    let last = 1.7;
    for (let t = LANDING.rise; t < LANDING.impact; t += 10) {
      const s = landingFrame(t, false).scale;
      expect(s).toBeLessThanOrEqual(last + 1e-9);
      last = s;
    }
    expect(landingFrame(LANDING.impact - 1, false).scale).toBeLessThan(1.05);
  });
  it("is quiet before impact: no shake, no glow, no ink", () => {
    for (let t = 0; t < LANDING.impact; t += 20) {
      const f = landingFrame(t, false);
      expect([f.shakeX, f.shakeY, f.glow, f.inkSpread]).toEqual([0, 0, 0, 0]);
    }
  });
  it("shakes hard on impact and settles to nothing", () => {
    const peak = Math.max(...Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => Math.abs(landingFrame(LANDING.impact + i * 3, false).shakeX)));
    expect(peak).toBeGreaterThan(1.5);
    expect(peak).toBeLessThanOrEqual(4);
    const f = landingFrame(LANDING.impact + LANDING.shake, false);
    expect(f.shakeX).toBeCloseTo(0);
    expect(f.shakeY).toBeCloseTo(0);
  });
  it("flares to full brass quickly and fades to nothing, and spreads ink once", () => {
    const glows = Array.from({ length: 120 }, (_, i) => landingFrame(LANDING.impact + i * 10, false).glow);
    expect(Math.max(...glows)).toBeCloseTo(1, 1);
    expect(glows.indexOf(Math.max(...glows))).toBeLessThan(40); // the peak comes early
    expect(landingFrame(LANDING.impact + LANDING.glow, false).glow).toBeCloseTo(0);
    expect(landingFrame(LANDING.impact + LANDING.ink, false).inkSpread).toBeCloseTo(1);
    expect(landingFrame(LANDING.total, false)).toMatchObject({ scale: 1, opacity: 1 });
  });
  it("with Reduce Motion only fades in: no scale, shake or flare", () => {
    for (const t of [0, 100, 220, 500, 1200]) {
      const f = landingFrame(t, true);
      expect(f.scale).toBe(1);
      expect([f.shakeX, f.shakeY, f.glow]).toEqual([0, 0, 0]);
    }
    expect(landingFrame(0, true).opacity).toBe(0);
    expect(landingFrame(220, true).opacity).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("what the landing says", () => {
  it("names the page and the progress through the neighborhood", () => {
    expect(landingCopy(3, progress())).toEqual({ added: "Added to page 3", progress: "East Village: 4 of 12 places.", completed: false });
  });
  it("says so when it is another visit, and when the neighborhood is finished", () => {
    expect(landingCopy(3, progress({ newPlace: false })).progress).toBe("Another visit. East Village: 4 of 12 places.");
    expect(landingCopy(3, progress({ justCompleted: true, complete: true, visited: 12 }))).toMatchObject({ completed: true, progress: "East Village is complete. 12 of 12 places." });
  });
  it("copes with no neighborhood and with no page", () => {
    expect(landingCopy(null, null)).toEqual({ added: "Stamped", progress: null, completed: false });
  });
  it("pins up to six photos in a row inside the page, tilted a little, the same way every time", () => {
    expect(photoPins(0, 300, "s")).toEqual([]);
    expect(photoPins(9, 300, "s")).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(photoPins(3, 300, "s")).toEqual(photoPins(3, 300, "s"));
    for (const n of [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]) {
      for (const p of photoPins(n, 300, "s")) {
        expect(p.cx - p.size / 2).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(p.cx + p.size / 2).toBeLessThanOrEqual(300);
        expect(Math.abs(p.rotation)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(7);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe("LandingScene", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.useFakeTimers();
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });
  afterEach(() => jest.useRealTimers());

  it("says which page the stamp went on and how far through the neighborhood you are, and gives a haptic knock on impact", async () => {
    await render(<LandingScene stamp={stamp()} others={[]} progress={progress()} pageWidth={300} pageHeight={426} onDone={jest.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Added to page 3")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("East Village: 4 of 12 places.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("New stamp, Katz's, page 3, slot 2")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(Haptics.notificationAsync).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await act(async () => jest.advanceTimersByTime(LANDING.impact));
    expect(Haptics.notificationAsync).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
  it("pins the photos it was given and shows the foil stamp when the neighborhood is finished", async () => {
    const photos = [0, 1].map((i) => ({ id: `ph${i}`, url: `https://x/${i}.jpg`, position: i, x: null, y: null, rotation: null }));
    await render(<LandingScene stamp={stamp({ photos })} others={[]} progress={progress({ justCompleted: true, complete: true, visited: 12 })} pageWidth={300} pageHeight={426} onDone={jest.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("East Village complete")).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("calls Done", async () => {
    const onDone = jest.fn();
    await render(<LandingScene stamp={stamp()} others={[]} progress={null} pageWidth={300} pageHeight={426} onDone={onDone} />);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Done"));
    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/open.test.tsx =====

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import { Text } from "react-native";

import { OpeningCover } from "../OpeningCover";
import { coverAngle, pageShadow, showsInside, swingShade } from "../open-math";

describe("open animation math", () => {
  it("turns the cover from 0 to minus 180 degrees and clamps", () => {
    expect([coverAngle(0), coverAngle(0.5), coverAngle(1), coverAngle(2), coverAngle(-1)]).toEqual([-0, -90, -180, -180, -0]);
  });
  it("shows the inside face only past halfway", () => {
    expect([showsInside(0.4), showsInside(0.5), showsInside(0.51)]).toEqual([false, false, true]);
  });
  it("shades most edge-on and not at all when flat", () => {
    expect([swingShade(0), swingShade(1)].map((n) => Math.round(n * 1000))).toEqual([0, 0]);
    expect(swingShade(0.5)).toBeGreaterThan(swingShade(0.2));
    expect(pageShadow(0.5)).toBeGreaterThan(pageShadow(0.9));
  });
});

describe("OpeningCover", () => {
  it("renders the cover and the page under it", async () => {
    await render(<OpeningCover width={200} height={284} open={false} outside={<Text>cover</Text>} page={<Text>page</Text>} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("cover")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("page")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/pages.test.tsx =====

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { bookStyle, stampStyle } from "@/theme";
import { edgeLines, pinnedPages, ribbonPath, ribbonsOnPage } from "../edges";
import { rosettePath, wavePaths } from "../guilloche";
import { PageView } from "../PageView";
import { Spread } from "../Spread";
import { groupByPage, pageCountFor, spreadAt, spreadCountFor, spreadIndexForPage } from "../spreads";
import { boxOf, overlapShare, slotPlacement } from "../slots";

const stamp = (id: string, page: number | null, slot: number | null, name = "Place"): StampView => ({
  id, photoUrl: null, photos: [], caption: "", song: null, page, slot, createdAt: "2026-10-03T00:00:00.000Z", editedAt: null,
  place: { id: `p-${id}`, slug: id, name, neighborhood: "East Village", neighborhoodId: null },
  author: { handle: "me", displayName: "Me", avatarUrl: null, verified: false }, mine: true, likes: { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false,
});

describe("spreads", () => {
  it("opens on the inside cover and the bearer page, then two stamp pages to a spread", () => {
    expect(spreadAt(0)).toEqual({ index: 0, left: { kind: "inside-cover" }, right: { kind: "bearer" } });
    expect(spreadAt(1)).toEqual({ index: 1, left: { kind: "stamps", page: 1 }, right: { kind: "stamps", page: 2 } });
    expect(spreadAt(3).right).toEqual({ kind: "stamps", page: 6 });
    expect(() => spreadAt(-1)).toThrow();
  });
  it("agrees with the shared layout: the spread of a page holds that page", () => {
    for (let page = 1; page <= 20; page++) {
      const s = spreadAt(spreadIndexForPage(page));
      expect([s.left, s.right]).toContainEqual({ kind: "stamps", page });
    }
  });
  it("always shows the page the next stamp lands on, in whole spreads, at least one", () => {
    expect(pageCountFor(0)).toBe(2);
    expect(pageCountFor(5)).toBe(2); // the next stamp is the 6th: page 2
    expect(pageCountFor(6)).toBe(4); // the 7th starts page 3, so page 4 shows too
    expect(pageCountFor(9)).toBe(4);
    expect(pageCountFor(12)).toBe(6);
    expect(spreadCountFor(0)).toBe(2);
    expect(spreadCountFor(6)).toBe(3);
  });
  it("groups stamps by page in slot order and leaves out hidden and removed ones", () => {
    const m = groupByPage([stamp("c", 1, 3), stamp("a", 1, 1), stamp("b", 1, 2), stamp("d", 2, 1), stamp("x", null, null)]);
    expect(m.get(1)!.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);
    expect(m.get(2)!.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(["d"]);
    expect(m.size).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe("slot placement", () => {
  const W = 300;
  const H = 426;
  it("is the same for the same seed and different for another", () => {
    expect(slotPlacement("s1", 2, W, H)).toEqual(slotPlacement("s1", 2, W, H));
    expect(slotPlacement("s1", 2, W, H)).not.toEqual(slotPlacement("s2", 2, W, H));
    expect(() => slotPlacement("s", 4, W, H)).toThrow();
  });
  it("keeps every stamp inside the page rule and within the tilt limit, for any seed", () => {
    for (let n = 0; n < 300; n++) {
      for (const slot of [1, 2, 3]) {
        const p = slotPlacement(`seed${n}`, slot, W, H);
        const b = boxOf(p);
        expect(b.left).toBeGreaterThan(W * 0.06);
        expect(b.right).toBeLessThan(W * 0.94);
        expect(b.top).toBeGreaterThan(H * 0.06);
        expect(b.bottom).toBeLessThan(H * 0.94);
        expect(Math.abs(p.rotation)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(stampStyle.maxTiltDeg);
      }
    }
  });
  it("lets neighbors overlap only a little, for any seed, at phone sizes", () => {
    for (const [w, h] of [[300, 426], [260, 369], [340, 483]] as const) {
      for (let n = 0; n < 300; n++) {
        const [a, b, c] = [1, 2, 3].map((s) => slotPlacement(`seed${n}`, s, w, h)) as [ReturnType<typeof slotPlacement>, ReturnType<typeof slotPlacement>, ReturnType<typeof slotPlacement>];
        expect(overlapShare(a, b)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(stampStyle.maxOverlap);
        expect(overlapShare(b, c)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(stampStyle.maxOverlap);
        expect(overlapShare(a, c)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(stampStyle.maxOverlap);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe("guilloche", () => {
  it("draws a closed rosette that fits its circle and is the same every time", () => {
    const d = rosettePath("page:1", 150, 200, 100);
    expect(d).toBe(rosettePath("page:1", 150, 200, 100));
    expect(d).not.toBe(rosettePath("page:2", 150, 200, 100));
    expect(d.endsWith("Z")).toBe(true);
    const pts = [...d.matchAll(/[ML]([\d.-]+) ([\d.-]+)/g)].map((m) => [Number(m[1]), Number(m[2])] as const);
    expect(pts[0]).toEqual(pts[pts.length - 1]); // closed
    for (const [x, y] of pts) expect(Math.hypot(x - 150, y - 200)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100.05);
  });
  it("draws interlocking waves inside their band", () => {
    const waves = wavePaths("top:1", 200, 20, 5);
    expect(waves).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(new Set(waves).size).toBe(5);
    for (const w of waves) {
      for (const m of w.matchAll(/[ML][\d.-]+ ([\d.-]+)/g)) {
        expect(Number(m[1])).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(Number(m[1])).toBeLessThanOrEqual(20);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe("edges and ribbons", () => {
  it("shows more page edge on the side with more pages, capped", () => {
    expect(edgeLines(0, 5)).toEqual({ left: 0, right: 4 });
    expect(edgeLines(2, 5)).toEqual({ left: 2, right: 2 });
    expect(edgeLines(4, 5)).toEqual({ left: 4, right: 0 });
    expect(edgeLines(40, 80).left).toBe(bookStyle.maxEdgeLines);
    expect(edgeLines(40, 80).right).toBe(bookStyle.maxEdgeLines);
  });
  it("cuts a swallowtail ribbon", () => {
    expect(ribbonPath(10, 40)).toBe("M0 0 L10 0 L10 40 L5 35 L0 40 Z");
  });
  it("marks pinned stamps on their page, and lists each pinned page once in book order", () => {
    const here = [stamp("a", 3, 1), stamp("b", 3, 2)];
    expect(ribbonsOnPage(here, ["z", "b", "a"])).toEqual(["b", "a"]);
    expect(pinnedPages([stamp("a", 7, 1), stamp("b", 2, 1), stamp("c", 7, 2), stamp("d", null, null)])).toEqual([2, 7]);
  });
});

describe("PageView and Spread", () => {
  it("shows the page number and one button per stamp, saying which are pinned", async () => {
    await render(<PageView page={4} side="right" width={300} height={426} stamps={[stamp("a", 4, 1, "Katz's"), stamp("b", 4, 2, "Cafe")]} pinnedIds={["b"]} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Page 4")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Stamp, Katz's, page 4, slot 1" })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Stamp, Cafe, page 4, slot 2, pinned" })).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("shows an empty page with only its number", async () => {
    await render(<PageView page={5} side="left" width={300} height={426} stamps={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Page 5")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryAllByRole("button")).toHaveLength(0);
  });
  it("frames the pages it is given", async () => {
    await render(<Spread pageWidth={300} pageHeight={426} spreadIndex={1} spreadTotal={3}>
        <PageView page={1} side="left" width={300} height={426} stamps={[]} />
        <PageView page={2} side="right" width={300} height={426} stamps={[]} />
      </Spread>);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Page 1")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Page 2")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/passport-book.test.tsx =====

/** Your passport as a book: the states, and opening it. */
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { PassportBook } from "../PassportBook";
import type { PassportData } from "../use-passport";

const mockPush = jest.fn();
let mockData: PassportData;
jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: (...a: unknown[]) => mockPush(...a) } }));
jest.mock("../use-passport", () => ({ usePassport: () => mockData }));
jest.mock("@/api", () => ({ api: {}, mediaUrl: (u: string) => u }));

const stamp = (id: string, page: number, slot: number, name: string): StampView => ({
  id, photoUrl: null, photos: [], caption: "", song: null, page, slot, createdAt: "2026-10-03T00:00:00.000Z", editedAt: null,
  place: { id: `p-${id}`, slug: id, name, neighborhood: "East Village", neighborhoodId: null },
  author: { handle: "ava", displayName: "Ava", avatarUrl: null, verified: false }, mine: true, likes: { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false,
});
const ready = (over: Partial<PassportData> = {}): PassportData => ({
  status: "ready", handle: "ava", displayName: "Ava Lee", avatarUrl: null, bio: "", memberSince: "2026-10-03T04:00:00.000Z", stamps: [], pins: [],
  counts: { stamps: 0, places: 0, neighborhoods: 0 }, neighborhoods: [], retry: jest.fn(), ...over,
});

describe("PassportBook", () => {
  beforeEach(() => mockPush.mockClear());

  it("while loading, says it is opening, and shows no book", async () => {
    mockData = ready({ status: "pending" });
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("progressbar", { name: "Opening your passport" })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /Open your passport/ })).toBeNull();
  });
  it("on a failed load, offers Try again and no book", async () => {
    mockData = ready({ status: "error" });
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Couldn't open your passport/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Try again")).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("starts closed: the cover is one button and the three counts sit under it", async () => {
    mockData = ready({ counts: { stamps: 7, places: 5, neighborhoods: 2 } });
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open your passport, Ava Lee\. 7 stamps, 5 places, 2 neighborhoods\./ })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("7 stamps, 5 places, 2 neighborhoods")).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("opens to the bearer page, with a close button and a next button, and says the first page is empty when it is", async () => {
    mockData = ready();
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open your passport/ }));
    expect(screen.getByText("NOSTOS PASSPORT")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Close the passport")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Bearer page")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Your first page is empty. Go to a place and stamp it.")).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("turns to the stamp pages with the next button, and a stamp opens its page", async () => {
    mockData = ready({ stamps: [stamp("s1", 1, 1, "Katz's"), stamp("s2", 1, 2, "Cafe")], counts: { stamps: 2, places: 2, neighborhoods: 1 } });
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open your passport/ }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Next page"));
    expect(screen.getByText("Pages 1 and 2")).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getAllByRole("button", { name: "Stamp, Katz's, page 1, slot 1" })[0]!);
    expect(mockPush).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ pathname: "/stamp/[id]", params: { id: "s1" } });
  });
  it("closes the book from the first spread", async () => {
    mockData = ready();
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open your passport/ }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Close the passport"));
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open your passport/ })).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("disables Next on the last spread", async () => {
    mockData = ready();
    await render(<PassportBook />);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Open your passport/ }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Next page"));
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Next page").props.accessibilityState?.disabled).toBe(true);
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/stamps.test.tsx =====

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import { BORDERS, MOTIFS, SHAPES, borderFor, completionDesign, fitName, motifPath, rayPath, ringsFor, shapePath, stampDesign, STAMP_BOX } from "../stamp-design";
import { CompletionStamp, StampMark } from "../StampMark";

const input = (id: string, name = "Katz's Delicatessen", hood: string | null = "n-east-village", hoodName = "East Village") => ({ placeId: id, placeName: name, neighborhoodId: hood, neighborhoodName: hoodName });

describe("stamp design", () => {
  it("is the same for the same place, every time, and different places differ", () => {
    expect(stampDesign(input("p1"))).toEqual(stampDesign(input("p1")));
    const designs = new Set(Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => JSON.stringify(stampDesign(input(`p${i}`)))));
    expect(designs.size).toBe(40);
  });
  it("uses every shape and every motif across many places, and nothing else", () => {
    const shapes = new Set<string>();
    const motifs = new Set<string>();
    for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
      const d = stampDesign(input(`place-${i}`));
      shapes.add(d.shape);
      motifs.add(d.motif);
    }
    expect([...shapes].sort()).toEqual([...SHAPES].sort());
    expect([...motifs].sort()).toEqual([...MOTIFS].sort());
  });
  it("carries no color: variety is shape and design, ink is the theme's brass", () => {
    expect(JSON.stringify(stampDesign(input("p1")))).not.toMatch(/#|rgb|hsl/i);
  });
  it("gives every place in a neighborhood the same border family, and different neighborhoods a spread of them", () => {
    const borders = new Set(Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => stampDesign(input(`a${i}`, "X", "hood-1")).border));
    expect(borders.size).toBe(1);
    const across = new Set(Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, i) => borderFor(`hood-${i}`)));
    expect(across.size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
    for (const b of across) expect(BORDERS).toContain(b);
  });
  it("gives a place with no neighborhood a plain ring", () => {
    expect(stampDesign(input("p1", "Nowhere", null, "")).border).toBe("plain");
  });
  it("sets circle stamps on an arc and the rest in lines", () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
      const d = stampDesign(input(`q${i}`));
      expect(d.layout).toBe(d.shape === "circle" ? "arc" : "banner");
    }
  });
  it("keeps ink strength and speckles in range, with every speckle inside the stamp", () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
      const d = stampDesign(input(`r${i}`));
      expect(d.ink).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.82);
      expect(d.ink).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
      expect(d.speckles.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6);
      expect(d.speckles.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(12);
      for (const s of d.speckles) expect(Math.hypot(s.cx - STAMP_BOX / 2, s.cy - STAMP_BOX / 2)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(STAMP_BOX / 2);
    }
  });
});

describe("fitting a name", () => {
  it("uppercases and wraps on words, at most three lines of twelve", () => {
    expect(fitName("Katz's Delicatessen")).toEqual(["KATZ'S", "DELICATESSEN"]);
  });
  it("cuts a word that is too long, and a name that is too long, with an ellipsis", () => {
    expect(fitName("Supercalifragilisticexpialidocious")[0]!.endsWith("…")).toBe(true);
    const long = fitName("One Two Three Four Five Six Seven Eight Nine Ten Eleven Twelve");
    expect(long).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(long[2]!.endsWith("…")).toBe(true);
    for (const l of long) expect(l.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(12);
  });
  it("copes with nothing, and with extra spaces", () => {
    expect(fitName("   ")).toEqual([]);
    expect(fitName("  The   Rock ")).toEqual(["THE ROCK"]);
  });
  it("shrinks the type for long names but never below a legible size", () => {
    expect(stampDesign(input("a", "Bar")).nameScale).toBe(1);
    expect(stampDesign(input("a", "Metropolitan Museum")).nameScale).toBeLessThan(1);
    expect(stampDesign(input("a", "Supercalifragilisticexpialidocious")).nameScale).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.55);
  });
});

describe("shapes, borders and motifs", () => {
  it("draws every shape as one closed outline inside the box", () => {
    for (const shape of SHAPES) {
      const d = shapePath(shape, 6);
      expect(d.endsWith("Z")).toBe(true);
      expect(d.match(/M/g)).toHaveLength(1);
      for (const m of d.matchAll(/-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g)) {
        // Arc flags and radii are small; every coordinate stays in the box.
        expect(Number(m[0])).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(-0.001);
        expect(Number(m[0])).toBeLessThanOrEqual(STAMP_BOX);
      }
    }
  });
  it("gives each border family rings from the outside in, using the theme's stroke widths", () => {
    const strokes = { ring: 3, hair: 1.2 };
    expect(ringsFor("plain", strokes)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(ringsFor("double", strokes).map((r) => r.width)).toEqual([3, 1.2]);
    expect(ringsFor("heavy", strokes)[0]!.width).toBeGreaterThan(3);
    expect(ringsFor("dotted", strokes)[0]!.round).toBe(true);
    for (const b of BORDERS) for (const [i, r] of ringsFor(b, strokes).entries()) if (i > 0) expect(r.inset).toBeGreaterThan(ringsFor(b, strokes)[0]!.inset);
  });
  it("draws each motif as a closed figure within its radius", () => {
    for (const m of MOTIFS) {
      const d = motifPath(m, 10);
      expect(d.endsWith("Z")).toBe(true);
      expect(d.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
    }
    expect(motifPath("star", 10).match(/L/g)).toHaveLength(9);
  });
  it("makes a completion stamp from the neighborhood alone", () => {
    expect(completionDesign("hood-1", "East Village")).toEqual(completionDesign("hood-1", "East Village"));
    const d = completionDesign("hood-1", "East Village");
    expect(d.label).toBe("COMPLETE");
    expect(d.area).toBe("EAST VILLAGE");
    expect(d.rays % 2).toBe(0);
    expect(rayPath(d.rays, 47, 56).match(/M/g)).toHaveLength(d.rays);
  });
});

describe("drawing", () => {
  it("renders a stamp for every shape without crashing", async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) await render(<StampMark input={input(`draw-${i}`)} size={100} />);
  });
  it("renders the completion stamp with a spoken label", async () => {
    await render(<CompletionStamp neighborhoodId="hood-1" name="East Village" size={140} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("East Village complete")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/visa-logic.test.ts =====

import type { StampPhotoView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { clampCenter, clampPan, clampScale, doubleTapScale, MAX_SCALE } from "../zoom";
import { bringToFront, defaultScrapbook, layoutRequest, photoShare, resolveLayout } from "../scrapbook";
import { typedCount } from "../typewriter";
import { visaLabel, visaStamp } from "../visa";

const photo = (id: string, position: number, x: number | null = null, y: number | null = null, rotation: number | null = null): StampPhotoView => ({ id, url: `https://x/${id}.jpg`, position, x, y, rotation });

describe("scrapbook layout", () => {
  it("has a default place for each of 0 to 6 photos, inside the page, the same every time", () => {
    for (let n = 0; n <= 6; n++) {
      const spots = defaultScrapbook(n, "s1");
      expect(spots).toHaveLength(n);
      expect(spots).toEqual(defaultScrapbook(n, "s1"));
      const half = photoShare(n) / 2;
      for (const s of spots) {
        expect(s.x - half).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(s.x + half).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
        expect(s.y - half * 0.7).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0); // the page is taller than wide
        expect(Math.abs(s.rotation)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(6);
      }
    }
    expect(defaultScrapbook(3, "a")).not.toEqual(defaultScrapbook(3, "b"));
    expect(defaultScrapbook(9, "s")).toHaveLength(6);
  });
  it("makes photos smaller as there are more of them", () => {
    const shares = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(photoShare);
    for (let i = 1; i < shares.length; i++) expect(shares[i]!).toBeLessThanOrEqual(shares[i - 1]!);
  });
  it("uses where the owner left a photo and the default for photos never moved, in page order", () => {
    const r = resolveLayout([photo("b", 1), photo("a", 0, 0.2, 0.9, 12)], "s");
    expect(r.map((x) => x.photo.id)).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    expect(r[0]!.spot).toEqual({ x: 0.2, y: 0.9, rotation: 12 });
    expect(r[1]!.spot).toEqual(defaultScrapbook(2, "s")[1]);
  });
  it("brings a dragged photo to the top and leaves the rest in order", () => {
    expect(bringToFront([{ id: "a" }, { id: "b" }, { id: "c" }], "a").map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(["b", "c", "a"]);
    expect(bringToFront([{ id: "a" }, { id: "b" }], "z").map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
  });
  it("saves a layout the way the server wants it: order as position, shares of the page, rounded, in range", () => {
    const body = layoutRequest([{ id: "a", spot: { x: 0.123456, y: 1.4, rotation: 60 } }, { id: "b", spot: { x: -1, y: 0.5, rotation: -3.14159 } }]);
    expect(body).toEqual({ photos: [{ id: "a", position: 0, x: 0.123, y: 1, rotation: 45 }, { id: "b", position: 1, x: 0, y: 0.5, rotation: -3.142 }] });
  });
});

describe("dragging and zooming", () => {
  it("keeps a dragged photo wholly on the page", () => {
    expect(clampCenter(-50, -50, 100, 300, 420)).toEqual({ cx: 50, cy: 50 });
    expect(clampCenter(999, 999, 100, 300, 420)).toEqual({ cx: 250, cy: 370 });
    expect(clampCenter(150, 200, 100, 300, 420)).toEqual({ cx: 150, cy: 200 });
    expect(clampCenter(5, 5, 400, 300, 420)).toEqual({ cx: 200, cy: 200 }); // bigger than the page: pinned to the middle-ish
  });
  it("zooms between 1 and 4 times, and double tap toggles in and out", () => {
    expect(clampScale(0.2)).toBe(1);
    expect(clampScale(9)).toBe(MAX_SCALE);
    expect(doubleTapScale(1)).toBe(2.5);
    expect(doubleTapScale(2.5)).toBe(1);
  });
  it("lets a zoomed photo be dragged only until its edges meet the screen's, and not at all when it fits", () => {
    expect(clampPan(500, -500, 2, 400, 800)).toEqual({ tx: 200, ty: -400 });
    expect(clampPan(30, 30, 1, 400, 800)).toEqual({ tx: 0, ty: 0 });
    expect(clampPan(10, 10, 3, 400, 800)).toEqual({ tx: 10, ty: 10 });
  });
});

describe("typing and dates", () => {
  it("types at a steady pace and never past the end", () => {
    expect(typedCount(0, 40)).toBe(0);
    expect(typedCount(500, 40)).toBe(19);
    expect(typedCount(99999, 40)).toBe(40);
    expect(typedCount(-5, 40)).toBe(0);
    expect(typedCount(500, 0)).toBe(0);
  });
  it("reads a stamp's moment in New York time, so a late night stays on its own date", () => {
    expect(visaStamp("2026-10-04T03:41:00.000Z")).toEqual({ date: "03 OCT 2026", time: "11:41 PM" });
    expect(visaStamp("2026-01-15T17:05:00.000Z")).toEqual({ date: "15 JAN 2026", time: "12:05 PM" });
    expect(visaStamp("nope")).toEqual({ date: "", time: "" });
  });
  it("has a spoken label", () => {
    expect(visaLabel("Katz's", "East Village", "2026-10-04T03:41:00.000Z", 3)).toBe("Katz's, East Village, 03 OCT 2026 at 11:41 PM. Page 3.");
    expect(visaLabel("Katz's", "", "bad", null)).toBe("Katz's.");
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/visa.test.tsx =====

import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import { fireGestureHandler, getByGestureTestId } from "react-native-gesture-handler/jest-utils";
import { State } from "react-native-gesture-handler";

import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { testQueryClient } from "@/test/query-client";
import { pinChange } from "../pin-change";
import { VisaSpread } from "../VisaSpread";

const mockLayout = jest.fn();
jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/api", () => {
  const client = jest.requireActual("@/api/client");
  return { api: { stamps: { layout: (...a: unknown[]) => mockLayout(...a) } }, mediaUrl: (u: string) => u, errorText: client.errorText };
});

const photos = [0, 1, 2].map((i) => ({ id: `ph${i}`, url: `https://x/${i}.jpg`, position: i, x: null, y: null, rotation: null }));
const stamp = (over: Partial<StampView> = {}): StampView => ({
  id: "s1", photoUrl: photos[0]!.url, photos, caption: "hello there", song: { title: "Heroes", artist: "David Bowie", artUrl: null, sourceId: "1" },
  page: 3, slot: 2, createdAt: "2026-10-04T03:41:00.000Z", editedAt: null, place: { id: "p1", slug: "velvet", name: "Velvet", neighborhood: "East Village", neighborhoodId: "n1" },
  author: { handle: "ava", displayName: "Ava", avatarUrl: null, verified: false }, mine: true, likes: { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false, ...over,
});
const wrap = (s: StampView) => render(<QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}><VisaSpread stamp={s} /></QueryClientProvider>);

describe("VisaSpread", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    mockLayout.mockResolvedValue({ stamp: stamp() });
  });

  it("shows the date and time in New York, the place, the neighborhood and the page, the caption and the song as a ticket stub", async () => {
    await wrap(stamp());
    expect(screen.getByText("03 OCT 2026  ·  11:41 PM")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Velvet")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("East Village  ·  page 3")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("hello there")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("David Bowie")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Song: Heroes by David Bowie")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Stamp, Velvet")).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("types the caption out: the whole caption is always there for a screen reader", async () => {
    jest.useFakeTimers();
    try {
      await wrap(stamp());
      expect(screen.getByText("hello there")).toBeTruthy(); // the whole caption is in place from the start
      expect(screen.queryByText("hel", { includeHiddenElements: true })).toBeNull();
      await act(async () => jest.advanceTimersByTime(160));
      expect(screen.getByText("hello", { includeHiddenElements: true })).toBeTruthy(); // typed so far, laid over it (hidden from a screen reader)
      await act(async () => jest.advanceTimersByTime(2000));
      expect(screen.getAllByText("hello there")).toHaveLength(1); // finished: one plain line
    } finally {
      jest.useRealTimers();
    }
  });
  it("shows no scrapbook photos and says so when there are none, and no caption line when there is none", async () => {
    await wrap(stamp({ photos: [], photoUrl: null, caption: "", song: null }));
    expect(screen.getByText("no photos on this page")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText(/^Photo \d/)).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText("SOUNDTRACK")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("lists each photo and opens one full screen, with a way to step through them and to close", async () => {
    await wrap(stamp());
    expect(screen.getAllByLabelText(/^Photo \d of 3$/)).toHaveLength(3);
    await fireEvent(screen.getByLabelText("Photo 2 of 3"), "accessibilityAction", { nativeEvent: { actionName: "activate" } });
    expect(screen.getByText("2 of 3")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Photo. Pinch to zoom, double tap to zoom in or out.")).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Next photo"));
    expect(screen.getByText("3 of 3")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Next photo").props.accessibilityState?.disabled).toBe(true);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Close photo"));
    expect(screen.queryByText("3 of 3")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("saves the layout when the owner drops a photo: the dragged one on top, where it was left", async () => {
    await wrap(stamp());
    await act(async () => {
      await fireGestureHandler(getByGestureTestId("photo-pan-ph0"), [
        { state: State.BEGAN, translationX: 0, translationY: 0 },
        { state: State.ACTIVE, translationX: 10, translationY: 10 },
        { state: State.END, translationX: 10, translationY: 10 },
      ]);
    });
    await waitFor(() => expect(mockLayout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    const [id, body] = mockLayout.mock.calls[0]!;
    expect(id).toBe("s1");
    expect(body.photos.map((p: { id: string }) => p.id)).toEqual(["ph1", "ph2", "ph0"]);
    expect(body.photos.map((p: { position: number }) => p.position)).toEqual([0, 1, 2]);
    for (const p of body.photos) {
      expect(p.x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      expect(p.x).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
      expect(p.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      expect(p.y).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    }
  });
  it("tells you whose stamp it is when it isn't yours, and doesn't offer to move its photos", async () => {
    await wrap(stamp({ mine: false }));
    expect(screen.getByText(/@ava · at Velvet/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getAllByLabelText(/^Photo \d of 3$/)[0]!.props.accessibilityHint).toBe("Opens it full screen");
  });
});

describe("pinning", () => {
  it("pins at the end, unpins in place, and refuses a fifth", () => {
    expect(pinChange(["a"], "b")).toEqual({ kind: "pin", ids: ["a", "b"] });
    expect(pinChange(["a", "b", "c"], "b")).toEqual({ kind: "unpin", ids: ["a", "c"] });
    expect(pinChange(["a", "b", "c", "d"], "e")).toEqual({ kind: "full" });
    expect(pinChange(["a", "b", "c", "d"], "d")).toEqual({ kind: "unpin", ids: ["a", "b", "c"] });
    expect(pinChange([], "a")).toEqual({ kind: "pin", ids: ["a"] });
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/worklet-files.test.ts =====

/**
 * Math that runs inside Reanimated worklets (gestures and animated styles) runs on the UI thread, where a plain imported function
 * can't be called. A file-level "worklet" directive makes every function in it callable there. Mocked render tests cannot catch a missing one, so this reads the files: each listed module must start with the directive.
 * worklet-transform.test.js separately checks the installed plugin output for the stageTransform function directive.
 */
import { readFileSync } from "fs";
import path from "path";

const WORKLET_MATH = ["foil.ts", "open-math.ts", "flip-math.ts", "landing.ts", "zoom.ts"];

describe("worklet math files", () => {
  it.each(WORKLET_MATH)("%s starts with the worklet directive and imports nothing that isn't a worklet", (file) => {
    const src = readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "..", file), "utf8");
    const code = src.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, "").replace(/^\s*\/\/.*$/gm, "").trimStart();
    expect(code.startsWith('"worklet";')).toBe(true);
    // Only other worklet math may be imported (a plain module would not be callable on the UI thread).
    for (const m of src.matchAll(/^import .* from "(.*)";$/gm)) expect(["./flip-math", "./foil", "./open-math", "./landing", "./zoom"]).toContain(m[1]);
  });
});


===== src/features/book/__tests__/worklet-transform.test.js =====

/* global describe, it, expect, __dirname */
import { transformSync } from "@babel/core";
import { readFileSync } from "fs";

import { runInNewContext } from "vm";

describe("passport UI runtime helpers", () => {

    const filename = path.join(__dirname, "..", "book-state.ts");
    const { code } = transformSync(readFileSync(filename, "utf8"), {
      filename,
      babelrc: false,
      configFile: false,

      plugins: ["react-native-worklets/plugin", "@babel/plugin-transform-modules-commonjs"],

    expect(onUI(0, 300, 0.55)).toEqual({ scale: 1, translateX: -150 });
