===== src/features/book/bearer.ts =====

/** The bearer page: what a passport prints about its holder. Pure functions, so every line is testable. */
import { hashString } from "@/lib/rng";

export type Bearer = { displayName: string; handle: string; memberSince: string; bio: string };

const MONTHS = ["JAN", "FEB", "MAR", "APR", "MAY", "JUN", "JUL", "AUG", "SEP", "OCT", "NOV", "DEC"];

/** "03 OCT 2026" in UTC, so the same instant reads the same on every phone. */
export function bookDate(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return "";
  return `${String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")} ${MONTHS[d.getUTCMonth()]} ${d.getUTCFullYear()}`;
}

/** A passport number made from the handle alone: "N" and eight digits. It is not the account id and cannot be turned back into it. */
export function documentNumber(handle: string): string {
  const h = hashString(`nostos:${handle.toLowerCase()}`);
  const h2 = hashString(`${handle.toLowerCase()}:nostos`);
  return `N${String(h % 10_000).padStart(4, "0")}${String(h2 % 10_000).padStart(4, "0")}`;
}

/** The standard 7-3-1 check digit used in machine-readable zones. Letters count 10 to 35, filler counts 0. */
export function checkDigit(field: string): number {
  const weights = [7, 3, 1];
  let sum = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < field.length; i++) {
    const c = field[i]!;
    const v = c === "<" ? 0 : c >= "0" && c <= "9" ? c.charCodeAt(0) - 48 : c >= "A" && c <= "Z" ? c.charCodeAt(0) - 55 : 0;
    sum += v * weights[i % 3]!;
  }
  return sum % 10;
}

const LINE = 44;
const fill = (s: string) => (s + "<".repeat(LINE)).slice(0, LINE);
/** Latin capitals and digits only; spaces and punctuation become filler, accents fold, other scripts drop out. */
const clean = (s: string) =>
  s
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, "")
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "<")
    .replace(/^<+|<+$/g, "");

/** Two 44-character lines in the style of a passport's machine zone, for decoration. Always A to Z, 0 to 9 and "<". */
export function machineZone(b: Bearer): [string, string] {
  const words = b.displayName.trim().split(/\s+/).map(clean).filter(Boolean);
  const name = words.length > 1 ? `${words[words.length - 1]}<<${words.slice(0, -1).join("<")}` : words[0] ?? clean(b.handle);
  const line1 = fill(`P<NYC${name}`);
  const num = documentNumber(b.handle);
  const issued = new Date(b.memberSince);
  const ymd = Number.isNaN(issued.getTime()) ? "000000" : `${String(issued.getUTCFullYear() % 100).padStart(2, "0")}${String(issued.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(issued.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  const line2Core = `${num}${checkDigit(num)}NYC${ymd}${checkDigit(ymd)}`;
  return [line1, fill(line2Core)];
}

/** The labeled fields of the bearer page, in reading order. Nothing here is private: no email, no id. */
export function bearerFields(b: Bearer): { label: string; value: string }[] {
  const fields = [
    { label: "SURNAME AND GIVEN NAMES", value: b.displayName },
    { label: "HANDLE", value: `@${b.handle}` },
    { label: "PASSPORT NO.", value: documentNumber(b.handle) },
    { label: "ISSUED", value: bookDate(b.memberSince) },
  ];
  if (b.bio.trim()) fields.push({ label: "REMARKS", value: b.bio.trim() });
  return fields;
}


===== src/features/book/book-state.ts =====

/** The book's four moments, and where the stage (the whole book) sits in each. Pure, so the sequence can be tested. */
export type Phase = "closed" | "opening" | "open" | "closing";
export type PhaseEvent = "tapCover" | "coverSettled" | "closeBook";

export function nextPhase(phase: Phase, event: PhaseEvent): Phase {
  if (phase === "closed" && event === "tapCover") return "opening";
  if (phase === "opening" && event === "coverSettled") return "open";
  if (phase === "open" && event === "closeBook") return "closing";
  if (phase === "closing" && event === "coverSettled") return "closed";
  return phase;
}

export const isOpenish = (phase: Phase) => phase === "opening" || phase === "open";

/**
 * The stage is two pages wide with the cover on its right half. Closed, the cover is centered on the screen at full size;
 * open, the whole spread is centered, scaled down to fit. `p` is 0 closed, 1 open.
 */
export function stageTransform(p: number, pageWidth: number, openScale: number): { scale: number; translateX: number } {
  "worklet";
  const t = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, p));
  const scale = 1 + (openScale - 1) * t;
  return { scale, translateX: -(pageWidth / 2) * scale * (1 - t) };
}

/** What the footer says about the spread on screen. */
export function spreadLabel(spread: number, pageCount: number): string {
  if (spread <= 0) return "Bearer page";
  const left = spread * 2 - 1;
  const right = Math.min(left + 1, pageCount);
  return right > left ? `Pages ${left} and ${right}` : `Page ${left}`;
}


===== src/features/book/dimensions.ts =====

/** The size of the book on this screen. Pure, so it can be tested and reused by every part of the book. */
export const BOOK_ASPECT = 1.42; // height / width of a closed passport
export const MAX_WIDTH_SHARE = 0.84; // of the screen width
export const MAX_HEIGHT_SHARE = 0.6; // of the screen height, leaving room for the counts below

export type BookSize = { width: number; height: number; pageWidth: number; pageHeight: number; spineInset: number };

export function bookSize(screenWidth: number, screenHeight: number): BookSize {
  const width = Math.floor(Math.min(screenWidth * MAX_WIDTH_SHARE, (screenHeight * MAX_HEIGHT_SHARE) / BOOK_ASPECT));
  const height = Math.floor(width * BOOK_ASPECT);
  // An open book is two pages wide: each page is as wide as the closed cover.
  return { width, height, pageWidth: width, pageHeight: height, spineInset: Math.round(width * 0.035) };
}

/** How wide the open book is: two pages. It is scaled to fit the screen, with a margin. */
export function openScale(screenWidth: number, size: BookSize, margin: number): number {
  return Math.min(1, (screenWidth - margin * 2) / (size.pageWidth * 2));
}


===== src/features/book/edges.ts =====

/** The visible edges of the page stacks on either side of an open book, and the ribbons that mark pinned stamps. */
import { bookStyle } from "@/theme";
import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";

/** How many page-edge lines show on each side: more when more pages lie that way, capped. */
export function edgeLines(spreadIndex: number, spreadTotal: number): { left: number; right: number } {
  const behind = Math.max(0, spreadIndex);
  const ahead = Math.max(0, spreadTotal - 1 - spreadIndex);
  return { left: Math.min(bookStyle.maxEdgeLines, behind), right: Math.min(bookStyle.maxEdgeLines, ahead) };
}

/** A ribbon: a strip with a swallowtail notch. Drawn in a box `w` wide and `len` long. */
export function ribbonPath(w: number, len: number, notch = w * 0.5): string {
  const f = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
  return `M0 0 L${f(w)} 0 L${f(w)} ${f(len)} L${f(w / 2)} ${f(len - notch)} L0 ${f(len)} Z`;
}

/** The stamp ids that carry a ribbon on this page: the pinned ones, in the order they were pinned. */
export function ribbonsOnPage(stamps: readonly StampView[], pinnedIds: readonly string[]): string[] {
  const here = new Set(stamps.map((s) => s.id));
  return pinnedIds.filter((id) => here.has(id));
}

/** Pages that hold a pinned stamp, once each, in book order. */
export function pinnedPages(pins: readonly StampView[]): number[] {
  return [...new Set(pins.map((s) => s.page).filter((p): p is number => p !== null))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}


===== src/features/book/emblem.ts =====

/**
 * The Nostos emblem, drawn as SVG path data in a unit circle (centered on 0,0, radius 1): two rings with ticks between them,
 * an eight-point compass star, and a small doorway at its heart. Nostos is the longing to return home: a compass to find the
 * way, a door to come back to. Original artwork; no seal, crest or logo of any country or app.
 */
const f = (n: number) => Number(n.toFixed(4));

const circle = (r: number) => `M ${f(-r)} 0 A ${r} ${r} 0 1 0 ${f(r)} 0 A ${r} ${r} 0 1 0 ${f(-r)} 0 Z`;

/** The eight-point star: long points N E S W, short points on the diagonals. */
export function starPath(long = 0.8, short = 0.5, inner = 0.17): string {
  const pts: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
    const angle = (i * Math.PI) / 8 - Math.PI / 2;
    const r = i % 2 === 0 ? (i % 4 === 0 ? long : short) : inner;
    pts.push(`${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"} ${f(Math.cos(angle) * r)} ${f(Math.sin(angle) * r)}`);
  }
  return `${pts.join(" ")} Z`;
}

/** Short radial ticks between the two rings. */
export function ticksPath(count = 48, from = 0.9, to = 0.97): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const a = (i * 2 * Math.PI) / count;
    out.push(`M ${f(Math.cos(a) * from)} ${f(Math.sin(a) * from)} L ${f(Math.cos(a) * to)} ${f(Math.sin(a) * to)}`);
  }
  return out.join(" ");
}

/** A doorway: an arch on two posts, at the heart of the star. */
export const doorPath = () => "M -0.07 0.1 L -0.07 -0.02 A 0.07 0.07 0 0 1 0.07 -0.02 L 0.07 0.1 Z";

export type Emblem = { rings: string[]; ticks: string; star: string; door: string };

export function emblem(): Emblem {
  return { rings: [circle(1), circle(0.9)], ticks: ticksPath(), star: starPath(), door: doorPath() };
}

/** Scale and place unit-circle path data: the paths are drawn by a canvas that applies `translate(cx, cy) scale(r)`. */
export const emblemTransform = (cx: number, cy: number, r: number) => ({ translate: [cx, cy] as const, scale: r });


===== src/features/book/first-run.ts =====

/** The one-time hint on the closed passport. Remembered on the phone only. */
import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";

export const FIRST_RUN_KEY = "nostos.firstRun.passport.v1";

export const FIRST_RUN_COPY = {
  title: "This is your passport",
  body: "Tap the cover to open it. When you're at a place, tap Stamp to add your first stamp.",
  button: "Got it",
};

export async function hasSeenFirstRun(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    return (await SecureStore.getItemAsync(FIRST_RUN_KEY)) === "1";
  } catch {
    return true; // if it can't be read, don't nag
  }
}

export async function markFirstRunSeen(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(FIRST_RUN_KEY, "1");
  } catch {
    // Nothing to do: the hint may show once more.
  }
}

/** `show` is true only after we know it hasn't been seen (never while loading). `dismiss` remembers it. */
export function useFirstRun(): { show: boolean; dismiss: () => void } {
  const [seen, setSeen] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void hasSeenFirstRun().then((s) => alive && setSeen(s));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);
  const dismiss = useCallback(() => {
    setSeen(true);
    void markFirstRunSeen();
  }, []);
  return { show: seen === false, dismiss };
}


===== src/features/book/flip-math.ts =====

"worklet";
/**
 * Pure math for turning pages. The book keeps ONE number, `pos`, the position between spreads (2.37 is 37 percent of the way
 * from spread 2 to spread 3). Turn k (between spread k and k+1) has progress clamp(pos - k, 0, 1), so every page in flight
 * is a function of that number, and a finger, a tap and a spring all just move it.
 */
export const clamp = (n: number, lo: number, hi: number) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, n));

/** Finger travel for one full turn, in page widths. */
export const SPAN = 1.7;
/** A drag may begin this close to the outer edge, as a share of a page width. */
export const DRAG_ZONE = 0.3;
/** A tap turns the page this close to the outer edge. */
export const TAP_ZONE = 0.16;
/** Release speed (px/s) that counts as a flick. */
export const FLICK = 450;

export type Zone = "next" | "prev" | null;

/** Which outer edge a point is on: the right page's outer edge turns forward, the left page's turns back. `x` runs 0 to 2 pages. */
export function zoneAt(x: number, pageWidth: number, share: number): Zone {
  if (x >= pageWidth * 2 - pageWidth * share && x <= pageWidth * 2) return "next";
  if (x >= 0 && x <= pageWidth * share) return "prev";
  return null;
}

/** Progress of the turn between spread k and k+1: 0 flat on the right, 1 flat on the left. */
export const turnProgress = (pos: number, k: number) => clamp(pos - k, 0, 1);

/** Degrees the page has turned about the spine. Negative: right to left. */
export const flipAngle = (progress: number) => 0 - 180 * clamp(progress, 0, 1);

/** The inside face of a turning page shows past halfway. */
export const showsBack = (progress: number) => progress >= 0.5;

/** Shade on the turning page: none when flat, darkest edge-on. */
export const flipShade = (progress: number) => Math.sin(clamp(progress, 0, 1) * Math.PI) * 0.65;

/** Shade the turning page casts on the page it is leaving or arriving on. */
export const castShade = (progress: number) => Math.sin(clamp(progress, 0, 1) * Math.PI) * 0.45;

/**
 * Where the book is while a finger drags from an edge. The page follows the finger one to one and stays wherever the
 * finger stops, so holding it halfway holds it halfway. It can only move the way the edge turns, and one spread at a time.
 */
export function dragPos(startPos: number, startX: number, x: number, pageWidth: number, zone: Exclude<Zone, null>, total: number): number {
  const last = Math.max(0, total - 1);
  if (zone === "next") return clamp(startPos + (startX - x) / (pageWidth * SPAN), startPos, Math.min(startPos + 1, last));
  return clamp(startPos - (x - startX) / (pageWidth * SPAN), Math.max(startPos - 1, 0), startPos);
}

/** The spread to settle on after a release: the nearest one, nudged along by the flick, never more than one away. */
export function settleTarget(pos: number, from: number, velocityX: number, pageWidth: number, total: number): number {
  const last = Math.max(0, total - 1);
  const ahead = pos - (velocityX / (pageWidth * SPAN)) * 0.2;
  return clamp(Math.round(ahead), Math.max(from - 1, 0), Math.min(from + 1, last));
}

/** One spread forward or back, held inside the book. */
export const stepTarget = (from: number, dir: 1 | -1, total: number) => clamp(from + dir, 0, Math.max(0, total - 1));


===== src/features/book/foil.ts =====

"worklet";
/** The foil highlight: where the bright band sits across the cover (0 = left edge, 1 = right edge), from a drag and from tilt. */
export const SWEEP_REST = 0.5;
export const clamp = (n: number, lo: number, hi: number) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, n));

/** A drag of `dx` points across a cover `width` wide moves the band across the whole cover. */
export const sweepFromDrag = (base: number, dx: number, width: number) => clamp(base + (dx / width) * 1.4, -0.4, 1.4);

/** Device roll (radians, from DeviceMotion) to a sweep offset: tilting about 25 degrees sweeps the band across. */
export const sweepFromTilt = (rollRadians: number) => clamp(SWEEP_REST + (rollRadians / (Math.PI / 7)) * 0.5, -0.4, 1.4);

/** The slow idle shimmer: a small band travel so the foil is alive without being busy. */
export const idleSweep = (t: number) => SWEEP_REST + Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2) * 0.55;

/** Letters of a wordmark with tracking: x offsets given each letter's measured width. */
export function trackedOffsets(widths: readonly number[], tracking: number): { xs: number[]; total: number } {
  const xs: number[] = [];
  let x = 0;
  for (const w of widths) {
    xs.push(x);
    x += w + tracking;
  }
  return { xs, total: Math.max(0, x - tracking) };
}


===== src/features/book/guilloche.ts =====

/** Security-paper patterns drawn behind a page: a rosette (a closed spirograph curve) and bands of interlocking waves. */
import { seeded } from "@/lib/rng";

const f2 = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
const gcd = (a: number, b: number): number => (b === 0 ? a : gcd(b, a % b));

/** A closed hypotrochoid fitted inside a circle of `radius` around (cx, cy). The same seed always gives the same rosette. */
export function rosettePath(seed: string, cx: number, cy: number, radius: number, steps = 720): string {
  const rng = seeded(`rosette:${seed}`);
  const R = 5 + rng.int(5); // 5..9
  let r = 2 + rng.int(3); // 2..4
  if (gcd(R, r) === R) r += 1;
  const d = r * rng.range(0.6, 1.2);
  const turns = r / gcd(R, r);
  const fit = radius / (R - r + d);
  const pts: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
    const t = (i / steps) * Math.PI * 2 * turns;
    const x = (R - r) * Math.cos(t) + d * Math.cos(((R - r) / r) * t);
    const y = (R - r) * Math.sin(t) - d * Math.sin(((R - r) / r) * t);
    pts.push(`${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${f2(cx + x * fit)} ${f2(cy + y * fit)}`);
  }
  return pts.join(" ") + " Z";
}

/** `lines` sine waves across a band `width` by `bandHeight`, each shifted a little, so they weave. */
export function wavePaths(seed: string, width: number, bandHeight: number, lines = 5, samples = 48): string[] {
  const rng = seeded(`waves:${seed}`);
  const freq = 2 + rng.int(3);
  const base = rng.range(0, Math.PI * 2);
  const amp = bandHeight * 0.3;
  return Array.from({ length: lines }, (_, k) => {
    const phase = base + (k / lines) * Math.PI;
    const pts: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= samples; i++) {
      const x = (i / samples) * width;
      const y = bandHeight / 2 + amp * Math.sin((i / samples) * Math.PI * 2 * freq + phase) + (k - (lines - 1) / 2) * (bandHeight * 0.08);
      pts.push(`${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${f2(x)} ${f2(y)}`);
    }
    return pts.join(" ");
  });
}


===== src/features/book/index-data.ts =====

/** The passport's index: neighborhoods, how far through each you are, and the page to jump to. Pure. */
import type { NeighborhoodIndexResponse, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { spreadOfPage } from "@/lib/layout";

export type IndexRow = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  borough: string;
  visited: number;
  total: number;
  complete: boolean;
  percent: number;
  /** The first page with a stamp in this neighborhood, or null when you haven't been. */
  page: number | null;
  /** The spoken form. */
  label: string;
};

/** The earliest page each neighborhood has a stamp on. Hidden and removed stamps (no page) don't count. */
export function firstPageByNeighborhood(stamps: readonly StampView[]): Map<string, number> {
  const pages = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const s of stamps) {
    const id = s.place.neighborhoodId;
    if (!id || s.page === null) continue;
    const have = pages.get(id);
    if (have === undefined || s.page < have) pages.set(id, s.page);
  }
  return pages;
}

export function indexRows(neighborhoods: NeighborhoodIndexResponse["neighborhoods"], stamps: readonly StampView[]): IndexRow[] {
  const pages = firstPageByNeighborhood(stamps);
  return neighborhoods.map((n) => {
    const page = pages.get(n.neighborhoodId) ?? null;
    const progress = `${n.visited} of ${n.total} places`;
    return {
      id: n.neighborhoodId,
      name: n.name,
      borough: n.borough,
      visited: n.visited,
      total: n.total,
      complete: n.complete,
      percent: n.percent,
      page,
      label: `${n.name}, ${n.borough}. ${n.complete ? "Complete. " : ""}${progress}.${page ? ` First stamp on page ${page}.` : " Not started."}`,
    };
  });
}

/** The spread to turn to for a page (spread 0 is the bearer page). */
export const spreadForPage = (page: number): number => spreadOfPage(page);

/** A ring drawn as a stroked circle: how much of the circumference is filled. */
export function ringGeometry(visited: number, total: number, radius: number): { circumference: number; filled: number; fraction: number } {
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const fraction = total <= 0 ? 0 : Math.min(1, Math.max(0, visited / total));
  return { circumference, filled: circumference * fraction, fraction };
}


===== src/features/book/landing-copy.ts =====

import type { NeighborhoodProgress } from "@/api/contracts";
import { seeded } from "@/lib/rng";

/** What the landing says once the stamp is down: where it went, and how far through the neighborhood you are. */
export function landingCopy(page: number | null, progress: NeighborhoodProgress | null): { added: string; progress: string | null; completed: boolean } {
  const added = page === null ? "Stamped" : `Added to page ${page}`;
  if (!progress) return { added, progress: null, completed: false };
  if (progress.justCompleted) return { added, progress: `${progress.name} is complete. ${progress.visited} of ${progress.total} places.`, completed: true };
  if (!progress.newPlace) return { added, progress: `Another visit. ${progress.name}: ${progress.visited} of ${progress.total} places.`, completed: false };
  return { added, progress: `${progress.name}: ${progress.visited} of ${progress.total} places.`, completed: false };
}

/** Where the small photos pinned beside a stamp sit, across a page `width` wide. Same seed, same pins. */
export function photoPins(count: number, width: number, seed: string): { cx: number; size: number; rotation: number }[] {
  const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(6, count));
  if (n === 0) return [];
  const rng = seeded(`pins:${seed}`);
  const size = Math.min(width * 0.2, (width * 0.84) / n - width * 0.02);
  const step = (width * 0.84) / n;
  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ cx: width * 0.08 + step * (i + 0.5), size, rotation: rng.range(-7, 7) }));
}


===== src/features/book/landing.ts =====

"worklet";
/**
 * The stamp landing, as a function of time. The stamp hovers large, slams down, and on impact the ink spreads, the page shakes
 * and a glow flares and fades. Pure: the screen reads `landingFrame(t)` every frame, and the haptic fires at `LANDING.impact`.
 * With Reduce Motion the stamp only fades in: no scale, no shake, no flare.
 */
export const LANDING = { rise: 140, impact: 230, ink: 420, glow: 900, shake: 280, total: 1200 };

export type LandingFrame = {
  /** Stamp scale: large while it hovers, 1 at rest, a small settle after impact. */
  scale: number;
  opacity: number;
  /** 0 at impact to 1 when the ink has finished spreading. */
  inkSpread: number;
  /** 0 to 1: flares on impact, fades out. */
  glow: number;
  shakeX: number;
  shakeY: number;
};

const clamp01 = (n: number) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, n));

export function landingFrame(tMs: number, reduced: boolean): LandingFrame {
  if (reduced) return { scale: 1, opacity: clamp01(tMs / 220), inkSpread: 1, glow: 0, shakeX: 0, shakeY: 0 };
  if (tMs <= 0) return { scale: 1.7, opacity: 0, inkSpread: 0, glow: 0, shakeX: 0, shakeY: 0 };
  if (tMs < LANDING.impact) {
    // Hover (fade up at full size), then fall: slow at first, fast at the end.
    const fall = clamp01((tMs - LANDING.rise) / (LANDING.impact - LANDING.rise));
    return { scale: 1.7 - 0.7 * fall * fall, opacity: 0.9 * clamp01(tMs / LANDING.rise) + 0.1 * fall, inkSpread: 0, glow: 0, shakeX: 0, shakeY: 0 };
  }
  const dt = tMs - LANDING.impact;
  const ink = clamp01(dt / LANDING.ink);
  const attack = LANDING.glow * 0.25;
  const glow = dt < attack ? dt / attack : clamp01(1 - (dt - attack) / (LANDING.glow - attack));
  const left = clamp01(1 - dt / LANDING.shake);
  const amp = 4 * left * left;
  return {
    scale: 1 + 0.05 * Math.sin(ink * Math.PI),
    opacity: 1,
    inkSpread: 1 - (1 - ink) * (1 - ink),
    glow,
    shakeX: amp * Math.sin(dt * 0.11),
    shakeY: amp * 0.6 * Math.cos(dt * 0.13),
  };
}


===== src/features/book/open-math.ts =====

"worklet";
/** Pure math for the cover swing. progress runs 0 (closed) to 1 (open); the cover turns on its left edge. */
export const OPEN_DEGREES = 180;
export const PERSPECTIVE = 1400;

export function coverAngle(progress: number): number {
  const p = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress));
  return -OPEN_DEGREES * p;
}

/** Past the halfway point the cover shows its inside face. */
export function showsInside(progress: number): boolean {
  return progress > 0.5;
}

/** Shade on the cover while it swings: darkest edge-on, clear when flat. 0 to 1. */
export function swingShade(progress: number): number {
  const p = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress));
  return Math.sin(p * Math.PI) * 0.7;
}

/** Shade the open cover casts on the page beneath it. It fades as the cover lies down. */
export function pageShadow(progress: number): number {
  const p = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progress));
  return Math.sin(p * Math.PI) * 0.55;
}


===== src/features/book/pin-change.ts =====

export const MAX_PINS = 4;

export type PinChange = { kind: "pin" | "unpin"; ids: string[] } | { kind: "full" };

/** Pin or unpin one stamp, given the ids pinned now (in order). A fifth pin is refused: unpin one first. */
export function pinChange(current: readonly string[], id: string): PinChange {
  if (current.includes(id)) return { kind: "unpin", ids: current.filter((x) => x !== id) };
  if (current.length >= MAX_PINS) return { kind: "full" };
  return { kind: "pin", ids: [...current, id] };
}


===== src/features/book/scrapbook.ts =====

/** The scrapbook page of a visa spread: up to six photos, laid out by default and rearranged by the owner. */
import type { PhotoLayoutRequest, StampPhotoView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { seeded } from "@/lib/rng";

export type PhotoSpot = { x: number; y: number; rotation: number };

/** Centers as shares of the page, by how many photos there are. */
const BASE: Record<number, readonly (readonly [number, number])[]> = {
  0: [],
  1: [[0.5, 0.5]],
  2: [[0.5, 0.3], [0.5, 0.7]],
  3: [[0.5, 0.22], [0.5, 0.5], [0.5, 0.78]],
  4: [[0.3, 0.3], [0.7, 0.3], [0.3, 0.7], [0.7, 0.7]],
  5: [[0.3, 0.22], [0.7, 0.22], [0.5, 0.5], [0.3, 0.78], [0.7, 0.78]],
  6: [[0.3, 0.2], [0.7, 0.2], [0.3, 0.5], [0.7, 0.5], [0.3, 0.8], [0.7, 0.8]],
};

/** Edge length of one photo, as a share of the page width. */
export function photoShare(count: number): number {
  return [0, 0.66, 0.5, 0.4, 0.4, 0.34, 0.34][Math.max(0, Math.min(6, count))] ?? 0.34;
}

/** Where `count` photos go before anyone has moved them. A little jitter, the same every time for the same seed. */
export function defaultScrapbook(count: number, seed: string): PhotoSpot[] {
  const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(6, count));
  const rng = seeded(`scrapbook:${seed}`);
  return BASE[n]!.map(([x, y]) => ({ x: x + rng.range(-0.02, 0.02), y: y + rng.range(-0.015, 0.015), rotation: rng.range(-6, 6) }));
}

/** Each photo's place: where the owner left it, or the default for photos never moved. In page order (position). */
export function resolveLayout(photos: readonly StampPhotoView[], seed: string): { photo: StampPhotoView; spot: PhotoSpot }[] {
  const ordered = [...photos].sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position);
  const defaults = defaultScrapbook(ordered.length, seed);
  return ordered.map((photo, i) => ({
    photo,
    spot: photo.x !== null && photo.y !== null ? { x: photo.x, y: photo.y, rotation: photo.rotation ?? defaults[i]!.rotation } : defaults[i]!,
  }));
}

/** Drag a photo to the top: it gets the last position and the others close ranks. */
export function bringToFront<T extends { id: string }>(items: readonly T[], id: string): T[] {
  const rest = items.filter((p) => p.id !== id);
  const moved = items.find((p) => p.id === id);
  return moved ? [...rest, moved] : [...items];
}

const r3 = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000;
const unit = (n: number) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, n));

/** The PATCH body that saves a layout: position is the order in the list, x and y are shares of the page. */
export function layoutRequest(items: readonly { id: string; spot: PhotoSpot }[]): PhotoLayoutRequest {
  return { photos: items.map((it, position) => ({ id: it.id, position, x: r3(unit(it.spot.x)), y: r3(unit(it.spot.y)), rotation: r3(Math.min(45, Math.max(-45, it.spot.rotation))) })) };
}


===== src/features/book/slots.ts =====

/** Where the three stamps of a page sit: a staggered base layout plus small seeded jitter, so a page looks stamped by hand. */
import { stampStyle } from "@/theme";
import { seeded } from "@/lib/rng";

export type Placement = { cx: number; cy: number; size: number; rotation: number };

/** Centers as a share of the page width, per slot, and the share of the page height the three slots share. */
const SLOT_X = [0.34, 0.66, 0.4] as const;
const TOP = 0.13;
const BOTTOM = 0.87;
export const STAMP_SHARE = 0.4; // stamp size, as a share of the page width
const JITTER = 0.025; // of the page width, either way

export function slotPlacement(seed: string, slot: number, width: number, height: number): Placement {
  if (!Number.isInteger(slot) || slot < 1 || slot > 3) throw new RangeError("slot is 1, 2 or 3");
  const rng = seeded(`${seed}:${slot}`);
  const pitch = ((BOTTOM - TOP) * height) / 3;
  const jx = rng.range(-JITTER, JITTER) * width;
  const jy = rng.range(-JITTER, JITTER) * width;
  return {
    cx: SLOT_X[slot - 1]! * width + jx,
    cy: TOP * height + pitch * (slot - 0.5) + jy,
    size: STAMP_SHARE * width,
    rotation: rng.range(-stampStyle.maxTiltDeg, stampStyle.maxTiltDeg),
  };
}

/** The unrotated box of a placement, for overlap checks. */
export const boxOf = (p: Placement) => ({ left: p.cx - p.size / 2, top: p.cy - p.size / 2, right: p.cx + p.size / 2, bottom: p.cy + p.size / 2 });

/** Share of the smaller box covered by the overlap of two boxes. 0 when they don't touch. */
export function overlapShare(a: Placement, b: Placement): number {
  const A = boxOf(a);
  const B = boxOf(b);
  const w = Math.min(A.right, B.right) - Math.max(A.left, B.left);
  const h = Math.min(A.bottom, B.bottom) - Math.max(A.top, B.top);
  if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return 0;
  return (w * h) / Math.min(a.size * a.size, b.size * b.size);
}


===== src/features/book/spreads.ts =====

/**
 * How the book is cut into spreads. Spread 0 is the inside front cover (left) facing the bearer page (right). From spread 1,
 * the stamp pages run in pairs: spread s holds pages 2s-1 and 2s, which matches `spreadOfPage` in src/lib/layout.ts.
 */
import { nextSlot, spreadOfPage } from "@/lib/layout";
import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";

export type PageFace = { kind: "inside-cover" } | { kind: "bearer" } | { kind: "stamps"; page: number };
export type Spread = { index: number; left: PageFace; right: PageFace };

/** Stamp pages to show: up to the page the next stamp will land on, rounded up to an even number, at least two. */
export function pageCountFor(stampCount: number): number {
  const next = nextSlot(Math.max(0, stampCount)).page;
  return Math.max(2, next + (next % 2));
}

export const spreadCountFor = (stampCount: number): number => 1 + pageCountFor(stampCount) / 2;

export function spreadAt(index: number): Spread {
  if (!Number.isInteger(index) || index < 0) throw new RangeError("spread index must be a whole number, 0 or more");
  if (index === 0) return { index, left: { kind: "inside-cover" }, right: { kind: "bearer" } };
  return { index, left: { kind: "stamps", page: index * 2 - 1 }, right: { kind: "stamps", page: index * 2 } };
}

/** The spread a stamp page is on. */
export const spreadIndexForPage = (page: number): number => spreadOfPage(page);

/** Stamps grouped by page, each page in slot order. Hidden and removed stamps (page null) never appear. */
export function groupByPage(stamps: readonly StampView[]): Map<number, StampView[]> {
  const pages = new Map<number, StampView[]>();
  for (const s of stamps) {
    if (s.page === null || s.slot === null) continue;
    const list = pages.get(s.page) ?? [];
    list.push(s);
    pages.set(s.page, list);
  }
  for (const list of pages.values()) list.sort((a, b) => (a.slot ?? 0) - (b.slot ?? 0));
  return pages;
}


===== src/features/book/stamp-design.ts =====

/**
 * What a stamp looks like, as a pure function of the place. The same place always gets the same stamp; places differ by
 * SHAPE and DESIGN, never by color (ink is always brass). The border family comes from the neighborhood, so the stamps of one
 * neighborhood read as a set. Nothing here knows about the screen: it returns plain data and path strings.
 */
import { seeded } from "@/lib/rng";

export const SHAPES = ["circle", "oval", "rounded", "octagon", "hexagon"] as const;
export const MOTIFS = ["star", "diamond", "crescent", "door", "compass", "sun"] as const;
export const BORDERS = ["plain", "double", "dotted", "ticks", "dashed", "heavy"] as const;
export type Shape = (typeof SHAPES)[number];
export type Motif = (typeof MOTIFS)[number];
export type Border = (typeof BORDERS)[number];

/** Every stamp is drawn in a 120 by 120 box, the size the stroke tokens in the theme are written for. */
export const STAMP_BOX = 120;
const C = STAMP_BOX / 2;

export type Speckle = { cx: number; cy: number; r: number };
export type StampDesign = {
  shape: Shape;
  motif: Motif;
  border: Border;
  /** Circle stamps curve the name along the top; the others set it in lines. */
  layout: "arc" | "banner";
  arcText: string;
  lines: string[];
  /** 0.55 to 1: smaller for long names. */
  nameScale: number;
  area: string;
  /** How much of the ink took, 0.82 to 1. */
  ink: number;
  /** Spots where the ink did not take, drawn in the color of the paper. */
  speckles: Speckle[];
};

export type StampInput = { placeId: string; placeName: string; neighborhoodId: string | null; neighborhoodName: string };

const ELLIPSIS = "…";
const clip = (s: string, n: number) => (s.length <= n ? s : `${s.slice(0, Math.max(1, n - 1)).trimEnd()}${ELLIPSIS}`);

/** Uppercase lines of at most `maxLine` characters, at most `maxLines` of them, ending in an ellipsis if the name is cut. */
export function fitName(name: string, maxLine = 12, maxLines = 3): string[] {
  const words = name.trim().toUpperCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length === 0) return [];
  const lines: string[] = [];
  let cur = "";
  for (const w of words) {
    const word = clip(w, maxLine);
    if (!cur) cur = word;
    else if ((cur + " " + word).length <= maxLine) cur += " " + word;
    else {
      lines.push(cur);
      cur = word;
    }
  }
  lines.push(cur);
  if (lines.length <= maxLines) return lines;
  const kept = lines.slice(0, maxLines);
  kept[maxLines - 1] = clip(kept[maxLines - 1]! + ELLIPSIS, maxLine);
  return kept;
}

export function stampDesign(input: StampInput): StampDesign {
  const rng = seeded(`stamp:${input.placeId}`);
  const shape = SHAPES[rng.int(SHAPES.length)]!;
  const motif = MOTIFS[rng.int(MOTIFS.length)]!;
  const lines = fitName(input.placeName);
  const longest = Math.max(1, ...lines.map((l) => l.length));
  const dots = Array.from({ length: 6 + rng.int(7) }, () => {
    const a = rng.range(0, Math.PI * 2);
    const d = Math.sqrt(rng.next()) * C * 0.82;
    return { cx: round(C + Math.cos(a) * d), cy: round(C + Math.sin(a) * d), r: round(rng.range(0.8, 2.6)) };
  });
  return {
    shape,
    motif,
    border: borderFor(input.neighborhoodId),
    layout: shape === "circle" ? "arc" : "banner",
    arcText: clip(input.placeName.trim().toUpperCase(), 20),
    lines,
    nameScale: Math.min(1, Math.max(0.55, 10 / longest)),
    area: clip(input.neighborhoodName.trim().toUpperCase(), 18),
    ink: round(rng.range(0.82, 1)),
    speckles: dots,
  };
}

/** The border family of a neighborhood. A place with no neighborhood gets a plain ring. */
export function borderFor(neighborhoodId: string | null): Border {
  if (!neighborhoodId) return "plain";
  return BORDERS[seeded(`border:${neighborhoodId}`).int(BORDERS.length)]!;
}

const round = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
const f = (n: number) => `${round(n)}`;

/** A closed outline for a shape, in the 120 box, `inset` units in from the edge. */
export function shapePath(shape: Shape, inset = 6): string {
  const r = C - inset;
  switch (shape) {
    case "circle":
      return `M${f(C - r)} ${f(C)} A${f(r)} ${f(r)} 0 1 0 ${f(C + r)} ${f(C)} A${f(r)} ${f(r)} 0 1 0 ${f(C - r)} ${f(C)} Z`;
    case "oval": {
      const ry = r * 0.78;
      return `M${f(C - r)} ${f(C)} A${f(r)} ${f(ry)} 0 1 0 ${f(C + r)} ${f(C)} A${f(r)} ${f(ry)} 0 1 0 ${f(C - r)} ${f(C)} Z`;
    }
    case "rounded": {
      const k = r * 0.3;
      const [a, b] = [C - r, C + r];
      return `M${f(a + k)} ${f(a)} L${f(b - k)} ${f(a)} Q${f(b)} ${f(a)} ${f(b)} ${f(a + k)} L${f(b)} ${f(b - k)} Q${f(b)} ${f(b)} ${f(b - k)} ${f(b)} L${f(a + k)} ${f(b)} Q${f(a)} ${f(b)} ${f(a)} ${f(b - k)} L${f(a)} ${f(a + k)} Q${f(a)} ${f(a)} ${f(a + k)} ${f(a)} Z`;
    }
    case "octagon":
      return polygon(8, r, Math.PI / 8);
    case "hexagon":
      return polygon(6, r, 0);
  }
}

function polygon(n: number, r: number, turn: number): string {
  const pts = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => {
    const a = turn + (i / n) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
    return `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${f(C + Math.cos(a) * r)} ${f(C + Math.sin(a) * r)}`;
  });
  return pts.join(" ") + " Z";
}

export type RingSpec = { inset: number; width: number; dash: number[] | null; round: boolean };
/** The rings a border family draws, outermost first. Widths are in the 120 box (see `stampStyle.strokes`). */
export function ringsFor(border: Border, strokes: { ring: number; hair: number }): RingSpec[] {
  const { ring, hair } = strokes;
  switch (border) {
    case "plain": return [{ inset: 6, width: ring, dash: null, round: false }];
    case "double": return [{ inset: 6, width: ring, dash: null, round: false }, { inset: 12, width: hair, dash: null, round: false }];
    case "dotted": return [{ inset: 6, width: ring, dash: [0.1, ring * 1.8], round: true }, { inset: 12, width: hair, dash: null, round: false }];
    case "ticks": return [{ inset: 6, width: ring, dash: [1.4, 3.4], round: false }, { inset: 12, width: hair, dash: null, round: false }];
    case "dashed": return [{ inset: 6, width: ring, dash: [10, 4], round: false }];
    case "heavy": return [{ inset: 6, width: ring * 1.7, dash: null, round: false }, { inset: 13, width: hair, dash: null, round: false }];
  }
}

/** The small figure at the heart of a stamp, drawn around (0, 0) inside a circle of radius `r`. Closed shapes, fill them. */
export function motifPath(motif: Motif, r: number): string {
  switch (motif) {
    case "star": {
      const pts = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => {
        const a = (i / 10) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
        const rad = i % 2 === 0 ? r : r * 0.42;
        return `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${f(Math.cos(a) * rad)} ${f(Math.sin(a) * rad)}`;
      });
      return pts.join(" ") + " Z";
    }
    case "diamond": return `M0 ${f(-r)} L${f(r * 0.7)} 0 L0 ${f(r)} L${f(-r * 0.7)} 0 Z`;
    case "crescent": return `M0 ${f(-r)} A${f(r)} ${f(r)} 0 1 0 0 ${f(r)} A${f(r * 0.75)} ${f(r * 0.75)} 0 1 1 0 ${f(-r)} Z`;
    case "door": return `M${f(-r * 0.6)} ${f(r)} L${f(-r * 0.6)} ${f(-r * 0.15)} A${f(r * 0.6)} ${f(r * 0.6)} 0 0 1 ${f(r * 0.6)} ${f(-r * 0.15)} L${f(r * 0.6)} ${f(r)} Z`;
    case "compass": {
      const pts = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => {
        const a = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
        const rad = i % 2 === 0 ? r : r * 0.3;
        return `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${f(Math.cos(a) * rad)} ${f(Math.sin(a) * rad)}`;
      });
      return pts.join(" ") + " Z";
    }
    case "sun": {
      const pts = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) => {
        const a = (i / 24) * Math.PI * 2;
        const rad = i % 2 === 0 ? r : r * 0.62;
        return `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${f(Math.cos(a) * rad)} ${f(Math.sin(a) * rad)}`;
      });
      return pts.join(" ") + " Z";
    }
  }
}

/** The foil stamp a finished neighborhood earns: rays and a ring, named for the neighborhood. */
export type CompletionDesign = { rays: number; label: string; area: string };
export function completionDesign(neighborhoodId: string, name: string): CompletionDesign {
  const rng = seeded(`complete:${neighborhoodId}`);
  return { rays: 12 + rng.int(7) * 2, label: "COMPLETE", area: clip(name.trim().toUpperCase(), 18) };
}

/** Rays of a completion stamp as one path, from `inner` to `outer` radius around (0, 0). */
export function rayPath(rays: number, inner: number, outer: number): string {
  return Array.from({ length: rays }, (_, i) => {
    const a = (i / rays) * Math.PI * 2;
    return `M${f(Math.cos(a) * inner)} ${f(Math.sin(a) * inner)} L${f(Math.cos(a) * outer)} ${f(Math.sin(a) * outer)}`;
  }).join(" ");
}


===== src/features/book/typewriter.ts =====

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useReducedMotion } from "react-native-reanimated";

export const TYPE_CPS = 38; // characters a second

/** How many characters have been typed after `elapsedMs`. */
export function typedCount(elapsedMs: number, length: number, cps = TYPE_CPS): number {
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(length, Math.floor((elapsedMs / 1000) * cps)));
}

/** Types `text` out a character at a time. With Reduce Motion, all of it at once. */
export function useTypewriter(text: string): number {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  // The count belongs to the text it was typed for, so new text starts again from nothing without an effect to reset it.
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState({ text, n: 0 });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (reduced) return;
    const start = Date.now();
    const id = setInterval(() => {
      const n = typedCount(Date.now() - start, text.length);
      setTyped({ text, n });
      if (n >= text.length) clearInterval(id);
    }, 40);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [text, reduced]);
  if (reduced) return text.length;
  return typed.text === text ? typed.n : 0;
}


===== src/features/book/use-foil-sweep.ts =====

/* eslint-disable react-hooks/immutability -- Reanimated shared values are written through .value by design */
import { DeviceMotion } from "expo-sensors";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { Gesture } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import { Easing, useReducedMotion, useSharedValue, withRepeat, withSpring, withTiming } from "react-native-reanimated";

import { bookMotion } from "@/theme";
import { SWEEP_REST, sweepFromDrag, sweepFromTilt } from "./foil";

/** The foil highlight position (a shared value) and the drag gesture that moves it. Idle shimmer and tilt are off with reduced motion. */
export function useFoilSweep(width: number) {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const sweep = useSharedValue(SWEEP_REST);
  const base = useSharedValue(SWEEP_REST);
  const dragging = useSharedValue(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (reduced) return;
    sweep.value = withRepeat(withTiming(SWEEP_REST + 0.9, { duration: 5200, easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.sin) }), -1, true);
  }, [reduced, sweep]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (reduced) return;
    let sub: { remove: () => void } | null = null;
    let alive = true;
    void DeviceMotion.isAvailableAsync().then((ok) => {
      if (!ok || !alive) return;
      DeviceMotion.setUpdateInterval(33);
      sub = DeviceMotion.addListener((m) => {
        if (!dragging.value && m.rotation) sweep.value = withSpring(sweepFromTilt(m.rotation.gamma), bookMotion.snap);
      });
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      sub?.remove();
    };
  }, [reduced, sweep, dragging]);

  const gesture = Gesture.Pan()
    .onBegin(() => {
      dragging.value = true;
      base.value = sweep.value;
    })
    .onUpdate((e) => {
      sweep.value = sweepFromDrag(base.value, e.translationX, width);
    })
    .onFinalize(() => {
      dragging.value = false;
      sweep.value = withSpring(SWEEP_REST, bookMotion.snap);
    });

  return { sweep, gesture };
}


===== src/features/book/use-passport.ts =====

import { useEffect } from "react";

import type { NeighborhoodIndexResponse, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useMe, useNeighborhoodIndex, usePins, useUserProfile, useUserStamps } from "@/api/queries";

export type PassportData = {
  status: "pending" | "error" | "ready";
  handle: string;
  displayName: string;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
  bio: string;
  memberSince: string;
  stamps: StampView[];
  pins: StampView[];
  counts: { stamps: number; places: number; neighborhoods: number };
  neighborhoods: NeighborhoodIndexResponse["neighborhoods"];
  retry: () => void;
};

/** Everything the book needs about your own passport. Loads every page of stamps (30 at a time) so the page count is right. */
export function usePassport(): PassportData {
  const me = useMe();
  const handle = me.data?.user.handle ?? "";
  const profile = useUserProfile(handle);
  const stamps = useUserStamps(handle, handle !== "");
  const pins = usePins();
  const index = useNeighborhoodIndex();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (stamps.hasNextPage && !stamps.isFetchingNextPage && !stamps.isFetchNextPageError) void stamps.fetchNextPage();
  }, [stamps]);

  const user = profile.data?.access === "full" ? profile.data.user : null;
  const flat = (stamps.data?.pages ?? []).flatMap((p) => p.stamps);
  const failed = me.isError || profile.isError || stamps.isError;
  const ready = !!me.data && !!user && !stamps.isPending;
  return {
    status: failed ? "error" : ready ? "ready" : "pending",
    handle,
    displayName: user?.displayName ?? me.data?.user.displayName ?? "",
    avatarUrl: user?.avatarUrl ?? null,
    bio: user?.bio ?? "",
    memberSince: user?.memberSince ?? me.data?.user.memberSince ?? "",
    stamps: flat,
    pins: pins.data?.stamps ?? [],
    counts: { stamps: user?.counts.stamps ?? flat.length, places: user?.counts.places ?? 0, neighborhoods: index.data?.totals.neighborhoodsStarted ?? 0 },
    neighborhoods: index.data?.neighborhoods ?? [],
    retry: () => {
      void me.refetch();
      void profile.refetch();
      void stamps.refetch();
    },
  };
}


===== src/features/book/visa.ts =====

/** How a stamp's date and place read on its visa page. Dates are New York time: that is where every place is. */
const MONTHS = ["JAN", "FEB", "MAR", "APR", "MAY", "JUN", "JUL", "AUG", "SEP", "OCT", "NOV", "DEC"];

function parts(iso: string) {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
  const f = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: "America/New_York", year: "numeric", month: "numeric", day: "numeric", hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit", hour12: true });
  const get = (type: string) => f.formatToParts(d).find((p) => p.type === type)?.value ?? "";
  return { year: get("year"), month: Number(get("month")), day: get("day").padStart(2, "0"), hour: get("hour"), minute: get("minute"), period: get("dayPeriod").toUpperCase() };
}

/** "03 OCT 2026" and "9:41 PM", in New York. Empty strings for a date that doesn't parse. */
export function visaStamp(iso: string): { date: string; time: string } {
  const p = parts(iso);
  if (!p) return { date: "", time: "" };
  return { date: `${p.day} ${MONTHS[p.month - 1]} ${p.year}`, time: `${p.hour}:${p.minute} ${p.period}` };
}

/** The spoken line under the stamp: "Katz's, East Village, 03 OCT 2026 at 9:41 PM. Page 3." */
export function visaLabel(placeName: string, neighborhood: string, iso: string, page: number | null): string {
  const { date, time } = visaStamp(iso);
  return [placeName, neighborhood, date && `${date} at ${time}`].filter(Boolean).join(", ") + (page ? `. Page ${page}.` : ".");
}


===== src/features/book/zoom.ts =====

"worklet";
/** Math for dragging a photo about a page and for pinching one up full screen. Runs inside gestures, so this file is a worklet. */

export const MIN_SCALE = 1;
export const MAX_SCALE = 4;
export const DOUBLE_TAP_SCALE = 2.5;

export const clampValue = (n: number, lo: number, hi: number) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, n));

/** Keep a photo's center so the whole photo stays on the page, with a small margin. Centers are in page units (px). */
export function clampCenter(cx: number, cy: number, photoSize: number, pageW: number, pageH: number, margin = 0): { cx: number; cy: number } {
  const half = photoSize / 2 + margin;
  return { cx: clampValue(cx, half, Math.max(half, pageW - half)), cy: clampValue(cy, half, Math.max(half, pageH - half)) };
}

export const clampScale = (s: number) => clampValue(s, MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE);

/** Double tap: zoom in, or back out if already zoomed. */
export const doubleTapScale = (current: number) => (current > 1.01 ? 1 : DOUBLE_TAP_SCALE);

/** How far a zoomed photo may be dragged: just enough to bring its edges to the edges of the view, and none at all when it fits. */
export function clampPan(tx: number, ty: number, scale: number, viewW: number, viewH: number): { tx: number; ty: number } {
  const maxX = Math.max(0, (viewW * scale - viewW) / 2);
  const maxY = Math.max(0, (viewH * scale - viewH) / 2);
  return { tx: clampValue(tx, -maxX, maxX), ty: clampValue(ty, -maxY, maxY) };
}


===== src/features/gallery/DesignGuard.tsx =====

import { Redirect } from "expo-router";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

/** The Design gallery exists in development builds only. In a release build (`__DEV__` is false) it sends you home and shows nothing. */
export function DesignGuard({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  if (!__DEV__) return <Redirect href="/" />;
  return <>{children}</>;
}


===== src/features/gallery/__tests__/gallery.test.tsx =====

import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import { Text } from "react-native";

import DesignGallery from "@/app/design/index";
import DesignSection from "@/app/design/[section]";
import { testQueryClient } from "@/test/query-client";
import { DesignGuard } from "../DesignGuard";
import { GALLERY_SECTIONS } from "../sections";
import { SAMPLE_PHOTOS } from "../sample-photos";
import { SAMPLE_PLACES, SAMPLE_VISA_STAMP, sampleStamps } from "../sample";

let mockSection = "palette";
const mockPush = jest.fn();
jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({
  router: { push: (...a: unknown[]) => mockPush(...a) },
  Stack: { Screen: () => null },
  Redirect: ({ href }: { href: string }) => {
    const { Text: T } = jest.requireActual("react-native");
    return <T>{`redirect:${href}`}</T>;
  },
  useLocalSearchParams: () => ({ section: mockSection }),
}));
jest.mock("@/api", () => {
  const client = jest.requireActual("@/api/client");
  return { api: {}, mediaUrl: (u: string) => u, errorText: client.errorText };
});

declare const global: { __DEV__: boolean };
const withDev = (value: boolean, run: () => Promise<void>) => async () => {
  const before = global.__DEV__;
  global.__DEV__ = value;
  try {
    await run();
  } finally {
    global.__DEV__ = before;
  }
};

describe("the Design gallery", () => {
  beforeEach(() => mockPush.mockClear());

  it("has a unique id, a title and a note for every section, and covers every piece of the book", () => {
    const ids = GALLERY_SECTIONS.map((s) => s.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    for (const s of GALLERY_SECTIONS) {
      expect(s.title.length).toBeGreaterThan(2);
      expect(s.note.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
    }
    for (const needed of ["palette", "type", "cover", "open", "bearer", "pages", "flip", "stamps", "completion", "landing", "visa", "viewer", "index", "map"]) expect(ids).toContain(needed);
  });

  it.each(GALLERY_SECTIONS.map((s) => [s.id, s.title] as const))("renders the %s section (%s) on made-up data without a server", async (id) => {
    mockSection = id;
    await render(
      <QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}>
        <DesignSection />
      </QueryClientProvider>,
    );
    expect(screen.toJSON()).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("lists every section and opens one", async () => {
    await render(<DesignGallery />);
    for (const s of GALLERY_SECTIONS) expect(screen.getAllByText(s.title).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(screen.getByText(/made-up data/)).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("says so for a section that doesn't exist", async () => {
    mockSection = "nope";
    await render(<DesignSection />);
    expect(screen.getByText("There is no section called nope.")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it(
    "is development only: with __DEV__ off it shows nothing and sends you home",
    withDev(false, async () => {
      const a = await render(<DesignGuard><Text>inside the gallery</Text></DesignGuard>);
      expect(screen.queryByText("inside the gallery")).toBeNull();
      expect(screen.getByText("redirect:/")).toBeTruthy();
      await a.unmount();
      const b = await render(<DesignGallery />);
      expect(screen.queryByText("Palette")).toBeNull();
      await b.unmount();
    }),
  );
  it("shows its children in development", async () => {
    await render(<DesignGuard><Text>inside the gallery</Text></DesignGuard>);
    expect(screen.getByText("inside the gallery")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("has made-up data that is deterministic and fills pages from the app's own layout", () => {
    expect(sampleStamps(7)).toEqual(sampleStamps(7));
    expect(sampleStamps(7).map((s) => `${s.page}.${s.slot}`)).toEqual(["1.1", "1.2", "1.3", "2.1", "2.2", "2.3", "3.1"]);
    expect(SAMPLE_PLACES.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(12);
    expect(SAMPLE_VISA_STAMP.photos).toHaveLength(4);
  });
  it("uses placeholder photos that are embedded, so it needs no network", () => {
    expect(SAMPLE_PHOTOS).toHaveLength(6);
    for (const p of SAMPLE_PHOTOS) expect(p.startsWith("data:image/png;base64,")).toBe(true);
  });
});

===== src/features/gallery/sample.ts =====

/** Made-up data for the Design gallery: no network, no account. Deterministic. */
import type { NeighborhoodIndexResponse, NeighborhoodProgress, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { pageAndSlot } from "@/lib/layout";
import { SAMPLE_PHOTOS } from "./sample-photos";

export const SAMPLE_USER = { displayName: "Ava Lee", handle: "avaa", memberSince: "2026-03-14T16:00:00.000Z", bio: "Out late, mostly." };

export const SAMPLE_PLACES = [
  ["Katz's Delicatessen", "East Village", "n-ev"], ["Tompkins Square Park", "East Village", "n-ev"], ["Veselka", "East Village", "n-ev"],
  ["The Strand", "Greenwich Village", "n-gv"], ["Washington Square Park", "Greenwich Village", "n-gv"], ["Joe's Pizza", "Greenwich Village", "n-gv"],
  ["The High Line", "Chelsea", "n-ch"], ["Chelsea Market", "Chelsea", "n-ch"], ["Printed Matter", "Chelsea", "n-ch"],
  ["Brooklyn Bridge Park", "Dumbo", "n-du"], ["Grimaldi's Pizzeria", "Dumbo", "n-du"], ["The Metropolitan Museum of Art", "Upper East Side", "n-ues"],
  ["Central Park Boathouse", "Upper East Side", "n-ues"], ["Russ & Daughters", "Lower East Side", "n-les"], ["Doughnut Plant", "Lower East Side", "n-les"],
  ["Rockaway Beach", "Far Rockaway", "n-fr"], ["Prospect Park", "Park Slope", "n-ps"], ["Grand Central Terminal", "Midtown", "n-mt"],
] as const;

export function sampleStamp(i: number, over: Partial<StampView> = {}): StampView {
  const [name, hood, hoodId] = SAMPLE_PLACES[i % SAMPLE_PLACES.length]!;
  const { page, slot } = pageAndSlot(i);
  return {
    id: `sample-${i}`, photoUrl: null, photos: [], caption: "", song: null, page, slot, createdAt: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 5, 1 + i, 2, 30)).toISOString(), editedAt: null,
    place: { id: `place-${i % SAMPLE_PLACES.length}`, slug: `place-${i}`, name, neighborhood: hood, neighborhoodId: hoodId },
    author: { handle: SAMPLE_USER.handle, displayName: SAMPLE_USER.displayName, avatarUrl: null, verified: false },
    mine: true, likes: { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false, ...over,
  };
}

export const sampleStamps = (n: number): StampView[] => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => sampleStamp(i));

export const SAMPLE_VISA_STAMP: StampView = sampleStamp(0, {
  caption: "Pastrami on rye at midnight. The man behind the counter remembered nothing and everything.",
  song: { title: "Heroes", artist: "David Bowie", artUrl: null, sourceId: "1" },
  photos: SAMPLE_PHOTOS.slice(0, 4).map((url, position) => ({ id: `sample-photo-${position}`, url, position, x: null, y: null, rotation: null })),
});

export const SAMPLE_PROGRESS: NeighborhoodProgress = { neighborhoodId: "n-ev", name: "East Village", borough: "Manhattan", visited: 3, total: 3, complete: true, justCompleted: true, newPlace: true };

export const SAMPLE_INDEX: NeighborhoodIndexResponse["neighborhoods"] = [
  { neighborhoodId: "n-ev", name: "East Village", borough: "Manhattan", visited: 3, total: 3, complete: true, completedAt: "2026-06-03T02:30:00.000Z", percent: 100 },
  { neighborhoodId: "n-gv", name: "Greenwich Village", borough: "Manhattan", visited: 2, total: 4, complete: false, completedAt: null, percent: 50 },
  { neighborhoodId: "n-ch", name: "Chelsea", borough: "Manhattan", visited: 1, total: 6, complete: false, completedAt: null, percent: 17 },
  { neighborhoodId: "n-du", name: "Dumbo", borough: "Brooklyn", visited: 0, total: 5, complete: false, completedAt: null, percent: 0 },
];


===== src/features/gallery/sections.tsx =====

import { type ReactNode, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, useWindowDimensions, View } from "react-native";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { BearerPage } from "@/features/book/BearerPage";
import { Counts, Cover } from "@/features/book/Cover";
import { FirstRunHint } from "@/features/book/FirstRunHint";
import { FlipBook } from "@/features/book/FlipBook";
import { IndexList } from "@/features/book/IndexList";
import { InsideCover } from "@/features/book/InsideCover";
import { LandingScene } from "@/features/book/LandingScene";
import { OpeningCover } from "@/features/book/OpeningCover";
import { PageView } from "@/features/book/PageView";
import { PhotoViewer } from "@/features/book/PhotoViewer";
import { Spread } from "@/features/book/Spread";
import { type PageFace, groupByPage, spreadCountFor } from "@/features/book/spreads";
import { BORDERS, MOTIFS, SHAPES, stampDesign } from "@/features/book/stamp-design";
import { CompletionStamp, StampMark } from "@/features/book/StampMark";
import { TicketStub } from "@/features/book/TicketStub";
import { VisaSpread } from "@/features/book/VisaSpread";
import { bookSize } from "@/features/book/dimensions";
import { indexRows } from "@/features/book/index-data";
import { PinMarker } from "@/features/map/PinMarker";
import { PlaceSheet } from "@/features/map/PlaceSheet";
import { bookMotion, colors, fonts, palette, radius, screenPadding, shadows, size, space, stampStyle, type as typeScale } from "@/theme";
import { SAMPLE_PHOTOS } from "./sample-photos";
import { SAMPLE_INDEX, SAMPLE_PROGRESS, SAMPLE_USER, SAMPLE_VISA_STAMP, sampleStamp, sampleStamps } from "./sample";

export type GallerySection = { id: string; title: string; note: string; Component: () => ReactNode };

const input = (i: number) => {
  const s = sampleStamp(i);
  return { placeId: s.place.id, placeName: s.place.name, neighborhoodId: s.place.neighborhoodId, neighborhoodName: s.place.neighborhood };
};

function useBook() {
  const { width, height } = useWindowDimensions();
  const book = bookSize(width, height);
  const page = Math.floor((width - screenPadding * 2) / 2);
  return { book, pageW: page, pageH: Math.floor(page * (book.pageHeight / book.pageWidth)), width };
}

/** A labelled box so a section reads as a specimen. */
const Specimen = ({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }) => (
  <View style={styles.specimen}>
    <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass">
      {label.toUpperCase()}
    </Text>
    {children}
  </View>
);

function Palette() {
  const rows = Object.entries(palette);
  return (
    <>
      <View style={styles.swatches}>
        {rows.map(([name, value]) => (
          <View key={name} style={styles.swatch} accessible accessibilityLabel={`${name}, ${value}`}>
            <View style={[styles.chip, { backgroundColor: value }]} />
            <Text variant="typewriterSmall">{name}</Text>
            <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textTertiary">
              {value}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>
      <Specimen label="Brass glow, hairline, scrim">
        <View style={styles.row}>
          {(["brassGlow", "brassGlowSoft", "hairline", "scrim", "foilHighlight"] as const).map((k) => (
            <View key={k} style={styles.swatch}>
              <View style={[styles.chip, { backgroundColor: colors[k] }]} />
              <Text variant="typewriterSmall">{k}</Text>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      </Specimen>
    </>
  );
}

function Type() {
  return (
    <>
      {(Object.keys(typeScale) as (keyof typeof typeScale)[]).map((variant) => (
        <View key={variant} style={styles.typeRow}>
          <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass">
            {variant}
          </Text>
          <Text variant={variant}>Go somewhere. Get a stamp.</Text>
        </View>
      ))}
      <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textTertiary">
        Serif {fonts.serif}. Typewriter {fonts.mono}. System sans for the rest.
      </Text>
    </>
  );
}

function SpaceSection() {
  return (
    <>
      <Specimen label="Space">
        {Object.entries(space).map(([k, v]) => (
          <View key={k} style={styles.row}>
            <View style={[styles.bar, { width: v }]} />
            <Text variant="typewriterSmall">
              {k} {v}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}
      </Specimen>
      <Specimen label="Radius">
        <View style={styles.row}>
          {Object.entries(radius).map(([k, v]) => (
            <View key={k} style={[styles.box, { borderRadius: Math.min(v, size.avatarLarge / 2) }]} accessible accessibilityLabel={`${k} ${v}`} />
          ))}
        </View>
      </Specimen>
      <Specimen label="Shadow">
        <View style={styles.row}>
          {(Object.keys(shadows) as (keyof typeof shadows)[]).map((k) => (
            <View key={k} style={[styles.box, shadows[k]]} accessible accessibilityLabel={`${k} shadow`} />
          ))}
        </View>
      </Specimen>
      <Specimen label="Motion (springs)">
        <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textSecondary">
          {Object.entries(bookMotion).filter(([k]) => k !== "durations").map(([k, v]) => `${k}: damping ${(v as { damping: number }).damping}, stiffness ${(v as { stiffness: number }).stiffness}`).join("\n")}
        </Text>
      </Specimen>
    </>
  );
}

function Controls() {
  return (
    <>
      <Button title="Primary" />
      <Button title="Secondary" variant="secondary" />
      <Button title="Plain" variant="plain" />
      <Button title="Disabled" disabled />
      <Button title="Loading" loading />
      <ListGroup header="A list">
        <ListRow icon="lock.fill" iconColor="dimBrass" title="Customize, coming soon" chevron />
        <ListRow icon="person.crop.circle" title="Edit profile" subtitle="Ava Lee · @avaa" chevron />
      </ListGroup>
    </>
  );
}

function CoverSection() {
  const { book } = useBook();
  return <Cover width={book.width} height={book.height} counts={{ stamps: 24, places: 18, neighborhoods: 6 }} name={SAMPLE_USER.displayName} />;
}

function OpenSection() {
  const { book } = useBook();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const w = Math.round(book.width * 0.6);
  const h = Math.round(book.height * 0.6);
  return (
    <>
      <View style={{ height: h + space.xl, paddingLeft: w }}>
        <OpeningCover
          width={w}
          height={h}
          open={open}
          outside={<Cover width={w} height={h} counts={{ stamps: 0, places: 0, neighborhoods: 0 }} showCounts={false} />}
          page={<BearerPage width={w} height={h} bearer={SAMPLE_USER} avatarUrl={null} />}
        />
      </View>
      <Button title={open ? "Close the cover" : "Open the cover"} variant="secondary" onPress={() => setOpen((o) => !o)} />
    </>
  );
}

function BearerSection() {
  const { book } = useBook();
  const w = Math.round(book.width * 0.85);
  return <BearerPage width={w} height={Math.round(w * 1.42)} bearer={SAMPLE_USER} avatarUrl={null} />;
}

const STAMPS = sampleStamps(14);
const BY_PAGE = groupByPage(STAMPS);

function PagesSection() {
  const { pageW, pageH } = useBook();
  return (
    <Spread pageWidth={pageW} pageHeight={pageH} spreadIndex={1} spreadTotal={4}>
      <View style={styles.pair}>
        <PageView page={1} side="left" width={pageW} height={pageH} stamps={BY_PAGE.get(1) ?? []} pinnedIds={["sample-1"]} />
        <PageView page={2} side="right" width={pageW} height={pageH} stamps={BY_PAGE.get(2) ?? []} />
      </View>
    </Spread>
  );
}

function FlipSection() {
  const { pageW, pageH } = useBook();
  const [spread, setSpread] = useState(0);
  const total = spreadCountFor(STAMPS.length);
  const face = (f: PageFace, side: "left" | "right") =>
    f.kind === "inside-cover" ? <InsideCover width={pageW} height={pageH} /> : f.kind === "bearer" ? <BearerPage width={pageW} height={pageH} bearer={SAMPLE_USER} avatarUrl={null} /> : <PageView page={f.page} side={side} width={pageW} height={pageH} stamps={BY_PAGE.get(f.page) ?? []} pinnedIds={["sample-4"]} />;
  return (
    <>
      <FlipBook pageWidth={pageW} pageHeight={pageH} total={total} spread={spread} onSpreadChange={setSpread} renderFace={face} />
      <View style={styles.row}>
        <Button title="Back" variant="secondary" compact block={false} disabled={spread === 0} onPress={() => setSpread((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1))} />
        <Button title="Next" variant="secondary" compact block={false} disabled={spread >= total - 1} onPress={() => setSpread((s) => Math.min(total - 1, s + 1))} />
      </View>
      <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textSecondary">
        Drag from an outer edge, or tap it. Spread {spread + 1} of {total}.
      </Text>
    </>
  );
}

function StampsSection() {
  const base = input(0);
  return (
    <>
      <Specimen label="Eighteen places">
        <View style={styles.grid}>
          {Array.from({ length: 18 }, (_, i) => (
            <StampMark key={i} input={input(i)} size={size.avatarLarge * 1.5} />
          ))}
        </View>
      </Specimen>
      <Specimen label="Shapes">
        <View style={styles.grid}>
          {SHAPES.map((shape) => (
            <StampMark key={shape} input={base} size={size.avatarLarge * 1.5} design={{ ...stampDesign(base), shape, layout: shape === "circle" ? "arc" : "banner" }} />
          ))}
        </View>
      </Specimen>
      <Specimen label="Borders (one per neighborhood)">
        <View style={styles.grid}>
          {BORDERS.map((border) => (
            <StampMark key={border} input={base} size={size.avatarLarge * 1.5} design={{ ...stampDesign(base), shape: "circle", layout: "arc", border }} />
          ))}
        </View>
      </Specimen>
      <Specimen label="Figures">
        <View style={styles.grid}>
          {MOTIFS.map((motif) => (
            <StampMark key={motif} input={base} size={size.avatarLarge * 1.5} design={{ ...stampDesign(base), motif }} />
          ))}
        </View>
      </Specimen>
      <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textSecondary">
        Ink is {stampStyle.ink === colors.brass ? "brass" : "brass"} on every stamp. Variety is shape and design only.
      </Text>
    </>
  );
}

function CompletionSection() {
  return (
    <View style={styles.center}>
      <CompletionStamp neighborhoodId="n-ev" name="East Village" size={size.avatarLarge * 3} />
    </View>
  );
}

function LandingSection() {
  const { pageW, pageH } = useBook();
  const [run, setRun] = useState(0);
  const stamp = useMemo(() => sampleStamp(7, { photos: SAMPLE_PHOTOS.slice(0, 3).map((url, position) => ({ id: `lp${position}`, url, position, x: null, y: null, rotation: null })) }), []);
  const w = Math.round(pageW * 1.1);
  return (
    <>
      <View style={{ height: Math.round(w * 1.42) + size.hitTarget * 4 }}>
        <LandingScene key={run} stamp={stamp} others={BY_PAGE.get(stamp.page ?? 1)?.filter((s) => s.id !== stamp.id) ?? []} progress={SAMPLE_PROGRESS} pageWidth={w} pageHeight={Math.round(w * 1.42)} onDone={() => setRun((n) => n + 1)} />
      </View>
      <Button title="Replay the landing" variant="secondary" onPress={() => setRun((n) => n + 1)} />
      <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textSecondary">
        {pageH > 0 ? "Slam, ink, glow, shake and a haptic knock at impact. Done also replays." : ""}
      </Text>
    </>
  );
}

function VisaSection() {
  return <VisaSpread stamp={SAMPLE_VISA_STAMP} onSaveLayout={() => undefined} />;
}

function ViewerSection() {
  const [at, setAt] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const photos = SAMPLE_PHOTOS.map((url, position) => ({ id: `v${position}`, url, position, x: null, y: null, rotation: null }));
  return (
    <>
      <Button title="Open the photo viewer" variant="secondary" onPress={() => setAt(0)} />
      <PhotoViewer photos={photos} index={at} onClose={() => setAt(null)} />
    </>
  );
}

function IndexSection() {
  const rows = indexRows(SAMPLE_INDEX, STAMPS);
  return (
    <View style={styles.indexBox}>
      <IndexList rows={rows} onJump={() => undefined} onClose={() => undefined} />
    </View>
  );
}

function MapSection() {
  const i = input(0);
  return (
    <>
      <View style={styles.row}>
        <PinMarker kind="lit" selected={false} input={i} />
        <PinMarker kind="lit" selected input={i} />
        <PinMarker kind="dim" selected={false} input={i} />
        <PinMarker kind="dim" selected input={i} />
      </View>
      <View style={styles.sheetBox}>
        <PlaceSheet model={{ title: "Katz's Delicatessen", subtitle: "East Village · 205 E Houston St", status: "Stamped 3 times. Last on 03 OCT 2026.", distance: "40 m away", canStamp: true }} onClose={() => undefined} onStamp={() => undefined} onOpenPlace={() => undefined} />
      </View>
    </>
  );
}

      <FirstRunHint onDismiss={() => undefined} />

}
