===== src/features/location/__tests__/dev-simulator.test.tsx =====

/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports -- jest.isolateModules needs require() */
/**
 * The location simulator must be impossible to reach in production builds, where `__DEV__` is false.
 */
jest.mock("expo-location", () => ({
  PermissionStatus: { GRANTED: "granted", UNDETERMINED: "undetermined", DENIED: "denied" },
  Accuracy: { High: 4 },
  getForegroundPermissionsAsync: jest.fn(),
  requestForegroundPermissionsAsync: jest.fn(),
  watchPositionAsync: jest.fn(),
  getCurrentPositionAsync: jest.fn(),
}));

type G = { __DEV__: boolean };

function loadWithDev(dev: boolean) {
  const g = globalThis as unknown as G;
  const previous = g.__DEV__;
  g.__DEV__ = dev;
  let mods!: {
    sim: typeof import("../dev-simulator");
    store: typeof import("../location-store");
    chip: typeof import("../sim-chip");
    rtl: typeof import("@testing-library/react-native/pure");
    React: typeof import("react");
  };
  jest.isolateModules(() => {
    mods = {
      sim: require("../dev-simulator"),
      store: require("../location-store"),
      chip: require("../sim-chip"),
      // Same module registry as the component, so there is one copy of React.
      rtl: require("@testing-library/react-native/pure"),
      React: require("react"),
    };
  });
  return {
    ...mods,
    restore: () => {
      g.__DEV__ = previous;
    },
  };
}

describe("dev location simulator", () => {
  it("works in development", () => {
    const { sim, store, restore } = loadWithDev(true);
    try {
      expect(sim.devSimulator).not.toBeNull();
      expect(sim.simulateAtPress).toBeDefined();
      sim.simulateAtPress!({ latitude: 40.72, longitude: -73.98 });
      expect(store.locationStore.current()).toEqual({ lat: 40.72, lng: -73.98 });
      sim.devSimulator!.clear();
      expect(store.locationStore.get().simulated).toBeNull();
    } finally {
      restore();
    }
  });

  it("does not exist in production builds", async () => {
    const { sim, store, chip, rtl, React, restore } = loadWithDev(false);
    try {
      expect(sim.devSimulator).toBeNull();
      // No long-press handler is attached to the map at all.
      expect(sim.simulateAtPress).toBeUndefined();
      // Even the store's internal setter refuses.
      store.locationStore._setSimulated({ lat: 1, lng: 2 });
      expect(store.locationStore.get().simulated).toBeNull();
      expect(store.locationStore.current()).toBeNull();
      // And the chip never renders.
      const screen = await rtl.render(React.createElement(chip.SimulatedLocationChip));
      expect(screen.queryByTestId("sim-chip")).toBeNull();
      expect(screen.queryByText("Simulated location")).toBeNull();
    } finally {
      restore();
    }
  });
});


===== src/features/location/__tests__/location-store.test.ts =====

import { locationStore, SORT_ANCHOR_M } from "../location-store";

const mockListeners: ((p: { coords: { latitude: number; longitude: number; accuracy: number }; timestamp: number }) => void)[] = [];

jest.mock("expo-location", () => ({
  PermissionStatus: { GRANTED: "granted", UNDETERMINED: "undetermined", DENIED: "denied" },
  Accuracy: { High: 4 },
  getForegroundPermissionsAsync: jest.fn(async () => ({ status: "granted", canAskAgain: true })),
  requestForegroundPermissionsAsync: jest.fn(),
  watchPositionAsync: jest.fn(async (_opts: unknown, cb: (typeof mockListeners)[number]) => {
    mockListeners.push(cb);
    return { remove: jest.fn() };
  }),
  getCurrentPositionAsync: jest.fn(),
}));

const move = (lat: number, lng: number) => mockListeners.forEach((cb) => cb({ coords: { latitude: lat, longitude: lng, accuracy: 5 }, timestamp: Date.now() }));

describe("sort anchor", () => {
  it("only moves after the user travels more than the threshold", async () => {
    await locationStore.refreshPermission();
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    move(40.72, -73.98);
    const first = locationStore.get().anchor;
    expect(first).toEqual({ lat: 40.72, lng: -73.98 });

    // ~11 m north: same anchor object, so memoized sorts don't rerun.
    move(40.7201, -73.98);
    expect(locationStore.get().anchor).toBe(first);
    expect(locationStore.current()).toEqual({ lat: 40.7201, lng: -73.98 });

    // ~111 m north: anchor follows.
    move(40.721, -73.98);
    expect(locationStore.get().anchor).toEqual({ lat: 40.721, lng: -73.98 });
    expect(SORT_ANCHOR_M).toBe(50);
  });
});


===== src/features/location/dev-simulator.ts =====

/**
 * Development-only location simulator: long-press the map to pretend you're there.
 *
 * `devSimulator` is `null` whenever `__DEV__` is false. Release builds are minified with
 * `__DEV__ === false`, so the branch below is removed and nothing can set a fake location.
 * The server still enforces the geofence against whatever coordinates arrive.
 */
import type { Coords } from "@/lib/geo";
import { locationStore } from "./location-store";

export type DevSimulator = {
  set: (coords: Coords) => void;
  clear: () => void;
};

export const devSimulator: DevSimulator | null = __DEV__
  ? {
      set: (coords) => locationStore._setSimulated(coords),
      clear: () => locationStore._setSimulated(null),
    }
  : null;

/** Map long-press handler, or undefined outside development (the map then has no long-press at all). */
export const simulateAtPress: ((coordinate: { latitude: number; longitude: number }) => void) | undefined = devSimulator
  ? (c) => devSimulator!.set({ lat: c.latitude, lng: c.longitude })
  : undefined;


===== src/features/location/location-store.ts =====

/**
 * The user's location, for display (Nearby sort, map dot) and for the coordinates sent with
 * reports, photos and presence pings. The server decides the geofence from those coordinates.
 *
 * Permission is when-in-use only, and only requested after the pre-permission screen.
 */
import * as Location from "expo-location";
import { useEffect, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { AppState } from "react-native";

import { distanceMeters, type Coords } from "@/lib/geo";

export type PermissionState = "unknown" | "undetermined" | "granted" | "denied";

type State = {
  permission: PermissionState;
  /** Real GPS fix. */
  device: (Coords & { accuracy: number | null; at: number }) | null;
  /** Development-only override (see dev-simulator.ts). Always null in release builds. */
  simulated: Coords | null;
  /** Position for distance sorting; moves only in steps of SORT_ANCHOR_M. */
  anchor: Coords | null;
};

export const SORT_ANCHOR_M = 50;

let state: State = { permission: "unknown", device: null, simulated: null, anchor: null };
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
let watcher: Location.LocationSubscription | null = null;

function set(patch: Partial<Omit<State, "anchor">>) {
  const next = { ...state, ...patch };
  const live = next.simulated ?? (next.device ? { lat: next.device.lat, lng: next.device.lng } : null);
  let anchor = state.anchor;
  if (!live) anchor = null;
  else if (!anchor || patch.simulated !== undefined || distanceMeters(anchor, live) > SORT_ANCHOR_M) anchor = live;
  state = { ...next, anchor };
  for (const l of listeners) l();
}

function toPermission(status: Location.PermissionStatus, canAskAgain: boolean): PermissionState {
  if (status === Location.PermissionStatus.GRANTED) return "granted";
  if (status === Location.PermissionStatus.UNDETERMINED || canAskAgain) return "undetermined";
  return "denied";
}

async function startWatching() {
  if (watcher) return;
  try {
    watcher = await Location.watchPositionAsync(
      { accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High, distanceInterval: 10, timeInterval: 5000 },
      (pos) => set({ device: { lat: pos.coords.latitude, lng: pos.coords.longitude, accuracy: pos.coords.accuracy, at: pos.timestamp } }),
    );
  } catch {
    watcher = null;
  }
}

export const locationStore = {
  get: () => state,
  subscribe(l: () => void) {
    listeners.add(l);
    return () => listeners.delete(l);
  },

  /** Read the current permission without prompting; start watching if already granted. */
  async refreshPermission(): Promise<PermissionState> {
    const res = await Location.getForegroundPermissionsAsync();
    const permission = toPermission(res.status, res.canAskAgain);
    set({ permission });
    if (permission === "granted") void startWatching();
    return permission;
  },

  /** Show the system prompt. Call only from the pre-permission screen. */
  async request(): Promise<PermissionState> {
    const res = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
    const permission = toPermission(res.status, res.canAskAgain);
    set({ permission });
    if (permission === "granted") void startWatching();
    return permission;
  },

  /** Where to send as the user's position: the simulated point in development, else the device fix. */
  current(): Coords | null {
    if (state.simulated) return state.simulated;
    return state.device ? { lat: state.device.lat, lng: state.device.lng } : null;
  },

  /** A fresh fix for posting (falls back to the last watched one). */
  async currentForPosting(): Promise<Coords | null> {
    if (state.simulated) return state.simulated;
    if (state.permission !== "granted") return null;
    try {
      const pos = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({ accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High });
      const device = { lat: pos.coords.latitude, lng: pos.coords.longitude, accuracy: pos.coords.accuracy, at: pos.timestamp };
      set({ device });
      return { lat: device.lat, lng: device.lng };
    } catch {
      return locationStore.current();
    }
  },

  /** @internal Used by dev-simulator.ts only. */
  _setSimulated(simulated: Coords | null) {
    if (!__DEV__) return;
    set({ simulated });
  },
};

export function useLocationState(): State {
  return useSyncExternalStore(locationStore.subscribe, locationStore.get, locationStore.get);
}

/** The effective position for display: simulated (dev) or device. */
export function useUserCoords(): Coords | null {
  const s = useLocationState();
  if (s.simulated) return s.simulated;
  return s.device ? { lat: s.device.lat, lng: s.device.lng } : null;
}

/** Re-read the permission when the app returns to the foreground (e.g. back from Settings). */
export function usePermissionRefresh(): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    void locationStore.refreshPermission();
    const sub = AppState.addEventListener("change", (s) => {
      if (s === "active") void locationStore.refreshPermission();
    });
    return () => sub.remove();
  }, []);
}

/**
 * The user's position for sorting by distance. It only changes after they move more than
 * `SORT_ANCHOR_M`, so lists don't reshuffle under a finger on every GPS tick. Stable identity.
 */
export function useSortCoords(): Coords | null {
  return useSyncExternalStore(locationStore.subscribe, () => state.anchor, () => state.anchor);
}


===== src/features/location/sim-chip.tsx =====

import { Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";

import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { devSimulator } from "@/features/location/dev-simulator";
import { useLocationState } from "@/features/location/location-store";
import { colors, hairlineWidth, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** "Simulated location" chip that clears the fake location. Renders nothing outside development. */
export function SimulatedLocationChip() {
  const { simulated } = useLocationState();
  if (!devSimulator || !simulated) return null;
  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={devSimulator.clear}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel="Simulated location. Clear it."
      style={({ pressed }) => [styles.chip, pressed && styles.pressed]}
      hitSlop={space.xs}
      testID="sim-chip"
    >
      <Icon name="location.slash" color="warning" size={size.iconTiny} />
      <Text variant="footnote" weight="semibold">
        Simulated location
      </Text>
      <Icon name="xmark" color="textSecondary" size={size.iconTiny} />
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  chip: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    alignSelf: "flex-start",
    gap: space.xs,
    minHeight: size.hitTarget - space.xs,
    paddingHorizontal: space.sm,
    borderRadius: radius.pill,
    backgroundColor: colors.elevated,
    borderWidth: hairlineWidth,
    borderColor: colors.hairline,
  },
  pressed: { backgroundColor: colors.surface },
});


===== src/features/map/MapScreen.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import MapView, { Marker } from "react-native-maps";

import { useMe, usePlaces, useUserPlaces } from "@/api/queries";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { placesRegion } from "@/features/people/places";
import { useUserCoords } from "@/features/location/location-store";
import { formatDistance } from "@/lib/format";
import { HOME_REGION } from "@/lib/geo";
import { colors, radius, selectedMarkerScale, size, space } from "@/theme";
import { type MapRegion, pinKind, sheetModel, statsByPlace, visiblePlaces } from "./map-model";
import { PinMarker } from "./PinMarker";
import { PlaceSheet } from "./PlaceSheet";

/**
 * The map: dark, with the places you have stamped glowing brass as little stamps and the rest as dim dots. Tap a pin for its sheet.
 * Your own position is a small bone ring (not the system's blue dot: nothing here is another color).
 */
export function MapScreen() {
  const me = useMe();
  const places = usePlaces();
  const mine = useUserPlaces(me.data?.user.handle ?? "", !!me.data);
  const here = useUserCoords();
  const stats = useMemo(() => statsByPlace(mine.data?.places ?? []), [mine.data]);
  const start = useMemo(() => placesRegion(mine.data?.places ?? []) ?? HOME_REGION, [mine.data]);
  const [region, setRegion] = useState<MapRegion>(HOME_REGION);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const all = useMemo(() => places.data?.places ?? [], [places.data]);
  const drawn = useMemo(() => visiblePlaces(all, region, stats, selectedId), [all, region, stats, selectedId]);
  const selected = all.find((p) => p.id === selectedId) ?? null;
  const model = selected ? sheetModel(selected, stats.get(selected.id), here, formatDistance) : null;

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <MapView
        style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
        initialRegion={start}
        userInterfaceStyle="dark"
        showsPointsOfInterests={false}
        showsCompass={false}
        pitchEnabled={false}
        onRegionChangeComplete={setRegion}
        onPress={() => setSelectedId(null)}
        accessibilityLabel="Map of your places"
      >
        {drawn.map((p) => {
          const kind = pinKind(p.id, stats);
          const isSelected = p.id === selectedId;
          return (
            <Marker
              key={`${p.id}-${kind}-${isSelected}`}
              coordinate={{ latitude: p.lat, longitude: p.lng }}
              onPress={() => setSelectedId(p.id)}
              tracksViewChanges={false}
              anchor={{ x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }}
              accessibilityLabel={`${p.name}, ${kind === "lit" ? "stamped" : "not stamped yet"}`}
              zIndex={isSelected ? 3 : kind === "lit" ? 2 : 1}
            >
              <PinMarker kind={kind} selected={isSelected} input={{ placeId: p.id, placeName: p.name, neighborhoodId: p.neighborhoodId, neighborhoodName: p.neighborhood }} />
            </Marker>
          );
        })}
        {here ? (
          <Marker coordinate={{ latitude: here.lat, longitude: here.lng }} tracksViewChanges={false} anchor={{ x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }} zIndex={4} accessibilityLabel="You are here" pointerEvents="none">
            <View style={styles.you} />
          </Marker>
        ) : null}
      </MapView>
      {places.data?.attribution ? (
        <View style={styles.attribution} pointerEvents="none">
          <Text variant="caption" color="boneDim">
            {places.data.attribution.text}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : null}
      <PlaceSheet
        model={model}
        onClose={() => setSelectedId(null)}
        onStamp={() => {
          setSelectedId(null);
          router.push("/stamp/new");
        }}
        onOpenPlace={selected ? () => router.push({ pathname: "/place/[id]", params: { id: selected.id } }) : undefined}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

const YOU = size.marker / 2;
const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  you: { width: YOU, height: YOU, borderRadius: YOU / 2, borderWidth: size.markerRing, borderColor: colors.bone, backgroundColor: colors.background, transform: [{ scale: selectedMarkerScale }] },
  attribution: { position: "absolute", left: space.sm, top: space.xxxl + space.md, paddingHorizontal: space.xs, paddingVertical: space.xxs, borderRadius: radius.chip, backgroundColor: colors.scrim },
});


===== src/features/map/PinMarker.tsx =====

import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { StampMark } from "@/features/book/StampMark";
import type { StampInput } from "@/features/book/stamp-design";
import { colors, radius, shadows, size } from "@/theme";
import type { PinKind } from "./map-model";

const LIT = size.marker;
const DIM = size.vibeDot;

/**
 * A pin on the map. Lit (you've stamped the place): a little brass stamp of that place in a glowing ring. Dim: a small dot.
 * Selected pins are drawn larger by the screen (it passes `selected`); a key change redraws the snapshot the map keeps.
 */
export function PinMarker({ kind, selected, input }: { kind: PinKind; selected: boolean; input: StampInput }) {
  if (kind === "dim") {
    return <View style={[styles.dim, selected && styles.dimSelected]} />;
  }
  const box = selected ? Math.round(LIT * 1.25) : LIT;
  return (
    <View style={[styles.lit, shadows.glow, { width: box, height: box, borderRadius: box / 2 }]}>
      <StampMark input={input} size={box - size.markerRing * 2} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  lit: { alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderWidth: size.markerRing, borderColor: colors.brass },
  dim: { width: DIM, height: DIM, borderRadius: radius.pill, backgroundColor: colors.dimBrass, opacity: 0.8 },
  dimSelected: { width: DIM * 2, height: DIM * 2, backgroundColor: colors.brass, opacity: 1 },
});


===== src/features/map/PlaceSheet.tsx =====

/* eslint-disable react-hooks/immutability -- Reanimated shared values are written through .value by design */
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { Gesture, GestureDetector } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import Animated, { useAnimatedStyle, useSharedValue, withSpring } from "react-native-reanimated";
import { scheduleOnRN } from "react-native-worklets";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { bookMotion, bookStyle, colors, radius, screenPadding, shadows, size, space } from "@/theme";
import { type SheetModel, sheetShouldClose } from "./map-model";

const HEIGHT = size.heroHeight;

/** The bottom sheet for the pin you tapped: its name, where it is, whether you've stamped it, and how far you are. Swipe it down to close. */
export function PlaceSheet({ model, onClose, onStamp, onOpenPlace }: { model: SheetModel | null; onClose: () => void; onStamp: () => void; onOpenPlace?: () => void }) {
  const y = useSharedValue<number>(HEIGHT);
  const startY = useSharedValue(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    y.value = withSpring(model ? 0 : HEIGHT, bookMotion.snap);
  }, [model, y]);

  const drag = Gesture.Pan()
    .withTestId("sheet-pan")
    .onBegin(() => {
      startY.value = y.value;
    })
    .onUpdate((e) => {
      y.value = Math.max(0, startY.value + e.translationY);
    })
    .onEnd((e) => {
      if (sheetShouldClose(e.translationY, e.velocityY, HEIGHT)) scheduleOnRN(onClose);
      else y.value = withSpring(0, bookMotion.snap);
    });
  const style = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({ transform: [{ translateY: y.value }] }));

  if (!model) return null;
  return (
    <GestureDetector gesture={drag}>
      <Animated.View style={[styles.sheet, style]} accessibilityViewIsModal={false}>
        <View style={styles.grabber} accessibilityElementsHidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants" />
        <View style={styles.headRow}>
          <View style={styles.names}>
            <Text variant="title2" color="brass" numberOfLines={2} accessibilityRole="header">
              {model.title}
            </Text>
            {model.subtitle ? (
              <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="textSecondary">
                {model.subtitle}
              </Text>
            ) : null}
          </View>
          <Pressable onPress={onClose} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Close" hitSlop={space.sm} style={styles.close}>
            <Icon name="xmark" color="dimBrass" size={size.iconSmall} />
          </Pressable>
        </View>
        <Text variant="typewriter" color="textPrimary">
          {model.status}
        </Text>
        {model.distance ? (
          <Text variant="typewriterSmall" color="dimBrass">
            {model.distance}
          </Text>
        ) : null}
        <View style={styles.actions}>
          {model.canStamp ? <Button title="Stamp here" icon="seal.fill" block={false} onPress={onStamp} /> : null}
          {onOpenPlace ? <Button title="Place page" variant="secondary" block={false} onPress={onOpenPlace} /> : null}
        </View>
      </Animated.View>
    </GestureDetector>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  sheet: {
    position: "absolute", left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, minHeight: HEIGHT, padding: screenPadding, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.xs,
    backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderTopLeftRadius: radius.sheet, borderTopRightRadius: radius.sheet, borderTopWidth: bookStyle.pageLine, borderColor: colors.dimBrass, ...shadows.soft,
  },
  grabber: { alignSelf: "center", width: size.avatarLarge / 2, height: space.xxs, borderRadius: radius.pill, backgroundColor: colors.boneFaint },
  headRow: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: space.sm },
  names: { flex: 1, gap: space.xxs },
  close: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  actions: { flexDirection: "row", gap: space.sm, marginTop: space.sm },
});

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

const place = (id: string, over: Partial<Place> = {}): Place => ({ id, slug: id, name: `Place ${id}`, address: "", neighborhood: "East Village", lat: 40.7222, lng: -73.9855, geofenceMeters: 100, neighborhoodId: "n1", fromOsm: false, ...over });

  place: { id: p.id, slug: p.slug, name: p.name, neighborhood: p.neighborhood, lat: p.lat, lng: p.lng }, stamps, latestStamp: { id: "s", createdAt: "2026-10-04T03:41:00.000Z", photoUrl: null, caption: "" },

describe("map model", () => {
  it("lights the places you have stamped and dims the rest", () => {
    const stats = statsByPlace([mineRow(place("a"), 3)]);
    expect(pinKind("a", stats)).toBe("lit");
    expect(pinKind("b", stats)).toBe("dim");
    expect(stats.get("a")).toEqual({ stamps: 3, latestAt: "2026-10-04T03:41:00.000Z" });
  });
  it("draws only places in view, lit ones first, the selected one always, and never more than the cap", () => {
    const near = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => place(`n${i}`, { lat: 40.7222 + i * 0.0003 }));
    const far = place("far", { lat: 41.5 });
    const stats = statsByPlace([mineRow(near[9]!)]);
    const out = visiblePlaces([...near, far], region, stats, null);
    expect(out.map((p) => p.id)).not.toContain("far");
    expect(out[0]!.id).toBe("n9"); // lit first
    expect(visiblePlaces([...near, far], region, stats, "far")[0]!.id).toBe("far");
    expect(visiblePlaces(near, region, stats, null, 4)).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(visiblePlaces([], region, stats, null)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("describes the sheet in words: stamped or not, how far, and whether you are inside the geofence", () => {
    const p = place("a", { name: "Katz's", address: "205 E Houston St", geofenceMeters: 100 });
    const inside = sheetModel(p, { stamps: 1, latestAt: "2026-10-04T03:41:00.000Z" }, { lat: p.lat, lng: p.lng }, formatDistance);
    expect(inside).toMatchObject({ title: "Katz's", subtitle: "East Village · 205 E Houston St", status: "Stamped once. Last on 03 OCT 2026.", canStamp: true });
    const far = sheetModel(p, undefined, { lat: p.lat + 0.02, lng: p.lng }, formatDistance);
    expect(far.status).toBe("Not stamped yet.");
    expect(far.canStamp).toBe(false);
    expect(far.distance).toMatch(/^2\.2 km away$/);
    expect(sheetModel(p, { stamps: 4, latestAt: "2026-10-04T03:41:00.000Z" }, null, formatDistance)).toMatchObject({ distance: null, canStamp: false, status: "Stamped 4 times. Last on 03 OCT 2026." });
  });
  it("closes the sheet when it is dragged far enough down or flicked", () => {
    expect(sheetShouldClose(100, 0, 220)).toBe(true);
    expect(sheetShouldClose(40, 0, 220)).toBe(false);
    expect(sheetShouldClose(10, 1200, 220)).toBe(true);
    expect(sheetShouldClose(-50, -900, 220)).toBe(false);
  });
});

===== src/features/map/map-model.ts =====

/** The map's logic, apart from the screen: which pins are lit, which are drawn, what the sheet says. Pure. */
import type { Place, UserPlacesResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { distanceMeters, type Coords } from "@/lib/geo";
import { visaStamp } from "@/features/book/visa";

export type MapRegion = { latitude: number; longitude: number; latitudeDelta: number; longitudeDelta: number };
export type PlaceStat = { stamps: number; latestAt: string };

/** What you have stamped, by place id. Places you have never stamped are not in it. */
export function statsByPlace(userPlaces: UserPlacesResponse["places"]): Map<string, PlaceStat> {
  return new Map(userPlaces.map((u) => [u.place.id, { stamps: u.stamps, latestAt: u.latestStamp.createdAt }]));
}

export type PinKind = "lit" | "dim";
/** A place you have stamped glows brass; one you haven't is a small dim dot. Never told apart by color alone: the sheet says it in words. */
export const pinKind = (placeId: string, stats: ReadonlyMap<string, PlaceStat>): PinKind => (stats.has(placeId) ? "lit" : "dim");

const MAX_PINS = 250;

/**
 * Which places to draw: those in view (with a margin so panning doesn't pop pins in), lit ones first, then the nearest to the middle
 * of the view, at most `max`. The selected place is always drawn.
 */
export function visiblePlaces(places: readonly Place[], region: MapRegion, stats: ReadonlyMap<string, PlaceStat>, selectedId: string | null, max = MAX_PINS): Place[] {
  const padLat = region.latitudeDelta * 0.6;
  const padLng = region.longitudeDelta * 0.6;
  const inView = places.filter(
    (p) => p.id === selectedId || (Math.abs(p.lat - region.latitude) <= padLat && Math.abs(p.lng - region.longitude) <= padLng),
  );
  const centre = { lat: region.latitude, lng: region.longitude };
  const ranked = inView
    .map((p) => ({ p, lit: stats.has(p.id), selected: p.id === selectedId, d: distanceMeters(centre, { lat: p.lat, lng: p.lng }) }))
    .sort((a, b) => Number(b.selected) - Number(a.selected) || Number(b.lit) - Number(a.lit) || a.d - b.d);
  return ranked.slice(0, max).map((r) => r.p);
}

export type SheetModel = {
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  /** In words, so lit and dim are not told apart by glow alone. */
  status: string;
  distance: string | null;
  /** True when your phone looks to be inside the geofence. The server decides for real when you stamp. */
  canStamp: boolean;
};

export function sheetModel(place: Place, stat: PlaceStat | undefined, here: Coords | null, format: (m: number) => string): SheetModel {
  const meters = here ? distanceMeters(here, { lat: place.lat, lng: place.lng }) : null;
  const status = stat
    ? `Stamped ${stat.stamps === 1 ? "once" : `${stat.stamps} times`}. Last on ${visaStamp(stat.latestAt).date}.`
    : "Not stamped yet.";
  return {
    title: place.name,
    subtitle: [place.neighborhood, place.address].filter(Boolean).join(" · "),
    status,
    distance: meters === null ? null : `${format(meters)} away`,
    canStamp: meters !== null && meters <= place.geofenceMeters,
  };
}

/** Whether a drag on the sheet closes it: dragged down far enough, or flicked down. */
export function sheetShouldClose(translationY: number, velocityY: number, sheetHeight: number): boolean {
  return translationY > sheetHeight * 0.35 || velocityY > 800;
}

===== src/features/moderation/__tests__/guideline-1-2.test.tsx =====

/** Apple Guideline 1.2: every stamp and every profile can be reported, and its author blocked, in the app. */
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import { ActionSheetIOS } from "react-native";

import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { StampCard } from "@/features/stamps/stamp-card";
import { testQueryClient } from "@/test/query-client";

jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn(), back: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/auth/auth-context", () => ({ useAuth: () => ({ status: "signedIn" }) }));
jest.mock("@/api", () => ({ api: {}, mediaUrl: (u: string) => u, errorText: (_e: unknown, f: string) => f }));

const stamp = (over: Partial<StampView> = {}): StampView => ({
  id: "s1", photoUrl: null, photos: [], page: 1, slot: 1, caption: "hello", song: null, createdAt: "2026-09-24T02:00:00.000Z", editedAt: null,
  place: { id: "p1", slug: "velvet", name: "Velvet", neighborhood: "East Village", neighborhoodId: null },
  author: { handle: "sam", displayName: "Sam", avatarUrl: null, verified: false }, mine: false, likes: { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false, ...over,
});

    const sheet = jest.spyOn(ActionSheetIOS, "showActionSheetWithOptions").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    await render(<QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}><StampCard stamp={stamp()} /></QueryClientProvider>);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("More options"));
    const menu = sheet.mock.calls[0]![0];
    expect(menu.options).toEqual(["Report stamp", "Block @sam", "Cancel"]);
    expect(menu.destructiveButtonIndex).toEqual([0, 1]);
    // Choosing Report opens the reasons.
    sheet.mock.calls[0]![1](0);
    const reasons = sheet.mock.calls[1]![0].options as string[];
    expect(reasons).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["Under 21 or looks underage", "Stalking or unwanted contact", "Not at this place"]));
    sheet.mockRestore();
  });

  it("your own stamp has no Report or Block (you can edit or delete it from its detail screen)", async () => {
    await render(<QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}><StampCard stamp={stamp({ mine: true })} /></QueryClientProvider>);
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText("More options")).toBeNull();
  });
});


===== src/features/moderation/__tests__/reasons.test.ts =====

import { REPORT_REASONS } from "../content-actions";

describe("report reasons", () => {
  it("include underage, stalking and not at this place, each with its category", () => {
    const by = Object.fromEntries(REPORT_REASONS.map((r) => [r.category, r.label]));
    expect(by.UNDERAGE).toMatch(/underage/i);
    expect(by.STALKING).toMatch(/stalking/i);
    expect(by.NOT_AT_PLACE).toBe("Not at this place");

    expect(new Set(REPORT_REASONS.map((r) => r.category)).size).toBe(REPORT_REASONS.length);
    for (const r of REPORT_REASONS) expect(r.label.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
  });
});


===== src/features/moderation/content-actions.ts =====

/**
 * Report and Block for user content (Guideline 1.2): every stamp and profile can be reported, and every author
 * blocked. Native action sheets and alerts.
 */
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { ActionSheetIOS, Alert } from "react-native";

import { api, ApiError, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { FlagCategory, TargetType } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { colors } from "@/theme";
import { flaggedStore } from "./flagged-store";

/** What a person can say is wrong. The server stores the label as the reason and the category for moderators. */
export const REPORT_REASONS: readonly { label: string; category: FlagCategory }[] = [
  { label: "Spam or scam", category: "SPAM" },
  { label: "Harassment or hate", category: "HARASSMENT" },
  { label: "Sexual or violent content", category: "SEXUAL_OR_VIOLENT" },
  { label: "Not at this place", category: "NOT_AT_PLACE" },
  { label: "Stalking or unwanted contact", category: "STALKING" },
  { label: "Under 21 or looks underage", category: "UNDERAGE" },
  { label: "Someone's private information", category: "PRIVATE_INFO" },
];

export type ChosenReason = { reason: string; category: FlagCategory };

export function useFlag() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { targetType: TargetType; targetId: string; reason: string; category: FlagCategory }) => api.flags.create(v),
    onSuccess: (res, v) => {
      haptics.success();
      if (v.targetType === "STAMP") flaggedStore.add(v.targetId);
      toast.show(res.autoHidden ? "Thanks. It's hidden while we review it." : "Thanks. We'll take a look.");
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["feed"] });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["user"] });
    },
    onError: (e, v) => {
      haptics.warning();
      // Already flagged (409): hide it for this person anyway. Limits (429) come with the server's sentence.
      if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 409 && v.targetType === "STAMP") flaggedStore.add(v.targetId);
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't send that report. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
  });
}

/** Block or unblock a person by handle. */
export function useBlock() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { handle: string; block: boolean }) => api.users.block(v.handle, v.block),
    onSuccess: (_r, v) => {
      toast.show(v.block ? `Blocked @${v.handle}.` : `Unblocked @${v.handle}.`);
      // Blocking hides each other's stamps everywhere.
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries();
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't do that. Try again."), "neutral"),
  });
}

/** Ask for a reason, then flag. */
export function chooseReportReason(
  title: string,
  onPick: (chosen: ChosenReason) => void,
  reasons: readonly { label: string; category: FlagCategory }[] = REPORT_REASONS,
  message = "Reports are reviewed by moderators. The person isn't told who reported them.",
) {
  ActionSheetIOS.showActionSheetWithOptions(
    {
      title,
      message,
      options: [...reasons.map((r) => r.label), "Cancel"],
      cancelButtonIndex: reasons.length,
      tintColor: colors.accent,
      userInterfaceStyle: "dark",
    },
    (i) => {
      const r = reasons[i];
      if (r) onPick({ reason: r.label, category: r.category });
    },
  );
}

export function confirmBlock(handle: string, onConfirm: () => void) {
  Alert.alert(`Block @${handle}?`, "You won't see each other's stamps or profile, and follows are removed. They aren't notified.", [
    { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
    { text: "Block", style: "destructive", onPress: onConfirm },
  ]);
}

export type StampMenuItem = {
  id: string;
  authorHandle: string;
  mine: boolean;
  /** Extra actions shown first (Edit, Delete from profile). */
  extra?: ContentAction[];
};

export type ContentAction = { label: string; destructive?: boolean; onPress: () => void };

/** The actions for a stamp: extras (Edit, Delete) first, then Report and Block for others' stamps. */
export function useStampActions(): (item: StampMenuItem) => ContentAction[] {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const flag = useFlag();
  const block = useBlock();

  return (item) => {
    const actions: ContentAction[] = [...(item.extra ?? [])];
    if (!item.mine) {
      actions.push({
        label: "Report stamp",
        destructive: true,
        onPress: () => {
          if (status !== "signedIn") return router.push("/sign-in");
          chooseReportReason("Report this stamp", (chosen) => flag.mutate({ targetType: "STAMP", targetId: item.id, ...chosen }));
        },
      });
      actions.push({
        label: `Block @${item.authorHandle}`,
        destructive: true,
        onPress: () => {
          if (status !== "signedIn") return router.push("/sign-in");
          confirmBlock(item.authorHandle, () => block.mutate({ handle: item.authorHandle, block: true }));
        },
      });
    }
    return actions;
  };
}

/** Native action sheet for a list of actions. */
export function showActions(actions: ContentAction[]): void {
  if (actions.length === 0) return;
  haptics.select();
  ActionSheetIOS.showActionSheetWithOptions(
    {
      options: [...actions.map((a) => a.label), "Cancel"],
      cancelButtonIndex: actions.length,
      destructiveButtonIndex: actions.map((a, i) => (a.destructive ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0),
      tintColor: colors.accent,
      userInterfaceStyle: "dark",
    },
    (i) => actions[i]?.onPress(),
  );
}

/** The same actions in VoiceOver's actions rotor, so nothing depends on long-press. */
export function a11yActions(actions: ContentAction[]) {
  return {
    accessibilityActions: actions.map((a) => ({ name: a.label, label: a.label })),
    onAccessibilityAction: (e: { nativeEvent: { actionName: string } }) => actions.find((a) => a.label === e.nativeEvent.actionName)?.onPress(),
  };
}

export function isApiError(e: unknown, status: number): e is ApiError {
  return e instanceof ApiError && e.status === status;
}


===== src/features/moderation/flagged-store.ts =====

/**
 * Content this person has reported during the session. Hidden from them right away (Guideline 1.2:
 * reported content shouldn't keep confronting the reporter); moderators decide what happens for
 * everyone else.
 */
import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

let ids = new Set<string>();
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

export const flaggedStore = {
  add(id: string) {
    ids = new Set(ids).add(id);
    listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  },
  has: (id: string) => ids.has(id),
  subscribe(l: () => void) {
    listeners.add(l);
    return () => listeners.delete(l);
  },
  get: () => ids,
};

export function useFlaggedIds(): Set<string> {
  return useSyncExternalStore(flaggedStore.subscribe, flaggedStore.get, flaggedStore.get);
}


===== src/features/moderation/format.ts =====

/** Moderation copy: the API's raw actions, in words. */
import type { z } from "zod";

import type { ModerationAction as ModerationActionSchema } from "@/api/contracts";

type ModerationAction = z.infer<typeof ModerationActionSchema>;

const ACTION_WORD: Record<ModerationAction, string> = {
  FLAGGED: "Flagged",
  AUTO_HIDE: "Auto-hidden",
  HIDE: "Hidden",
  DISMISS: "Dismissed",
  RESTORE: "Restored",
  ACCOUNT_DELETED: "Account deleted",
  SUSPEND: "Suspended",
  UNSUSPEND: "Unsuspended",
};

export function actionWord(a: ModerationAction): string {
  return ACTION_WORD[a];
}

/** Reputation (0.2 to 1.5, new users 0.5) as a percentage of the default. */
export function reputationText(rep: number): string {
  return `trust ${Math.round((rep / 0.5) * 100)}%`;
}


===== src/features/people/__tests__/delete-account.test.tsx =====

/** Guideline 5.1.1(v): account deletion works end to end from the in-app screen. */
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import { Alert } from "react-native";

import { ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import { farewellNotice } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import DeleteAccount from "@/app/(app)/settings/delete-account";
import { testQueryClient } from "@/test/query-client";

const mockForget = jest.fn(async () => undefined);
const mockDelete = jest.fn();

jest.mock("@/auth/auth-context", () => {
  const actual = jest.requireActual("@/auth/auth-context");
  return { useAuth: () => ({ forget: mockForget }), farewellNotice: actual.farewellNotice };
});
jest.mock("@/api", () => {
  const client = jest.requireActual("@/api/client");
  return { api: { me: { deleteAccount: (pw: string) => mockDelete(pw) } }, ApiError: client.ApiError, errorText: client.errorText };
});

async function renderScreen() {
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}>
      <DeleteAccount />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

/** Press the destructive button in the confirmation alert. */
const alert = jest.spyOn(Alert, "alert").mockImplementation((_t, _m, buttons) => {
  buttons?.find((b) => b.style === "destructive")?.onPress?.();
});

const flush = () => act(() => new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, 0)));

describe("Delete account", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    mockForget.mockClear();
    mockDelete.mockReset();
    alert.mockClear();
  });

  it("explains what happens and needs a password", async () => {
    await renderScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText("Your stamps are removed from every passport, and so are your follows and likes.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account")).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("confirms, calls the API with the password, then signs out locally", async () => {
    mockDelete.mockResolvedValue({ deleted: true });
    await renderScreen();
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("delete-password"), "correct horse battery");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account"));
    await flush();
    expect(alert).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Delete your account?", "This can't be undone.", expect.any(Array));
    expect(mockDelete).toHaveBeenCalledWith("correct horse battery");
    await flush();
    expect(mockForget).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(farewellNotice.take()).toBe("Your account is deleted.");
  });

  it("treats a dead token on retry as already deleted", async () => {
    mockDelete.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError(401, "invalid_token", "Sign in again."));
    await renderScreen();
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("delete-password"), "pw");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account"));
    await flush();
    await flush();
    expect(mockForget).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("says nothing about venues or plans (they no longer exist)", async () => {
    await renderScreen();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/venue/i)).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/plans/i)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows a wrong password inline and stays signed in", async () => {
    mockDelete.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError(403, "wrong_password", "Password is incorrect"));
    await renderScreen();
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("delete-password"), "nope");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account"));
    await flush();
    expect(await screen.findByText("That password isn't right.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(mockForget).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("does nothing if the confirmation is cancelled", async () => {
    alert.mockImplementationOnce(() => undefined);
    await renderScreen();
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("delete-password"), "pw");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account"));
    expect(mockDelete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});


===== src/features/people/__tests__/edit-profile.test.ts =====

import { goesPublic, goingPublicAlert, privacyNote, profileChanges, shouldOfferApproveAll } from "../edit-profile";

const priv = { displayName: "Sam", bio: "", isPrivate: true };

describe("going public", () => {
  it("is detected only when a private account is switched to public", () => {
    expect(goesPublic(priv, profileChanges(priv, { ...priv, isPrivate: false }))).toBe(true);
    expect(goesPublic(priv, profileChanges(priv, { ...priv, bio: "hi" }))).toBe(false);
    const pub = { ...priv, isPrivate: false };
    expect(goesPublic(pub, profileChanges(pub, { ...pub, isPrivate: true }))).toBe(false);
    expect(goesPublic(pub, profileChanges(pub, pub))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("the confirm alert lists exactly what changes: all stamps and every place, to anyone", () => {
    const a = goingPublicAlert({ stamps: 12, places: 5 });
    expect(a.title).toBe("Make your passport public?");
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/Anyone, signed in or not/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/all of your stamps \(photos, captions and songs\)/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/every place you've been/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/already stamped/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/12 stamps and 5 places/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/follow you without asking/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/switch back to private/);
    expect(a.confirm).toBe("Make public");
  });

  it("uses singular counts and works without counts", () => {
    expect(goingPublicAlert({ stamps: 1, places: 1 }).message).toMatch(/1 stamp and 1 place so far/);
    expect(goingPublicAlert().message).not.toMatch(/so far/);
    expect(goingPublicAlert({ stamps: 0, places: 0 }).message).not.toMatch(/so far/);
  });

  it("the toggle's note says what each state means and never mentions Anonymous", () => {
    expect(privacyNote(true)).toMatch(/Only people you approve see your stamps, places and counts/);
    expect(privacyNote(false)).toMatch(/Anyone can see your passport/);
    expect(privacyNote(true) + privacyNote(false)).not.toMatch(/Anonymous/);
  });

  it("offers Approve all only after going public with requests waiting", () => {
    expect(shouldOfferApproveAll({ isPrivate: false }, 3)).toBe(true);
    expect(shouldOfferApproveAll({ isPrivate: false }, 0)).toBe(false);
    expect(shouldOfferApproveAll({ isPrivate: true }, 3)).toBe(false);
  });
});

/** The passport screen and the follow button: what each viewer may see. */
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";

let mockProfile: ProfileResponse;
const mockStamps = { data: { pages: [{ stamps: [], nextBefore: null }] }, isPending: false, hasNextPage: false, isFetchNextPageError: false, refetch: jest.fn() };

const relationship: ProfileResponse["relationship"] = { isSelf: false, follow: "none", followsYou: false, mutual: false };

    expect(profileChanges(initial, { displayName: "Sam Lee", bio: "Hi", isPrivate: false })).toEqual({ displayName: "Sam Lee", bio: "Hi", isPrivate: false });
  });
  it("checks the name and bio", () => {
    expect(profileProblem({ ...initial, displayName: " " })).toBe("Add a display name.");
    expect(profileProblem({ ...initial, bio: "x".repeat(151) })).toMatch(/150/);
  });
});

describe("places map", () => {
  it("frames every place, with a minimum span", () => {
    const at = (lat: number, lng: number) => ({ place: { id: `${lat}`, name: "", slug: `${lat}`, lat, lng, neighborhood: "" }, stamps: 1, latestStamp: { id: "s", createdAt: "2026-09-01T00:00:00.000Z", photoUrl: null, caption: "" } });
    expect(placesRegion([])).toBeNull();
    expect(placesRegion([at(40.72, -73.98)])).toMatchObject({ latitude: 40.72, longitude: -73.98, latitudeDelta: 0.01 });
    const r = placesRegion([at(40.7, -74), at(40.8, -73.9)])!;
    expect(r.latitude).toBeCloseTo(40.75);
    expect(r.latitudeDelta).toBeCloseTo(0.14);
  });
});

describe("approve-all prompt", () => {
  it("asks with the count, and closes Edit profile whichever button is pressed", () => {
    const alert = jest.spyOn(Alert, "alert").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const approve = jest.fn();
    const done = jest.fn();
    confirmApproveAll(4, approve, done);
    const [title, , buttons] = alert.mock.calls[0];
    expect(title).toBe("Approve 4 pending requests?");
    buttons?.find((b) => b.text === "Not now")?.onPress?.();
    expect(approve).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(done).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    buttons?.find((b) => b.text === "Approve")?.onPress?.();
    expect(approve).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(done).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    alert.mockRestore();
  });
});


===== src/features/people/edit-profile.ts =====

import type { ProfileSettingsRequest } from "@/api/contracts";

export const BIO_MAX = 150;
export const NAME_MAX = 60;

export type ProfileValues = { displayName: string; bio: string; isPrivate: boolean };

/** Only the fields that changed (the server takes a partial update). Text is compared trimmed. */
export function profileChanges(initial: ProfileValues, values: ProfileValues): ProfileSettingsRequest {
  const out: ProfileSettingsRequest = {};
  if (values.displayName.trim() !== initial.displayName.trim()) out.displayName = values.displayName.trim();
  if (values.bio.trim() !== initial.bio.trim()) out.bio = values.bio.trim();
  if (values.isPrivate !== initial.isPrivate) out.isPrivate = values.isPrivate;
  return out;
}

/** A sentence for what's wrong, or null. */
export function profileProblem(values: ProfileValues): string | null {
  if (values.displayName.trim() === "") return "Add a display name.";
  if (values.displayName.trim().length > NAME_MAX) return `Keep your name under ${NAME_MAX + 1} characters.`;
  if (values.bio.trim().length > BIO_MAX) return `Keep your bio to ${BIO_MAX} characters.`;
  return null;
}

/** The toggle's one-line explanation, for the state it's in now. */
export function privacyNote(isPrivate: boolean): string {
  return isPrivate
    ? "Only people you approve see your stamps, places and counts. Everyone else sees your handle and a Follow button."
    : "Anyone can see your passport: all your stamps and every place you've been.";
}

/** True when this save switches the account from private to public, which changes what strangers can see. */
export function goesPublic(initial: ProfileValues, change: ProfileSettingsRequest): boolean {
  return initial.isPrivate && change.isPrivate === false;
}

/**
 * The alert shown before switching from private to public. It lists exactly what changes, so nobody makes their
 * passport public by accident.
 */
export function goingPublicAlert(counts?: { stamps: number; places: number }): { title: string; message: string; confirm: string } {
  const have = counts && (counts.stamps > 0 || counts.places > 0) ? ` That includes your ${counts.stamps} ${counts.stamps === 1 ? "stamp" : "stamps"} and ${counts.places} ${counts.places === 1 ? "place" : "places"} so far.` : "";
  return {
    title: "Make your passport public?",
    message:
      "Anyone, signed in or not, will be able to see all of your stamps (photos, captions and songs) and every place you've been, including the ones you've already stamped." +
      have +
      " People will be able to follow you without asking. Pending follow requests stay pending until you approve them. You can switch back to private any time.",
    confirm: "Make public",
  };
}

/** Ask "Approve N pending requests?" only after switching from private to public with requests waiting. */
export function shouldOfferApproveAll(change: ProfileSettingsRequest, pendingRequests: number): boolean {
  return change.isPrivate === false && pendingRequests > 0;
}


===== src/features/people/person-row.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import type { PersonView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { VerifiedBadge } from "@/components/ui/verified-badge";

/** Spoken name for a person: "Sam Lee, @sam, verified". */
export function personLabel(p: PersonView): string {
  return [p.displayName, `@${p.handle}`, p.verified ? "verified" : null].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
}

/**
 * A person in a list (search, followers, following, requests, activity). Private accounts you don't follow arrive
 * without a name or photo: the handle and a generic avatar only.
 */
export function PersonRow({
  person,
  subtitle,
  trailing,
  actions,
  onPress,
  accessibilityHint = "Opens their profile",
}: {
  person: PersonView;
  subtitle?: string;
  /** Default: open their profile. */
  onPress?: () => void;
  accessibilityHint?: string;
  trailing?: ReactNode;
  actions?: { label: string; onPress: () => void }[];
}) {
  const title = person.displayName ?? `@${person.handle}`;
  const sub = [person.displayName ? `@${person.handle}` : null, subtitle].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
  return (
    <ListRow
      leading={<Avatar name={person.displayName} uri={person.avatarUrl} />}
      title={title}
      titleAccessory={person.verified ? <VerifiedBadge accessible={false} /> : undefined}
      subtitle={sub || undefined}
      onPress={onPress ?? (() => router.push({ pathname: "/user/[handle]", params: { handle: person.handle } }))}
      accessibilityLabel={[personLabel(person), subtitle].filter(Boolean).join(", ")}
      accessibilityHint={accessibilityHint}
      actions={actions}
      trailing={trailing}
    />
  );
}


===== src/features/people/places.ts =====

import type { UserPlacesResponse } from "@/api/contracts";

type Region = { latitude: number; longitude: number; latitudeDelta: number; longitudeDelta: number };

/** Smallest span shown, so a single place isn't zoomed in to one building. */
const MIN_DELTA = 0.01;
/** Padding around the outermost places, as a share of the span. */
const PAD = 0.4;

/** A map region that frames every place (null when there are none). */
export function placesRegion(places: UserPlacesResponse["places"]): Region | null {
  if (places.length === 0) return null;
  const lats = places.map((p) => p.place.lat);
  const lngs = places.map((p) => p.place.lng);
  const [minLat, maxLat, minLng, maxLng] = [Math.min(...lats), Math.max(...lats), Math.min(...lngs), Math.max(...lngs)];
  return {
    latitude: (minLat + maxLat) / 2,
    longitude: (minLng + maxLng) / 2,
    latitudeDelta: Math.max(MIN_DELTA, (maxLat - minLat) * (1 + PAD)),
    longitudeDelta: Math.max(MIN_DELTA, (maxLng - minLng) * (1 + PAD)),
  };
}


===== src/features/people/profile-screen.tsx =====

import { router, Stack } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { ActionSheetIOS, Pressable, RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View, type NativeScrollEvent, type NativeSyntheticEvent } from "react-native";

import { ApiError, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { ProfileResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useMe, useUserProfile, useUserStamps } from "@/api/queries";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SegmentedControl } from "@/components/ui/segmented-control";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { VerifiedBadge } from "@/components/ui/verified-badge";
import { chooseReportReason, confirmBlock, useBlock, useFlag } from "@/features/moderation/content-actions";
import { SOCIAL_ENABLED } from "@/config";
import { colors, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";
import { PlacesTab, StampsGrid } from "./profile-tabs";
import { followButton, useFollow } from "./use-follow";

type Tab = "stamps" | "places";
/** Ask for the next page of stamps this close to the end of the scroll view. */
const NEXT_PAGE_DISTANCE = 600;

/**
 * A passport: yours (Edit profile, requests, settings) or someone else's (Follow / Requested / Following, Report, Block).
 * A private account you don't follow arrives `access: "limited"`: handle, badge, a generic avatar and the follow button.
 */
export function ProfileScreen({ handle, modal, own }: { handle: string; modal?: boolean; own?: boolean }) {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const signedIn = status === "signedIn";
  const profile = useUserProfile(handle);
  const p = profile.data;
  const full = p?.access === "full";
  const stamps = useUserStamps(handle, full);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("stamps");
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const flag = useFlag();
  const block = useBlock();

  const requireAccount = () => {
    if (signedIn) return true;
    router.push("/sign-in");
    return false;
  };

  const openMenu = () => {
    ActionSheetIOS.showActionSheetWithOptions(
      { options: ["Report account", `Block @${handle}`, "Cancel"], destructiveButtonIndex: 1, cancelButtonIndex: 2, tintColor: colors.accent, userInterfaceStyle: "dark" },
      (i) => {
        if (i === 2 || !requireAccount()) return;
        if (i === 0) chooseReportReason(`Report @${handle}`, (chosen) => flag.mutate({ targetType: "USER", targetId: handle, ...chosen }));
        if (i === 1) confirmBlock(handle, () => block.mutate({ handle, block: true }, { onSuccess: () => router.back() }));
      },
    );
  };

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await Promise.all([profile.refetch(), full ? stamps.refetch() : null]).finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  const onScroll = (e: NativeSyntheticEvent<NativeScrollEvent>) => {
    const { layoutMeasurement, contentOffset, contentSize } = e.nativeEvent;
    const nearEnd = layoutMeasurement.height + contentOffset.y >= contentSize.height - NEXT_PAGE_DISTANCE;
    if (nearEnd && tab === "stamps" && stamps.hasNextPage && !stamps.isFetchingNextPage && !stamps.isFetchNextPageError) void stamps.fetchNextPage();
  };

  const notFound = profile.error instanceof ApiError && profile.error.status === 404;
  const isSelf = own || p?.relationship.isSelf;

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: p ? `@${p.user.handle}` : "",
          headerLeft: modal ? () => <HeaderButton label="Done" bold onPress={() => router.back()} /> : undefined,
          headerRight: () =>
            isSelf ? (
              <Pressable onPress={() => router.push("/settings")} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Settings" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.headerButton}>
                <Icon name="gearshape" color="accent" size={size.icon} />
              </Pressable>
            ) : p ? (
              <Pressable onPress={openMenu} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="More options" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.headerButton}>
                <Icon name="ellipsis.circle" color="accent" size={size.icon} />
              </Pressable>
            ) : null,
        }}
      />
      <ScrollView
        contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        onScroll={onScroll}
        scrollEventThrottle={250}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      >
        {p ? (
          <>
            <Header p={p} />
            {isSelf ? <OwnActions /> : <FollowAction p={p} requireAccount={requireAccount} />}

            {p.access === "limited" ? (
              <View style={styles.private}>
                <Icon name="lock.fill" color="textSecondary" size={size.icon} />
                <Text variant="headline">This account is private</Text>
                <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
                  {p.relationship.follow === "requested" ? "Your request is waiting for their approval." : "Follow to see their stamps and places."}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <>
                <View style={styles.tabs}>
                  <SegmentedControl
                    segments={[
                      { key: "stamps", label: "Stamps" },
                      { key: "places", label: "Places" },
                    ]}
                    value={tab}
                    onChange={setTab}
                    accessibilityLabel="Passport sections"
                  />
                  {tab === "stamps" ? <StampsGrid stamps={stamps} own={!!isSelf} /> : <PlacesTab handle={p.user.handle} />}
                </View>
              </>
            )}
          </>
        ) : notFound ? (
          <View style={styles.gone}>
            <Text variant="title2">This profile isn&apos;t available</Text>
            <Text color="textSecondary">The account may have been deleted.</Text>
            <Button title="Close" variant="secondary" compact block={false} onPress={() => router.back()} />
          </View>
        ) : profile.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(profile.error, "Couldn't load this profile.")} onRetry={() => void profile.refetch()} retrying={profile.isFetching} />
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.identity}>
            <Skeleton width={size.avatarLarge} height={size.avatarLarge} round />
            <View style={styles.names}>
              <Skeleton width="60%" height={size.iconLarge} />
              <Skeleton width="40%" />
            </View>
          </View>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

/** Photo, name, handle and badge, bio, counts. Limited: handle, badge and a generic avatar only. */
function Header({ p }: { p: ProfileResponse }) {
  const full = p.access === "full" ? p : null;
  const handle = p.user.handle;
  const spoken = [full?.user.displayName, `@${handle}`, p.user.verified ? "verified" : null].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
  const openList = (list: "followers" | "following") => router.push({ pathname: "/connections", params: { handle, list } });

  return (
    <View style={styles.header}>
      <View style={styles.identity}>
        <Avatar name={full?.user.displayName ?? null} uri={full?.user.avatarUrl} size={size.avatarLarge} variant="title" />
        <View style={styles.names} accessible accessibilityLabel={spoken}>
          {full && <Text variant="title2">{full.user.displayName}</Text>}
          <View style={styles.handleRow}>
            <Text variant={full ? "subhead" : "title2"} color={full ? "textSecondary" : "textPrimary"}>
              @{handle}
            </Text>
            {p.user.verified && <VerifiedBadge accessible={false} />}
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {full && (
        <View style={styles.counts}>
          <Count value={full.user.counts.stamps} label="Stamps" />
          <Count value={full.user.counts.places} label="Places" />
          {SOCIAL_ENABLED && <Count value={full.user.counts.followers} label="Followers" onPress={() => openList("followers")} />}
          {SOCIAL_ENABLED && <Count value={full.user.counts.following} label="Following" onPress={() => openList("following")} />}
        </View>
      )}
      {full && full.user.bio !== "" && <Text variant="body">{full.user.bio}</Text>}
      {full?.relationship.isSelf && full.user.isPrivate && (
        <View style={styles.privateNote}>
          <Icon name="lock.fill" color="textTertiary" size={size.iconTiny} />
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
            Private account
          </Text>
        </View>
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

function Count({ value, label, onPress }: { value: number; label: string; onPress?: () => void }) {
  const body = (
    <>
      <Text variant="headline" tabular>
        {value}
      </Text>
      <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary">
        {label}
      </Text>
    </>
  );
  const spoken = `${value} ${label.toLowerCase()}`;
  if (!onPress) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.count} accessible accessibilityLabel={spoken}>
        {body}
      </View>
    );
  }
  return (
    <Pressable onPress={onPress} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={spoken} hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.count}>
      {body}
    </Pressable>
  );
}

function FollowAction({ p, requireAccount }: { p: ProfileResponse; requireAccount: () => boolean }) {
  const follow = useFollow();
  const b = followButton(p.relationship.follow, p.user.handle);
  return (
    <Button
      title={b.title}
      icon={b.icon}
      variant={b.variant}
      onPress={() => requireAccount() && follow.toggle(p.user.handle, p.relationship.follow)}
      accessibilityLabel={b.label}
      accessibilityState={{ busy: follow.isPendingFor(p.user.handle) }}
    />
  );
}

/** Your own profile: Edit profile, and entries for follow requests (with the badge count) and activity. */
function OwnActions() {
  const me = useMe();
  const pending = me.data?.social.pendingFollowRequests ?? 0;
  return (
    <View style={styles.own}>
      <Button title="Edit profile" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push("/edit-profile")} />
      {SOCIAL_ENABLED && <ListGroup>
        <ListRow
          icon="person.badge.plus"
          title="Follow requests"
          trailing={pending > 0 ? <CountBadge count={pending} /> : undefined}
          accessibilityLabel={pending > 0 ? `Follow requests, ${pending} waiting` : "Follow requests"}
          chevron
          onPress={() => router.push("/follow-requests")}
        />
      </ListGroup>}
    </View>
  );
}

/** A small count pill (pending requests). Neutral, not the accent: it's information, not an action. */
export function CountBadge({ count }: { count: number }) {
  return (
    <View style={styles.countBadge} accessible={false}>
      <Text variant="footnote" weight="semibold" tabular maxFontSizeMultiplier={1.3}>
        {count > 99 ? "99+" : count}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.xl },
  header: { gap: space.lg },
  identity: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.md },
  names: { flex: 1, gap: space.xxs, alignItems: "flex-start" },
