});


===== src/features/people/profile-tabs.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { memo, useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import MapView, { Marker } from "react-native-maps";

import { errorText, mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useUserPlaces, type useUserStamps } from "@/api/queries";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { Skeleton, SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { plural } from "@/lib/format";
import { colors, hairlineWidth, pressedOpacity, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";
import { placesRegion } from "./places";

const COLUMNS = 3;

/** The Stamps tab: a 3-column grid, newest first. The screen's scroll view asks for the next page near the end. */
export function StampsGrid({ stamps, own }: { stamps: ReturnType<typeof useUserStamps>; own: boolean }) {
  const [width, setWidth] = useState(0);
  const tile = width > 0 ? (width - space.xxs * (COLUMNS - 1)) / COLUMNS : 0;
  const tiles = stamps.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.stamps) ?? [];
  // A failed next page leaves the query in error with the earlier pages kept: keep showing them.
  const nextPageFailed = stamps.isFetchNextPageError;
  const loadMore = () => void stamps.fetchNextPage();

  if (stamps.isPending) return <SkeletonRows count={3} />;
  if (!stamps.data) return <InlineError message={errorText(stamps.error, "Couldn't load stamps.")} onRetry={() => void stamps.refetch()} retrying={stamps.isFetching} />;
  if (tiles.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
        {own ? "Your stamps show here. Go to a place and tap Stamp." : "No stamps yet."}
      </Text>
    );
  }
  return (
    <View style={styles.grid} onLayout={(e) => setWidth(e.nativeEvent.layout.width)}>
      {tile > 0 && tiles.map((s) => <Tile key={s.id} stamp={s} size={tile} />)}
      {stamps.isFetchingNextPage && (
        <View style={styles.more}>
          <Skeleton width={tile} height={tile} />
        </View>
      )}
      {nextPageFailed && <InlineError message="Couldn't load more stamps." onRetry={loadMore} retrying={stamps.isFetchingNextPage} />}
    </View>
  );
}

/** A grid tile: the photo when there is one, otherwise the caption or the song on a plain tile. */
const Tile = memo(function Tile({ stamp, size: side }: { stamp: StampView; size: number }) {
  const label = [`Stamp at ${stamp.place.name}`, stamp.caption || null, stamp.song ? `Song: ${stamp.song.title}` : null].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: "/stamp/[id]", params: { id: stamp.id } })}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={label}
      accessibilityHint="Opens the stamp"
      style={({ pressed }) => [{ width: side, height: side }, styles.tile, pressed && styles.pressed]}
    >
      {stamp.photoUrl ? (
        <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(stamp.photoUrl), cacheKey: stamp.id }} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} contentFit="cover" transition={150} recyclingKey={stamp.id} cachePolicy="memory-disk" />
      ) : (
        <View style={styles.textTile}>
          <Icon name={stamp.song ? "music.note" : "text.quote"} color="textTertiary" size={size.iconSmall} />
          <Text variant="caption" color="textSecondary" numberOfLines={3}>
            {stamp.caption || stamp.song?.title}
          </Text>
        </View>
      )}
      <View style={styles.placeTag}>
        <Text variant="caption" weight="semibold" numberOfLines={1} maxFontSizeMultiplier={1.2}>
          {stamp.place.name}
        </Text>
      </View>
    </Pressable>
  );
});

/** The Places view: a small dark map of every place they've stamped, plus a list with their stamp count at each. */
export function PlacesTab({ handle }: { handle: string }) {
  const places = useUserPlaces(handle);
  const list = places.data?.places ?? [];
  const region = placesRegion(list);

  if (places.isPending) return <SkeletonRows count={3} />;
  if (places.isError) return <InlineError message={errorText(places.error, "Couldn't load places.")} onRetry={() => void places.refetch()} retrying={places.isFetching} />;
  if (list.length === 0 || !region) {
    return (
      <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
        No places yet.
      </Text>
    );
  }
  return (
    <View style={styles.places}>
      <View style={styles.mapWrap} accessible accessibilityLabel={`Map of ${plural(list.length, "place")}`}>
        <MapView
          style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
          initialRegion={region}
          userInterfaceStyle="dark"
          showsPointsOfInterests={false}
          showsCompass={false}
          showsScale={false}
          showsBuildings={false}
          pitchEnabled={false}
          rotateEnabled={false}
          scrollEnabled={false}
          zoomEnabled={false}
          toolbarEnabled={false}
          importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants"
        >
          {list.map((p) => (
            <Marker key={p.place.slug} coordinate={{ latitude: p.place.lat, longitude: p.place.lng }} tracksViewChanges={false} anchor={{ x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }}>
              <View style={styles.dot} />
            </Marker>
          ))}
        </MapView>
      </View>
      <ListGroup>
        {list.map((p) => (
          <ListRow
            key={p.place.slug}
            title={p.place.name}
            subtitle={`${p.place.neighborhood} · ${plural(p.stamps, "stamp")}`}
            trailing={
              <Text variant="headline" tabular>
                {p.stamps}
              </Text>
            }
            chevron
            accessibilityLabel={`${p.place.name}, ${plural(p.stamps, "stamp")}`}
            accessibilityHint="Opens the latest stamp there"
            onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: "/stamp/[id]", params: { id: p.latestStamp.id } })}
          />
        ))}
      </ListGroup>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  grid: { flexDirection: "row", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: space.xxs },
  tile: { backgroundColor: colors.elevated, overflow: "hidden" },
  pressed: { opacity: pressedOpacity },
  textTile: { flex: 1, padding: space.xs, gap: space.xxs, justifyContent: "center" },
  placeTag: {
    position: "absolute",
    left: space.xxs,
    right: space.xxs,
    bottom: space.xxs,
    paddingHorizontal: space.xxs,
    borderRadius: radius.chip,
    backgroundColor: colors.scrim,
  },
  more: { width: "100%" },
  places: { gap: space.lg },
  mapWrap: { height: size.heroHeight, borderRadius: radius.card, overflow: "hidden", backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  dot: { width: size.vibeDot, height: size.vibeDot, borderRadius: size.vibeDot / 2, backgroundColor: colors.textPrimary, borderWidth: hairlineWidth, borderColor: colors.background },
});


===== src/features/people/use-follow.ts =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { FollowState, PeopleListResponse, ProfileResponse, UserSearchResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { qk, useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import type { SymbolName } from "@/components/ui/icon";

const SEARCH = ["users", "search"] as const;
const LISTS = ["users", "connections"] as const;

/** Button copy for a follow state. Private accounts answer a follow with "Requested". */
export function followButton(state: FollowState, handle: string): { title: string; icon: SymbolName; variant: "primary" | "secondary"; label: string } {
  if (state === "following") return { title: "Following", icon: "checkmark", variant: "secondary", label: `Unfollow @${handle}` };
  if (state === "requested") return { title: "Requested", icon: "clock", variant: "secondary", label: `Cancel follow request to @${handle}` };
  return { title: "Follow", icon: "plus", variant: "primary", label: `Follow @${handle}` };
}

function withFollowers(p: ProfileResponse, from: FollowState, to: FollowState): ProfileResponse {
  const relationship = { ...p.relationship, follow: to, mutual: to === "following" && p.relationship.followsYou };
  if (p.access !== "full") return { ...p, relationship };
  const delta = (to === "following" ? 1 : 0) - (from === "following" ? 1 : 0);
  return { ...p, relationship, user: { ...p.user, counts: { ...p.user.counts, followers: Math.max(0, p.user.counts.followers + delta) } } };
}

/**
 * Follow, request, unfollow or cancel. Unfollowing and cancelling are optimistic; a follow waits for the server,
 * which knows whether the account is private (it answers "requested") or public ("following"). In-flight fetches
 * are cancelled first, errors restore the saved copies, and `isPendingFor` lets buttons ignore taps until the
 * server answers, so a follow and an unfollow never race.
 */
export function useFollow() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const me = useMe();

  const setState = (handle: string, from: FollowState, to: FollowState) => {
    const profile = queryClient.getQueryData<ProfileResponse>(qk.user(handle));
    if (profile) queryClient.setQueryData<ProfileResponse>(qk.user(handle), withFollowers(profile, from, to));
    const patch = <T extends { handle: string; follow: FollowState }>(rows: T[]) => rows.map((u) => (u.handle === handle ? { ...u, follow: to } : u));
    queryClient.setQueriesData<UserSearchResponse>({ queryKey: SEARCH }, (r) => (r ? { users: patch(r.users) } : r));
    queryClient.setQueriesData<PeopleListResponse>({ queryKey: LISTS }, (r) => (r ? { people: patch(r.people) } : r));
  };

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { handle: string; current: FollowState }) => api.users.follow(v.handle, v.current === "none"),
    onMutate: async ({ handle, current }) => {
      haptics.select();
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: qk.user(handle) }),
        queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: SEARCH }),
        queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: LISTS }),
      ]);
      const saved = {
        profile: queryClient.getQueryData<ProfileResponse>(qk.user(handle)),
        searches: queryClient.getQueriesData<UserSearchResponse>({ queryKey: SEARCH }),
        lists: queryClient.getQueriesData<PeopleListResponse>({ queryKey: LISTS }),
      };
      if (current !== "none") setState(handle, current, "none");
      return saved;
    },
    onSuccess: (res, { handle, current }) => {
      if (current === "none") setState(handle, "none", res.state);
      if (res.state === "requested") toast.show("Request sent", "neutral");
    },
    onError: (e, { handle }, saved) => {
      haptics.warning();
      if (saved?.profile) queryClient.setQueryData(qk.user(handle), saved.profile);
      for (const [key, data] of [...(saved?.searches ?? []), ...(saved?.lists ?? [])]) queryClient.setQueryData(key, data);
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't update that. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
    onSettled: (_r, _e, v) => {
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.user(v.handle) });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.following });
      // Your own profile's Following count and list.
      const mine = me.data?.user.handle;
      if (mine) {
        void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.user(mine) });
        void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.people(mine, "following") });
      }
    },
  });

  return {
    ...mutation,
    /** Taps on a Follow / Requested / Following button: `current` is the state the button shows. */
    toggle: (handle: string, current: FollowState) => {
      if (mutation.isPending && mutation.variables?.handle === handle) return;
      mutation.mutate({ handle, current });
    },
    isPendingFor: (handle: string) => mutation.isPending && mutation.variables?.handle === handle,
  };
}


===== src/features/people/use-requests.ts =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient, type QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Alert } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { FollowRequestsResponse, PeopleListResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { qk, useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { plural } from "@/lib/format";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";

/** Everything a request decision or a removed follower changes: the list, the badge count, your counts, activity. */
function refreshSocial(queryClient: QueryClient, myHandle: string | undefined) {
  void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.followRequests });
  void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.me, exact: true });
  void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.activity });
  if (myHandle) {
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.user(myHandle) });
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.people(myHandle, "followers") });
  }
}

/** Approve or decline one request. The row leaves the list at once and comes back if the server says no. */
export function useDecideRequest() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const me = useMe();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { handle: string; approve: boolean }) => api.me.decideRequest(v.handle, v.approve),
    onMutate: async ({ handle, approve }) => {
      if (approve) haptics.success();
      else haptics.select();
      await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: qk.followRequests });
      const saved = queryClient.getQueryData<FollowRequestsResponse>(qk.followRequests);
      if (saved) queryClient.setQueryData<FollowRequestsResponse>(qk.followRequests, { requests: saved.requests.filter((r) => r.person.handle !== handle) });
      return saved;
    },
    onError: (e, _v, saved) => {
      haptics.warning();
      if (saved) queryClient.setQueryData(qk.followRequests, saved);
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't update that request. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
    onSettled: () => refreshSocial(queryClient, me.data?.user.handle),
  });
}

/** Approve every pending request (after going public, or from the requests screen). */
export function useApproveAll() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const me = useMe();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: api.me.approveAllRequests,
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      haptics.success();
      toast.show(res.approved > 0 ? `Approved ${plural(res.approved, "request")}` : "No requests to approve", "neutral");
    },
    onError: (e) => {
      haptics.warning();
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't approve those requests. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
    onSettled: () => refreshSocial(queryClient, me.data?.user.handle),
  });
}

/** "Approve 4 pending requests?" Used after switching to public and on the requests screen. */
export function confirmApproveAll(count: number, onApprove: () => void, onDone?: () => void) {
  Alert.alert(`Approve ${plural(count, "pending request")}?`, "They'll start following you.", [
    { text: "Not now", style: "cancel", onPress: onDone },
    {
      text: "Approve",
      onPress: () => {
        onApprove();
        onDone?.();
      },
    },
  ]);
}

/** Remove someone who follows you, after a confirmation. They aren't told; they can request again. */
export function useRemoveFollower() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const me = useMe();
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (handle: string) => api.me.removeFollower(handle),
    onMutate: async (handle) => {
      const myHandle = me.data?.user.handle;
      if (!myHandle) return undefined;
      const key = qk.people(myHandle, "followers");
      await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: key });
      const saved = queryClient.getQueryData<PeopleListResponse>(key);
      if (saved) queryClient.setQueryData<PeopleListResponse>(key, { people: saved.people.filter((p) => p.handle !== handle) });
      return saved ? { key, saved } : undefined;
    },
    onSuccess: () => haptics.success(),
    onError: (e, _h, ctx) => {
      haptics.warning();
      if (ctx) queryClient.setQueryData(ctx.key, ctx.saved);
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't remove that follower. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
    onSettled: () => refreshSocial(queryClient, me.data?.user.handle),
  });
  return {
    ...mutation,
    confirm: (handle: string) =>
      Alert.alert(`Remove @${handle}?`, "They won't be told. They can ask to follow you again.", [
        { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
        { text: "Remove", style: "destructive", onPress: () => mutation.mutate(handle) },
      ]),
  };
}


===== src/features/photos/pick.ts =====

import * as Device from "expo-device";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import { Alert, Linking } from "react-native";

export type PickedImage = { uri: string; width: number; height: number };
/** Why the camera didn't produce a photo. "cancelled" means the person backed out. */
export type CameraOutcome = PickedImage | "cancelled" | "denied" | "no-camera" | "failed";

const OPTIONS: ImagePicker.ImagePickerOptions = { mediaTypes: ["images"], quality: 1, exif: false, allowsEditing: false };

function first(res: ImagePicker.ImagePickerResult): PickedImage | null {
  if (res.canceled || !res.assets?.[0]) return null;
  const a = res.assets[0];
  return { uri: a.uri, width: a.width, height: a.height };
}

/**
 * Camera first. The system asks for camera access (with the purpose string) the first time. If access
 * was already refused before this tap, offer Settings; a fresh "Don't Allow" is respected silently.
 */
export async function takePhoto(): Promise<CameraOutcome> {
  if (!Device.isDevice) return "no-camera";
  const before = await ImagePicker.getCameraPermissionsAsync();
  const perm = before.granted ? before : await ImagePicker.requestCameraPermissionsAsync();
  if (!perm.granted) {
    if (before.status === ImagePicker.PermissionStatus.DENIED && !before.canAskAgain) {
      Alert.alert("Camera is off for Nostos", "Turn on camera access in Settings to take a photo for your stamp.", [
        { text: "Not now", style: "cancel" },
        { text: "Open Settings", onPress: () => void Linking.openSettings() },
      ]);
    }
    return "denied";
  }
  try {
    return first(await ImagePicker.launchCameraAsync(OPTIONS)) ?? "cancelled";
  } catch {
    return "failed";
  }
}

/** The system photo picker (no library permission needed on iOS 14+). */
export async function pickFromLibrary(): Promise<PickedImage | null> {
  return first(await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync(OPTIONS));
}

/** What to tell someone when the camera didn't open. */
export const CAMERA_NOTE: Record<Exclude<CameraOutcome, PickedImage | "cancelled">, string> = {
  denied: "Camera access is off. Turn it on in Settings to take a photo for your stamp.",
  "no-camera": "This device has no camera. Choose a photo from your library instead.",
  failed: "Couldn't open the camera. Try again.",
};


===== src/features/photos/prepare.ts =====

import { ImageManipulator, SaveFormat, type ImageRef } from "expo-image-manipulator";

/** The server re-encodes to 2048 px anyway; sending less keeps uploads fast and under its 5 MB cap. */
export const MAX_EDGE = 2048;
const QUALITY = 0.8;

export type PreparedImage = { uri: string; width: number; height: number };

/**
 * Re-encode as JPEG (HEIC from the library too) with the long edge at most MAX_EDGE. Dimensions come
 * from the decoded image (already rotated), not the picker. Native images are released right away.
 */
export async function prepareImage(uri: string): Promise<PreparedImage> {
  const refs: { release(): void }[] = [];
  try {
    const baseCtx = ImageManipulator.manipulate(uri);
    refs.push(baseCtx);
    const base: ImageRef = await baseCtx.renderAsync();
    refs.push(base);
    let out = base;
    const long = Math.max(base.width, base.height);
    if (long > MAX_EDGE) {
      const ctx = ImageManipulator.manipulate(base);
      refs.push(ctx);
      ctx.resize(base.width >= base.height ? { width: MAX_EDGE } : { height: MAX_EDGE });
      out = await ctx.renderAsync();
      refs.push(out);
    }
    const saved = await out.saveAsync({ compress: QUALITY, format: SaveFormat.JPEG });
    return { uri: saved.uri, width: saved.width, height: saved.height };
  } finally {
    for (const r of refs.reverse()) {
      try {
        r.release();
      } catch {
        // Already released.
      }
    }
  }
}

  });
});

    expect(screen.getByText("Try again")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});


===== src/features/place/place-page.ts =====

/** The place page's words. Pure. */
import type { z } from "zod";

import type { MyPlacePageResponse as MyPlaceSchema } from "@/api/contracts";
import { visaStamp } from "@/features/book/visa";
import { plural } from "@/lib/format";

type MyPlacePageResponse = z.infer<typeof MyPlaceSchema>;

const MONTHS = ["JAN", "FEB", "MAR", "APR", "MAY", "JUN", "JUL", "AUG", "SEP", "OCT", "NOV", "DEC"];

/** "2026-10-03" as "03 OCT 2026". The server's day is already a New York calendar day, so it is read as written. */
export function dayLabel(day: string): string {
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(day);
  if (!m) return day;
  const month = MONTHS[Number(m[2]) - 1];
  return month ? `${m[3]} ${month} ${m[1]}` : day;
}

/** "3 stamps over 2 nights", "1 stamp, 1 night", or null when there are none. */
export function visitSummary(visits: MyPlacePageResponse["visits"]): string | null {
  const stamps = visits.reduce((n, v) => n + v.stamps.length, 0);
  if (stamps === 0) return null;
  return `${plural(stamps, "stamp")} over ${plural(visits.length, "night")}`.replace("1 stamp over 1 night", "1 stamp, 1 night");
}

/** How you stand to the place right now: inside, so you can stamp; or how far; or nothing without a position. */
export function standing(data: Pick<MyPlacePageResponse, "inside" | "distanceM">, format: (m: number) => string): { text: string | null; canStamp: boolean } {
  if (data.inside === null) return { text: null, canStamp: false };
  if (data.inside) return { text: "You're here", canStamp: true };
  return { text: data.distanceM === null ? "Not here yet" : `${format(data.distanceM)} away`, canStamp: false };
}

/** One line for a stamp in the list: its time, then its caption or song if it has one. */
export function stampLine(s: MyPlacePageResponse["stamps"][number]): string {
  const { time } = visaStamp(s.createdAt);
  const what = s.caption.trim() || (s.song ? `${s.song.title}, ${s.song.artist}` : s.photos.length > 0 ? `${plural(s.photos.length, "photo")}` : "");
  return [time, what].filter(Boolean).join("  ·  ");
}


===== src/features/stamps/__tests__/composer.test.ts =====

import { autoPlace, hasContent, stampChanges, orderPlaces, placeDistanceLabel, stampFields, stampProblem, CAPTION_MAX } from "../composer";
import { formatDistance } from "@/lib/format";

const song = { title: "Heroes", artist: "David Bowie", artUrl: "https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/a.jpg", sourceId: "1" };
const place = (over: Partial<{ id: string; inside: boolean; distanceM: number; geofenceMeters: number }>) =>
  ({ id: "p", slug: "p", name: "P", address: "", neighborhood: "East Village", lat: 1, lng: 2, geofenceMeters: 100, neighborhoodId: null, fromOsm: false, distanceM: 10, inside: true, ...over });

describe("stamp content rule", () => {
  it("needs at least one of photo, caption or song", () => {
    expect(hasContent({ photoUris: [], caption: "", song: null })).toBe(false);
    expect(hasContent({ photoUris: [], caption: "   ", song: null })).toBe(false);
    expect(hasContent({ photoUris: ["file://p.jpg"], caption: "", song: null })).toBe(true);
    expect(hasContent({ photoUris: [], caption: "hi", song: null })).toBe(true);
    expect(hasContent({ photoUris: [], caption: "", song })).toBe(true);
  });

  it("says what's missing, in order: place first, then content, then length", () => {
    const empty = { photoUris: [], caption: "", song: null };
    expect(stampProblem(empty, null)).toBe("Choose where you are.");
    expect(stampProblem(empty, "p1")).toBe("Add a photo, a caption or a song.");
    expect(stampProblem({ ...empty, caption: "x".repeat(CAPTION_MAX + 1) }, "p1")).toMatch(/280 characters/);
    expect(stampProblem({ ...empty, caption: "ok" }, "p1")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("stampFields", () => {
  it("sends coordinates always, and only the parts that exist", () => {
    expect(stampFields({ caption: "", song: null }, { lat: 40.7, lng: -73.9 })).toEqual([["lat", "40.7"], ["lng", "-73.9"]]);
    expect(stampFields({ caption: "  hello  ", song }, { lat: 1, lng: 2 })).toEqual([
      ["lat", "1"], ["lng", "2"], ["caption", "hello"], ["songTitle", "Heroes"], ["songArtist", "David Bowie"],
      ["songArtUrl", "https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/a.jpg"], ["songSourceId", "1"],
    ]);
    expect(stampFields({ caption: "", song: { ...song, artUrl: null, sourceId: null } }, { lat: 1, lng: 2 }).map((f) => f[0])).toEqual(["lat", "lng", "songTitle", "songArtist"]);
  });
});

describe("choosing a place", () => {
  it("picks a place for you only when exactly one geofence has you inside", () => {
    expect(autoPlace([place({ id: "a" })])?.id).toBe("a");
    expect(autoPlace([place({ id: "a" }), place({ id: "b" })])).toBeNull();
    expect(autoPlace([place({ id: "a", inside: false })])).toBeNull();
    expect(autoPlace([])).toBeNull();
  });

  it("lists places you're inside first, then the nearest ones", () => {
    const ordered = orderPlaces([place({ id: "far", inside: false, distanceM: 250 }), place({ id: "near", inside: false, distanceM: 150 }), place({ id: "here", inside: true, distanceM: 30 })]);
    expect(ordered.map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(["here", "near", "far"]);
  });

  it("says how far away, in km from 1000 m up", () => {
    expect(placeDistanceLabel(place({ inside: true }), formatDistance)).toBe("You're here");
    expect(placeDistanceLabel(place({ inside: false, distanceM: 140, geofenceMeters: 100 }), formatDistance)).toBe("140 m away, need to be within 100 m");
    expect(placeDistanceLabel(place({ inside: false, distanceM: 1815, geofenceMeters: 100 }), formatDistance)).toBe("1.8 km away, need to be within 100 m");
  });
});

describe("stampChanges (editing)", () => {
  const before = { caption: "before", song };
  it("sends nothing when nothing changed, and only what did", () => {
    expect(stampChanges(before, "before", song)).toEqual({});
    expect(stampChanges(before, "  before  ", { ...song })).toEqual({});
    expect(stampChanges(before, "after", song)).toEqual({ caption: "after" });
    expect(stampChanges(before, "before", null)).toEqual({ song: null });
    expect(stampChanges({ caption: "", song: null }, "", { ...song, title: "New" })).toEqual({ song: { title: "New", artist: "David Bowie", artUrl: song.artUrl, sourceId: "1" } });
    expect(stampChanges(before, "", null)).toEqual({ caption: "", song: null });
  });
});


===== src/features/stamps/__tests__/config.test.tsx =====

/** Social off (the default): no like button. */
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import { testQueryClient } from "@/test/query-client";
import { StampCard } from "../stamp-card";

let mockFlag = false;
jest.mock("@/config", () => ({ get SOCIAL_ENABLED() { return mockFlag; } }));

jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/auth/auth-context", () => ({ useAuth: () => ({ status: "signedIn" }) }));
jest.mock("@/api", () => ({ api: {}, mediaUrl: (u: string) => u, errorText: (_e: unknown, f: string) => f }));

const stamp = {
  id: "s1", photoUrl: null, caption: "hello", song: null, createdAt: "2026-09-24T02:00:00.000Z", editedAt: null,
  place: { id: "p1", slug: "v", name: "Velvet", neighborhood: "East Village", neighborhoodId: null },
  author: { handle: "sam", displayName: "Sam", avatarUrl: null, verified: false }, mine: true, likes: { count: 2, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false,
};

function configWith(flag: string | undefined): boolean {
  const prev = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SOCIAL_ENABLED;
  if (flag === undefined) delete process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SOCIAL_ENABLED;
  else process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SOCIAL_ENABLED = flag;
  let value = true;
  jest.isolateModules(() => {
    value = (jest.requireActual("@/config") as { SOCIAL_ENABLED: boolean }).SOCIAL_ENABLED;
  });
  process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SOCIAL_ENABLED = prev;
  return value;
}

const card = () => render(<QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}><StampCard stamp={stamp as never} /></QueryClientProvider>);

describe("SOCIAL_ENABLED", () => {
  it("is off unless the build sets it to 1", () => {
    expect(configWith(undefined)).toBe(false);
    expect(configWith("0")).toBe(false);
    expect(configWith("1")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("off: a stamp shows no like button", async () => {
    mockFlag = false;
    await card();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText(/like/i)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("on: the like button is there", async () => {
    mockFlag = true;
    await card();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("2 likes")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

const stamp: StampView = {

    mockAuth = "signedIn";
    mockLocalId = "s1";

  it("loading, gone, failed and loaded all show something", async () => {
    mockStamp = { isPending: true };
    const first = await wrap(<StampDetail />);
    expect(first.toJSON()).not.toBeNull();
    await first.unmount();

    mockStamp = { isError: true, error: new ApiError(404, "not_found", "Stamp not found"), refetch: jest.fn() };
    const gone = await wrap(<StampDetail />);
    expect(screen.getByText("This stamp isn't available")).toBeTruthy();
    await gone.unmount();

    mockStamp = { isError: true, error: new ApiError(0, "timeout", "Nostos isn't responding."), refetch: jest.fn() };
    const failed = await wrap(<StampDetail />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Nostos isn't responding.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Try again")).toBeTruthy();
    await failed.unmount();

    mockStamp = { data: { stamp } };
    await wrap(<StampDetail />);
    expect(screen.getByText("hello there")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("David Bowie")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(/@sam · at Velvet/)).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a private account's stamp (404 to you) reads as unavailable, with no author, place or caption leaked", async () => {
    mockStamp = { isError: true, error: new ApiError(404, "not_found", "Stamp not found"), refetch: jest.fn() };
    await wrap(<StampDetail />);
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Sam|sam|Velvet|hello there/)).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Anonymous")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("Song picker", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    quiet();
    jest.useFakeTimers();
    mockSongQueries = [];
    songDraft.clear();
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });
  afterEach(() => { jest.useRealTimers(); jest.restoreAllMocks(); });

  const searchFor = async (text: string) => {
    expect(mockSearchChange).toBeDefined();
    await act(async () => mockSearchChange!({ nativeEvent: { text } }));
    await act(async () => jest.advanceTimersByTime(350));
  };

  it("starts with a typing prompt and can clear the selected song", async () => {
    mockSongs = { isPending: true, fetchStatus: "idle" };
    const a = await wrap(<SongPicker />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Type a song or an artist.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("No song")).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("No song"));
    expect(songDraft.take()).toEqual({ song: null });
    expect(router.back).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await a.unmount();
  });
  it("debounces typing and shows a searching state without results or an empty message", async () => {
    mockSongs = { isPending: true, fetchStatus: "fetching" };
    await wrap(<SongPicker />);
    await act(async () => mockSearchChange?.({ nativeEvent: { text: "  Heroes  " } }));
    await act(async () => jest.advanceTimersByTime(349));
    expect(screen.getByText("Type a song or an artist.")).toBeTruthy();
    await act(async () => jest.advanceTimersByTime(1));
    expect(mockSongQueries.at(-1)).toBe("Heroes");
    expect(screen.getByRole("progressbar", { name: "Loading" })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Nothing found/)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows search results and hands the selected metadata back to the composer", async () => {
    const song = { title: "Heroes", artist: "David Bowie", album: "Heroes", artUrl: null, sourceId: "1" };
    mockSongs = { data: { songs: [song] } };
    await wrap(<SongPicker />);
    await searchFor("Heroes");
    expect(screen.getByText("Song info from Apple Music. Nostos doesn't play music.")).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Heroes by David Bowie"));
    expect(songDraft.take()).toEqual({ song: { title: "Heroes", artist: "David Bowie", artUrl: null, sourceId: "1" } });
    expect(router.back).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("shows the searched text when there are no results", async () => {
    mockSongs = { data: { songs: [] }, isPending: false };
    await wrap(<SongPicker />);
    await searchFor("zz");
    expect(screen.getByText("Nothing found for “zz”.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("progressbar")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Try again")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows upstream failure, retries the search and displays recovered results", async () => {
    const refetch = jest.fn();
    mockSongs = { isError: true, error: new ApiError(503, "song_search_unavailable", "Song search is unavailable."), refetch };
    const view = await wrap(<SongPicker />);
    await searchFor("Heroes");
    expect(screen.getByText("Couldn't search for songs right now.")).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Try again"));
    expect(refetch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    mockSongs = { data: { songs: [{ title: "Heroes", artist: "David Bowie", album: null, artUrl: null, sourceId: "1" }] } };
    await view.rerender(<QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}><SongPicker /></QueryClientProvider>);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Heroes by David Bowie")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Couldn't search for songs right now.")).toBeNull();
  });

});


===== src/features/stamps/__tests__/stamp-flow.test.ts =====

import type { NearbyPlacesResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { HONESTY, MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS, addPhoto, calmState, photoCount, photosLeft, placeRowText, placeSections, removePhotoAt, stampReadiness } from "../stamp-flow";

type Nearby = NearbyPlacesResponse["places"][number];
const place = (over: Partial<Nearby> = {}): Nearby => ({ id: "p", slug: "p", name: "Katz's", address: "", neighborhood: "East Village", lat: 1, lng: 2, geofenceMeters: 100, neighborhoodId: null, fromOsm: false, distanceM: 20, inside: true, ...over });

describe("photos", () => {
  it("holds six at most, and a seventh leaves the list as it was", () => {
    let list: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) list = addPhoto(list, `p${i}`);
    expect(list).toEqual(["p0", "p1", "p2", "p3", "p4", "p5"]);
    expect(MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS).toBe(6);
  });
  it("removes the one you choose and keeps the order", () => {
    expect(removePhotoAt(["a", "b", "c"], 1)).toEqual(["a", "c"]);
    expect(removePhotoAt(["a"], 5)).toEqual(["a"]);
  });
  it("counts what is left, and says n of 6", () => {
    expect(photosLeft([])).toBe(6);
    expect(photosLeft(["a", "b"])).toBe(4);
    expect(photosLeft(Array(9).fill("x"))).toBe(0);
    expect(photoCount(2)).toBe("2 of 6");
    expect(photoCount(9)).toBe("6 of 6");
  });
});

describe("the places list", () => {
  it("splits Here from Nearby and leaves out an empty group", () => {
    const secs = placeSections([place({ id: "a" }), place({ id: "b", inside: false, distanceM: 150 }), place({ id: "c" })]);
    expect(secs.map((s) => [s.title, s.places.map((p) => p.id)])).toEqual([["Here", ["a", "c"]], ["Nearby", ["b"]]]);
    expect(placeSections([place({ inside: false })]).map((s) => s.title)).toEqual(["Nearby"]);
    expect(placeSections([])).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("says how far you are, in kilometers from 1000 m up", () => {
    expect(placeRowText(place({ inside: false, distanceM: 140 })).subtitle).toBe("East Village · 140 m away, need to be within 100 m");
    expect(placeRowText(place({ inside: false, distanceM: 1815 })).subtitle).toBe("East Village · 1.8 km away, need to be within 100 m");
    expect(placeRowText(place({ inside: false, distanceM: 999 })).subtitle).toContain("999 m away");
    expect(placeRowText(place({ inside: false, distanceM: 1000 })).subtitle).toContain("1.0 km away");
    expect(placeRowText(place()).spoken).toBe("Katz's, You're here");
  });
});

describe("the calm disabled state", () => {
  it("is quiet and says how far away and how close you need to be, in km when far", () => {
    expect(calmState(place({ inside: false, distanceM: 1815, geofenceMeters: 100 }))).toEqual({
      title: "Not quite there yet",
      body: "You're 1.8 km from Katz's. Stamping opens when you're within 100 m.",
    });
    expect(calmState(place({ inside: false, distanceM: 140 }))!.body).toContain("140 m from Katz's");
  });
  it("is absent when you're inside or have chosen nothing", () => {
    expect(calmState(place())).toBeNull();
    expect(calmState(null)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("never reads like an error", () => {
    const body = JSON.stringify(calmState(place({ inside: false, distanceM: 500 })));
    expect(body).not.toMatch(/error|failed|wrong|denied|can't|cannot|invalid/i);
  });
});

describe("when the Stamp button is on", () => {
  it("is on when you're inside and the stamp has something in it", () => {
    expect(stampReadiness({ place: place(), problem: null, busy: false })).toEqual({ ready: true, hint: null, calm: null });
  });
  it("is off while stamping, and says nothing", () => {
    expect(stampReadiness({ place: place(), problem: null, busy: true })).toMatchObject({ ready: false, hint: null });
  });
  it("outside the geofence it is off and shows the calm state instead of a hint", () => {
    const r = stampReadiness({ place: place({ inside: false, distanceM: 300 }), problem: "Add a photo, a caption or a song.", busy: false });
    expect(r.ready).toBe(false);
    expect(r.hint).toBeNull();
    expect(r.calm?.title).toBe("Not quite there yet");
  });
  it("inside but empty it says what to add; with no place chosen it says to choose", () => {
    expect(stampReadiness({ place: place(), problem: "Add a photo, a caption or a song.", busy: false })).toMatchObject({ ready: false, hint: "Add a photo, a caption or a song." });
    expect(stampReadiness({ place: null, problem: "Choose where you are.", busy: false })).toMatchObject({ ready: false, hint: "Choose where you are." });
  });
});

describe("the honesty note", () => {
  it("is plain and does not claim the position is never stored, because the server saves it with the stamp", () => {
    expect(HONESTY.body).not.toMatch(/never stored|not stored|isn't stored|don't store|never saved/i);
    expect(HONESTY.body).toMatch(/saved with the stamp/);
    expect(HONESTY.body).toMatch(/never shown/);
  });
});

      await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Take photo" }));
    });
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("Couldn't take that photo. Try again.")).toBeTruthy());
    expect(photoDraft.get()).toEqual([]);
  });
});


===== src/features/stamps/cache.ts =====

import type { InfiniteData, QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import type { StampResponse, StampsPageResponse, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { qk } from "@/api/keys";

/** Apply `patch` to one stamp wherever it's cached: its detail, the feed pages and every passport grid. */
export function patchStamp(queryClient: QueryClient, stampId: string, patch: (s: StampView) => StampView) {
  const page = (p: StampsPageResponse): StampsPageResponse => ({ ...p, stamps: p.stamps.map((s) => (s.id === stampId ? patch(s) : s)) });
  queryClient.setQueryData<StampResponse>(qk.stamp(stampId), (r) => (r ? { stamp: patch(r.stamp) } : r));
  for (const key of [qk.feed, ["user"]] as const) {
    queryClient.setQueriesData<InfiniteData<StampsPageResponse>>({ queryKey: key }, (d) => (d && "pages" in d ? { ...d, pages: d.pages.map(page) } : d));
  }
}

/** Drop a stamp from the feed pages and passport grids (after "Delete from profile" or a block). */
export function dropStamp(queryClient: QueryClient, stampId: string) {
  const page = (p: StampsPageResponse): StampsPageResponse => ({ ...p, stamps: p.stamps.filter((s) => s.id !== stampId) });
  queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: qk.stamp(stampId) });
  for (const key of [qk.feed, ["user"]] as const) {
    queryClient.setQueriesData<InfiniteData<StampsPageResponse>>({ queryKey: key }, (d) => (d && "pages" in d ? { ...d, pages: d.pages.map(page) } : d));
  }
}


===== src/features/stamps/composer.ts =====

/** The pure parts of the stamp composer: what's required, what's sent, what to say. The server decides everything else. */
import type { NearbyPlacesResponse, Song, StampEditRequest, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import type { Coords } from "@/lib/geo";

export const CAPTION_MAX = 280;

export type Draft = { photoUris: readonly string[]; caption: string; song: Song | null };

/** A stamp needs at least one of a photo, a caption (not just spaces) or a song. */
export function hasContent(d: Draft): boolean {
  return d.photoUris.length > 0 || d.caption.trim() !== "" || d.song !== null;
}

/** Why the Stamp button is off, or null when it's ready. `placeId` is the chosen place. */
export function stampProblem(d: Draft, placeId: string | null): string | null {
  if (!placeId) return "Choose where you are.";
  if (!hasContent(d)) return "Add a photo, a caption or a song.";
  if (d.caption.length > CAPTION_MAX) return `Keep the caption to ${CAPTION_MAX} characters.`;
  return null;
}

/** The multipart text fields for a stamp (the server reads numbers as strings). Empty parts are left out. */
export function stampFields(d: Pick<Draft, "caption" | "song">, coords: Coords): [string, string][] {
  const fields: [string, string][] = [
    ["lat", String(coords.lat)],
    ["lng", String(coords.lng)],
  ];
  if (d.caption.trim() !== "") fields.push(["caption", d.caption.trim()]);
  if (d.song) {
    fields.push(["songTitle", d.song.title], ["songArtist", d.song.artist]);
    if (d.song.artUrl) fields.push(["songArtUrl", d.song.artUrl]);
    if (d.song.sourceId) fields.push(["songSourceId", d.song.sourceId]);
  }
  return fields;
}

type Nearby = NearbyPlacesResponse["places"][number];

/** The place to pick without asking: exactly one place has you inside its geofence. */
export function autoPlace(places: readonly Nearby[]): Nearby | null {
  const inside = places.filter((p) => p.inside);
  return inside.length === 1 ? inside[0]! : null;
}

/** Inside places first (nearest first), then the ones close by. The list from the server is already nearest first. */
export function orderPlaces(places: readonly Nearby[]): Nearby[] {
  return [...places].sort((a, b) => Number(b.inside) - Number(a.inside) || a.distanceM - b.distanceM);
}

/** A place row's second line: "Inside" or the distance, via the shared formatter. */
export function placeDistanceLabel(p: Pick<Nearby, "inside" | "distanceM" | "geofenceMeters">, format: (m: number) => string): string {
  return p.inside ? "You're here" : `${format(p.distanceM)} away, need to be within ${format(p.geofenceMeters)}`;
}

/** The edit screen sends only the fields that changed (the server takes a partial update). A removed song is `null`. */
export function stampChanges(initial: Pick<StampView, "caption" | "song">, caption: string, song: Song | null): StampEditRequest {
  const out: StampEditRequest = {};
  if (caption.trim() !== initial.caption.trim()) out.caption = caption.trim();
  const before = initial.song;
  const same = (before === null && song === null) || (before !== null && song !== null && before.title === song.title && before.artist === song.artist && before.sourceId === song.sourceId);
  if (!same) out.song = song ? { title: song.title, artist: song.artist, artUrl: song.artUrl, sourceId: song.sourceId } : null;
  return out;
}



===== src/features/stamps/landing-draft.ts =====

/** Hands a fresh stamp from the composer to the landing screen without route params (a stamp is too big for a URL). */
import type { NeighborhoodProgress, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";

let landed: { stamp: StampView; progress: NeighborhoodProgress | null } | null = null;

export const landingDraft = {
  set(stamp: StampView, progress: NeighborhoodProgress | null) {
    landed = { stamp, progress };
  },
  /** Read without clearing, so the screen can re-render. */
  peek: () => landed,
  clear() {
    landed = null;
  },
};


===== src/features/stamps/like-button.tsx =====

import { Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";

import type { LikeSummary } from "@/api/contracts";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { plural } from "@/lib/format";
import { size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Spoken like state: "Like, 4 likes" / "Unlike, 5 likes, you liked this". */
export function likeLabel(likes: LikeSummary): string {
  return `${likes.viewerLiked ? "Unlike" : "Like"}, ${plural(likes.count, "like")}${likes.viewerLiked ? ", you liked this" : ""}`;
}

/** Heart and count. `disabled` for your own stamps: you see the count but can't like them. */
export function LikeButton({ likes, onPress, disabled }: { likes: LikeSummary; onPress: () => void; disabled?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={onPress}
      disabled={disabled}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={disabled ? plural(likes.count, "like") : likeLabel(likes)}
      accessibilityState={{ selected: likes.viewerLiked, disabled: !!disabled }}
      hitSlop={space.xs}
      style={styles.button}
    >
      <Icon name={likes.viewerLiked ? "heart.fill" : "heart"} color={likes.viewerLiked ? "accent" : "textSecondary"} size={size.icon} />
      <Text variant="subhead" weight="semibold" tabular color="textSecondary">
        {likes.count}
      </Text>
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  button: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.xxs, minHeight: size.hitTarget, minWidth: size.hitTarget },
});


===== src/features/stamps/photo-draft.ts =====

/**
 * The photos for the stamp being made, shared between the composer and the in-app camera. Photos come from the camera only:
 * a stamp's photos are proof you were there, so there is no way to add one from the library.
 */
import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

import { addPhoto, removePhotoAt } from "./stamp-flow";

let uris: string[] = [];
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
const emit = () => listeners.forEach((l) => l());

export const photoDraft = {
  get: (): readonly string[] => uris,
  subscribe(l: () => void) {
    listeners.add(l);
    return () => listeners.delete(l);
  },
  /** Adds a photo; false when there are already six. */
  add(uri: string): boolean {
    const next = addPhoto(uris, uri);
    if (next.length === uris.length) return false;
    uris = next;
    emit();
    return true;
  },
  remove(index: number) {
    uris = removePhotoAt(uris, index);
    emit();
  },
  clear() {
    if (uris.length === 0) return;
    uris = [];
    emit();
  },
};

export const usePhotoDraft = (): readonly string[] => useSyncExternalStore(photoDraft.subscribe, photoDraft.get, photoDraft.get);


===== src/features/stamps/song-draft.ts =====

/**
 * Hands a picked song from the song picker screen back to the screen that opened it (composer or edit), without
 * route params. The opener reads and clears it when it regains focus.
 */
import type { Song } from "@/api/contracts";

let picked: { song: Song | null } | null = null;

export const songDraft = {
  /** `song: null` means "remove the song". */
  choose(song: Song | null) {
    picked = { song };
  },
  /** Returns the pick once, then forgets it. undefined = nothing was picked. */
  take(): { song: Song | null } | undefined {
    const p = picked ?? undefined;
    picked = null;
    return p;
  },
  clear() {
    picked = null;
  },
};


===== src/features/stamps/song-row.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";

/** The spoken form: "Song: Heroes by David Bowie". */
export function songLabel(song: Pick<Song, "title" | "artist">): string {
  return `Song: ${song.title} by ${song.artist}`;
}

/** A song's art, title and artist. Metadata only: there is no playback. */

          {song.title}
        </Text>
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary" numberOfLines={1}>
          {song.artist}
        </Text>
      </View>

    </View>
  );
}

===== src/features/stamps/stamp-card.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { memo } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { VerifiedBadge } from "@/components/ui/verified-badge";
import { a11yActions, showActions, useStampActions } from "@/features/moderation/content-actions";
import { formatAge } from "@/lib/format";
import { SOCIAL_ENABLED } from "@/config";
import { aspect, colors, pressedOpacity, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";
import { LikeButton } from "./like-button";
import { SongRow, songLabel } from "./song-row";
import { useLike } from "./use-like";

/** Spoken summary of a stamp: "Stamp by @sam at The Place: caption. Song: ... 3 likes". */
export function stampLabel(s: StampView): string {
  return [`Stamp by @${s.author.handle}${s.author.verified ? ", verified" : ""} at ${s.place.name}`, s.caption || null, s.song ? songLabel(s.song) : null, s.photoUrl ? "with a photo" : null]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(". ");
}

/**
 * One stamp in the feed: who stamped where and when, the photo (if any), the caption, the song, and the like button.
 * Opens the stamp. The author is always a full profile view, because a stamp only reaches people who may see it.
 */
export const StampCard = memo(function StampCard({ stamp }: { stamp: StampView }) {
  const like = useLike();
  const build = useStampActions();
  const actions = build({ id: stamp.id, authorHandle: stamp.author.handle, mine: stamp.mine });
  const open = () => router.push({ pathname: "/stamp/[id]", params: { id: stamp.id } });

  return (
    <View style={styles.card}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: "/user/[handle]", params: { handle: stamp.author.handle } })}
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityLabel={`@${stamp.author.handle}${stamp.author.verified ? ", verified" : ""}`}
          accessibilityHint="Opens their passport"
          style={styles.who}
        >
          <Avatar name={stamp.author.displayName} uri={stamp.author.avatarUrl} />
          <View style={styles.names}>
            <View style={styles.handleRow}>
              <Text variant="subhead" weight="semibold" numberOfLines={1} style={styles.shrink}>
                {stamp.author.displayName}
              </Text>
              {stamp.author.verified && <VerifiedBadge accessible={false} />}
            </View>
            <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary" numberOfLines={1}>
              {stamp.place.name} · {formatAge(stamp.createdAt)}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </Pressable>
        {actions.length > 0 && (
          <Pressable onPress={() => showActions(actions)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="More options" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.more}>
            <Icon name="ellipsis" color="textSecondary" size={size.icon} />
          </Pressable>
        )}
      </View>

      <Pressable onPress={open} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={stampLabel(stamp)} accessibilityHint="Opens the stamp" {...a11yActions(actions)} style={({ pressed }) => [styles.body, pressed && styles.pressed]}>
        {stamp.photoUrl && <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(stamp.photoUrl), cacheKey: stamp.id }} style={styles.photo} contentFit="cover" transition={150} recyclingKey={stamp.id} accessible={false} />}
        {stamp.caption !== "" && (
          <Text variant="body" style={styles.caption}>
            {stamp.caption}
          </Text>
        )}
        {stamp.song && (
          <View style={styles.song}>
            <SongRow song={stamp.song} />
          </View>
        )}
      </Pressable>

      <View style={styles.footer}>
        {SOCIAL_ENABLED && <LikeButton likes={stamp.likes} disabled={stamp.mine} onPress={() => like.toggle(stamp.id, stamp.likes)} />}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
});

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  card: { gap: space.xs, paddingVertical: space.sm },
  header: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.sm, paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  who: { flex: 1, flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.sm, minHeight: size.hitTarget },
  names: { flex: 1, gap: space.xxs },
  handleRow: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.xxs },
  shrink: { flexShrink: 1 },
  more: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "flex-end", justifyContent: "center" },
  body: { gap: space.xs },
  pressed: { opacity: pressedOpacity },
  photo: { width: "100%", aspectRatio: aspect.post, backgroundColor: colors.elevated },
  caption: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  song: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  footer: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
});


===== src/features/stamps/stamp-flow.ts =====

/** The pure parts of the stamp flow beyond the composer's rules: the photo cap, the place list, the calm "not there yet" state, the honesty note. */
import type { NearbyPlacesResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { formatDistance } from "@/lib/format";
import { placeDistanceLabel } from "./composer";

type Nearby = NearbyPlacesResponse["places"][number];

export const MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS = 6;

/**
 * What the app says about location, and it has to be true: the phone's position is checked by the server when you stamp and is saved
 * with the stamp (that is how the check can be audited); it is never shown in the app. Whether this wording is enough for a privacy
 * policy is a legal question (OPEN-QUESTIONS).
 */
export const HONESTY = {
  title: "Stamps only count where you really are",
  body: "When you stamp, our server checks where your phone is. That position is saved with the stamp and is never shown in the app.",
};

/** Add a photo taken in the app, up to six. Past six the list is returned as it was. */
export const addPhoto = (uris: readonly string[], uri: string): string[] => (uris.length >= MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS ? [...uris] : [...uris, uri]);
export const removePhotoAt = (uris: readonly string[], index: number): string[] => uris.filter((_, i) => i !== index);
export const photosLeft = (uris: readonly string[]): number => Math.max(0, MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS - uris.length);

/** "n of 6" for the camera and the strip. */
export const photoCount = (n: number): string => `${Math.min(n, MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS)} of ${MAX_STAMP_PHOTOS}`;

/** The places list split into "Here" (you're inside the geofence) and "Nearby" (close, not inside yet); empty groups are left out. */
export function placeSections(places: readonly Nearby[]): { title: string; places: Nearby[] }[] {
  const here = places.filter((p) => p.inside);
  const near = places.filter((p) => !p.inside);
  return [
    ...(here.length > 0 ? [{ title: "Here", places: here }] : []),
    ...(near.length > 0 ? [{ title: "Nearby", places: near }] : []),
  ];
}

/** One place row's lines: its name, then the neighborhood and how you stand. Distances over a kilometer are in km. */
export function placeRowText(p: Nearby): { title: string; subtitle: string; spoken: string } {
  const standing = placeDistanceLabel(p, formatDistance);
  return { title: p.name, subtitle: `${p.neighborhood} · ${standing}`, spoken: `${p.name}, ${standing}` };
}

/**
 * When the chosen place is outside its geofence the button is off, and the screen says so gently: no alarm, just how far you are
 * and what to do. Null when you're inside (or have chosen nothing).
 */
export function calmState(place: Nearby | null): { title: string; body: string } | null {
  if (!place || place.inside) return null;
  return {
    title: "Not quite there yet",
    body: `You're ${formatDistance(place.distanceM)} from ${place.name}. Stamping opens when you're within ${formatDistance(place.geofenceMeters)}.`,
  };
}

/** Whether the Stamp button is on, and if not, the one thing to say. The calm state wins over a missing caption: get there first. */
export function stampReadiness(input: { place: Nearby | null; problem: string | null; busy: boolean }): { ready: boolean; hint: string | null; calm: ReturnType<typeof calmState> } {
  const calm = calmState(input.place);
  if (input.busy) return { ready: false, hint: null, calm };
  if (calm) return { ready: false, hint: null, calm };
  if (input.problem) return { ready: false, hint: input.problem, calm: null };
  return { ready: true, hint: null, calm: null };
}


===== src/features/stamps/use-like.ts =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router } from "expo-router";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { LikeSummary } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { patchStamp } from "./cache";

/** What a like or unlike does to a summary, before the server answers. */
export function toggledLikes(current: LikeSummary): LikeSummary {
  const like = !current.viewerLiked;
  return { viewerLiked: like, count: Math.max(0, current.count + (like ? 1 : -1)) };
}

/**
 * Like or unlike a stamp, optimistically, with a light haptic. Signed-out taps go to sign-in. A second tap while one is
 * in flight is ignored, so the count can't drift.
 */
export function useLike() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const set = (stampId: string, likes: LikeSummary) => patchStamp(queryClient, stampId, (s) => ({ ...s, likes }));
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { stampId: string; current: LikeSummary }) => api.stamps.like(v.stampId, !v.current.viewerLiked),
    onMutate: ({ stampId, current }) => {
      haptics.select();
      set(stampId, toggledLikes(current));
    },
    onSuccess: (res, { stampId }) => set(stampId, { count: res.count, viewerLiked: res.viewerLiked }),
    onError: (e, { stampId, current }) => {
      haptics.warning();
      set(stampId, current);
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't update that like. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
  });
  return {
    ...mutation,
    /** `current` is the summary the heart shows. `onlyLike` (double-tap) never unlikes. */
    toggle: (stampId: string, current: LikeSummary, onlyLike = false) => {
      if (status !== "signedIn") return router.push("/sign-in");
      if (onlyLike && current.viewerLiked) return haptics.select();
      if (mutation.isPending && mutation.variables?.stampId === stampId) return;
      mutation.mutate({ stampId, current });
    },
  };
}

===== src/components/brand/emblem-mark.tsx =====

import Svg, { Path } from "react-native-svg";

import { emblem } from "@/features/book/emblem";
import { bookStyle, colors, type ColorToken } from "@/theme";

/** The Nostos emblem (two rings, ticks, a compass star and a doorway) as a small flat mark, for headers and quiet screens. */
export function EmblemMark({ size, color = "brass" }: { size: number; color?: ColorToken }) {
  const e = emblem();
  const ink = colors[color];
  return (
    <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="-1.05 -1.05 2.1 2.1" accessibilityElementsHidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
      {e.rings.map((d, i) => (
        <Path key={i} d={d} fill="none" stroke={ink} strokeWidth={i === 0 ? bookStyle.emblemRing * 2 : bookStyle.emblemInner * 2} />
      ))}
      <Path d={e.ticks} stroke={ink} strokeWidth={bookStyle.emblemTick * 2} />
      <Path d={e.star} fill={ink} />
      <Path d={e.door} fill={colors.black} />
    </Svg>
  );
}

===== src/components/ui/avatar.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import { initial } from "@/lib/format";
import { avatarOverlap, colors, hairlineWidth, size as sizes, type TypeVariant } from "@/theme";
import { Icon } from "./icon";
import { Text } from "./text";

type Props = {
  name: string | null | undefined;
  /** Profile photo. null when there is none or the viewer may not see it (private account): show the initial. */
  uri?: string | null;
  size?: number;
  variant?: TypeVariant;
};

/** Profile photo, or the initial on an elevated circle. With no name either, a generic person symbol. */
export function Avatar({ name, uri, size = sizes.avatar, variant = "headline" }: Props) {
  const circle = [styles.circle, { width: size, height: size, borderRadius: size / 2 }];
  if (uri) {
    return <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(uri) }} style={circle} contentFit="cover" transition={150} accessible={false} />;
  }
  return (
    <View style={circle} accessible={false}>
      {name ? (
        <Text variant={variant} color="textSecondary" weight="semibold" maxFontSizeMultiplier={1}>
          {initial(name)}
        </Text>
      ) : (
        <Icon name="person.fill" color="textTertiary" size={size / 2} />
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

/** Up to three overlapping avatars. The row around it carries the spoken label. */
export function AvatarStack({ names, max = 3 }: { names: string[]; max?: number }) {
  const shown = names.slice(0, max);
  if (shown.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <View style={styles.stack} accessible={false}>
      {shown.map((n, i) => (
        <View key={`${n}-${i}`} style={[styles.ring, i > 0 && styles.overlap]}>
          <Avatar name={n} size={sizes.avatarSmall} variant="caption" />
        </View>
      ))}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  circle: { backgroundColor: colors.elevated, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", borderWidth: hairlineWidth, borderColor: colors.hairline },
  stack: { flexDirection: "row" },
  ring: { borderRadius: sizes.avatarSmall, borderWidth: sizes.markerRing, borderColor: colors.surface },
  overlap: { marginLeft: avatarOverlap },
});
